# Task briefs — build-initial-mvp

Companion to tasks.md: the full brief for every unit, same ids, same order. Generated 2026-09-07 from the decomposition run; aligned to design.md D16–D40.

## Dropped or merged units

- 4.5.2 → absorbed by 2.6.2: Check-in completeness lives only in packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts `checkinStatus` (D16); 4.5.3 and 6.1 call it directly, so no API-side status module exists and its six cases are covered by 2.6.2's seven unit cases plus 4.5.3 integration cases 3–5.
- 5.7.1 → absorbed by 2.3.2: D16 places recall rules (thresholds, deriveRecallFlags, recall scoring) in packages/shared/src/domain/recall.ts, owned by unit 2.3.2, not by a separate group-5b shared unit; 5.7.2 now imports RECALL_DELAY_FLAG_SECONDS, RECALL_OVERRUN_FLAG_SECONDS, recallDelaySeconds and deriveRecallFlags directly from 2.3.2, and 5.8.3 imports deriveRecallScore from the same module.
- 8.1.1 → absorbed by 7.1.1: D16 assigns the web component-test harness (jsdom, Testing Library, renderWithProviders, mockClient, fakeOutbox, expectNoIdentifiers) to group 7 (7.1.1) as the single owner; every 8.x unit that depended on 8.1.1 now depends on 7.1.1 instead, and every in-prose reference to '8.1.1 harness' now reads '7.1.1 harness'.
- 8.6.1 → absorbed by 7.1.1: The apps/web component-test harness (jsdom, Testing Library, renderWithProviders, mockClient with the design.md error envelope, expectNoIdentifiers) is created once in group 7 per D16; every frontend unit in this group now depends on 7.1.1 instead.
- 8.10.1 → absorbed by 8.5.2: SyncStatus (Pending + Retry, Saved, 'could not be saved on this device' + keep-trying) is a single component owned by group 8a's 8.5.2 and reused by Focus and Benchmark Running per D16; group 8b's units that showed sync state now consume it instead of rebuilding it.
- 8.10.3 → absorbed by 7.4.2: There is no page-load auto-redirect (D17): the SessionModeProvider (7.4.2) fetches GET /sessions/active on boot, replays that session's outbox once and purges other sessions' rows, and screens (ActiveSessionCard, session routes) decide what to render from that state — no separate resolver component exists.
- 8.10.4 → absorbed by 8.2.5: ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5, group 8a) replaces Start controls on Today and Benchmark Ready and renders the 409 active_session_exists / stale-transition notices per D16, so a dedicated ActiveSessionNotice in this group is redundant.
- 8.10.5 → absorbed by 7.3.5: useFinalizeSession moves into group 7 as 7.3.5 per D16, wrapping finalizeWithSync/abandonWithPurge (7.3.4) and owning the sessionStorage finalize:{sessionId} key; PracticeReview (8.6.2) now only consumes it.
- 8.10.6 → absorbed by 7.4.4: useStartSession (one idempotency key per logical start, same-key retries, 409 active_session_exists hand-off) is owned by group 7's 7.4.4 per D16; the Today/Benchmark-ready call sites consume that hook instead of a group-8b copy.

## Decisions recorded by the condensing pass

- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] The shared eligibility function is named evaluateEligibility with a field-level EligibilityInput (completeInterval, recallLockedAt, recallScores, episodeCount, materiallyDisrupted, timerQuality, sessionLocalDate, slotAssignedLocalDate, excludedByAmendment, realm, timeSource); 5.8.4 passes the stored columns into these fields under this name.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] deriveFirstSwitch consults event timing only when countMethod is 'event'; a retrospective count uses the optional estimate or yields Unknown, and event tallies are never added to a retrospective count.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Band ceilings: Day 0 uses the first band (600 s); days beyond 14 use the last band; bandCeilingSeconds and suggestProgression accept the governing revision's bandCeilings and default to DEFAULT_BAND_CEILINGS (renamed from BAND_CEILINGS so 4.1.1 stores the same constant in revision settings).
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Progression needs two adjacent local dates that both qualify at the current target; a missed day between two qualifying days breaks the pair, and a held qualification survives later missed days.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Comparability warnings compare only the eligible slot candidates (baseline:A vs final:A, baseline:B vs final:B) and emit nothing for a label whose either side has no eligible attempt.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Result-state day boundary: day14Finished = currentDay > 14; Day 14 itself counts as not finished.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] RESULT_STATE_COPY entries are {headline: string|null, message, action}: message is the PRD §5 line, headline is 'Fewer reported switches' only for improvement_maintained_recall (D38), actions come from the PRD Action column, and more_switches and unchanged share the action 'Inspect context'.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Practice target sets: CreateProgramBody.practiceTargetSeconds is 300|600|900; revision settings and CreateSessionBody.targetSeconds are 300..1500 step 300; firstSwitchEstimateSeconds is 1..1199 in both the contract and deriveFirstSwitch.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] selectSlotCandidates throws when a slot has two eligible attempts; the system never chooses the better one.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] AttemptSummary.firstSwitch and countMethod are widened to nullable (null only when S was not reported; such an attempt is never eligible), and assertSameRealm is widened to accept any readonly {realm} rows so programs, practice blocks and check-ins share it; both are additive edits to types.ts.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] A platform 'all' feed row must be scope 'feed' with source 'estimate'; any other 'all' row reports feed_platform_conflict; validateFeedRows uses the D19 error codes (feed_subset_violation, duplicate_feed_row, feed_platform_conflict) so 6.1.2 maps them one-to-one; today.checkin.values phone/desktop are feedAggregates.feedByDevice totals.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] DayResponse.checkin.localDate is the requested date even when no row exists; every other checkin field is null in the D22 no-row form.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Text limits: intendedOutput 1..200, replacementReason 1..500, transition reason 1..100, outputNote ≤ 200, reviewNote and disruptionNote ≤ 2000, amendment reason 1..2000, recall points ≤ 500 each, agent plan workstream/waitingTask ≤ 200 and resumeNote ≤ 500, check-in note ≤ 2000, materialRef 1..500.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] ERROR_CODES (37 codes) and ERROR_CODE_STATUS live in packages/shared/src/contracts/common.ts, not in domain/types.ts; REALM_MISMATCH_MESSAGE is exported beside them.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Event details are typed per event type: clock_gap requires details {gapSeconds ≥ 0, resolution?}; every other type allows {alsoOffTask?, reason?}; visibility events carry no details.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] NextAction benchmark and final variants carry slotId, phase, label, assignedLocalDate and plannedLocalTime; SlotResponse carries frozenAt (nullable) rather than a derived frozen flag; there is one SessionResponse for every session route and no separate SessionDetailResponse.
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] Fixtures: a shared anchorScenario(scenario, loadInstant) helper resolves D35 day numbers and offsets so 3.5.4 only inserts; every session row of any lifecycle has one review row (D31); comparison scenarios stay status active on Day 15; the recovery session started 7 minutes before load; attemptsOf maps unfinalized benchmark sessions with eligible false and exclusionReasons [].
- [2. Shared domain rules and contracts] recallDelaySeconds throws when recall starts before the interval ended; the API turns that into 422 recall_before_interval_end (D27).
- [3. API foundation] 3.1.1 moves 1.3.2's `validateRequiredSettings` and `formatRefusal` from server.ts into config.ts (server.ts re-exports them) so `loadConfig` can run the required-settings refusal first without an import cycle; 1.3.2's tests keep passing unchanged.
- [3. API foundation] 3.1.2 removes 1.2.1's `buildServer()` and rewrites apps/api/test/server.test.ts's three cases against `buildApp` with a test config pointing at DATABASE_URL_TEST; the dev/start scripts stay exactly as 1.2.1 defines them (tsx from source, D37).
- [3. API foundation] The fixed principal's initial `user_profiles.timezone` is 'UTC' until Setup confirms a timezone; 3.5.1's GET /me test asserts it so the placeholder stays visible.
- [3. API foundation] The API reads no DEFAULT_HIDE_TIMER/DEFAULT_END_CHIME/DEFAULT_VISIBILITY_CONTEXT environment variables; `DEFAULT_PREFERENCES` in apps/api/src/preferences.ts is the only source of preference defaults and endChime defaults to false.
- [3. API foundation] AJV options `coerceTypes:false, removeAdditional:false, useDefaults:false` are fixed in `buildApp` and no route or plugin overrides them; the 3.1.2 and 3.2.4 null-not-coerced tests guard this.
- [3. API foundation] 3.2.4 imports 2.7.2's `ErrorResponse` schema from packages/shared/src/contracts/common.ts as the envelope contract and asserts it declares the optional D18 `details` object; the API keeps no duplicate envelope schema.
- [3. API foundation] `withIdempotency` reports `replayed` and the caller chooses the status: POST /programs and POST /sessions reply 201 fresh / 200 replay (D21); recall and finalize reply 200 on both paths as 5.7.2 and 5.8.1 specify.
- [3. API foundation] 3.2.5 depends on 3.5.1 in addition to 3.2.2 and 3.2.4 so its '/api/v1/me still JSON' case runs against the real route; the SPA fallback answers GET and HEAD only, and static responses carry x-request-id but no no-store header.
- [3. API foundation] Scenario load resolves every child timestamp relative to its session's `started_at` (event `occurred_at` from `elapsedMs`; recall and finalize instants as seconds after start) and every fixture local date as a program-day number added to `baseline_date`; `viewDay` and `startedAtOffsetSeconds` follow D35.
- [3. API foundation] The scenario-load route declares no body schema and ignores any body it receives; 3.6.1's test-only route throws the D19 code `impossible_offset` rather than a code outside the vocabulary.
- [3. API foundation] 3.6.2 posts an `end` transition before every finalize (D24) and therefore depends on 5.4.2; the missing-attestation case expects 400 `fieldErrors.materiallyDisrupted` as 5.8.3 specifies.
- [4. Programs API] nextAction edge cases D23 does not name: before Day 0 a pending baseline slot is still the next action (benchmark A; Start availability is 5.1.2's before_slot_date rule shown by the Ready screen); Day 0 with both baselines finalized → practice block 1; Days 1–13 with both blocks finalized → practice block 2; Day 14 or later with both finals finalized → progress even when a baseline slot still lacks a finalized attempt (the practice-sessions requirement literal: after Day 14 a pending final, otherwise Progress).
- [4. Programs API] NextAction benchmark and final results carry only { kind, slotId } (2.7.3); the client reads phase, label, assigned date and planned time from slots[].
- [4. Programs API] The Block DTO follows the 2.7.3 TodayResponse block { index, status, targetSeconds, sessionId } with sessionId typed string | null (null, never omitted; the 2.7.3 schema is amended to Union([Uuid, Null])); status mapping: completed = finalized with complete_interval true, partial = finalized with false or NULL, in_progress = running | paused | awaiting_review; abandoned rows and any third practice session of a day are not blocks; elapsed seconds and output quality are not block fields.
- [4. Programs API] governingRevisionFor clamps the lookup day to 0 so revision 1 governs before Day 0; effectiveDayAllowed = effectiveDay ≥ max(currentDay, 0) and ≤ 14.
- [4. Programs API] CreateRevisionBody carries only practiceTargetSeconds and an optional leisureAllowanceMin (2.7.3); bandCeilings is server-owned (BAND_CEILINGS) and leisureAllowanceMin defaults to programs.leisure_allowance_min; bandCeilings or benchmarkSeconds in the body is 400, so the accept path never resends ceilings.
- [4. Programs API] PatchProgramBody.status is narrowed to 'completed' | 'archived' in 2.7.3; 'active', 'draft' and 'baseline_ready' are 400 because baseline_ready → active is automatic in 5.8; draft → completed is 409 invalid_status_transition; stale_version details carry { current: <program DTO> } on PUT benchmark-slots and PATCH.
- [4. Programs API] A duplicate (phase,label) in a PUT body is 400 malformed_request with fieldErrors['slots[i]']; readiness_regression (422) applies to baseline_ready and active programs and status never falls back to draft; PUT benchmark-slots stays accepted while the program is draft, baseline_ready or active (D38's Edit benchmark materials link); GET /programs/current and PUT share one SlotResponse carrying attempts.
- [4. Programs API] The suggestion input is practice rows for Days 1..day−1 (today's blocks count from tomorrow, matching the Day 6 scenario); per local date only the first two non-abandoned practice sessions by started_at are passed to suggestProgression; day > 14 yields no suggestion; a foreign-realm row throws RealmMixingError (422) rather than being filtered.
- [4. Programs API] 4.1.1 keeps insertSession (an attempt seed with its D31 review row) because every group-4 route test needs finalized attempts before group 5 exists; 5.1.3 extends these helpers with event, amendment and plan seeds rather than re-creating them; no group-4 helper re-creates buildTestApp, truncateAll or the principal profile (3.2.1/3.2.3).
- [4. Programs API] loadOwnedProgram: not owned → 404; owned but foreign realm → 422 realm_mismatch via assertOwnRealm, and the same guard is applied to every loaded session, attempt and check-in row (never silent filtering).
- [4. Programs API] POST /programs validates timezone with 3.5.1's isIanaTimeZone from apps/api/src/preferences.ts (one helper in the API; no apps/api/src/lib/timezone.ts); baselineDate is unbounded because past dates are how tests and demo scenarios reach later program days.
- [4. Programs API] today.checkin.values carries only the three D22 fields (sleepMinutes, phoneFeedMinutes, desktopFeedMinutes); stress, mindfulness and note belong to the days route (6.1); the device totals are feedAggregates(...).feedByDevice from 2.6.1.
- [4. Programs API] No realm column is written on protocol_revisions or benchmark_slots (D34); a realm property on a slot body is a 400 unknown key.
- [4. Programs API] Group-4 unit test files stay under apps/api/src/services/program/*.test.ts and integration files under apps/api/test/programs/*.test.ts; both are selected by the file arguments in each verify command.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] A :date outside the program's Days 0–14 (calendar.ts) is not a resource of the program: GET and PUT /programs/{id}/days/{date} return 404 not_found; any date inside the window is readable and writable regardless of today's day.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] PUT days on a program whose status is completed or archived returns 409 program_terminal (D19 code; the daily-checkin spec makes check-ins editable until the program is completed).
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The D36 all-vs-platform conflict check is implemented once in packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts by extending validateFeedRows (2.6.1) additively with error code platform_conflict in 6.1.2; the API maps the three feed.ts codes to D19's feed_subset_violation, duplicate_feed_row and feed_platform_conflict, answers with the first error's code and lists every offending row in fieldErrors.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The DayResponse carries no realm field (2.7.5); a daily_checkins row whose realm differs from ctx.realm makes GET/PUT days answer 422 realm_mismatch via assertOwnRealm, and 6.1.1 does not use 4.5.2's local status module — completeness comes only from shared checkinStatus (D16).
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] resolveResultState's finalAttemptsExist is true only when a final-phase attempt with lifecycle finalized exists; a running, awaiting_review or abandoned final attempt leaves the state at final_pending on or before Day 14, consistent with D23 keeping the final slot as the next action.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] Report contract additions are owned by group 6 and made additively in packages/shared/src/contracts/report.ts: AttemptSchema gains lifecycle, replacementReason and excludedByAmendment (6.2.1); the practice entry gains sessionId, programDay, lifecycle, completedSeconds, timerQuality, countMethod and mindWanderingCount (6.2.4).
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The report's days[] covers program Days 0..min(today's program day, 14); future days are not listed, so they never appear as gaps.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The JSON report carries the demo label only as realm: 'demo'; the textual label is rendered by the web banner (7.1) and by the export's first line (6.3.x).
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] Exports omit every free-text field (check-in note, output_note, disruption_note, recall points, review_note, intended_output) and practice sessions; the progress-spec column list governs the export content.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] CSV layout: five sections in fixed order (program, revisions, attempts, days, feed_rows), each introduced by a single `# <section-name>` line followed by its header row, separated by one blank line, after the demo-label first line; the attempts section adds a lifecycle column so never-finalized attempts are distinguishable.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] Export responses set Content-Type and Cache-Control only; no Content-Disposition header is sent, and download file naming belongs to the Progress screen (8.8).
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] exported_at in the export is ctx.now (demo-shifted under a non-zero offset, D8), pinned in tests with vi.setSystemTime.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] Comparability warnings reuse 2.4.3's shared function over 2.4.1's slot candidates; a label lacking an eligible attempt on either side emits no warning and no fallback attempt is chosen.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] GET /research/cards is declared config: { public: true } and is the only route without Cache-Control: no-store (3.2.2); its response uses the 2.7.5 field names curatedOn, note and discoveryNote.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The new-user acceptance case creates an empty program through POST /programs (4.1.2) because the loader returns programId null (D22); its report state is baseline_pending.
- [6. Days, report, export and research API] The 6.2.5 load script seeds through direct Drizzle inserts and cleans up with deletePrincipalData (3.5.3) rather than a test-only truncation, so it never touches rows outside the local-demo principal.
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: `/benchmark/:sessionId/scoring` is read as nested under the session id, not a bare `/scoring`, because design.md's route map shows the step only as a relative '-> /scoring' arrow (7.1.1).
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: the gap detector (7.2.2) signals on wall/monotonic drift only; no separate tick-gap (frozen-tab) signal is added, since D16-D40 does not introduce one.
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: on a 422 impossible-offset rejection, flush.ts (7.3.2) keeps the queued rows and reports only the offending clientEventId; whether the consuming screen (8.5) retries, drops, or holds that entry is left to that screen, not covered by D16-D40.
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: purgeOtherSessions is added to the outbox store (7.3.1) beyond its original scope because D17 requires the boot-time provider (7.4.2) to discard a stale session's rows, and no prior export enumerated or bulk-deleted by exclusion.
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: 7.1.5's shell smoke spec keeps its own dev-server webServer pair (dev:api + dev:web via the Vite proxy), separate from 1.5.3's build-and-serve default Playwright project, because design.md's single-origin static-serving path has no owning task and D16-D40 does not assign one.
- [7. Web foundation] Decision: 7.3.5's three-mismatch cap counts calls to finalizeWithSync (7.3.4), each of which already performs its own flush-and-retry-once on event_count_mismatch, so three such calls (not three raw HTTP attempts) trigger unsaved_entries -- D16 fixes the cap but not its granularity relative to 7.3.4's internal retry.
- [7. Web foundation] Cross-group note (not a group-7 drop): 7.1.1 now creates the component-test harness that 8.1.1 and 8.6.1 previously each created; 7.1.5 now creates the Playwright support helpers whose function set 9.1.1 previously defined; 7.4.4 absorbs 8.10.6. These other-group units are expected to be repointed to depend on 7.1.1/7.1.5/7.4.4 and drop their own duplicate implementations when those groups are reconciled.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] The edit's reference to '9.1.7 timing-deviation journey' is applied to 9.1.9, the unit that actually contains the clock-gap/timing-deviation journey in this group's post-split numbering (9.1.7 is comparison-guards, missing-final and zero-baseline).
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] Scenario slugs are passed to demo.load() as the kebab-case string literals exported in DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES (e.g. demo.load('working-day')) rather than named ALL-CAPS constants, since the edit did not specify the accessor shape.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] 9.1.2 keeps its id and keeps a role in this group even though 7.1.5 now creates the helper files it previously created (D16): it is repurposed to only verify those helpers (helpers.spec.ts, including the negative-control cases), never redefining them.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] 9.1.4 test 6 keeps its assumption that a practice session may start on Day 0, since D16-D40 does not settle whether 5.1 accepts that start.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] 9.2.5 and 9.2.6 keep the assumption that a qualifying Day 5-6 pair followed by a sessionless Day 7 still lets Day 8 offer the progression suggestion (missed days hold the suggestion state), since D16-D40 does not settle 2.5's exact qualifying-window rule.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] 9.1.9 test 5 keeps the assumption that GET /programs/current.day reports a day number past 14 without clamping, since D16-D40 does not settle this for 4.2.
- [9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility] 9.2.2's leave-guard test now uses the D38 copy; the stale-write conflict UI copy (9.2.7) and the 'session could not be started' copy (9.2.4) keep their original role/regex-based wording since D16-D40 gives no exact copy for either, and 9.2.2 still does not test whether the recall countdown survives a reload since that remains undesigned.
- [1+10. Kickoff, workspace, documents and handoff] End-of-block chime defaults to off in code (a constant alongside hideTimerDefault, milestoneAnnouncements and visibilityContext per D22), resolving design.md's open question without an env var.
- [1+10. Kickoff, workspace, documents and handoff] 1.4.1's `db:push` against the empty schema barrel is treated as a no-op success (drizzle-kit 0.31 prints 'No changes detected'); D16–D40 does not settle drizzle-kit's behavior on a zero-table schema, so if a future drizzle-kit release errors instead, 1.4.1's verify is satisfied once 3.3 adds the first table.
- [1+10. Kickoff, workspace, documents and handoff] MANIFEST.sha256 is not regenerated after README.md and CLAUDE.md are edited by 10.1.1/10.2.1; sha256sum -c is expected to report 7 of 9 passing (README.md and CODEX-HANDOFF.md failing) for the remainder of this change.
- [1+10. Kickoff, workspace, documents and handoff] Static serving of the built web app under WEB_DIST_DIR (D37) is implemented by 3.2.5 in group 3; 1.2.1, 1.3.1 and 1.5.3 in this group only document and reference that setting, not implement it.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] EVENT_OFFSET_TOLERANCE_MS is set to 5000 milliseconds (5 seconds); D16–D40 fixes no value, so 5.3.1 decides it and records it for LIMITATIONS.md.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] A replacementReason supplied on a first benchmark attempt is accepted (the start succeeds) but is not stored on that attempt's replacement_reason column, since only the second, replacement attempt has a reason to record.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] session_events has no realm column in the 3.3 schema, so 5.3.1 scopes realm entirely through the parent focus_sessions row rather than stamping a column on each event.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] Voiding a server-written pause or resume row (5.3.2) uses 422 code invalid_transition, since D19 names no dedicated code for refusing to void a transition-record; voiding on a finalized session instead uses 409 already_finalized, distinct from the transitions route's 409 session_not_active, because abandoned sessions remain voidable at the event level while abandoned sessions cannot transition further.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] All non-terminal applyTransition failures (both invalid_for_kind and invalid_from_state) map to 422 invalid_transition in the transitions route (5.4.2); only an attempted transition on an already finalized or abandoned session maps to 409 session_not_active, since D19 lists no separate 409 code for a same-state or wrong-kind transition on an otherwise active session.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] A benchmark-kind field mismatch (missing slotId, targetSeconds forced away from 1200) uses 422 benchmark_only_field, and a practice-kind field mismatch (intendedOutput on a benchmark body, slotId on a practice body) uses 422 practice_only_field / benchmark_only_field respectively; a blank or whitespace-only intendedOutput on a practice body remains a plain 422 with fieldErrors.intendedOutput, since no D19 code names a simple required-field violation.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] Governing revision selection for a session start (5.1.1) picks the greatest effective_day <= the session's program day, ties broken by the highest revision number, matching 4.1.1's governingRevisionFor so both groups agree without importing each other's pure function.
- [5a. Sessions API — start, read, events, transitions] 5.1.3's session-level seed helpers live in a new file apps/api/test/helpers/sessions.ts, separate from 4.1.1's apps/api/test/helpers/programs.ts, so tests can import only the layer they need.
- [5b. Sessions API — clock gap, agent plan, recall, finalize, amendments] 5.7.2: startedAt > ctx.now is rejected with the same 422 code recall_before_interval_end as startedAt < ended_at, since D19's code vocabulary names only one recall-timing error code and none of D16-D40 distinguishes the two failure directions.
- [5b. Sessions API — clock gap, agent plan, recall, finalize, amendments] 5.8.3: when the shared deriveRecallScore (2.3.2) returns ok:false (a non-blank recall point left unscored), finalize still returns 200 and stores both recall_score and recall_scores as NULL rather than rejecting the request; D16-D40 does not say whether partial scoring is a finalize-blocking error, and this preserves the original unit's precedent (scoring_incomplete is an eligibility outcome, not a mechanical rejection).
- [5b. Sessions API — clock gap, agent plan, recall, finalize, amendments] reviewNote (D31) is wired into the benchmark finalize only (5.8.3), per the literal scope of the reconciliation edit; practice finalize (5.8.2) does not accept it in this group and session_reviews.review_note stays NULL for every practice attempt.
- [5b. Sessions API — clock gap, agent plan, recall, finalize, amendments] D33's baseline_ready -> active auto-activation is implemented as an idempotent conditional UPDATE inside 5.8.3's finalize transaction (scoped to baseline-phase attempts only) because no other unit in this group or in group 4 owns a program-status write triggered by a session-side event; it deliberately never fires for midpoint or final-phase finalizes.
- [5b. Sessions API — clock gap, agent plan, recall, finalize, amendments] 409 codes for a closed-off session lifecycle (finalized/abandoned) use session_not_active and 409 codes for an interval that has not yet ended (running/paused) use session_not_ended, applied consistently across 5.5.1, 5.6.1, 5.7.2 and 5.8.1 for D19 compliance, since D19 lists both codes but design.md does not itself map every lifecycle to one or the other.
- [8a. Web screens — setup, today, benchmark, recall/scoring, focus] Whether an abandoned or unfinalized benchmark attempt counts toward a slot's two-attempt replacement limit is not covered by D16–D40; Ready (8.3.1) keeps deferring entirely to the server's 422 response, asserting no count of its own from slot.attempts.
- [8a. Web screens — setup, today, benchmark, recall/scoring, focus] Demo-scenario slugs used by e2e specs (e.g. 'working-day') are not enumerated by D16–D40; they are treated as the literal exported constants from packages/shared/src/fixtures (2.8), so a slug mismatch is a fixtures-module bug, not a spec bug.
- [8a. Web screens — setup, today, benchmark, recall/scoring, focus] ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) is relocated to apps/web/src/features/session/ActiveSessionCard.tsx and re-exported from features/today, since D16 makes it shared by Today, Benchmark Ready and the stale-conflict notice rendered from session-mode screens (Running, TransitionControls, AgentPanel); this is a file-organization choice, not a behavior change.
- [8a. Web screens — setup, today, benchmark, recall/scoring, focus] 8.2.2 (BlockCard) no longer lists 8.2.5 (ActiveSessionCard) in dependsOn even though Today swaps BlockCard for ActiveSessionCard on active_session_exists: BlockCard's own verify only needs to prove it performs no 409 routing itself, which does not require ActiveSessionCard's code to exist, and listing it would create a dependency cycle since 8.2.5 already depends on 8.2.2.
- [8a. Web screens — setup, today, benchmark, recall/scoring, focus] 6.2 (report) was dropped from 8.3.4 and 8.4.6's dependsOn and replaced with 4.2, since D22 moves prior-attempt data for Ready (8.3.1) from GET /programs/{id}/report to GET /programs/current slots[].attempts.
- [8b. Web screens — practice review, check-in, progress, research/settings, recovery UI] 8.8.1: Progress with no open program renders a neutral empty state (no zero-valued cards) linking to /setup; not addressed by D16-D40, kept as the unit's originally decided behavior.
- [8b. Web screens — practice review, check-in, progress, research/settings, recovery UI] 8.6.2: the finalize contract's review object is treated as gaining an optional reviewNote string field (session_reviews.review_note, D31) named to match the existing outputNote/disruptionNote convention, since D31 states the new column but not the wire field name.
- [8b. Web screens — practice review, check-in, progress, research/settings, recovery UI] Cross-group wiring: 8.10.2, 8.10.7 and 8.10.8 consume group 8a's ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) and SyncStatus (8.5.2) plus group 7's SessionModeProvider (7.4.2), useStartSession (7.4.4) and useFinalizeSession/finalizeWithSync/abandonWithPurge (7.3.5/7.3.4); the orchestrator should re-run 8a's Playwright suite after this group's consuming units land, since no unit in either group re-verifies 8a's screens against the changed call sites.

## 1. Kickoff and workspace

### 1.1.1 — Pin registry-verified dependency versions and commit the lockfile

- **Layer:** infra
- **Implementation:** Create apps/api/package.json (name @attention-lab/api, private, type module, version 0.1.0) and apps/web/package.json (name @attention-lab/web, private, type module, version 0.1.0) with empty scripts objects; edit packages/shared/package.json and the root package.json. Install with `npm install <pkg>@<major.minor> --save-exact -w <workspace>` so each design.md registry entry (6 Sep 2026) resolves to the latest patch of that minor and is pinned exactly: apps/web dependencies react 19.2, react-dom 19.2, react-router 8.3, @tanstack/react-query 5.102, radix-ui 1.6, recharts 3.10; apps/web devDependencies vite 8.2, @vitejs/plugin-react 6.1, tailwindcss 4.3, @tailwindcss/vite (same 4.3 line), @types/react and @types/react-dom on the 19.x line; apps/api dependencies fastify 5.12, @fastify/type-provider-typebox 6.1, @sinclair/typebox 0.34, drizzle-orm 0.45, postgres 3.4, pino 10.3, tsx 4.23; apps/api devDependencies drizzle-kit 0.31, vitest 5.0; packages/shared: replace ^0.34.52 with the exact @sinclair/typebox 0.34.x pin and pin vitest 5.0.x exactly; root devDependencies typescript 5.9.3 (never 7.x, D3), vitest 5.0.x exact (replace ^5.0.0), @playwright/test 1.63, @axe-core/playwright 4.13, @types/node ^24.10.1 left as is. Keep the existing root scripts untouched. If `npm install` itself fails with ERESOLVE, apply D3 on the spot: step ONLY the named package back one major with --save-exact, rerun, and list the step-back in the commit message so 1.1.2 records it in LIMITATIONS.md; typecheck/build/boot conflicts are 1.1.2's. Commit package-lock.json.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: manifests and lockfile only; no code
- **Integration tests:** n/a: no server exists yet
- **Verify:** npm install && npm ls --depth=0 --workspaces --include-workspace-root exits 0 with no 'invalid', 'missing', 'extraneous' or 'ERESOLVE' lines; git ls-files package-lock.json prints package-lock.json; node -e "console.log(require('typescript/package.json').version)" prints 5.9.3; node -e "const l=require('./package-lock.json');for(const p of ['react','vite','fastify','drizzle-orm','@playwright/test','tsx'])if(!l.packages['node_modules/'+p])throw p;console.log('LOCK_OK')" prints LOCK_OK
- **After:** nothing

No 'Preference defaults' block: hideTimerDefault, endChime, milestoneAnnouncements and visibilityContext are constants in the API service layer, not env settings (D22) — end-chime stays off by default, same as the other three, resolving design.md's open question in code rather than in .env. Add an optional '## Static web serving (D37)' block: `# WEB_DIST_DIR=apps/web/dist` with a comment that when this is set the API serves the named directory as the built web app with an SPA fallback for every non-/api path (3.2.5); left unset in dev, where Vite serves the web app separately, and set by the Playwright acceptance project (9.1.1) after `npm run build`. Create LIMITATIONS.md with these headings: ## Identity gate (D2), ## Version pins (D3), ## Specification defects settled (D7), ## What works, ## What was tested, ## Recovery: simulated vs field-tested, ## Thresholds chosen, ## Deferred, ## Reversible decision: local-pilot, ## Task-group coverage. Under D3 put only the sentinel table header `| package | registry version (2026-09-06) | pinned | step-back reason |` for 1.1.2 to fill. Fill D2 now (local-demo fully working on loopback with fixed principal; real refuses to boot until the four prerequisites exist and Better Auth is wired in follow-up change add-real-identity; local-pilot deferred and reversible by one decision, needing no schema change because realm and user_id columns already exist; therefore no pilot row can exist in this change — D7.6) and D7 (one line per defect, pointing to design.md D7); leave the rest marked 'filled in 10.4'.

### 1.2.1 — Create apps/api Fastify skeleton with dev, start and typecheck scripts

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/package.json (created in 1.1.1) is named @attention-lab/api. apps/api/tsconfig.json (extends ../../tsconfig.base.json; rootDir '.', noEmit true, types ['node'], include src/**/*.ts and test/**/*.ts — the same shape as packages/shared/tsconfig.json). No tsc emit and no tsconfig.build.json: @attention-lab/shared exports TypeScript source, which tsc cannot emit from another package's rootDir (TS6059), so per D37 the API runs from source under tsx in every mode — dev, start and the built/Playwright-acceptance mode alike — and produces no compiled API artifact. Scripts in apps/api/package.json: dev = `node --env-file-if-exists=../../.env --watch --import tsx src/server.ts`, start = `node --env-file-if-exists=../../.env --import tsx src/server.ts`, build = `tsc --noEmit` (the typecheck-as-build convention packages/shared already uses), typecheck = `tsc --noEmit`, test = `vitest run` (config from 1.5.1); db:* scripts arrive in 1.4.x. Because --env-file-if-exists needs Node >= 22.9, root package.json engines.node is '>=22.9' (D37; verified on 24.14). src/server.ts: export buildServer(): Fastify instance with logger: true (pino) and an empty plugin registered under prefix '/api/v1' (route files arrive in 3.x); export start(): reads API_HOST and API_PORT from process.env with NO defaults (1.3.2 adds the named-settings refusal), listens, logs 'listening on http://<host>:<port>'. server.ts never reads a .env file itself — the env comes from the scripts' --env-file-if-exists flag, so a spawned process with a bare env sees exactly a missing-.env boot. start() runs only when server.ts is the entry module: compare path.resolve(process.argv[1]) with fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) so tests import buildServer without listening. Static serving of a built web app under WEB_DIST_DIR (D37) is added later by 3.2.5 — this unit's server.ts registers no static-file plugin and no SPA fallback.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/server.test.ts: 'buildServer returns a Fastify instance and inject GET /api/v1/me yields 404 with no routes registered'; 'buildServer never calls listen (server.addresses() is empty after ready)'; 'importing server.ts as a module does not start a listener (a TCP connect to 127.0.0.1:8787 is refused during the test)'
- **Integration tests:** n/a: skeleton has no database plugin or routes; the first inject-against-Postgres tests land in 3.2
- **Verify:** cp .env.example .env (if absent); in terminal 1 npm run dev:api prints 'listening on http://127.0.0.1:8787' within 10 s; in terminal 2 `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/v1/me` prints 404 (no routes until 3.5); Ctrl+C terminal 1. Then npm run test:api passes apps/api/test/server.test.ts (3 cases)
- **After:** 1.1.1, 1.5.1

### 1.3.2 — Refuse API boot when required settings are missing, naming them

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** In apps/api/src/server.ts export validateRequiredSettings(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): { ok: true; settings: { databaseUrl: string; apiHost: string; apiPort: number; identityMode: string } } | { ok: false; missing: string[] } over the ordered list DATABASE_URL, API_HOST, API_PORT, IDENTITY_MODE; a setting is missing when absent or empty, and API_PORT is also missing when it is not an integer (WEB_ORIGIN and the DEFAULT_* preference settings stay optional). Export formatRefusal(missing: string[]): string returning 'Refusing to start: missing required settings: <names>. Copy .env.example to .env and fill them in. IDENTITY_MODE accepts exactly: local-demo, real.' start(): run the validator first; on failure write formatRefusal to stderr and process.exit(1) BEFORE listen. No default is substituted for any of the four. Scope boundary: value enumeration of IDENTITY_MODE, the loopback host check and the real-mode prerequisite listing are implemented by 3.1 on top of this function — do not implement them here.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/boot.env.test.ts: 'empty env → ok:false with missing = [DATABASE_URL, API_HOST, API_PORT, IDENTITY_MODE] in declared order'; 'env lacking only IDENTITY_MODE → missing = [IDENTITY_MODE]'; 'API_PORT="abc" → missing includes API_PORT'; 'complete env → ok:true with apiPort parsed as the number 8787'; 'formatRefusal names every missing setting, .env.example, and both accepted IDENTITY_MODE values local-demo and real'; 'no default value is substituted for IDENTITY_MODE (absent stays absent, never local-demo)'
- **Integration tests:** Fastify inject n/a: the process exits before any route registers. Process-level boot test apps/api/test/boot.process.test.ts spawns process.execPath with ['--import','tsx','src/server.ts'], cwd apps/api, env = a copy of process.env with DATABASE_URL, API_HOST, API_PORT and IDENTITY_MODE deleted (no --env-file flag, so no .env file is read): 'exit code is 1'; 'stderr contains DATABASE_URL, API_HOST, API_PORT, IDENTITY_MODE and .env.example'; 'stdout never contains "listening on"'; 'a TCP connect to 127.0.0.1:8787 is refused after exit'
- **Verify:** [ -f .env ] && mv .env .env.bak; (npm run start -w @attention-lab/api; echo EXIT=$?); [ -f .env.bak ] && mv .env.bak .env — stderr prints the refusal line naming DATABASE_URL, API_HOST, API_PORT, IDENTITY_MODE and .env.example, EXIT=1 is printed, and `curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/v1/me` afterwards prints 000 (nothing listening). Then npm run test:api passes boot.env.test.ts and boot.process.test.ts

### 1.4.2 — Add test-database bootstrap creating attention_lab_test and migrating it

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/scripts/bootstrap-test-db.ts exporting parseDatabaseName(url), maintenanceUrl(url) (same server and credentials, database 'postgres') and bootstrapTestDb(env): requires DATABASE_URL_TEST (refuse with a message naming the setting when absent); refuses when the test database name equals DATABASE_URL's database name or does not end in _test (tests truncate tables); connects with the postgres driver to the maintenance database and CREATE DATABASE when pg_database has no row (logs 'already exists' otherwise); then, if src/db/migrations/meta/_journal.json exists, runs migrate() from drizzle-orm/postgres-js/migrator against DATABASE_URL_TEST (design.md: test DB migrated with drizzle-kit output), else logs 'no migrations yet'; ends by logging 'test database <name> ready'. CLI entry guard (same argv[1] pattern as server.ts); npm script db:test = tsx scripts/bootstrap-test-db.ts in apps/api and a root script db:test = npm run db:test -w @attention-lab/api (same delegation pattern as db:push, so CLAUDE.md can name it). 1.5.2 wires bootstrapTestDb as the Vitest globalSetup.

- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run db:test && npm run db:test && docker exec attention-lab-pg psql -U attention -d postgres -tAc "select datname from pg_database where datname='attention_lab_test'" — both runs exit 0 (the second logs 'already exists' and is a no-op) and the query prints attention_lab_test; npm run test:api passes bootstrapTestDb.test.ts and bootstrapTestDb.integration.test.ts

(paths resolve relative to the config file), reporter 'list', use.trace 'retain-on-failure', one `toolchain` chromium project used only by this unit's smoke spec — it declares no webServer and needs none, because toolchain.smoke.spec.ts only imports AxeBuilder and asserts it is a constructor function; --list needs no server either. ### 2.1.1 — Implement calendar date arithmetic for program Days 0–14

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/calendar.ts. Export `type LocalDate = string` (YYYY-MM-DD), `PROGRAM_LENGTH_DAYS = 14`, `isValidLocalDate(s)`, `addDays(date, n)` (calendar-field arithmetic via Date.UTC at noon; no timezone involved), `localDateForProgramDay(baselineDate, day)`, `programDayForLocalDate(baselineDate, localDate)` (returns the raw offset; negative before Day 0, >14 after; never clamped), `finalDate(baselineDate)` (= Day 14), `isWithinProgram(day)` (0..14), `programDates(baselineDate)` (15 dates). Invalid inputs throw. No timezone logic in this unit (2.1.2 owns it). Export the module from packages/shared/src/index.ts (the barrel created by 1.2.3).
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/calendar.test.ts — cases: 'Day 0 equals the baseline date'; 'finalDate is baseline + 14 calendar days'; 'programDates yields 15 distinct consecutive dates across a month boundary (baseline 2026-09-25)'; 'programDayForLocalDate inverts localDateForProgramDay for days 0..14'; 'date before baseline returns a negative day and Day 15 returns 15 (not clamped)'; 'leap day: 2028-02-28 + 1 = 2028-02-29'; 'malformed date string throws'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.2.3 (GET /programs/current day and nextAction) and 4.5.3 (GET /programs/{id}/today)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- domain/calendar.test` prints `Tests  7 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0

### 2.1.2 — Derive current program day from an instant in the stored timezone

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/calendar.ts: `localDateAt(instant: Date, timeZone: string): LocalDate` using Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', {timeZone, year:'numeric', month:'2-digit', day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts; `isValidIanaTimezone(tz)` (Intl constructor throws RangeError → false; '' → false; reused by PATCH /me/preferences (3.5.1) and POST /programs validation (4.1.2)); `currentProgramDay(program: {baselineDate: LocalDate, timezone: string}, now: Date): {day: number, localDate: LocalDate}` which consults ONLY the program's stored timezone (the profile timezone is not an input, per program-setup spec) and takes `now` from the caller so D8's demo clock (`ctx.now = realNow + offset`) works unchanged. Day is not clamped.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/calendar.timezone.test.ts — cases: 'fall-back DST (America/New_York, baseline 2026-10-25, DST ends 2026-11-01): stepping hourly instants from the Day 0 00:00 local instant for 15 × 24 + 1 hours yields exactly 15 distinct dates, non-decreasing, none skipped or duplicated (the Day 0 instant is found by scanning UTC minutes until localDateAt first equals the baseline date; no inverse-timezone math)'; 'spring-forward DST (Europe/Madrid, baseline 2027-03-21, DST starts 2027-03-28) same property'; 'profile timezone change on Day 8 does not move program days: instant 2026-09-09T00:00Z with baseline 2026-09-01 is Day 8 in Europe/Madrid and would be Day 7 in America/Los_Angeles; currentProgramDay returns Day 8 because only the program timezone is consulted'; '23:30 local in Asia/Tokyo is the same program day although the UTC date is one earlier'; 'now advanced by a demo offset of 14 days resolves to Day 14 and localDate equals finalDate (D8)'; 'isValidIanaTimezone: "Europe/Madrid" true, "Mars/Olympus" false, "" false'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.5.3 (GET /programs/{id}/today uses program tz / demo clock) and 3.5.2 (POST /demo/clock changes ctx.now)

### 2.3.1 — Implement benchmark eligibility with explicit exclusion reasons

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/eligibility.ts: `EligibilityInput {completeInterval: boolean|null, recallLockedAt: string|null, recallScores: readonly (0|1)[]|null, episodeCount: ReportedCount, materiallyDisrupted: boolean, timerQuality: TimerQuality, sessionLocalDate: LocalDate, slotAssignedLocalDate: LocalDate, excludedByAmendment: boolean, realm: Realm, timeSource: TimeSource}` and `evaluateEligibility(input) → {eligible: boolean, exclusionReasons: ExclusionReason[]}`. Reasons, emitted in ExclusionReason declaration order: completeInterval !== true → interval_incomplete (null, i.e. deadline reached but never confirmed by an `end` transition per D24, is NOT complete); recallLockedAt null → recall_missing (D25: an incomplete attempt may be finalized without a recall lock and then carries this reason); recallScores null or length ≠ 5 → scoring_incomplete; episodeCount null → count_unknown (explicit 0 passes); materiallyDisrupted → materially_disrupted; timerQuality 'uncertain' → timer_uncertain (D26: an unresolved clock gap at finalize is forced to 'uncertain' by 5.8.x before this call); sessionLocalDate ≠ slotAssignedLocalDate → timing_deviation (D23: a late start is accepted and labelled here, never blocked); excludedByAmendment → excluded_by_amendment; realm 'pilot' and timeSource !== 'measured' → simulated_time. eligible = reasons.length === 0. External count is not an input (E never disqualifies). A `satisfies Record<ExclusionReason, ...>` check keeps EXCLUSION_REASON_COPY exhaustive. API callers (5.8.4) pass the stored columns straight into these fields. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/eligibility.test.ts — cases: 'all conditions met → eligible with []'; one case per reason alone (9 cases: interval_incomplete, recall_missing, scoring_incomplete, count_unknown, materially_disrupted, timer_uncertain, timing_deviation, excluded_by_amendment, simulated_time for pilot+demo_clock); 'completeInterval null (timer expired, never confirmed by an end transition) → interval_incomplete — expiry never proves completion'; 'early stop plus blank S → [interval_incomplete, count_unknown] in that order'; 'incomplete attempt finalized without recall (completeInterval false, recallLockedAt null, recallScores null, S 2) → [interval_incomplete, recall_missing, scoring_incomplete] (D25)'; 'explicit 0 for S is not count_unknown and can be eligible'; 'E=2 with materiallyDisrupted=false adds no reason (E is not an input)'; 'demo realm with demo_clock never yields simulated_time'; 'pilot with attested → simulated_time'; 'baseline B completed on Day 1 (date ≠ slot date) → timing_deviation only'; 'final A on Day 15 → timing_deviation only'; 'amendment exclusion keeps every other input untouched (original values not needed to exclude)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.8.4 (finalize persists eligible/exclusion_reasons), 5.5.1 (uncertain → timer_uncertain) and 5.9.3 (excluded_by_amendment in report)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- domain/eligibility.test` prints `Tests  20 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.1.1
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / All conditions met · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / Multiple failing conditions · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / Attempt outside its date · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Blank count · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Explicit zero · benchmark-assessment: Disruption is a required self-attestation / External interruptions without disruption · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Interval reached · benchmark-assessment: Self-scoring is unavailable until recall is locked / Incomplete attempt finalized without recall · benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only / Exclude a finalized attempt · benchmark-assessment: Final benchmarks on Day 14 / Final completed on Day 15

### 2.7.1 — Extend types.ts with the literal unions and const arrays shared by domain and contracts

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/types.ts (append only; existing declarations kept, with the widenings noted) so every literal set has ONE source that both domain modules and TypeBox contracts derive from. Add `as const` arrays with matching unions: `SESSION_EVENT_TYPES` / `SessionEventType` ('off_task'|'external'|'agent_check'|'pause'|'resume'|'clock_gap'|'visibility'); `SESSION_KINDS` / `SessionKind` ('practice'|'benchmark'); `SESSION_LIFECYCLES` / `SessionLifecycle` ('running'|'paused'|'awaiting_review'|'finalized'|'abandoned'); `PROGRAM_STATUSES` / `ProgramStatus` ('draft'|'baseline_ready'|'active'|'completed'|'archived'); `RECALL_FLAGS` / `RecallFlag` ('recall_delayed'|'recall_overrun'); `FEED_DEVICES` / `FeedDevice` ('phone'|'desktop'|'tablet'|'unspecified'); `FEED_PLATFORM_ALL = 'all'` (D36 device-level row marker); `MEASUREMENT_SCOPES` / `MeasurementScope` ('feed'|'app_total'); `FEED_SOURCES` / `FeedSource` ('estimate'|'device_report'); `CHECKIN_STATUSES` / `CheckinStatus` ('complete'|'incomplete'|'not_reported'); `CHECKIN_FIELDS` / `CheckinField` ('sleep'|'feed'); `RESULT_STATES` / `ResultState` in precedence order ('baseline_pending','final_pending','insufficient_samples','zero_baseline','more_switches','unchanged','fewer_switches_lower_recall','improvement_maintained_recall'); `IDENTITY_MODES` / `IdentityMode` ('local-demo'|'real'); `TRANSITION_TYPES` / `TransitionType` ('pause'|'resume'|'end'|'abandon'); `CLOCK_GAP_RESOLUTIONS` / `ClockGapResolution` ('continued'|'uncertain'|'save_incomplete'); `FIRST_SWITCH_KINDS` / `FirstSwitchKind`; `FIRST_SWITCH_METHODS` / `FirstSwitchMethod` ('event'|'estimate'); `BLOCK_STATUSES` / `BlockStatus` ('not_started'|'in_progress'|'completed'|'partial'); `NEXT_ACTION_KINDS` / `NextActionKind` ('setup'|'readiness'|'benchmark'|'practice'|'final'|'progress'); `DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES` / `DemoScenarioName` ('new-user','working-day','comparable-change','mixed-result','missing-final','zero-baseline','recovery','timing-deviation'); and arrays for the unions that already exist: `REALMS`, `TIME_SOURCES`, `COUNT_METHODS`, `BENCHMARK_PHASES`, `SLOT_LABELS`, `OUTPUT_QUALITIES`, `TIMER_QUALITIES`, `EXCLUSION_REASONS` (tuple checked with `satisfies readonly ExclusionReason[]` and a type-level equality against keyof typeof EXCLUSION_REASON_COPY); `ACCOMMODATIONS` already exists. Widen two existing AttemptSummary fields: `firstSwitch: FirstSwitch | null` and `countMethod: CountMethod | null`, documented as 'null only when S was not reported; such an attempt is never eligible (count_unknown)' — so blank-S attempts can be listed and passed through selectSlotCandidates (6.2.1 relies on this). Widen `assertSameRealm` to `(rows: readonly { realm: Realm }[]) => Realm | null` so programs, practice blocks and check-ins share it (2.5.2, 6.2.x). Keep the deliberate absence of any countOrZero helper.

### 2.2.1 — Implement event tallies with void, visibility and agent-check rules

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/firstSwitch.ts importing `SessionEventType`/`SESSION_EVENT_TYPES` from types.ts (added by 2.7.1). Export `EventLike {clientEventId: string, type: SessionEventType, elapsedMs: number|null, voidedAt: string|null, details?: {alsoOffTask?: boolean}}`, `countedEvents(events)` (drops rows with voidedAt set and drops pause/resume/clock_gap/visibility — D9, D15), `episodeEvents(events)` (off_task plus agent_check with details.alsoOffTask === true, sorted by elapsedMs ascending, nulls last — D11), `tallyEvents(events) → {offTask, external, agentChecks}` (offTask = episodeEvents length; external = counted external; agentChecks = ALL counted agent_check; the returned object has NO combined/total field), and `prefillCounts(events) → {episodeCount: ReportedCount, externalCount: ReportedCount, unplannedAgentChecks: ReportedCount, countMethod: CountMethod|null}` returning all null and countMethod null when no counted events exist (blank, never 0), otherwise all three tallies (0 included) with countMethod 'event' — the D31 prefill rule. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/tally.test.ts — cases: 'visibility events contribute to no tally and no prefill (hidden tab creates no episode)'; 'agent_check with alsoOffTask increments offTask by 1 and agentChecks by 1; the result has exactly the keys offTask/external/agentChecks (nothing summed across tallies)'; 'agent_check without alsoOffTask increments agentChecks only'; 'voided duplicate is excluded: two off_task presses then one void → offTask 1'; 'one Record press after visiting five apps is one episode (tally counts recorded events only)'; 'pause/resume/clock_gap contribute nothing'; 'no events → prefillCounts all null with countMethod null, never 0'; 'only external events → episodeCount 0, externalCount 1, unplannedAgentChecks 0 with countMethod event (D31: any counted event prefills every count)'; 'benchmark events and practice events use the same function; kind is not an input (benchmark ≠ practice is enforced by callers)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.2.2/5.2.3 (session tallies on GET) and 5.3.2 (void excludes the row)

### 2.2.2 — Derive and format the three-state FirstSwitch

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/firstSwitch.ts: `deriveFirstSwitch({events, episodeCount: ReportedCount, countMethod: CountMethod|null, estimateSeconds: number|null}) → {firstSwitch: FirstSwitch, method: FirstSwitchMethod|null} | null`. Rules: episodeCount null → null (not derivable; maps to the nullable first_switch_* columns); episodeCount 0 → {kind:'none_capped'}, method null; episodeCount > 0 and countMethod 'event' and the earliest episodeEvent has elapsedMs → {kind:'known', seconds: floor(elapsedMs/1000)}, method 'event'; otherwise estimateSeconds provided → {kind:'known', seconds: estimateSeconds}, method 'estimate' (estimate must satisfy 0 < s < FIRST_SWITCH_CAP_SECONDS, i.e. 1..1199 matching the 2.7.4 contract, else throws); otherwise {kind:'unknown'}, method null. episodeCount is taken as given: when countMethod is 'retrospective' event timing is not consulted and the event tally is never added to it (the two methods are alternatives). `formatFirstSwitch(fs: FirstSwitch)` → '20+, capped' | 'm:ss' | 'Unknown'. `toStoredFirstSwitch(result|null)` / `fromStoredFirstSwitch(row)` map to/from `{first_switch_kind, first_switch_seconds, first_switch_method}` (all null when the result is null). Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/firstSwitch.test.ts — cases: 'S=0 → none_capped and formats "20+, capped"'; 'first off_task event at 370000 ms → known 370 s, method event, formats "6:10"'; 'S=3 retrospective with no estimate → unknown, formats "Unknown" and the string never contains "20+"'; 'S=3 retrospective with estimate 480 → known 480, method estimate'; 'S null → null (never none_capped, never unknown)'; 'voided first event is skipped; the next counted episode times T'; 'countMethod event but the only episode event lacks elapsedMs → unknown'; 'estimate 1200 or 0 throws'; 'stored round-trip preserves all three kinds and the null case'; 'exhaustive formatting: only none_capped renders a cap label'; 'retrospective S=4 with two off_task events present → episodeCount stays 4 (never 6) and T is unknown without an estimate (methods are alternatives, never summed)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.8.4 (finalize stores first_switch_*)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- domain/firstSwitch` prints `Tests  11 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.2.1
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / Retrospective count without time · benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / No switches · benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / Event-timed first switch · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Paper tally entered · progress-report: Charts carry exact values / Chart with capped value

### 2.3.2 — Implement recall flag thresholds and blank-aware recall scoring in recall.ts

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/recall.ts (D16: recall rules live here, not in eligibility.ts) importing `RecallFlag` and `ReportedCount` from types.ts (2.7.1) and export it from packages/shared/src/index.ts. Exports: `RECALL_DELAY_FLAG_SECONDS = 600`, `RECALL_OVERRUN_FLAG_SECONDS = 210` (3 min + 30 s), `RECALL_POINT_COUNT = 5`, `RECALL_WINDOW_SECONDS = 180`; `recallDelaySeconds(intervalEndedAt: Date, recallStartedAt: Date): number` (floor, never negative — throws when recall starts before the interval ended; the API maps that case to 422 recall_before_interval_end per D27); `deriveRecallFlags({delaySeconds, durationSeconds}): RecallFlag[]` (delay > 600 → recall_delayed; duration > 210 → recall_overrun; strictly greater; flags only, never an ExclusionReason, D7.3); `isBlankPoint(text: string): boolean` (trim === ''); `scoreRecall(points: readonly string[] (5), scores: readonly (0|1|null)[] (5) | null) → {recallScores: readonly (0|1)[] | null, recallScore: ReportedCount, complete: boolean}` — a blank point is forced to 0 whatever the input; a non-blank point with no score (null entry, or `scores === null`) makes complete false, recallScore null and recallScores null (never 0, never a partial array: the stored jsonb shape is 5 × 0|1); otherwise recallScore = number of 1s (0–5, a number even when 0). No `?? 0` / `|| 0` anywhere (2.9.1 guard). Absorbs 5.7.1.

- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.7.2 (recall write sets delay, duration and flags) and 5.8.3 (finalize stores recall_scores/recall_score or null)

### 2.4.1 — Implement slot candidates, sample counts and comparison math

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/comparison.ts: `SlotKey = 'baseline:A'|'baseline:B'|'final:A'|'final:B'`; `selectSlotCandidates(attempts: readonly AttemptSummary[]) → Record<SlotKey, AttemptSummary|null>` (the single eligible attempt of each slot; midpoint ignored; two eligible attempts in one slot throws — the system never picks the better one); `sampleCounts(candidates) → {baselineEligible: 0|1|2, finalEligible: 0|1|2}`; `computeComparison(attempts) → ComparisonResult|null`: calls `assertSameRealm(attempts)` first (widened in 2.7.1 to any `{realm}` rows), returns null unless all four candidates exist, throws (never coalesces) if any candidate has episodeCount null, recallScore null, firstSwitch null or countMethod null (an eligible attempt always has them — anything else is corrupt input), then returns `{s0, s14, absoluteChange: s0−s14, percentageReduction: s0 > 0 ? round1(100·(s0−s14)/s0) : null, lowBaseline: s0 < 3, recallBaselineMean, recallFinalMean, firstSwitches: Record<SlotKey, FirstSwitch>, firstSwitchMeanSeconds: number|null (only when all four are 'known')}`. Uses `isReported` from types.ts; no `?? 0`. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/comparison.math.test.ts — cases: 'comparable change 6,4 → 3,3 with recall 4: s0 5, s14 3, absolute 2, percentage 40, recall 4 → 4'; 'zero baseline 0,0 → 0,0: percentageReduction null, absoluteChange 0, every numeric field passes Number.isFinite (no NaN/Infinity/100)'; 'low baseline s0 2, s14 1: absoluteChange 1, lowBaseline true'; 'two 20+ and two known T → firstSwitchMeanSeconds null'; 'four known T → mean'; 'one eligible final → null and sampleCounts {2,1}'; 'baseline A eligible, B ineligible → sampleCounts baselineEligible 1'; 'demo + pilot attempts → RealmMixingError, no partial result'; 'candidate with null episodeCount or null recallScore → throws, never treated as 0'; 'replacement after disruption: first attempt ineligible, second eligible → second is the slot candidate'; 'amendment-excluded attempt (eligible false, excluded_by_amendment) is never a candidate'; 'two eligible attempts in one slot → throws'. Type-level (checked by typecheck, not counted): an object literal with `phase: 'practice'` carries `// @ts-expect-error` against AttemptSummary — practice rows cannot enter the comparison.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 6.2.1 (GET /programs/{id}/report comparison and samples)

### 2.4.2 — Implement result-state precedence and PRD copy

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/comparison.ts using `ResultState`/`RESULT_STATES` from types.ts (2.7.1). `RESULT_STATE_COPY: Record<ResultState, {headline: string|null, message: string, action: string}>` with messages verbatim from the PRD §5 table: baseline_pending 'Complete two baseline sessions to establish your starting point.' / action 'Go to next slot'; final_pending 'Your final comparison is available after the Day 14 assessments.' / 'View baseline / continue practice'; insufficient_samples 'There is not enough comparable data for the full comparison.' / 'View attempts and missing slots'; zero_baseline 'No switches were reported at baseline; a percentage reduction does not apply.' / 'Compare recall and absolute counts'; more_switches 'More switches were reported in the final sessions.' / 'Inspect context'; unchanged 'The reported switch count did not change.' / 'Inspect context' (the only new message, per progress-report spec); fewer_switches_lower_recall 'Switches decreased, but recall was lower. These results are mixed.' / 'Review material and conditions'; improvement_maintained_recall 'You reported fewer switches; recall was maintained.' / 'Inspect conditions / export'. `headline` is 'Fewer reported switches' for improvement_maintained_recall (D38: rendered above the PRD line) and null for every other state. `CAUSE_NOTE = 'Observed change does not establish cause.'` `resolveResultState({baselineEligible, finalEligible, finalAttemptsExist, day14Finished (currentDay > 14), comparison: ComparisonResult|null}) → ResultState` evaluating in the fixed order (1) baseline_pending: baselineEligible < 2 && !day14Finished (2) final_pending: baselineEligible === 2 && !finalAttemptsExist && !day14Finished (3) insufficient_samples: comparison === null (4) zero_baseline: s0 === 0 (5) more_switches: s14 > s0 (6) unchanged: s14 === s0 (7) fewer_switches_lower_recall: recallFinalMean < recallBaselineMean (8) improvement_maintained_recall — first that applies (D7.4). Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/resultState.test.ts (table-driven) — rows: 'one eligible baseline before Day 15 → baseline_pending'; 'two baseline, no finals, Day 14 → final_pending'; 'two baseline, no finals, Day 15 → insufficient_samples (precedence 1/2 over 3)'; 'two baseline, one ineligible final attempt exists, Day 14 → insufficient_samples'; 'one baseline eligible after Day 14 → insufficient_samples, not baseline_pending'; 'PRD missing final (2 baseline, 1 final eligible) → insufficient_samples'; '0,0 → 0,0 → zero_baseline'; '0 → 1 → zero_baseline, not more_switches'; '3 → 5 → more_switches with message "More switches were reported in the final sessions."'; '4 → 4 → unchanged with message "The reported switch count did not change."'; 'PRD mixed 6,4 → 3,3, recall 4,4 → 2,2 → fewer_switches_lower_recall with message "Switches decreased, but recall was lower. These results are mixed." and headline null'; 'PRD comparable → improvement_maintained_recall with headline "Fewer reported switches", message "You reported fewer switches; recall was maintained." and CAUSE_NOTE non-empty (D38)'; 'no headline, message, action or CAUSE_NOTE contains "attention +", "improved by", "%", "significan" or "confidence"'; 'every ResultState has copy, headline is null for all states except improvement_maintained_recall, and RESULT_STATES order equals the evaluation order (exhaustive satisfies + array equality)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 6.2.2 (report resultState for every PRD §6 scenario and the extra states)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- resultState` prints `Tests  14 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.4.1
- **Spec refs:** progress-report: Result state with precedence / Mixed result fixture · progress-report: Result state with precedence / More switches · progress-report: Result state with precedence / Zero baseline beats direction · progress-report: Result state with precedence / Unchanged · progress-report: No complete comparison until two plus two / Missing final · progress-report: No invented scores / Improvement copy

### 2.4.3 — Implement comparability warnings between same-label attempts

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/comparison.ts per D7.5: `ComparabilityWarning {label: SlotLabel, field: 'deviceFormat'|'language'|'materialLevel'|'accommodations', baseline: string|null|readonly Accommodation[], final: same, message: string}`; `comparabilityWarnings(candidates: Record<SlotKey, AttemptSummary|null>) → ComparabilityWarning[]` comparing baseline:A with final:A and baseline:B with final:B only when both slot candidates (the eligible attempt of each slot) exist; accommodations compared as sets (order-insensitive); null vs value is a difference whose message says 'not recorded at baseline' / 'not recorded at final'; messages name the field and both values. The function reads `conditions` only — eligibility fields are never consulted or modified. Export from index.ts.

- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 6.2.3 (report warnings[])

### 2.5.1 — Implement band ceilings and practice-block qualification predicates

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/progression.ts: `BandCeiling {fromDay: number, toDay: number, minutes: number}`; `DEFAULT_BAND_CEILINGS: readonly BandCeiling[] = [{fromDay:1,toDay:3,minutes:10},{fromDay:4,toDay:7,minutes:15},{fromDay:8,toDay:10,minutes:20},{fromDay:11,toDay:14,minutes:25}]` (this is the value 4.1.1 stores in revision 1 settings.bandCeilings); `bandCeilingSeconds(day, ceilings = DEFAULT_BAND_CEILINGS)` (day ≤ first band's toDay → first band, so Day 0 → 600; days beyond the last band use the last band); `PracticeBlockRecord {sessionId, realm: Realm, kind: SessionKind, localDate: LocalDate, targetSeconds, completeInterval: boolean|null, outputQuality: OutputQuality|null, episodeCount: ReportedCount, finalized: boolean}`; `blockQualifies(block, currentTargetSeconds)` = finalized && kind === 'practice' && targetSeconds === currentTargetSeconds && completeInterval === true && outputQuality === 'yes' && episodeCount !== null && episodeCount <= 1; `dayQualifies(blocksOfOneDate, currentTargetSeconds)` = at least two qualifying blocks. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/progression.bands.test.ts — cases: 'default ceilings: Days 1–3 600, 4–7 900, 8–10 1200, 11–14 1500; Day 0 600; Day 15 1500'; 'initial 5-minute block: Day 1 ceiling remains 600'; 'custom ceilings passed from a revision override the defaults'; 'Partly output does not qualify'; 'episodeCount null does not qualify (unknown is not ≤ 1)'; 'episodeCount 2 does not qualify, 1 does, 0 does'; 'early finish (completeInterval false) does not qualify'; 'block at previous target 600 while current is 900 does not qualify'; 'benchmark kind never qualifies'; 'unfinalized block that reached its target never qualifies (expiry is not completion)'; 'one qualifying block → day does not qualify; two → qualifies; two plus a Partly third → still qualifies'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.5.3 (GET /programs/{id}/today suggestion)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- progression.bands` prints `Tests  11 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.1.1, 2.7.1
