### 2.5.2 — Implement the two-consecutive-day progression suggestion

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/progression.ts: `PROGRESSION_STEP_SECONDS = 300`, `PROGRESSION_ACCEPTED_REASON = 'progression accepted'`; `suggestProgression({day, currentTargetSeconds, blocks: readonly PracticeBlockRecord[], bandCeilings?: readonly BandCeiling[]}) → {suggestedTargetSeconds, qualifiedOn: [LocalDate, LocalDate]} | null` (bandCeilings defaults to DEFAULT_BAND_CEILINGS; 4.5.3 passes the governing revision's value). Throws RealmMixingError if blocks mix realms. Groups blocks by localDate, finds any pair (d, addDays(d,1)) where both days qualify at the current target (qualification is held: later missed days do not clear it; a missed day between two otherwise-qualifying days means they are not a pair), then returns a suggestion only when currentTargetSeconds + 300 ≤ bandCeilingSeconds(day, bandCeilings); otherwise null. Benchmark blocks are ignored; the benchmark duration is never an input or output. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/progression.suggest.test.ts — cases: 'Days 4 and 5 qualify at 900 → Day 6 null (ceiling 900)'; 'same blocks evaluated on Day 7 → null'; 'same blocks evaluated on Day 8 → suggests 1200 with qualifiedOn [Day 4, Day 5]'; 'missed Days 6 and 7 (no sessions) then Day 8 → still suggests (held, nothing reset)'; 'qualifying Days 4 and 6 with nothing on Day 5 → null (not adjacent)'; 'one of the two days has a Partly block → null'; 'target 1200 on Day 9 → null (never above ceiling)'; 'target 300 with Days 1–2 qualifying → suggests 600 on Day 3'; 'benchmark sessions on the qualifying days are ignored and 1200 (20 min) is never suggested from them'; 'after acceptance (target now 1200) the Day 4–5 blocks at 900 no longer qualify → null until two new days qualify at 1200'; 'mixed demo/pilot blocks → RealmMixingError'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.5.3 (GET today suggestion) and 4.4.1 (accepted suggestion creates a revision with reason 'progression accepted')
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- progression.suggest` prints `Tests  11 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.5.1
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Two qualifying days · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Partly output breaks the streak of qualifying days · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Suggestion accepted · practice-sessions: Practice requires a short intended output / Hold the target · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result

### 2.6.1 — Implement feed row validation with the platform-all rule and scope-aware aggregates

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts importing `FeedDevice`, `MeasurementScope`, `FeedSource`, `FEED_PLATFORM_ALL` from types.ts (2.7.1). `FeedRowInput {device: FeedDevice, platform: string, minutes: number, shortVideoMinutes: number|null, measurementScope: MeasurementScope, source: FeedSource, plannedWindow: boolean|null}`; `validateFeedRows(rows) → {ok: true} | {ok: false, errors: {index, code: 'feed_subset_violation'|'duplicate_feed_row'|'feed_platform_conflict', message}[]}` — codes are the D19 error codes so 6.1.2 maps them one-to-one: shortVideoMinutes > minutes → feed_subset_violation; same device+platform+measurementScope twice → duplicate_feed_row; D36 platform rule → feed_platform_conflict when a device has both a platform 'all' row and any platform-specific row, or when an 'all' row is not scope 'feed' with source 'estimate'. No allowance rule — overruns are valid. `feedAggregates(rows) → {unitLabel: 'device-minutes', feedDeviceMinutes: number|null (sum of scope 'feed' rows; null when none), feedByDevice: Record<FeedDevice, ReportedCount> (a device's 'all' row is its total; otherwise the sum of its feed-scope platform rows; null when the device has no feed-scope row), partial: boolean (D36: phone or desktop lacks a feed-scope row), shortVideoDeviceMinutes: number|null (subset over feed-scope rows reporting it, never added), appTotals: FeedRowInput[] (scope app_total, shown separately, never summed)}`. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/feed.aggregates.test.ts — cases: 'phone Instagram app_total 60 alongside phone Instagram feed 25 → feedDeviceMinutes 25 and appTotals contains the 60'; 'phone all 20 + desktop all 20 → 40 with unitLabel device-minutes, feedByDevice {phone 20, desktop 20}, partial false'; 'desktop all 20 only → feedByDevice.phone null, partial true, total 20'; 'explicit phone all 0 row → feedByDevice.phone 0, included, partial false with desktop present'; 'no rows → feedDeviceMinutes null, never 0'; 'short-video 45 on a 30-minute row → feed_subset_violation naming the subset rule'; 'short-video 20 on a 30-minute row → total 30 (subset not added)'; 'duplicate device/platform/scope → duplicate_feed_row'; 'phone all 30 together with phone instagram feed 10 → feed_platform_conflict; phone all 30 with desktop youtube feed 10 → ok and feedByDevice.phone 30'; 'an all row with scope app_total or source device_report → feed_platform_conflict'; 'phone instagram feed 10 + phone youtube feed 15 with no all row → feedByDevice.phone 25'; '55 minutes against a 20-minute allowance validates (no allowance rule)'; 'device_report source is carried through unchanged'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 6.1.2 (PUT days: subset 422 feed_subset_violation, platform conflict 422 feed_platform_conflict, partial total)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- feed.aggregates` prints `Tests  13 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.7.1
- **Spec refs:** daily-checkin: Blank is unknown; zero is explicit / No phone entry · daily-checkin: Blank is unknown; zero is explicit / Explicit zero · daily-checkin: Feed rows carry device, platform, scope and source / Short-video exceeds total · daily-checkin: Feed rows carry device, platform, scope and source / Device report transcription · daily-checkin: Aggregates respect scope and subset rules / App total alongside feed estimate · daily-checkin: Aggregates respect scope and subset rules / Two devices at once · daily-checkin: Overruns and missed days never block / Allowance exceeded

### 2.6.2 — Implement the single check-in completeness status and headline values

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Extend packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts importing `CheckinStatus`, `CHECKIN_FIELDS` from types.ts (2.7.1): `checkinStatus(checkin: {sleepMinutes: ReportedCount}|null, rows: readonly FeedRowInput[]) → {status: CheckinStatus, missing: ('sleep'|'feed')[]}` — THE single completeness implementation (D16), called by GET today (4.5.3) and the days routes (6.1.1, 6.1.2): null checkin → 'not_reported' with missing ['sleep','feed'] (a day gap, never zero); 'complete' only when sleepMinutes !== null AND at least one row with measurementScope 'feed' exists (an explicit-zero 'all' row counts; an app_total-only row does not complete — D36); otherwise 'incomplete' with missing listing 'sleep' when sleepMinutes is null and 'feed' when no feed-scope row exists. Status is derived from field values, never from the record's existence; `partial` (2.6.1) is not part of status. Also `checkinValues(checkin, rows) → {sleepMinutes: ReportedCount, phoneFeedMinutes: ReportedCount, desktopFeedMinutes: ReportedCount}` = sleepMinutes plus feedAggregates(rows).feedByDevice.phone/desktop (nulls preserved) — the D22 `today.checkin.values` shape. Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/domain/feed.status.test.ts — cases: 'sleep only → incomplete, missing [feed]'; 'one feed row only → incomplete, missing [sleep]'; 'sleep + explicit-zero phone all row → complete'; 'sleep 0 (explicit) is reported, not missing'; 'existing check-in with sleep null and no rows → incomplete with missing [sleep, feed], never complete by existence'; 'no check-in → not_reported with missing [sleep, feed]'; 'sleep + app_total-only row → incomplete, missing [feed] (D36)'; 'sleep + one platform feed row without an all row → complete'; 'checkinValues: no rows → phone and desktop null; phone all 25 + desktop youtube 10 + desktop twitter 5 → {phone 25, desktop 15}; sleep null stays null'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 6.1.1 (GET days sleep-only incomplete, no-row not_reported) and 4.5.3 (today check-in status, missing and values)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- feed.status` prints `Tests  9 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.6.1
- **Spec refs:** daily-checkin: Incomplete saves and completeness status / Sleep only · daily-checkin: Overruns and missed days never block / Skipped day · daily-checkin: Required and optional fields / Minimal check-in · daily-checkin: Low burden / Default visible fields · progress-report: Practice and daily trends are separate sections / Missing check-in day

`ReportedCountSchema = Type.Union([Type.Integer({minimum: 0}), Type.Null()])`; `LocalDateSchema` (pattern ^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$), `UuidSchema`, `IsoTimestampSchema`, `IdempotencyKeyHeader`; `ERROR_CODES` as const (the D19 vocabulary: malformed_request; not_found; program_exists, active_session_exists, idempotency_mismatch, stale_version, slot_frozen, event_count_mismatch, already_finalized, session_not_active, session_not_ended, interval_not_ended, recall_locked, not_finalized, invalid_status_transition, program_terminal; realm_mismatch, feed_subset_violation, feed_platform_conflict, duplicate_feed_row, baseline_times_too_close, readiness_regression, before_slot_date, slot_full, replacement_reason_required, eligible_attempt_not_retaken, practice_only, benchmark_only, benchmark_only_field, practice_only_field, impossible_offset, invalid_transition, recall_before_interval_end, recall_not_locked, effective_day_in_past, date_locked; write_limit — 37 codes) with `ErrorCode` and `ERROR_CODE_STATUS: Record<ErrorCode, 400|404|409|422|429>`; `REALM_MISMATCH_MESSAGE = 'Simulated and real results are never combined.'`; `ErrorResponse {code: Lit(ERROR_CODES), message, fieldErrors?: Record<string, string> (field path → short code; never a value echo), details?: Type.Object({}, {additionalProperties: true}) (D18: a small structured object — activeSessionId, expected/stored, current, phase/label, existingProgramId/existingStatus — never note text; the one deliberately open object in the barrel), retryable: boolean, requestId}`; `RealmSchema` (responses only); and Lit schemas for BenchmarkPhase, SlotLabel, SessionKind, SessionLifecycle, ProgramStatus, TimeSource, TimerQuality, CountMethod, OutputQuality, ExclusionReason, FirstSwitchKind, FirstSwitchMethod, SessionEventType, RecallFlag, FeedDevice, MeasurementScope, FeedSource, CheckinStatus, CheckinField, ResultState, IdentityMode, TransitionType, ClockGapResolution, BlockStatus, Accommodation. contracts/me.ts: `PreferencesSchema {hideTimerDefault: boolean, endChime: boolean, visibilityContext: boolean, milestoneAnnouncements: boolean (D22; default false)}`, `MeResponse {principalId, identityMode, realm, timezone, preferences: PreferencesSchema, demoClockOffsetSeconds: integer}`, `PatchPreferencesBody {timezone?, hideTimerDefault?, endChime?, visibilityContext?, milestoneAnnouncements?}`. contracts/demo.ts: `DemoClockBody {offsetSeconds: integer}` (absolute offset, D35), `ScenarioLoadParams {name: Lit(DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES)}`, no scenario-load body (D22: the UI confirms), `ScenarioLoadResponse {programId: Type.Union([UuidSchema, Type.Null()])}`. Export `Static<>` types for each; export the contracts from index.ts.

`TransitionBody {expectedVersion: integer ≥ 1, type: TransitionType, reason?: string 1..100}` (D29: stored only on pause as details.reason, accepted and ignored otherwise); `ClockGapBody {gapSeconds: integer ≥ 0, resolution: ClockGapResolution}`; `AgentPlanBody {expectedVersion, workstream? ≤ 200, waitingTask? ≤ 200, reviewCheckpoint?: 'end_of_block', resumeNote? ≤ 500}`; `AgentPlanResponse {sessionId, workstream: string|null, waitingTask, reviewCheckpoint, resumeNote, reviewAt: ISO|null, version}`; `RecallBody {points: exactly 5 strings each ≤ 500 (blank allowed), startedAt: ISO (server-aligned, D27), durationSeconds: integer ≥ 0}`; `ReviewInputSchema {episodeCount?: ReportedCount, countMethod?: CountMethod, firstSwitchEstimateSeconds?: integer 1..1199, externalCount?: ReportedCount, unplannedAgentChecks?: ReportedCount, mindWanderingCount?: ReportedCount, outputQuality?: OutputQuality, outputNote?: string ≤ 200, reviewNote?: string ≤ 2000 | null (D31), materiallyDisrupted?: boolean, disruptionNote?: string ≤ 2000, recallScores?: exactly 5 × (0|1|null), conditions?: ObservedConditions}` (no eligible, exclusionReasons, recallScore, points or realm keys — server-derived or locked); `FinalizeBody {expectedEventCount: integer ≥ 0 (D21: all stored rows, voided included), lastBatch?: EventsBatchBody, review: ReviewInputSchema}`; `FirstSwitchSchema` = union of the three kinds (only 'known' carries seconds); `ReviewResponse {sessionId, episodeCount, countMethod, firstSwitch: FirstSwitchSchema|null, firstSwitchMethod, externalCount, unplannedAgentChecks, mindWanderingCount, outputQuality, outputNote, reviewNote, materiallyDisrupted: boolean|null, disruptionNote, recallPoints: 5 strings|null, recallStartedAt, recallLockedAt, recallDelaySeconds: integer|null, recallDurationSeconds: integer|null, recallFlags: RecallFlag[], recallScores: 5 × (0|1) | null, recallScore: ReportedCount, conditions: ObservedConditions, finalizedAt: ISO|null, version}` (every count nullable, never 0 for blank); `AmendmentBody {reason: 1..2000, excludeFromReport: boolean}`; `AmendmentResponse {id, sessionId, reason, excludeFromReport, createdAt}`; ONE `SessionResponse` per D20 used by POST /sessions, GET active, GET by id, transitions, clock-gap and finalize: `{id, programId, slotId: uuid|null, revisionId, realm, kind, lifecycle, targetSeconds, startedAt, endedAt: ISO|null, pausedSeconds, currentPauseStartedAt: ISO|null, localDate, intendedOutput: string|null, timeSource, timerQuality, clockGapSeconds: integer|null, completeInterval: boolean|null, eligible: boolean|null, exclusionReasons: ExclusionReason[], replacementReason: string|null, version, serverNow: ISO, timing: {elapsedSeconds, remainingSeconds, deadlineReached: boolean, isPaused: boolean}, tallies: {offTask, external, agentChecks} (no combined field), eventCount: integer ≥ 0 (voided included), events: EventResponse[], review: ReviewResponse (D31: the row exists from start), agentPlan: AgentPlanResponse|null, amendments: AmendmentResponse[]}` (never userId); `FinalizeResponse {session: SessionResponse, review: ReviewResponse, eligible: boolean|null, exclusionReasons: ExclusionReason[]}`; `SessionIdParams {id: uuid}`, `EventIdParams {id: uuid, clientEventId: uuid}`. Static types exported; export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/contracts/sessions.test.ts — cases: 'CreateSessionBody: intendedOutput "" rejected, 201 chars rejected, 200 accepted'; 'CreateSessionBody.targetSeconds: 420 rejected, 1800 rejected, 300 and 1500 accepted (D30)'; 'EventsBatchBody: 101 events rejected, 100 accepted, 0 rejected'; 'clock_gap event: details {gapSeconds: 300} without resolution valid; with resolution "continued" valid; resolution "ignored" invalid; clock_gap without gapSeconds invalid; off_task with details {alsoOffTask: true} valid (D26)'; 'FinalizeBody review: episodeCount null accepted, absent accepted, "4" rejected, -1 rejected; reviewNote null and 2000 chars accepted, 2001 rejected (D31)'; 'FinalizeBody rejects `eligible`, `exclusionReasons`, `recallScore` and `points` keys (server-derived or locked)'; 'recallScores: [1,1,null,0,1] accepted; 4 entries rejected; a value of 2 rejected'; 'RecallBody with 4 points rejected, 5 with two blanks accepted'; 'TransitionBody: reason absent accepted, "planned_break" accepted, type "finalize" rejected (D29)'; 'ClockGapBody resolution "ignored" rejected, gapSeconds -1 rejected'; 'FirstSwitchSchema: {kind:"unknown"} valid, {kind:"known"} without seconds invalid, {kind:"none_capped", seconds: 5} invalid; ReviewResponse.firstSwitch null valid'; 'SessionResponse: the D20 example validates; tallies rejects an extra `total` key; timing without deadlineReached rejected; events entry with voidedAt null valid; agentPlan null valid; FinalizeResponse.eligible null valid'.

### 2.7.6 — Export contract barrel and prove realm rejection and cross-app Static types

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/contracts/index.ts re-exporting every schema and `Static<>` type, plus `REQUEST_BODY_SCHEMAS` (registry of every request body: PatchPreferences, DemoClock, CreateProgram, PatchProgram, PutSlots, CreateRevision, CreateSession, EventsBatch, Transition, ClockGap, AgentPlan, Recall, Finalize, Amendment, PutDay — 15 entries; scenario load has no body per D22) with one valid example each. Ensure packages/shared/src/index.ts exports `contracts` (each domain module and fixture module exports itself when it lands; this unit does not pre-export absent modules). Add `apps/api/src/contracts.typecheck.ts` and `apps/web/src/contracts.typecheck.ts` (no runtime code) that assign example literals to `Static<typeof CreateProgramBody>` and `Static<typeof FinalizeBody>` and carry a `@ts-expect-error` for an object with a `realm` key, so both apps' `typecheck` scripts compile the shared contracts under exactOptionalPropertyTypes. Route-level enforcement of what Value.Check proves here holds because 3.1.2 configures AJV with `coerceTypes: false, removeAdditional: false, useDefaults: false` (D22), so an unknown key is 400 malformed_request and null is never coerced to 0.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/contracts/realm.test.ts — cases: 'for every entry in REQUEST_BODY_SCHEMAS the example passes, and the example plus `realm: "pilot"` fails with an error whose path is /realm'; 'the example plus `userId` fails likewise'; 'nested: EventInput inside EventsBatchBody with `realm` fails; FinalizeBody.review with `realm` fails'; 'every object schema reachable from REQUEST_BODY_SCHEMAS and from every response schema has additionalProperties === false (recursive walk over nodes whose [Kind] is Object, descending into properties, items, anyOf), with ErrorResponse.details asserted as the single open object (D18)'; 'no request body schema declares a `realm` or `userId` property at any depth'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: schemas; the route-level rejection (400 malformed_request on a realm key) is covered by API integration in 4.1.2 (realm stamped demo regardless of body) and 3.2.4 (error envelope)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- contracts/realm` prints `Tests  5 passed`; `npm run typecheck` at the repo root exits 0 with apps/api and apps/web both compiling their contracts.typecheck.ts files
- **After:** 2.7.3, 2.7.4, 2.7.5, 1.2.3

### 2.8.1 — Define the relative-time demo scenario fixture shape and the four comparison scenarios

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/src/fixtures/demoScenarios.ts: `DemoScenario {name: DemoScenarioName, description, viewDay: number (the program day shown at load, D35), program: ProgramRow|null, revisions[], slots[], sessions[], events[], reviews[], amendments[], agentPlans[], checkins[], feedRows[], expected: {resultState: ResultState|null, percentageReduction?: number|null}}` with row types mirroring the design.md tables in camelCase and the D35 relative encoding: dates are program-day numbers (`assignedDay` 0|7|14 on slots, `localDay` on sessions, `day` on check-ins), instants are seconds relative to the load instant (`startedAtOffsetSeconds`, `endedAtOffsetSeconds: number|null`, `occurredAtOffsetSeconds`, `recallStartedAtOffsetSeconds`, `recallLockedAtOffsetSeconds`, `finalizedAtOffsetSeconds`), rows reference each other by fixture keys (`slotKey`, `sessionKey`, `checkinDay`) that the loader (3.5.4) maps to fresh UUIDs. Every root row (program, session, checkin) carries realm 'demo' (D34: child rows inherit); every session row carries timeSource 'demo_clock' (D34); every session row of any lifecycle has exactly one review row (D31: created at start; all-null for running sessions, observed conditions from the slot for benchmarks, empty for practice); ReportedCount fields are null when the scenario description calls them blank; benchmark sessions carry slotKey, practice sessions never; revision 1 is {effectiveDay 0, reason 'initial plan'} (D33); programs are status 'active' on Day 15 (completed is explicit via PATCH, D33). Each program uses timezone 'Europe/Madrid' and four slots (baseline A/B assignedDay 0, final A/B assignedDay 14). Export `anchorScenario(scenario, loadInstant: Date) → AnchoredScenario` computing baselineDate = addDays(localDateAt(loadInstant, tz), −viewDay) and turning every day number into a LocalDate (calendar.ts) and every offset into an ISO instant, so 3.5.4 is a thin insert. Content for this unit: comparable-change (viewDay 15; baseline S 6,4; final 3,3; recall 4 everywhere; expected improvement_maintained_recall, percentageReduction 40); mixed-result (same S; baseline recall 4,4; final 2,2; expected fewer_switches_lower_recall per D7.4); missing-final (final B stopped early with S blank → eligible false [interval_incomplete, count_unknown], firstSwitch null; expected insufficient_samples, percentageReduction null); zero-baseline (0,0 → 0,0; expected zero_baseline, percentageReduction null). Export `DEMO_SCENARIOS: Partial<Record<DemoScenarioName, DemoScenario>>` for now (2.8.2 completes it and narrows the type) and `attemptsOf(scenario) → AttemptSummary[]` mapping benchmark sessions + reviews + amendments to the widened AttemptSummary (unfinalized sessions map with eligible false and exclusionReasons [], nulls preserved). Export from index.ts.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/fixtures/demoScenarios.comparison.test.ts — cases: 'every program/session/checkin row in the four scenarios has realm demo and every session row timeSource demo_clock (D34)'; 'for each finalized benchmark attempt, evaluateEligibility over its stored fields returns exactly the stored eligible/exclusionReasons'; 'anchorScenario with loadInstant 2026-09-20T10:00:00Z and viewDay 15 yields baselineDate 2026-09-05, baseline slots on Day 0 and final slots on Day 14 of that date (D35)'; 'comparable-change: attemptsOf → selectSlotCandidates → computeComparison → resolveResultState yields improvement_maintained_recall = expected.resultState, s0 5, s14 3, percentageReduction 40, recall means 4 → 4'; 'mixed-result: yields fewer_switches_lower_recall with recall means 4 → 2'; 'missing-final: computeComparison null, insufficient_samples, sampleCounts {2,1}, final B episodeCount null (not 0) with [interval_incomplete, count_unknown] and firstSwitch null'; 'zero-baseline: zero_baseline, percentageReduction null, absoluteChange 0'; 'every benchmark session row carries a slotKey, every session row has exactly one review row (D31), and every finalized session has finalizedAtOffsetSeconds set'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: fixture data; loading is covered by API integration in 3.5.4 (POST /demo/scenarios/{name}/load anchors and replaces data) and 6.2.2 (report state per scenario)
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- demoScenarios.comparison` prints `Tests  8 passed`; `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` exits 0
- **After:** 2.1.2, 2.3.1, 2.4.2, 2.7.1

Plan changes are your choice.'`, `DISCOVERY_NOTE = 'Automated discovery not enabled'`, and `RESEARCH_CARDS` (exactly three, typed `Static<typeof ResearchCardSchema>`) taken from the protocol's evidence table and reference list: Castelo et al. 2025 (PNAS Nexus 4(2) pgaf017; randomized delayed-intervention trial, 467 enrolled; finding: blocking phone internet for two weeks improved objectively measured sustained attention; limitation: adherence and attrition limit interpretation, broader than stopping Reels alone; relevance: keeping the phone outside focus sessions is a practical adaptation, not a replication; https://academic.oup.com/pnasnexus/article/4/2/pgaf017/8016017); Mrazek et al. 2013 (Psychological Science 24(5); randomized trial of 48 undergraduates; finding: a two-week mindfulness course improved working memory and reading comprehension and reduced mind-wandering; limitation: structured instruction, not a few minutes on an app; relevance: 5–10 minutes daily is an adjunct, not the studied dose; https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/23538911/); Leroy & Glomb 2018 (Organization Science 29(3); four studies; finding: brief ready-to-resume plans reduced attention residue; limitation: did not directly establish improved performance after returning to the original task; relevance: leave a next-action note; batched agent review is an application, not a validated treatment; https://doi.org/10.1287/orsc.2017.1184). provenance.peerReview 'peer_reviewed'; provenance.reviewed 'abstract' (D40: the weaker claim, recorded in LIMITATIONS.md as pending Douglas's confirmation). ### 2.9.1 — Add the repository no-coalesce guard test

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Create packages/shared/test/tools/findZeroCoalesce.ts and packages/shared/test/noCoalesce.test.ts. The tool takes tsconfig paths (packages/shared/tsconfig.json and apps/api/tsconfig.json when present) and uses the TypeScript compiler API (typescript 5.9.3 is already a devDependency) to: (a) report every BinaryExpression with operator `??` or `||` whose right operand is the numeric literal 0 and whose left operand's checked type is a union containing null or undefined and number (a ReportedCount-shaped value); (b) run a line-based regex pass over all .ts/.sql files under packages/shared/src, apps/api/src and apps/api/src/db/migrations for `/coalesce\s*\([^)]*,\s*0\s*\)/i` (covers drizzle `sql` templates and raw SQL); (c) report lines matching `/integer\(\s*['"](episode_count|external_count|unplanned_agent_checks|mind_wandering_count|recall_score|sleep_minutes|stress|mindfulness_minutes|feed_estimate_min|clock_gap_seconds)['"]\s*\)[^\n]*\.default\(\s*0\s*\)/` (D7.1 nullable columns plus D26's clock_gap_seconds). Findings are `{file, line, snippet, rule}`. Fixtures live under packages/shared/test/fixtures/coalesce/ and are excluded from type-checking by adding `"exclude": ["test/fixtures/coalesce/**"]` to packages/shared/tsconfig.json; the tool compiles them ad hoc with their imports.
- **Unit tests:** packages/shared/test/noCoalesce.test.ts — cases: 'fixture `count ?? 0` where count: ReportedCount is reported with file and line'; 'fixture `n || 0` where n: number (non-nullable) is not reported'; 'fixture sql template COALESCE(episode_count, 0) is reported'; 'fixture `integer(\'recall_score\').default(0)` is reported'; 'current tree: zero findings across packages/shared/src and apps/api/src (skips apps/api gracefully when its tsconfig is absent)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: static analysis over source; the runtime counterpart (export writes empty cells for blank counts) is covered by API integration in 6.3.1
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- noCoalesce` prints `Tests  5 passed` with the last case reporting 0 findings; temporarily adding `const y = x ?? 0` with x: ReportedCount to any file under packages/shared/src makes the same command fail naming that file and line
- **After:** 1.5.1, 2.2.2, 2.3.2, 2.4.3, 2.5.2, 2.6.2
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Blank count · progress-report: Export preview and download / Export with a blank count · daily-checkin: Blank is unknown; zero is explicit

## 3. API foundation

### 3.1.1 — Implement loadConfig with identity-mode, loopback and real-mode gates

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/config.ts: `export class ConfigError extends Error` (boot exits 1 on it); `export type IdentityMode = 'local-demo' | 'real'`; `export interface AppConfig { identityMode; host; port; databaseUrl; webOrigin?; webDistDir?; logLevel }`; `export function loadConfig(env: Record<string, string | undefined>): AppConfig` — pure, never touches process.env. Order: (0) run 1.3.2's `validateRequiredSettings(env)` (moved from server.ts into config.ts; server.ts re-exports it and `formatRefusal` so 1.3.2's tests keep passing) — missing DATABASE_URL/API_HOST/API_PORT/IDENTITY_MODE → ConfigError with the `formatRefusal` text; (1) IDENTITY_MODE must be exactly 'local-demo' or 'real' (no trimming, no case normalization) else 'IDENTITY_MODE must be one of: local-demo, real'; no fallback between modes. (2) local-demo: `export const LOOPBACK_HOSTS = ['127.0.0.1', 'localhost', '::1'] as const` (D35); any other API_HOST ('0.0.0.0', '::', a LAN IP) → ConfigError naming API_HOST and containing 'demo mode must never be exposed on a network interface'. (3) real: `export const REAL_MODE_PREREQUISITES = ['AUTH_SECRET','GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID','GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET','AUTH_INVITE_ALLOWLIST'] as const`; the ConfigError lists exactly the missing names one per line in that order, states 'real authentication is not yet implemented' and points to LIMITATIONS.md; with all four present it still refuses with 'real authentication is not yet implemented (see LIMITATIONS.md)' and no fixed principal is ever configured (D2, D7.6). (4) API_PORT must parse as an integer 0..65535 (message names it); WEB_ORIGIN and WEB_DIST_DIR are read through (empty string → undefined; directory existence is checked by 3.2.5 at registration); LOG_LEVEL defaults to 'info'. `export function assertLocalDemoConfig(config: AppConfig): void` re-runs rules (1)–(2) on a built config (used by buildApp in 3.1.2). apps/api/src/preferences.ts: `export const DEFAULT_PREFERENCES = { hideTimerDefault: false, endChime: false, visibilityContext: false, milestoneAnnouncements: false } as const` (D15, D22, D39) — created here because 3.2.3 seeds the principal profile with it before 3.5.1 exists. No preference environment variables are read; the DEFAULT_* lines in .env.example are documentation only.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/config.test.ts: (1) IDENTITY_MODE absent → ConfigError whose message contains 'IDENTITY_MODE', 'local-demo' and 'real'; (2) IDENTITY_MODE '' → same; (3) 'demo' → refused, message lists both accepted values; (4) 'Local-Demo' → refused (no normalization); (5) local-demo + API_HOST 127.0.0.1 → ok, host '127.0.0.1'; (6) localhost → ok; (7) '::1' → ok (D35); (8) 0.0.0.0 → refused, message contains 'API_HOST' and 'demo mode must never be exposed on a network interface'; (9) '::' → refused; (10) 192.168.1.5 → refused; (11) API_HOST unset → refused naming API_HOST; (12) real with none of the four → message lists AUTH_SECRET, GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, AUTH_INVITE_ALLOWLIST in order plus 'not yet implemented' and 'LIMITATIONS.md'; (13) real with only AUTH_SECRET → lists the other three, not AUTH_SECRET; (14) real with all four dummy values → still ConfigError containing 'not yet implemented' and 'LIMITATIONS.md'; (15) DATABASE_URL missing → message names DATABASE_URL; (16) API_PORT 'abc' → names API_PORT; (17) API_PORT '70000' → names API_PORT; (18) purity: with process.env.IDENTITY_MODE set to 'local-demo' inside the test, loadConfig({}) still throws the missing-mode error; (19) DEFAULT_PREFERENCES deep-equals { hideTimerDefault: false, endChime: false, visibilityContext: false, milestoneAnnouncements: false }; (20) WEB_DIST_DIR 'apps/web/dist' → webDistDir 'apps/web/dist'; absent or '' → undefined; (21) LOOPBACK_HOSTS deep-equals ['127.0.0.1','localhost','::1'].
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function; process-level exit behavior is covered by the spawn tests in 3.1.2
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/config.test.ts  → 21 tests pass, 0 failures
- **After:** 1.2.1, 1.3.1, 1.3.2
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Identity mode is an explicit startup setting / Missing identity mode · identity-realm: Identity mode is an explicit startup setting / Unrecognized identity mode · identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Loopback binding accepted · identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Non-loopback binding refused · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode without prerequisites · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode is never satisfied by demo defaults

### 3.1.2 — Implement buildApp factory, strict AJV options and server.ts boot with spawn tests

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/app.ts: `export async function buildApp(config: AppConfig, opts?: { loggerInstance?: pino.Logger }): Promise<FastifyInstance>` — calls `assertLocalDemoConfig(config)` first and throws ConfigError when `config.identityMode !== 'local-demo'` (defense in depth: no app, no routes and no fixed principal exist outside local-demo); creates Fastify 5 with the TypeBox type provider, `genReqId: () => randomUUID()`, `requestIdHeader: false` (a client-supplied x-request-id is never trusted), `disableRequestLogging: true` (3.2.2 emits the single completion entry), and `ajv: { customOptions: { coerceTypes: false, removeAdditional: false, useDefaults: false, allErrors: true, verbose: false } }` (D22: AJV coercion would turn `null` into `0` for integer schemas, violating unknown != zero; removeAdditional false plus `additionalProperties: false` contracts make unknown keys 400); decorates `app.config` (frozen AppConfig); registers plugins in a fixed order under prefix `/api/v1` (db 3.2.1, requestId/requestLog/noStore 3.2.2, errors 3.2.4, identity 3.2.3, routes 3.5.x and later groups), then the static web plugin (3.2.5) outside the prefix. apps/api/src/boot.ts: `startServer(config)` = buildApp + `app.listen({ host, port })` + log `listening on http://<host>:<actual port>` from `app.addresses()` (port 0 resolves to the real port); `installSignalHandlers(app, proc = process)` — SIGINT/SIGTERM → `await app.close()` → `proc.exit(0)`. apps/api/src/server.ts (entry, keeps 1.2.1's argv entry guard; re-exports 1.3.2's `validateRequiredSettings`/`formatRefusal` from config.ts): `loadConfig(process.env)` in try/catch; on ConfigError write the message to stderr and `process.exit(1)` before any listen; else `startServer` + `installSignalHandlers`. server.ts never loads a .env file itself — env comes from 1.2.1's scripts (`node --env-file-if-exists=../../.env --import tsx src/server.ts`; D37: the API runs from source under tsx, no compiled artifact, no `dist/server.js`). 1.2.1's `buildServer()` is removed and apps/api/test/server.test.ts is rewritten against `buildApp` with a test config (its three cases kept).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/app.test.ts: (1) buildApp with identityMode 'real' rejects with ConfigError whose message contains 'local-demo', before any plugin registers; (2) buildApp local-demo with host '0.0.0.0' rejects (assertLocalDemoConfig re-run in the factory); (3) buildApp local-demo with host '127.0.0.1', port 0 and `databaseUrl: process.env.DATABASE_URL_TEST` resolves and `app.ready()` succeeds; (4) installSignalHandlers with a fake EventEmitter and a fake exit: emitting 'SIGTERM' awaits `app.close()` (spied) then calls exit(0); (5) a test-only route with body schema `Type.Object({ n: Type.Union([Type.Integer(), Type.Null()]) })`: `{ n: null }` reaches the handler as null, not 0 (no coercion); (6) `{ n: '3' }` → 400 (string never coerced to integer).
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/boot.test.ts spawns `process.execPath --import tsx src/server.ts` (cwd apps/api) with a fully controlled env per case (never inheriting IDENTITY_MODE from the parent), 15 s timeout: (a) IDENTITY_MODE unset → exit 1, stderr contains 'IDENTITY_MODE' and 'local-demo, real', nothing listening; (b) IDENTITY_MODE=pilot → exit 1, stderr lists the accepted values; (c) local-demo + API_HOST=0.0.0.0 → exit 1, stderr contains 'demo mode must never be exposed on a network interface'; (d) real with no secrets → exit 1, stderr lists AUTH_SECRET, GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, AUTH_INVITE_ALLOWLIST plus 'not yet implemented' and 'LIMITATIONS.md'; (e) real with all four dummy values → exit 1 and no 'listening' line; (f) local-demo + API_HOST=127.0.0.1 + API_PORT=0 + DATABASE_URL=$DATABASE_URL_TEST → stdout has a listening line matching `127.0.0.1:(\d+)`, a TCP connect to that port succeeds, then `child.kill('SIGTERM')`; on non-win32 the exit code is 0, on win32 the child is observed exited and the port refuses connections afterwards (D40: graceful shutdown verified on POSIX only; unit case 4 covers the close path).
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/app.test.ts test/boot.test.ts  → 12 tests pass (6 unit + 6 boot cases)
- **After:** 3.1.1
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Identity mode is an explicit startup setting / Missing identity mode · identity-realm: Identity mode is an explicit startup setting / Unrecognized identity mode · identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Loopback binding accepted · identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Non-loopback binding refused · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode without prerequisites · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode is never satisfied by demo defaults

### 3.2.1 — Implement db plugin and the API test foundation (buildTestApp, captureLogs, truncateAll)

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/plugins/db.ts (fastify-plugin): creates a `postgres(config.databaseUrl)` client and `drizzle(client, { schema })` (schema barrel `src/db/schema/index.ts` from 1.4.1, filled by 3.3.x); decorates `app.db` and `app.sql`; `onClose` ends the client. Type augmentation in apps/api/src/types/fastify.d.ts (`db`, `sql`, `config`). apps/api/test/helpers/captureLogs.ts: a pino destination collecting JSON lines in memory with `lines()`, `text()`, `entries()` and `entriesWith(reqId)`. apps/api/test/helpers/testDb.ts: `resolveTestDatabaseUrl(env)` returns DATABASE_URL_TEST or throws naming it, and throws when the database name (via 1.4.2's `parseDatabaseName`) does not end in `_test` — tests can never point at the dev database. apps/api/test/helpers/buildTestApp.ts: `buildTestApp(overrides?)` builds the app with `{ identityMode: 'local-demo', host: '127.0.0.1', port: 0, databaseUrl: resolveTestDatabaseUrl(process.env), logLevel: 'info' }` and a pino logger writing to a fresh captureLogs destination; returns `{ app, db, sql, logs, truncateAll(), close() }`; `truncateAll` truncates every table exported by the schema barrel with CASCADE (3.2.3 extends it to re-seed the principal profile). This is the API test foundation (D16): 4.1.1's program seed helpers and 5.1.3's session seed helpers import `buildTestApp`/`truncateAll` from here and never re-create them. Test runner wiring is not touched: 1.5.1 owns apps/api/vitest.config.ts (`fileParallelism: false`, `setupFiles: ['./test/setup/env.ts']` loading the root .env) and 1.5.2 owns `test/setup/globalSetup.ts` (bootstrapTestDb creates attention_lab_test and runs migrate() whenever `src/db/migrations/meta/_journal.json` exists) — this unit adds no globalSetup and no .env loader.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/plugins/db.test.ts (unit part): (1) resolveTestDatabaseUrl({}) throws an error naming DATABASE_URL_TEST; (2) resolveTestDatabaseUrl({ DATABASE_URL_TEST: 'postgres://u:p@127.0.0.1:55432/attention_lab' }) throws because the database name does not end in `_test`; (3) captureLogs parses three JSON lines into three entries, `entriesWith('abc')` returns only entries with reqId 'abc', `text()` contains all three.
- **Integration tests:** same file against attention_lab_test: (4) after `ready()` the plugin exposes `app.db` and `app.sql` and registering it twice is refused (fastify-plugin name guard); (5) `app.sql\`select 1 as one\`` returns [{ one: 1 }]; (6) `db.execute(sql\`select current_database()\`)` returns 'attention_lab_test'; (7) after `close()` a further query rejects (client ended); (8) two sequential buildTestApp()/close() cycles succeed and vitest exits without open-handle warnings.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/plugins/db.test.ts  → 8 tests pass and the run exits without open-handle warnings
- **After:** 1.4.2, 1.5.1, 1.5.2, 3.1.2
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Loopback binding accepted

### 3.2.2 — Implement request-id, no-store and privacy-safe logger with one completion entry

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/logging.ts: `createLogger({ level, destination? })` returns pino with serializers `req → { id, method, url, route: req.routeOptions?.url }` and `res → { statusCode }` only (never headers, query, params or body; Fastify 5 has no routerPath), `redact: ['req.headers.authorization', 'req.headers.cookie']` as a belt-and-braces guard, and no `err` serializer (3.2.4 logs errors through `sanitizeErrorForLog`). apps/api/src/plugins/requestId.ts: `onSend` hook sets `x-request-id: request.id` on every response including errors and not-found (genReqId/requestIdHeader are set in 3.1.2). apps/api/src/plugins/requestLog.ts: `onResponse` hook emits exactly one entry per request: `request.log.info({ method, url, route: request.routeOptions?.url ?? null, statusCode: reply.statusCode, responseTime: reply.elapsedTime }, 'request completed')` — the observability NFR (request id, route, status, duration) and the only per-request entry (Fastify request logging disabled in 3.1.2; no per-tick telemetry). apps/api/src/plugins/noStore.ts: `onSend` hook sets `Cache-Control: no-store` on every `/api/v1/*` response — including unknown-route 404s and error responses — unless the matched route declares `config: { public: true }` (only `GET /research/cards` in 6.4.1 opts out; every other route is private). Static web responses (3.2.5) are outside `/api/v1` and untouched.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/plugins/headers.test.ts (unit part): (1) the `req` serializer applied to a request-like object carrying headers, query, params and body emits only the keys id, method, url, route; (2) the `res` serializer emits only statusCode; (3) `createLogger` redact paths include 'req.headers.authorization' and 'req.headers.cookie'.
- **Integration tests:** same file, inject against buildTestApp with test-only routes registered in the test (`GET /api/v1/__test/private`, `GET /api/v1/__test/public` with config.public, `GET /api/v1/__test/throw`): (4) every response carries `x-request-id` matching the UUID v4 regex; (5) two consecutive requests get different ids; (6) a request sent with header `x-request-id: attacker-supplied` gets a fresh UUID, never the supplied value, in header and log; (7) unknown route 404 carries `x-request-id` and `cache-control: no-store`; (8) private route → `cache-control: no-store`; (9) public route → no `no-store`; (10) throwing handler → 500 still carries both headers; (11) after one request, `logs.entriesWith(reqId)` holds exactly one entry, with method, url, route, statusCode, responseTime and no `headers`, `query`, `params` or `body` key anywhere in it.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/plugins/headers.test.ts  → 11 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.1
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

### 3.2.4 — Implement error classes, log sanitizer and the error-envelope mapper plugin

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/errors.ts: `AppError { status, code, message, fieldErrors?, details?, retryable }`; `MalformedError(message, fieldErrors?)` (400 'malformed_request'); `NotFoundError()` (404 'not_found', fixed message 'Not found' — missing and not-owned alike, never 403, so existence is not disclosed); `ConflictError(code: ConflictCode, message, opts?)` (409) with `CONFLICT_CODES = ['program_exists','active_session_exists','idempotency_mismatch','stale_version','slot_frozen','event_count_mismatch','already_finalized','session_not_active','session_not_ended','interval_not_ended','recall_locked','not_finalized','invalid_status_transition','program_terminal']`; `DomainError(code: DomainCode, message, opts?)` (422) with `DOMAIN_CODES = ['realm_mismatch','feed_subset_violation','feed_platform_conflict','duplicate_feed_row','baseline_times_too_close','readiness_regression','before_slot_date','slot_full','replacement_reason_required','eligible_attempt_not_retaken','practice_only','benchmark_only','benchmark_only_field','practice_only_field','impossible_offset','invalid_transition','recall_before_interval_end','recall_not_locked','effective_day_in_past','date_locked']`; `WriteLimitError()` (429 'write_limit') exists as a class only — the code is reserved (D19) and no limiter is implemented in this change; no route throws it. `retryable` defaults true for 429, 5xx and 'event_count_mismatch', false otherwise, overridable via `opts.retryable`. `opts.details` is the D18 structured object emitted under `details`: `active_session_exists → { activeSessionId }`, `event_count_mismatch → { expected, stored }`, `stale_version → { current: <resource> }`, `slot_frozen → { phase, label }`, `program_exists → { existingProgramId, existingStatus }`; details never carry note text. `sanitizeErrorForLog(err): { name, sqlstate?, stackTop }` — only the constructor name, a Postgres SQLSTATE `code` if present and the first stack frame; never `message`, `detail`, `hint`, `where` or `query` (postgres-js errors echo failing rows, which can contain notes). The envelope contract is 2.7.2's `ErrorResponse` in packages/shared/src/contracts/common.ts (`{ code, message, fieldErrors?, details?, retryable, requestId }`); this unit imports it for the reply schema and asserts it declares the optional `details` object. apps/api/src/plugins/errors.ts: `setErrorHandler` maps AppError → envelope with `requestId = request.id`; Fastify validation errors (`error.validation`) → 400 'malformed_request' with `fieldErrors: Record<string,string>` keyed by dotted instance path ('review.episodeCount'), `additionalProperties` → the offending property name with 'is not an accepted field', `required` → the missing property name with 'is required'; messages are AJV-keyword derived plain words and never contain submitted values; `RealmMixingError` from shared → 422 'realm_mismatch' with the fixed message 'Simulated and real results are never combined.' (realm values never echoed); any other error → 500 'internal_error', generic message, `retryable: true`, logged as `{ reqId, code: 'internal_error', statusCode: 500, route, err: sanitizeErrorForLog(err) }` — never the request body, query, headers or raw error; 4xx AppErrors logged at info as `{ reqId, code, statusCode, route }`; `setNotFoundHandler` → the same envelope. Messages for 'idempotency_mismatch' and 'stale_version' are plain sentences that never include the key, version numbers or ids (app-shell).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/plugins/errors.test.ts (unit part): (1) toEnvelope(MalformedError) → 400 'malformed_request' retryable false; (2) NotFoundError → 404 'not_found' message 'Not found'; (3) ConflictError('active_session_exists') retryable false and ConflictError('event_count_mismatch') retryable true; (4) DomainError → 422 retryable false, WriteLimitError → 429 retryable true; (5) `new ConflictError('stale_version', msg, { retryable: true })` honored; (6) fieldErrorsFromValidation on a sample AJV error list (type error at /review/episodeCount, additionalProperties 'realm', required 'baselineDate') built with submitted value 'SECRET-VALUE' → keys 'review.episodeCount', 'realm' ('is not an accepted field'), 'baselineDate' ('is required') and no message contains 'SECRET-VALUE'; (7) RealmMixingError → 422 'realm_mismatch' with a message containing neither 'demo' nor 'pilot'; (8) sanitizeErrorForLog on an Error with message 'boom SECRET', `detail: 'Failing row contains (SECRET-DETAIL)'` and `code: '23514'` → `{ name: 'Error', sqlstate: '23514', stackTop }` and JSON.stringify of it contains neither marker; (9) details carriers: event_count_mismatch `{ expected: 5, stored: 4 }`, active_session_exists `{ activeSessionId }`, slot_frozen `{ phase, label }`, program_exists `{ existingProgramId, existingStatus }`, stale_version `{ current }` each appear verbatim under envelope.details; (10) CONFLICT_CODES and DOMAIN_CODES deep-equal the D19 lists and 2.7.2's ErrorResponse schema declares an optional `details` object.
- **Integration tests:** same file, test-only routes with TypeBox schemas (`additionalProperties: false`) and handlers throwing each class: (11) POST body `{ realm: 'pilot', baselineDate: '2026-09-06' }` → 400 with `fieldErrors.realm` and the string 'pilot' absent from the response body; (12) missing required field → 400 with `fieldErrors.baselineDate: 'is required'`; (13) `{ review: { episodeCount: null } }` against `Type.Union([Type.Integer(), Type.Null()])` reaches the handler as null (not 0) and `{ review: { episodeCount: '3' } }` → 400 (never coerced); (14) NotFoundError → 404 envelope with message 'Not found'; (15) ConflictError('idempotency_mismatch') → 409 and the message matches no UUID regex; (16) thrown RealmMixingError → 422 'realm_mismatch' with the fixed message; (17) WriteLimitError → 429 retryable true; (18) `throw new Error('boom SECRET')` → 500 generic message not containing 'SECRET'; the log entry for that reqId has code 'internal_error', statusCode 500, err.name and no 'SECRET'; (19) unknown route → 404 envelope; (20) for every response above `body.requestId === headers['x-request-id']`; (21) `fieldErrors` and `details` keys are absent (not null) when not applicable; (22) every error response carries `cache-control: no-store`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/plugins/errors.test.ts  → 22 tests pass
- **After:** 2.7.2, 3.2.2
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Client cannot choose the realm · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result / Mixed realms in a comparison · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch · session-recovery: Stale writes conflict instead of overwriting · app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing

### 3.3.1 — Drizzle schema: user_profiles, programs, protocol_revisions, benchmark_slots + migration 0000

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/db/schema/{userProfiles,programs,protocolRevisions,benchmarkSlots}.ts and barrel index.ts (created empty in 1.4.1, filled here). pgEnums: `realm_enum('demo','pilot')`, `program_status_enum('draft','baseline_ready','active','completed','archived')`, `benchmark_phase_enum('baseline','midpoint','final')`, `slot_label_enum('A','B')`. Columns exactly as design.md: user_profiles(id text PK, timezone text NOT NULL, preferences jsonb NOT NULL, demo_clock_offset_seconds int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()); programs(id uuid PK, user_id text NOT NULL FK user_profiles, realm realm_enum NOT NULL with NO default (the server stamps it explicitly; D34 realm root), baseline_date date NOT NULL, timezone text NOT NULL, status program_status_enum NOT NULL, leisure_allowance_min int NOT NULL, feed_estimate_min int NULL no default, current_revision_id uuid NULL FK protocol_revisions via `AnyPgColumn` (nullable only because of the circular FK; set inside the creation transaction), version int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1) with partial unique index `programs_one_open_per_user` ON (user_id) WHERE status IN ('draft','baseline_ready','active'); protocol_revisions(id uuid PK, program_id FK, revision int NOT NULL, effective_day int NOT NULL, settings jsonb NOT NULL {practiceTargetSeconds, bandCeilings, leisureAllowanceMin}, reason text NOT NULL, created_at) UNIQUE(program_id, revision); benchmark_slots(id uuid PK, program_id FK, phase, label, material_ref text NOT NULL, language text NULL, device_format text NULL, material_level text NULL, planned_local_time time NULL, assigned_local_date date NOT NULL, frozen_at timestamptz NULL) UNIQUE(program_id, phase, label). Generate `npx drizzle-kit generate --name profiles_programs_slots` → src/db/migrations/0000_profiles_programs_slots.sql, review, commit with meta/. Schema tests use their own profile id 'schema-test-user' (never 'local-demo') so they stay independent of the identity seeding in 3.2.3.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/db/programs.schema.test.ts (unit part, drizzle `getTableConfig`, no DB): (1) programs has an index named programs_one_open_per_user whose `where` SQL mentions draft, baseline_ready and active; (2) feed_estimate_min is nullable with no default; (3) programs.realm is NOT NULL and has no default; (4) protocol_revisions unique on (program_id, revision); (5) benchmark_slots unique on (program_id, phase, label).
- **Integration tests:** same file after 1.5.2's globalSetup migrates attention_lab_test: (6) insert profile 'schema-test-user' + draft program ok; (7) a second program for the same user with status 'active' → SQLSTATE 23505 naming programs_one_open_per_user; (8) a second program with status 'completed' → allowed; (9) a program inserted without realm → 23502 (not-null violation, no default leaks); (10) two revisions with the same (program_id, revision) → 23505; (11) two slots (baseline, A) → 23505; (12) (baseline, A) and (final, A) → ok; (13) a program inserted with feed_estimate_min omitted reads back null, never 0.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/db/programs.schema.test.ts  → 13 tests pass; then `(cd apps/api && npx drizzle-kit check)` exits 0 with no reported conflicts
- **After:** 1.4.1, 3.2.1
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm

### 3.2.3 — Implement identity context plugin with demo clock, realm stamp and owner guards

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/plugins/identity.ts: `export const LOCAL_DEMO_PRINCIPAL_ID = 'local-demo'`; `export interface RequestContext { principalId: string; realm: Realm; identityMode: IdentityMode; now: Date; demoClockOffsetSeconds: number; timeSource: 'measured' | 'demo_clock' }` (Realm from @attention-lab/shared). Pure `deriveContext({ identityMode, offsetSeconds, realNow })` throws ConfigError for any mode other than local-demo, else returns principalId 'local-demo', realm 'demo', `now = realNow + offsetSeconds*1000`, `timeSource = offsetSeconds === 0 ? 'measured' : 'demo_clock'` (D8). `ensurePrincipalProfile(db)` inserts the `user_profiles` row for 'local-demo' with `ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING` (timezone 'UTC' until Setup confirms one, preferences = DEFAULT_PREFERENCES from 3.1.1, demo_clock_offset_seconds 0); called from the plugin's `onReady` hook and from `buildTestApp.truncateAll()` (3.2.1 helper extended here) so tests that truncate keep a principal. `onRequest` hook reads `demo_clock_offset_seconds` for the principal on every request and decorates `request.ctx`; client-supplied `x-principal-id` / `x-realm` headers are ignored; a missing profile row is an internal error (never a silent default). Exports `ownerStamp(ctx) → { userId: ctx.principalId, realm: ctx.realm }` for inserts on the three realm roots (D34), `assertOwnRealm(ctx, realm)` throwing DomainError('realm_mismatch') when a loaded row's realm differs from ctx.realm (a pilot row can never be created or read in local-demo), and `assertOwnedBy(ctx, row: { user_id: string })` throwing NotFoundError (404, never 403) when `row.user_id !== ctx.principalId` — the helper every service uses for owned resources. No route or service reads env directly; all receive ctx.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/plugins/identity.test.ts (unit part): (1) deriveContext offset 0 → timeSource 'measured', now === realNow; (2) offset 86400 → 'demo_clock', now = realNow + 1 day; (3) negative offset → 'demo_clock'; (4) identityMode 'real' → throws ConfigError; (5) ownerStamp returns { userId: 'local-demo', realm: 'demo' }; (6) assertOwnRealm(ctx, 'pilot') throws DomainError code 'realm_mismatch' and assertOwnRealm(ctx, 'demo') passes; (7) assertOwnedBy(ctx, { user_id: 'other-user' }) throws NotFoundError with status 404 and message 'Not found', and passes for user_id 'local-demo'.
- **Integration tests:** same file with a test-only route `GET /api/v1/__test/ctx` (defined in apps/api/test/helpers/testRoutes.ts so 3.5.2 can reuse it) returning `request.ctx` plus `Date.now()`: (8) principalId 'local-demo', realm 'demo', identityMode 'local-demo', demoClockOffsetSeconds 0, timeSource 'measured'; (9) with headers `x-principal-id: someone-else` and `x-realm: pilot` the ctx is unchanged; (10) after `UPDATE user_profiles SET demo_clock_offset_seconds = 1209600` the next request's `now - realNow` is 1209600 s (+/- 2 s) and timeSource is 'demo_clock'; (11) on an empty database the user_profiles row for 'local-demo' exists immediately after `app.ready()` with timezone 'UTC' and preferences deep-equal DEFAULT_PREFERENCES; (12) after `truncateAll()` the ctx route still returns 200 with principalId 'local-demo' (profile re-seeded).
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/plugins/identity.test.ts  → 12 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.1, 3.2.4, 3.3.1
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Loopback binding accepted · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode is never satisfied by demo defaults · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Records created in demo mode · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner / Another principal's session · identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode

### 3.3.2 — Drizzle schema: sessions aggregate with nullable counts + migration 0001

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/db/schema/{focusSessions,sessionEvents,sessionReviews,sessionAmendments,agentPlans}.ts. pgEnums: session_kind('practice','benchmark'), lifecycle('running','paused','awaiting_review','finalized','abandoned'), time_source('measured','demo_clock','attested'), timer_quality('ok','uncertain'), event_type('off_task','external','agent_check','pause','resume','clock_gap','visibility'), count_method('event','retrospective'), first_switch_kind('none_capped','known','unknown'), first_switch_method('event','estimate'), output_quality('yes','partly','no'). focus_sessions: id uuid PK, user_id text NOT NULL FK user_profiles, program_id FK, revision_id FK protocol_revisions NOT NULL, slot_id uuid NULL FK benchmark_slots, realm realm_enum NOT NULL no default (D34 root), kind, lifecycle, target_seconds int NOT NULL, started_at timestamptz NOT NULL, ended_at NULL, paused_seconds int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, current_pause_started_at NULL, local_date date NOT NULL, intended_output text NULL, time_source NOT NULL, timer_quality NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ok', clock_gap_seconds int NULL (D26: accumulates with an explicit NULL branch, never `?? 0`), complete_interval bool NULL, eligible bool NULL, exclusion_reasons text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', replacement_reason text NULL, version int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1; index (program_id, kind, local_date). session_events: id uuid PK, session_id FK, client_event_id uuid NOT NULL, type NOT NULL, occurred_at timestamptz NOT NULL, elapsed_ms int NULL, received_at NOT NULL DEFAULT now(), details jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', voided_at NULL; UNIQUE `session_events_client_event_unique` (session_id, client_event_id); index (session_id, elapsed_ms); no realm column (inherits through focus_sessions, D34). session_reviews: session_id PK FK; episode_count, external_count, unplanned_agent_checks, mind_wandering_count, recall_score all `integer` NULL with NO default (D7.1); count_method NULL, first_switch_kind NULL, first_switch_seconds NULL, first_switch_method NULL, output_quality NULL, output_note text NULL (the one-line 'what did you finish'), review_note text NULL (D31: optional free-text note), materially_disrupted bool NULL, disruption_note NULL, recall_points jsonb NULL, recall_started_at NULL, recall_locked_at NULL, recall_delay_seconds NULL, recall_duration_seconds NULL, recall_flags text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', recall_scores jsonb NULL, observed_conditions jsonb NOT NULL (D31: the row is created at session start with the slot's conditions for benchmarks and the empty ObservedConditions for practice), finalized_at NULL, version int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1. session_amendments: id uuid PK, session_id FK, user_id text NOT NULL, reason text NOT NULL, exclude_from_report bool NOT NULL, created_at NOT NULL DEFAULT now(). agent_plans: session_id PK FK, workstream NULL, waiting_task NULL, resume_note NULL, review_checkpoint text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'end_of_block', review_at NULL, version int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1. Generate migration 0001_sessions, review, commit.

ON CONFLICT (session_id, client_event_id) DO NOTHING` reports 0 rows on the second insert (the mechanism 5.3.1 relies on); (15) review inserted with every count omitted reads back null for all five and review_note null (unknown != zero); (16) review inserted with episode_count = 0 reads back 0 (an explicit zero survives distinct from null); (17) exclusion_reasons defaults to an empty array and clock_gap_seconds omitted reads back null.

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Review src/db/migrations/0000_*.sql, 0001_*.sql, 0002_*.sql and meta/_journal.json: statements are additive only (no DROP/ALTER ...
