### 3.4.1 — Implement canonical request hash and Idempotency-Key preHandler

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/idempotency/requestHash.ts: `canonicalJson(value)` (object keys sorted recursively, arrays kept in order, `undefined` properties dropped, `null` preserved as the literal null) and `requestHash(operation: string, params: object, body: unknown): string` = sha256 hex of `operation + '\n' + canonicalJson({ params, body })`. apps/api/src/idempotency/keyHook.ts: `requireIdempotencyKey` preHandler for the routes marked (IK) in design.md (POST /programs 4.1.2, POST /sessions 5.1.1/5.1.2, POST /sessions/{id}/recall 5.7.2, POST /sessions/{id}/finalize 5.8.1): header `Idempotency-Key` must be present and a UUID, else `MalformedError` with `fieldErrors: { 'Idempotency-Key': 'must be a UUID' }`; decorates `request.idempotencyKey`. Export `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_HEADER = 'idempotency-key'`.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/idempotency/hash.test.ts: (1) `{a:1,b:2}` and `{b:2,a:1}` hash equal; (2) nested key order ignored; (3) array order changes the hash; (4) `{ episodeCount: null }`, `{ episodeCount: 0 }` and `{}` produce three different hashes (unknown != zero != absent survives hashing); (5) same body, different operation → different; (6) same body, different params → different; (7) output is 64 lowercase hex chars; (8) a fixed vector matches a stored expected hash across runs.
- **Integration tests:** same file, a test-only POST route with the hook: (9) missing header → 400 malformed_request with fieldErrors['Idempotency-Key']; (10) header 'abc' → 400; (11) a valid UUID sent as `IDEMPOTENCY-KEY` (upper-case header name) → 200 and the handler sees `request.idempotencyKey` equal to the header value.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/idempotency/hash.test.ts  → 11 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.4
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent

### 3.4.2 — Implement mutation_receipts helper with 201/200 replay, mismatch 409, no receipt on failure and 7-day expiry

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/idempotency/withIdempotency.ts: `export const RECEIPT_TTL_SECONDS = 7 * 24 * 3600`; `withIdempotency<T>(db, ctx, { key, operation, requestHash }, execute: (tx) => Promise<{ resultRef: string; value: T }>, load: (tx, resultRef) => Promise<T>): Promise<{ replayed: boolean; value: T }>`. In one transaction: first `SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext($principalId || ':' || $key))` so concurrent duplicates serialize instead of racing the primary key; then `SELECT ... FROM mutation_receipts WHERE user_id = ctx.principalId AND idempotency_key = key`; if a row exists and `isReceiptLive(row, ctx.now)`: same request_hash → `{ replayed: true, value: await load(tx, row.result_ref) }`; different hash → throw ConflictError('idempotency_mismatch', 'This request was already sent with different content.'); if absent or expired → `execute(tx)`, then upsert the receipt `{ user_id, idempotency_key, operation, request_hash, result_ref, created_at: ctx.now, expires_at: ctx.now + RECEIPT_TTL_SECONDS }` in the same transaction (an expired row is overwritten) → `{ replayed: false, value }`. Any error thrown by `execute` (validation, domain 4xx such as event_count_mismatch, database) aborts the transaction, so a rejected request records no receipt and the same key is accepted on a later attempt (D21). Status codes (D21): creators (POST /programs, POST /sessions) reply 201 when `replayed` is false and 200 when true; recall and finalize reply 200 on both paths (5.7.2, 5.8.1). The helper never coalesces or reinterprets body values; the hash comes from 3.4.1. Pure `isReceiptLive(receipt, now)` (`expires_at > now`) exported. Receipts expire on `ctx.now`, i.e. the demo clock (D40, recorded in LIMITATIONS.md).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/idempotency/receipts.test.ts (unit part): (1) isReceiptLive with expires_at == now → false; (2) now - 1 s → true; (3) now + 1 s → false; (4) hash comparison is exact-string (differing case → mismatch).
- **Integration tests:** same file with a test-only route `POST /api/v1/__test/idem` (IK hook) that inserts a draft program row through withIdempotency (operation 'test.create'; replies 201 fresh / 200 replay; body `{ fail?: boolean }` makes execute throw ConflictError('event_count_mismatch', msg, { details: { expected: 5, stored: 4 } }) after inserting): (a) two identical requests with the same key → 201 then 200 with the same id and exactly one program row; (b) same key, different body → 409 idempotency_mismatch, still one row, message matches no UUID; (c) `UPDATE user_profiles SET demo_clock_offset_seconds = RECEIPT_TTL_SECONDS + 1` so ctx.now passes expiry, then same key + different body → 201 fresh execution, receipt rewritten with the new hash and a later expires_at; (d) a receipt inserted directly for user_id 'other' with the same key does not affect the demo principal's request; (e) execute throws a generic Error → 500, no receipt row and no program row (rollback); (f) 10 concurrent identical injects → one program row, one 201 and nine 200 with the same id; (g) result_ref stored equals the created program id; (h) same key, same body, operation 'test.other' → 409 (the operation is part of the hash); (i) `{ fail: true }` → 409 event_count_mismatch with details `{ expected: 5, stored: 4 }`, zero receipt rows and zero program rows for the principal; (j) the same key with `{ fail: false }` → 201 (not idempotency_mismatch), one program row, receipt now present.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/idempotency/receipts.test.ts  → 14 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.3, 3.3.3, 3.4.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Finalize retried · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch

### 3.5.1 — Implement GET /me and PATCH /me/preferences

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/preferences.ts (extends 3.1.1): `isIanaTimeZone(tz)` = membership in `Intl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone')` or exactly 'UTC'; `mergePreferences(current, patch)` keeping keys absent from the patch and ignoring undefined values. apps/api/src/routes/me.ts registered in buildApp: `GET /api/v1/me` → `{ principalId, identityMode: app.config.identityMode, realm, timezone, preferences, demoClockOffsetSeconds }` read from user_profiles for ctx.principalId (the 7.1.2 banner reads identityMode/realm from here); `preferences` always carries the four keys hideTimerDefault, endChime, visibilityContext, milestoneAnnouncements (D22, D39). `PATCH /api/v1/me/preferences` body `{ timezone?, hideTimerDefault?, endChime?, visibilityContext?, milestoneAnnouncements? }` (2.7.2 `PatchPreferencesBody`, additionalProperties false): invalid timezone → MalformedError with `fieldErrors.timezone: 'must be an IANA time zone name'`; partial merge into preferences jsonb and the timezone column; returns the GET shape. Both private (no-store).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/routes/me.test.ts (unit part): (1) isIanaTimeZone('America/Costa_Rica') true; (2) 'Costa Rica' false; (3) 'utc' false (case-sensitive) and 'UTC' true; (4) mergePreferences keeps keys absent from the patch and overrides present ones; (5) mergePreferences ignores undefined values; (6) DEFAULT_PREFERENCES.visibilityContext and .milestoneAnnouncements are false.
- **Integration tests:** same file: (7) GET /api/v1/me → 200 with principalId 'local-demo', identityMode 'local-demo', realm 'demo', timezone 'UTC' (the 3.2.3 placeholder until Setup confirms one), demoClockOffsetSeconds 0, preferences deep-equal DEFAULT_PREFERENCES (four keys), headers cache-control no-store and x-request-id; (8) PATCH { timezone: 'Europe/Madrid' } → 200 and a following GET shows it; (9) PATCH { timezone: 'Mars/Olympus' } → 400 with fieldErrors.timezone; (10) PATCH { endChime: true } → timezone unchanged and hideTimerDefault still false; (11) PATCH { realm: 'pilot' } → 400 fieldErrors.realm and the profile unchanged; (12) PATCH { principalId: 'x' } → 400; (13) PATCH {} → 200 unchanged; (14) PATCH { milestoneAnnouncements: true } → 200 with milestoneAnnouncements true and the other three keys unchanged.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/routes/me.test.ts  → 14 tests pass
- **After:** 2.7.2, 3.2.3, 3.2.4
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Local-demo mode uses a single fixed principal and binds only to loopback / Loopback binding accepted · identity-realm: Demo mode is permanently and unmistakably labeled · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Client cannot choose the realm · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

### 3.2.5 — Serve the built web app from the API under WEB_DIST_DIR

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/plugins/staticWeb.ts (fastify-plugin, registered by buildApp after every `/api/v1` plugin and outside that prefix): `export function isApiPath(url: string): boolean` — true when the path (query stripped) is `/api` or starts with `/api/`; `resolveWebDistDir(dir)` resolves the directory and throws ConfigError naming WEB_DIST_DIR when `<dir>/index.html` does not exist. When `config.webDistDir` is set: register `@fastify/static` with `root: dir, prefix: '/', wildcard: false`, then `setNotFoundHandler` scoped to this plugin: for `GET`/`HEAD` requests with `!isApiPath(url)` reply `sendFile('index.html')` (SPA fallback for deep links, D37); every other case (any `/api` path, non-GET methods) falls through to the 3.2.4 JSON 404 envelope. When `config.webDistDir` is unset nothing is registered and non-`/api` GETs get the JSON 404 envelope. Static responses carry `x-request-id` (3.2.2 hook is global) and no `Cache-Control: no-store` (the noStore plugin is limited to `/api/v1/*`). Add `@fastify/static` to apps/api dependencies at the D3-verified version. The Playwright acceptance project (group 9) starts the API with `WEB_DIST_DIR=apps/web/dist` after `npm run build` and relies on this fallback for direct navigation to `/progress`, `/benchmark/:slotId` and `/focus/:sessionId`.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/plugins/staticWeb.test.ts (unit part): (1) isApiPath('/api/v1/me') and isApiPath('/api/v1/me?x=1') true; (2) isApiPath('/api') and isApiPath('/api/') true; (3) isApiPath('/apix/y'), isApiPath('/progress'), isApiPath('/') false; (4) resolveWebDistDir on a temp directory without index.html throws ConfigError whose message names WEB_DIST_DIR.
- **Integration tests:** same file; a temp dist directory holding `index.html` (contains the marker `<!-- ATTENTION-LAB-INDEX -->`) and `assets/app.js`: (5) buildTestApp({ webDistDir }) GET `/` → 200, content-type text/html, body contains the marker; (6) GET `/assets/app.js` → 200 with a javascript content-type and the file body; (7) deep link GET `/progress` → 200 index.html with the marker and an `x-request-id` header; (8) GET `/benchmark/abc/recall` → index.html; (9) GET `/api/v1/me` → 200 application/json with principalId 'local-demo' (static never shadows the API); (10) GET `/api/v1/nope` → 404 JSON envelope `code: 'not_found'` with `cache-control: no-store`, body does not contain the marker; (11) POST `/progress` → 404 JSON envelope (fallback is GET/HEAD only); (12) buildTestApp() without webDistDir: GET `/progress` → 404 JSON envelope, not html.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/plugins/staticWeb.test.ts  → 12 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.2, 3.2.4, 3.5.1
- **Spec refs:** app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only

### 3.5.2 — Implement demo-only POST /demo/clock registered only in local-demo

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/demo.ts: `demoRoutes` Fastify plugin that throws ConfigError at registration if `app.config.identityMode !== 'local-demo'`; buildApp registers it only inside `if (config.identityMode === 'local-demo')` so the routes do not exist otherwise (buildApp already refuses non-demo modes entirely; the guard is defense in depth). `POST /api/v1/demo/clock` body `{ offsetSeconds: integer }` (2.7.2 `DemoClockBody`, additionalProperties false) → `UPDATE user_profiles SET demo_clock_offset_seconds = $1 WHERE id = ctx.principalId` (an absolute offset, D35) → 200 `{ demoClockOffsetSeconds }`. The identity plugin (3.2.3) reads the offset per request, so the next request's ctx.now and timeSource reflect it (D8); nothing client-side is trusted for time. The Today derivation (4.5.3) and the `time_source` stamp on started sessions (5.1.1/5.1.2) consume this offset through ctx.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/routes/demoClock.test.ts (unit part): (1) registering demoRoutes on an app whose config.identityMode is 'real' throws ConfigError; (2) a local-demo app has `app.hasRoute({ method: 'POST', url: '/api/v1/demo/clock' })` true.
- **Integration tests:** same file (registering the `GET /api/v1/__test/ctx` route from test/helpers/testRoutes.ts): (3) POST { offsetSeconds: 1209600 } → 200 { demoClockOffsetSeconds: 1209600 }; (4) GET /me shows demoClockOffsetSeconds 1209600; (5) the ctx route shows now - realNow within 2 s of 1209600 and timeSource 'demo_clock'; (6) POST { offsetSeconds: 0 } → timeSource back to 'measured'; (7) POST { offsetSeconds: 1.5 } → 400 fieldErrors.offsetSeconds; (8) POST { offsetSeconds: 10, realm: 'pilot' } → 400 fieldErrors.realm and offset unchanged; (9) empty body → 400; (10) response carries cache-control no-store.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/routes/demoClock.test.ts  → 10 tests pass
- **After:** 2.7.2, 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.5.1
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode / Skip to Day 14 in demo mode · identity-realm: Real mode refuses to start until real authentication is configured / Real mode is never satisfied by demo defaults

### 3.5.3 — Implement POST /demo/reset with principal-scoped deletePrincipalData

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/db/seed/deletePrincipalData.ts: `deletePrincipalData(tx, ctx) → Record<string, number>` deleting only rows owned by `ctx.principalId` with `realm = ctx.realm` on the three realm roots (child tables without a realm column are selected through their parent's user_id/realm, D34), in FK order: feed_usage (via check-ins of the principal's programs), daily_checkins, session_events, session_reviews, session_amendments, agent_plans, focus_sessions (user_id), benchmark_slots, `UPDATE programs SET current_revision_id = NULL`, protocol_revisions, programs, mutation_receipts (user_id); then `UPDATE user_profiles SET demo_clock_offset_seconds = 0` — the profile row is kept with its timezone and preferences and only the offset is zeroed (D35). Export `USER_OWNED_TABLES` in delete order. `POST /api/v1/demo/reset` in routes/demo.ts runs it in one transaction and returns 204; the log line carries per-table counts only (never row content). 3.5.4 calls the same function before loading a scenario, and group 9's Playwright project calls the route between tests (D37).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/routes/demoReset.test.ts (unit part): (1) every table exported from the schema barrel except user_profiles appears exactly once in USER_OWNED_TABLES (guards future tables being forgotten); (2) the order places each child table before every table it references (derived from drizzle foreign-key metadata).
- **Integration tests:** same file: seed one row in every user-owned table by direct insert for 'local-demo' plus a mutation_receipts row and demo offset 999, and set the profile timezone to 'Europe/Madrid' with endChime true; also insert a profile, program, session and receipt for a second user_id 'other-user'; (3) POST /api/v1/demo/reset → 204; (4) every user-owned table has 0 rows for 'local-demo' (checked per table via the parent join); (5) mutation_receipts has 0 rows for 'local-demo'; (6) the user_profiles row still exists with offset 0, timezone 'Europe/Madrid' and endChime true; (7) 'other-user' rows are untouched (counts unchanged in every table); (8) a second reset on an empty state → 204; (9) GET /me still 200.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/routes/demoReset.test.ts  → 9 tests pass
- **After:** 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.3.4, 3.5.2
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode / Reset demo data · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner

### 3.5.4 — Implement POST /demo/scenarios/{name}/load from the shared fixtures

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/db/seed/loadScenario.ts: `loadScenario(tx, ctx, scenario)` calls deletePrincipalData (3.5.3; this zeroes the demo clock offset) then inserts the scenario row set from packages/shared/src/fixtures/demoScenarios.ts (2.8.1/2.8.2) with `user_id = ctx.principalId` and `realm = 'demo'` on programs, focus_sessions and daily_checkins (D34), fixture-local ids remapped to fresh UUIDs on every load, and every session row `time_source = 'demo_clock'`. Date resolution (D35): `loadInstant = realNow` (the offset is already 0), `baseline_date = today(program timezone at loadInstant) − scenario.viewDay`; every fixture local date is a program-day number added to baseline_date; each session's `started_at = loadInstant − startedAtOffsetSeconds` and its `local_date` the program day of that instant; every other timestamp on a session's children (event `occurred_at` from `elapsedMs`, recall/finalize instants as seconds after `started_at`) is resolved relative to that session's `started_at`. The row mapper throws if a fixture row carries any realm other than 'demo' (a fixture bug is never silently rewritten) and passes ReportedCount fields through unchanged (null stays null, 0 stays 0). The `timing-deviation` fixture inserts baseline A as a RUNNING session (started 1080 s before load) with one unresolved `clock_gap` event (`details: { gapSeconds: 300 }`, no `resolution`) so the journey resolves it (D26, D35); `recovery` inserts a running practice session with an agent_plans row and no events. `POST /api/v1/demo/scenarios/:name/load` in routes/demo.ts: `name` validated by 2.7.2 `ScenarioLoadParams` against `DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES` (the eight PRD §6 scenarios) else 404 not_found; the route declares no body schema and ignores any body (D22: the UI confirms replacement); one transaction → 200 `{ programId: string | null }` (null for new-user). Returns the fixture's `expected.resultState` to nobody — 6.2.2 reads it from the fixture directly.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/routes/demoScenarios.test.ts (unit part): (1) DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES has exactly the eight PRD §6 names and every name has a fixture; (2) the row mapper throws on a fixture row with realm 'pilot'; (3) the row mapper maps a review with episode_count null to null and episode_count 0 to 0 (never coalesced).
- **Integration tests:** same file: (4) unknown name → 404 envelope; (5) it.each over the eight names: load → 200 with `programId` a UUID (null only for new-user), and `SELECT count(*) WHERE realm <> 'demo' OR user_id <> 'local-demo'` is 0 across programs, focus_sessions and daily_checkins, and every focus_sessions row has time_source 'demo_clock' (8 cases); (6) comparable-change: 4 finalized eligible benchmark sessions; (7) missing-final: 2 baseline attempts and fewer than 2 eligible finals; (8) zero-baseline: baseline reviews store episode_count 0 (not null); (9) timing-deviation: exactly one focus_sessions row, kind benchmark, lifecycle 'running', started_at within 5 s of load − 1080 s, one clock_gap event whose details.gapSeconds is 300 and has no `resolution` key, timer_quality 'ok', eligible null; (10) recovery: one running practice session with an agent_plans row and zero session_events rows; (11) loading mixed-result after comparable-change with no body → 200, exactly one program row and the previous programId gone; (12) new-user → 200 `{ programId: null }` and zero program rows for the principal; (13) a mutation_receipts row for the principal does not survive a load; (14) with demo offset 1209600 set before the load, after the load GET /me shows offset 0 and the program's baseline_date equals today's real date in the program timezone minus the fixture's viewDay.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/routes/demoScenarios.test.ts  → 21 tests pass (13 named + 8 from it.each)
- **After:** 2.8.2, 3.3.4, 3.5.3
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode / Load a demonstration scenario · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Records created in demo mode

### 3.6.1 — Add log-privacy assertions for validation, domain and database error paths

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/test/privacy/logCapture.test.ts using the captureLogs helper from 3.2.1 (already wired into buildTestApp). Confirms the error plugin (3.2.4) logs errors as `{ reqId, code, statusCode, route, err: sanitizeErrorForLog(err) }` and never `request.body`, `query` or `headers`; confirms the only per-request entry is the 3.2.2 completion entry. Registers a test-only `POST /api/v1/__test/privacy` route with schema `{ note: string minLength 1, points: string[5], count: integer | null, mode: 'domain' | 'db' }` whose handler throws `DomainError('impossible_offset', 'Offset is outside the interval.')` for mode 'domain' (a D19 code; the route exists only to exercise the log path) and, for mode 'db', inserts a daily_checkins row with stress 11 and `note` = the request note so Postgres raises 23514 whose `detail` echoes the failing row.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: this unit is an end-to-end privacy assertion; the pure pieces are unit-tested in 3.2.1 (captureLogs parsing), 3.2.2 (serializers) and 3.2.4 (sanitizeErrorForLog, fieldErrorsFromValidation)
- **Integration tests:** logCapture.test.ts: (a) validation failure with note 'SECRET-NOTE-9f3a' and points containing 'SECRET-POINT-11aa' (points has 6 items) → 400; neither marker appears in logs.text() or the response; fieldErrors names 'points'; (b) valid body with the markers and mode 'domain' → 422; the error log entry has reqId equal to x-request-id, code 'impossible_offset', statusCode 422, route, and no marker; (c) `count: null` passes validation and reaches the handler as null, not 0; (d) mode 'db' with the note marker → 500 internal_error; the response and logs.text() contain no marker even though the Postgres error detail does (sanitizeErrorForLog), and the log entry carries sqlstate '23514'; (e) no log entry across the run has a `body`, `query`, `headers`, `cookie` or `authorization` key; (f) each request produced exactly one completion entry with method, url, statusCode and responseTime (observability NFR) and no other per-request entries (no per-tick telemetry).
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/privacy/logCapture.test.ts  → 6 tests pass with zero marker occurrences in the captured log text
- **After:** 3.2.2, 3.2.4, 3.3.4
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

## 4. Programs API

### 4.1.1 — Program service base, DTO mappers and DB seed helpers

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/programService.ts` (service base; routes arrive in 4.1.2+) exporting: (a) `loadOwnedProgram(db, ctx, programId, { forUpdate })` selecting `programs WHERE id = $1 AND user_id = ctx.principalId` (`FOR UPDATE` when asked); no row → `NotFoundError` (3.2.4; 404, never 403); a row whose `realm !== ctx.realm` → `assertOwnRealm(ctx, row.realm)` (3.2.3) throws `DomainError('realm_mismatch')` (422) — a foreign-realm row is rejected, never silently filtered. Used by every `/programs/{id}` route in this group. (b) pure `governingRevisionFor(revisions, day)` = the revision with the greatest `effective_day <= max(day, 0)`, ties resolved by highest `revision` (D33; days before Day 0 are governed by revision 1); exported for 5.1 to stamp `focus_sessions.revision_id` at start. (c) `INITIAL_REVISION = { revision: 1, effectiveDay: 0, reason: 'initial plan' }` (D33) and `initialRevisionSettings(practiceTargetSeconds, leisureAllowanceMin)` = `{ practiceTargetSeconds, bandCeilings: BAND_CEILINGS (packages/shared/src/domain/progression.ts, 2.5.1), leisureAllowanceMin }`. (d) DTO mappers to the 2.7.3 schemas: `toProgramDto` (`feedEstimateMinutes`: NULL → `null`, never omitted and never 0; no `userId`/`user_id` key), `toRevisionDto`, `toSlotDto(row, attempts)` (`plannedLocalTime` NULL → null; `frozenAt` ISO string or null; `attempts: [{ sessionId, lifecycle, eligible (stored value, null until finalized), excludedByAmendment }]` per D22). (e) `mapPgError(err)`: Postgres `23505` whose constraint is `programs_one_open_per_user` (3.3.1) → `ConflictError('program_exists', ...)` (D19); every other error rethrown unchanged. Create `apps/api/test/helpers/programs.ts` (DB-level seeds on top of 3.2.1's `buildTestApp`/`truncateAll`; the principal profile is seeded by 3.2.3 after `ready()`/`truncateAll()` — no app builder, truncation or profile seeding is re-created here): `insertProgram(db, { baselineDate, timezone, status, practiceTargetSeconds, leisureAllowanceMin?, feedEstimateMin?, userId?, realm? })` inserting `programs` plus revision 1 per `INITIAL_REVISION` and setting `current_revision_id`; `insertSlotSet(db, programId, overrides?)` inserting the four required `benchmark_slots` (+ optional midpoint) with `assigned_local_date` = `localDateForProgramDay(baselineDate, 0|7|14)` (2.1.1); `insertSession(db, { programId, kind, slotId?, lifecycle, localDate, startedAt, endedAt?, targetSeconds, pausedSeconds?, completeInterval?, realm?, review?: { episodeCount?, outputQuality?, countMethod? } })` filling every NOT NULL column (`user_id`, `realm`, `revision_id` = the program's current revision, `time_source` 'measured', `timer_quality` 'ok', `exclusion_reasons` '{}', `version` 1) and always inserting the D31 `session_reviews` row (counts NULL unless given — a blank `episodeCount` is NULL, never 0; `observed_conditions` from the slot for benchmarks, the empty `ObservedConditions` for practice; `finalized_at = endedAt` when lifecycle is finalized); `seedActiveProgram(db, { day, tz })` = insertProgram(baselineDate = today − day local days in tz, status 'active') + insertSlotSet + one finalized benchmark attempt per baseline slot with `local_date` = Day 0; `baselineDateDaysAgo(n, tz)`; `setDemoOffsetSeconds(db, seconds)` and `setDemoNow(db, isoInstant)` writing `user_profiles.demo_clock_offset_seconds` (D8); `insertOtherPrincipalProgram(db)` (user_id 'someone-else' with its own `user_profiles` row); `insertCheckin(db, { programId, localDate, sleepMinutes?, feed?: [{ device, platform, minutes, measurementScope, source? }] })` filling `realm` and `version`, sleep omitted → NULL. 5.1.3 extends these helpers with event, amendment and plan seeds rather than re-creating them.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/programService.test.ts`: (1) governingRevisionFor returns revision 1 for days −1, 0, 7 and revision 2 (effective_day 8) for days 8..14; (2) two revisions with the same effective_day → highest revision number wins; (3) initialRevisionSettings(300, 20) carries BAND_CEILINGS with the Days 1–3 entry at 10 minutes (600 s) and INITIAL_REVISION reads { revision 1, effectiveDay 0, reason 'initial plan' }; (4) toProgramDto maps NULL feed_estimate_min to `null`, not 0 and not undefined; (5) toProgramDto output has no userId or user_id key; (6) toSlotDto maps planned_local_time NULL to null, a set frozen_at to an ISO string, and an unfinalized attempt to `eligible: null` with `excludedByAmendment: false`; (7) mapPgError turns 23505 on programs_one_open_per_user into ConflictError code `program_exists`; (8) 23505 on any other constraint is rethrown unchanged; (9) a non-23505 error is rethrown unchanged.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/helpers/programs.test.ts` (helper smoke against `attention_lab_test` via 3.2.1 buildTestApp): (1) insertProgram → exactly one `programs` row with realm 'demo' and one `protocol_revisions` row { revision 1, effective_day 0, reason 'initial plan' }, `current_revision_id` equals that revision's id; (2) insertSlotSet → four rows, baseline `assigned_local_date` = baselineDate, finals = baselineDate + 14 local days; (3) insertSession kind 'benchmark' with slotId → `focus_sessions` and `session_reviews` rows exist, `episode_count` SQL NULL when review omitted and 0 when given 0; insertSession kind 'practice' with a slotId is rejected by the 3.3.2 CHECK `focus_sessions_benchmark_has_slot`; (4) seedActiveProgram({ day: 4 }) → status 'active', two finalized benchmark rows on the baseline slots with `local_date` = Day 0, zero practice rows; (5) loadOwnedProgram on insertOtherPrincipalProgram's row → NotFoundError 404 with message 'Not found'; (6) a program row updated directly to realm 'pilot' → loadOwnedProgram throws DomainError code `realm_mismatch` (422), not 404 and not an empty result.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- src/services/program/programService.test.ts test/helpers/programs.test.ts` → vitest reports 15 named cases passed (9 unit + 6 integration), 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.5, 2.1.1, 2.5.1, 2.7.3, 3.2.1, 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.3.1, 3.3.2, 3.3.3
- **Spec refs:** program-setup: Protocol settings are immutable revisions · program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required / Save with optional fields blank · program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required / Initial duration below ten minutes · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result

### 4.1.2 — POST /programs create route with idempotency and realm stamping

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `createProgram(ctx, body, idempotencyKey)` to `programService.ts` and register `POST /api/v1/programs` in `apps/api/src/routes/programs.ts` with the `CreateProgramBody` / `ProgramResponse` contracts (2.7.3) via the TypeBox type provider (unknown properties such as `realm`, `userId`, `status` → 400; missing `timezone` → 400). Order: the 3.4.1 `requireIdempotencyKey` preHandler (missing or non-UUID `Idempotency-Key` → 400 `fieldErrors['Idempotency-Key']`); `timezone` validated with `isIanaTimeZone` from `apps/api/src/preferences.ts` (3.5.1 — the one IANA helper in the API; no second helper is written) → 400 `fieldErrors.timezone`; then 3.4.2 `withIdempotency(db, ctx, { key, operation: 'program.create', requestHash: requestHash('program.create', {}, body) }, execute, load)` — a replay returns the stored program (`result_ref = programId`) re-read with its revision before any other check, so a replay never becomes a 409. `execute` runs ONE transaction: pure `buildProgramInsert(ctx, body, id)` → `{ id, user_id: ctx.principalId, realm: ctx.realm (never read from the body), baseline_date, timezone, status: 'draft', leisure_allowance_min: body.leisureAllowanceMinutes ?? 20 (a goal default, not a count), feed_estimate_min: body.feedEstimateMinutes ?? null (NULL = not reported; never 0), version: 1 }`; insert `protocol_revisions` from `INITIAL_REVISION` (revision 1, effective_day 0, reason 'initial plan' — D33) with `settings = initialRevisionSettings(body.practiceTargetSeconds, leisure)`; set `programs.current_revision_id`; Postgres errors pass through `mapPgError` (4.1.1). Respond 201 `{ program, revision }` on the first write and 200 with the identical body on replay (D21), `Cache-Control: no-store` (3.2.2). `baselineDate` is unbounded (past dates are how tests and demo scenarios reach later program days). Add `createProgramViaApi(app, overrides?, idempotencyKey?)` to `apps/api/test/helpers/programs.ts`.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/createProgram.test.ts` (pure `buildProgramInsert` and the revision-1 builder): (1) leisureAllowanceMinutes omitted → 20, feedEstimateMinutes omitted → null (not 0, not undefined), status 'draft', version 1; (2) `user_id` and `realm` come from ctx even when the body object carries `realm: 'pilot'` and `userId`; (3) feedEstimateMinutes 0 → 0 (an explicit zero survives distinct from null); (4) the revision row built for the insert reads `{ revision: 1, effective_day: 0, reason: 'initial plan' }` with `settings.practiceTargetSeconds` equal to the body value and `settings.bandCeilings` equal to BAND_CEILINGS.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/create.test.ts` (Fastify inject, `attention_lab_test`): (1) valid body → 201, program.status 'draft', version 1, revision.revision 1, effectiveDay 0, reason 'initial plan', exactly one `programs` row and one `protocol_revisions` row, `current_revision_id` equals the revision id; (2) feedEstimateMinutes omitted → DB `feed_estimate_min IS NULL` and response `feedEstimateMinutes: null`; leisureAllowanceMinutes omitted → 20; (3) body containing `realm: 'pilot'` → 400 `fieldErrors.realm` and zero rows; (4) body without realm → stored realm 'demo'; (5) practiceTargetSeconds 300 → stored `settings.bandCeilings` Days 1–3 entry is 10 minutes (600 s; the ceiling stays below ten minutes only in the sense that the target 300 < 600); (6) timezone 'Mars/Olympus' → 400 `fieldErrors.timezone`; 'America/Bogota' stored verbatim on the program; (7) body without timezone → 400 (timezone is confirmed, never assumed from the profile); (8) missing `Idempotency-Key` → 400 `fieldErrors['Idempotency-Key']`; non-UUID key → 400; (9) same key + same body twice → 201 then 200, same programId, byte-identical body, still one row (replay precedes the one-open-program rule); (10) same key + different body → 409 `idempotency_mismatch`; (11) response carries `x-request-id` and `Cache-Control: no-store`; (12) after create, `focus_sessions` count for the program is 0 (saving never starts a timer).
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/create.test.ts src/services/program/createProgram.test.ts` → vitest reports 16 named cases passed (4 unit + 12 integration), 0 failed.
- **After:** 4.1.1, 2.7.3, 3.2.2, 3.2.4, 3.4.1, 3.4.2, 3.5.1
- **Spec refs:** program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required · program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required / Save with optional fields blank · program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required / Timezone is confirmed, not assumed · program-setup: Basic plan captures only what is required / Initial duration below ten minutes · program-setup: Protocol settings are immutable revisions · program-setup: Saving never starts a timer · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Records created in demo mode · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Client cannot choose the realm · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout

### 4.1.3 — Enforce one open program per user on POST /programs

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Inside the `createProgram` transaction (4.1.2), after the idempotency lookup and before inserting: `SELECT id, status FROM programs WHERE user_id = ctx.principalId AND status IN ('draft','baseline_ready','active') FOR UPDATE`; if a row exists throw `ConflictError('program_exists', 'A program is already open.', { details: { existingProgramId, existingStatus } })` (409; D18/D19). When the SELECT finds nothing but the insert still raises `23505` on `programs_one_open_per_user` (3.3.1; concurrent race), `mapPgError` (4.1.1) yields the same 409 and the service re-queries the open program to fill `details`, so a race never surfaces as 500. Export `isOpenStatus(status)` (true for draft | baseline_ready | active) from `programService.ts`. Completed and archived programs never block creation; other principals' open programs never block (the index is per user_id). A rejected request records no idempotency receipt (D21).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/programService.test.ts` (extend): (1) isOpenStatus true for 'draft', 'baseline_ready', 'active'; (2) false for 'completed', 'archived'.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/create-conflict.test.ts`: (1) a draft program exists → second POST with a new key → 409 code `program_exists`, `details.existingProgramId` equal to the draft's id, `details.existingStatus: 'draft'`, no new row, and the envelope message contains no UUID; (2) status updated directly to 'baseline_ready' → 409; (3) 'active' → 409; (4) 'completed' → 201 and a new program row; (5) 'archived' → 201; (6) two concurrent inject POSTs with different keys via Promise.all → exactly one 201 and one 409 `program_exists`, exactly one open row in the DB (the partial index is the backstop); (7) an open program seeded for user_id 'someone-else' (insertOtherPrincipalProgram) does not block → 201 and the seeded row is untouched.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/create-conflict.test.ts src/services/program/programService.test.ts` → vitest reports 18 named cases passed (9 from 4.1.1 + 2 + 7), 0 failed.
- **After:** 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 3.3.1
- **Spec refs:** program-setup: One program per user at a time · program-setup: One program per user at a time / Second program while one is active

### 4.2.1 — Derive today's two practice block statuses (pure)

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/blocks.ts` exporting `deriveBlocks(input: { sessions: BlockSessionInput[]; targetSeconds: number }): [Block, Block]` where `BlockSessionInput = { id, kind: SessionKind, lifecycle: SessionLifecycle, startedAt: Date, completeInterval: boolean | null, outputQuality: OutputQuality | null }` (one program local date's `focus_sessions` joined to `session_reviews`) and `Block` is the 2.7.3 `TodayResponse` block `{ index: 1 | 2, status: BlockStatus, targetSeconds: number, sessionId: string | null }` (`sessionId` is null, never omitted, when no session; the 2.7.3 block schema types it `Type.Union([UuidSchema, Type.Null()])`). Rules: only `kind = 'practice'` rows with lifecycle ≠ 'abandoned' are candidates (benchmarks never become blocks); order by startedAt; first candidate → block 1, second → block 2, further rows ignored (a third practice session of a day is not a block); status: no candidate → `not_started`; running | paused | awaiting_review → `in_progress` (reaching the target moves a session to awaiting_review, which is never `completed` until the review is finalized — timer expiry never proves completion); finalized with `completeInterval === true` → `completed`; finalized with `false` OR `null` → `partial` (a null completion flag is never promoted to completed); `outputQuality` never changes the status (partial output ≠ partial block). `targetSeconds` on both blocks is the governing revision's target passed in; no session field overrides it. No DB import.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/blocks.test.ts`: (1) no sessions → both not_started, sessionId null, targetSeconds from input, index 1 then 2; (2) one finalized complete → [completed, not_started]; (3) one running → [in_progress, not_started] carrying its sessionId; (4) finalized with completeInterval false (early finish) → partial; (5) finalized with completeInterval null → partial, never completed; (6) outputQuality 'partly' with completeInterval true → status completed (partial output ≠ partial block; status never reads outputQuality); (7) abandoned session excluded, block stays not_started; (8) a benchmark session on the same date is never a block; (9) three practice sessions → only the first two by startedAt are used; (10) lifecycle awaiting_review after the target elapsed → in_progress, never completed (timer expiry never proves completion); (11) paused → in_progress; (12) each Block has exactly the keys index, status, targetSeconds, sessionId and the tuple order is [1, 2].
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.2.3 and 4.5.3
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- src/services/program/blocks.test.ts` → vitest reports 12 named cases passed, 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.5, 2.7.1, 2.7.3

### 4.2.2 — Derive nextAction from program state (pure)

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/nextAction.ts` exporting `deriveNextAction(input: { program: { status: ProgramStatus } | null; day: number; slots: SlotState[]; blocks: [Block, Block] }): NextAction` with `SlotState = { id, phase, label, hasFinalizedAttempt }` and `NextAction` the 2.7.3 union — `{ kind: 'benchmark' | 'final', slotId }` carries only the slot id; the client reads phase, label, assigned date and planned time from `slots[]`. A slot is pending when it has no finalized attempt (abandoned, running or awaiting_review attempts leave it pending; replacing an ineligible finalized attempt is user-initiated, never a next action). Rules in order (D23 and the practice-sessions requirement text): (1) program null or status completed | archived → `{ kind: 'setup' }`; (2) status draft → `{ kind: 'readiness' }`; (3) day ≤ 13 and a pending baseline slot → `{ kind: 'benchmark', slotId }`, A before B — this also holds before Day 0 (the slot is the next action; start availability is 5.1.2's `before_slot_date` rule shown by the Ready screen) and on Days 1–13 for a baseline never attempted (a late baseline is recorded with `timing_deviation` by 5.8, never blocked); program status never overrides slot state; (4) day ≥ 14 and a pending final slot → `{ kind: 'final', slotId }`, A before B (late finals stay reachable from Today after Day 14, D23); (5) day ≥ 14 otherwise → `{ kind: 'progress' }` (spec: after Day 14 a final slot that still lacks a finalized attempt, otherwise Progress — a baseline still pending after Day 14 no longer drives the next action); (6) otherwise (Day 0 with both baselines finalized; Days 1–13 with baselines finalized) → `{ kind: 'practice', block }` with block = first `in_progress`, else first `not_started`, else 2 (both blocks finalized: Today shows both done). A midpoint slot is never returned. Also export `nextPracticeBlock(blocks)` used by rule 6.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/nextAction.test.ts`: (1) null program → setup; (2) archived → setup; (3) completed → setup; (4) draft → readiness; (5) baseline_ready day 0, both baselines pending → benchmark with slot A's id; (6) A finalized → benchmark B; (7) A abandoned only → benchmark A (no finalized attempt); (8) day −2 → benchmark A (a slot before its date is still the next action); (9) day 0 both baselines finalized → practice block 1; (10) day 4 blocks [completed, not_started] → practice 2; (11) day 4 [in_progress, not_started] → practice 1; (12) day 4 [partial, not_started] → practice 2 (the next block remains available after an early finish); (13) day 6 no sessions → practice 1; (14) day 1 with baseline B pending → benchmark B; (15) day 13 with baseline B pending → benchmark B; (16) day 14 baselines done, finals pending → final A; (17) day 14 final A finalized → final B; (18) day 14 both finals finalized → progress; (19) day 15 with final B pending → final B (D23); (20) pending midpoint on day 7 → practice, never a midpoint action; (21) day 14 with baseline B AND finals pending → final A (finals govern Day 14 and after); (22) day 4 [completed, completed] → practice 2; (23) status 'active' with both baselines pending on day 3 → benchmark A (status does not override slot state); (24) day 20 both finals finalized, baseline B pending → progress; (25) a benchmark or final result has exactly the keys kind and slotId.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.2.3 and 4.5.3
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- src/services/program/nextAction.test.ts` → vitest reports 25 named cases passed, 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.5, 2.7.1, 2.7.3, 4.2.1

### 4.3.1 — Readiness validator for benchmark slot sets (pure)

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/readiness.ts` exporting `assignedDateFor(phase, baselineDate)` (baseline → `localDateForProgramDay(baselineDate, 0)`, midpoint → Day 7, final → Day 14 via calendar.ts 2.1.1; the date is a calendar offset in the program's local calendar, so no timezone argument is needed) and `normalizeSlotSet(slots: SlotInput[], program: { baselineDate })` → `{ slots: NormalizedSlot[]; missing: MissingSlot[]; complete: boolean }` throwing the 3.2.4 error classes. Rules: phase in baseline | midpoint | final, label A | B (contract 2.7.3); duplicate (phase,label) in the body → `MalformedError` with `fieldErrors: { ['slots[' + i + ']']: 'duplicate slot' }` (400 `malformed_request`, D19); `assigned_local_date` via `assignedDateFor`; a final slot with no `plannedLocalTime` inherits the same-label baseline time (final A ← baseline A) and stores it on the row; when both baseline times are present, `|minutesOfDay(A) − minutesOfDay(B)| >= 60` else `DomainError('baseline_times_too_close', ...)` (422) whose message states the one-hour minimum (same-local-date semantics: 23:30 and 00:15 are 23 h 15 m apart); `missing` lists, for each of the four required (phase,label), the absent row, a whitespace-only `materialRef` (an empty string is already a contract 400), or (baseline only) an absent `plannedLocalTime` as `{ phase, label, fields: ('materialRef'|'plannedLocalTime')[] }`; `complete = missing.length === 0`; the midpoint slot is optional and never appears in `missing`. Time format HH:MM is enforced by the contract (2.7.3).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/readiness.test.ts`: (1) 09:00 and 09:59 → baseline_times_too_close with message containing 'one hour'; (2) 09:00 and 10:00 → ok; (3) B at 08:00 before A at 12:00 → ok; (4) 23:30 and 00:15 → ok (same-date semantics); (5) finals without times inherit baseline A/B times respectively; (6) final with explicit time keeps it; (7) three references → missing exactly [{final, B, ['materialRef']}]; (8) whitespace materialRef → missing materialRef; (9) baseline A without time → missing ['plannedLocalTime']; (10) baseline A without ref and time → both fields listed; (11) midpoint absent → not in missing, complete true when four present; (12) midpoint present → assigned Day 7 date; (13) duplicate (baseline, A) → MalformedError with fieldErrors key 'slots[1]'; (14) assignedDateFor: Day 0 = baselineDate, Day 14 = baselineDate + 14 local days across a DST boundary (2026-10-28 → 2026-11-11).
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.3.2
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- src/services/program/readiness.test.ts` → vitest reports 14 named cases passed, 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.5, 2.1.1, 2.7.3, 3.2.4

### 4.3.2 — PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots atomic replace with freeze and status

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Register `PUT /api/v1/programs/:id/benchmark-slots` (contract `PutSlotsBody` 2.7.3; a slot property `realm` or any unknown key → 400) backed by `replaceBenchmarkSlots(ctx, programId, body)` in `programService.ts`: one transaction: `loadOwnedProgram(..., { forUpdate: true })` (404 for other principals); `body.expectedVersion !== program.version` → 409 `stale_version` with `details: { current: toProgramDto(program) }` (D18); status completed | archived → 409 `program_terminal`; the route stays accepted while the program is draft, baseline_ready or active (D38 keeps `/setup/readiness` reachable); run `normalizeSlotSet` (4.3.1); load existing `benchmark_slots` with their attempt rows; export `isFrozen(row, attemptCount)` = `frozen_at IS NOT NULL` OR any `focus_sessions.slot_id` references it (5.1.2 sets `frozen_at` at the first attempt; the reference check is the backstop), and `frozenFieldsUnchanged(existing, incoming)` comparing `material_ref`, `planned_local_time`, `language`, `device_format`, `material_level` (null and undefined treated equal); for every frozen slot the incoming row must exist and be unchanged else 409 `slot_frozen` with `details: { phase, label }` (D18/D19); omitting a frozen slot is the same 409; upsert rows on `UNIQUE(program_id, phase, label)` with `assigned_local_date` from `assignedDateFor`, delete unfrozen rows absent from the body; no realm column is written on slot rows (D34: child rows inherit the program's realm); if the program is beyond draft and the resulting set is incomplete → 422 `readiness_regression` (status never falls back to draft); when status is draft and `complete` → status 'baseline_ready'; `version += 1`; respond 200 `{ program, slots (toSlotDto with attempts), missing }` per D22 with `Cache-Control: no-store`. Never inserts a `focus_sessions` row.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/readiness.test.ts` (extend): (1) frozenFieldsUnchanged identical → true; (2) materialRef changed → false; (3) plannedLocalTime changed → false; (4) language null vs undefined → true; (5) isFrozen({ frozen_at: null }, attemptCount 1) → true; (6) isFrozen({ frozen_at: date }, 0) → true; (7) isFrozen({ frozen_at: null }, 0) → false.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/benchmark-slots.test.ts`: (1) complete four-slot body on a draft → 200, program.status 'baseline_ready', version 2, four rows, finals' `planned_local_time` equal the baselines', baseline `assigned_local_date` = Day 0 and finals = Day 14 dates, every slot's `attempts` is []; (2) three references → 200, status stays 'draft', `missing` = [{ phase: 'final', label: 'B', fields: ['materialRef'] }]; (3) baseline 09:00/09:45 → 422 `baseline_times_too_close`, message contains 'one hour', no rows written; (4) wrong expectedVersion → 409 `stale_version` with `details.current.version` equal to the stored version; (5) insertSession finalized benchmark on baseline A + changed materialRef → 409 `slot_frozen` with details { phase: 'baseline', label: 'A' } and rows unchanged; (6) frozen A unchanged + final B time changed before Day 14 → 200 and final B updated (edit an unattempted final slot); (7) frozen slot omitted from body → 409 `slot_frozen`; (8) `frozen_at` set with no session row → also frozen; (9) a slot with a `realm` property → 400, no rows changed; (10) another principal's program → 404; (11) after a successful save `focus_sessions` count is 0 (saving never starts a timer); (12) duplicate (baseline, A) in body → 400 `malformed_request` with fieldErrors 'slots[1]'; (13) seedActiveProgram then a body with three references → 422 `readiness_regression`, rows unchanged, status still 'active'; (14) after (5), the response of a valid save lists slot A's attempt as { lifecycle 'finalized', excludedByAmendment false }.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/benchmark-slots.test.ts src/services/program/readiness.test.ts` → vitest reports 35 named cases passed (14 from 4.3.1 + 7 + 14), 0 failed.
- **After:** 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 4.3.1, 3.2.4, 3.3.1, 3.3.2

### 4.2.3 — GET /programs/current route with day, attempts and nextAction

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Register `GET /api/v1/programs/current` in `apps/api/src/routes/programs.ts` backed by `getCurrentProgram(ctx)` in `programService.ts`: select the principal's program with `status IN ('draft','baseline_ready','active') AND user_id = ctx.principalId`; none → 200 `{ program: null, revision: null, slots: [], day: null, nextAction: { kind: 'setup' } }` (D22 — completed and archived programs are not open); found → `assertOwnRealm(ctx, program.realm)` (3.2.3). Export `loadProgramSnapshot(db, ctx, program)` (reused by 4.5.3) returning `{ localDate, day, revisions, governingRevision, slots (rows + attempts + hasFinalizedAttempt), todaySessions, blocks }`: `{ day, localDate } = currentProgramDay({ baselineDate: program.baseline_date, timezone: program.timezone }, ctx.now)` from `packages/shared/src/domain/calendar.ts` (2.1.2) — the program's stored timezone, never `user_profiles.timezone`; `ctx.now` already carries the demo offset (D8); `slotAttempts(db, slotIds)` loads `focus_sessions WHERE slot_id IN (...)` across all lifecycles ordered by `started_at` with `eligible` and `excludedByAmendment = EXISTS (session_amendments WHERE session_id = s.id AND exclude_from_report)` → `attempts: [{ sessionId, lifecycle, eligible, excludedByAmendment }]` per slot (D22) and `hasFinalizedAttempt = attempts.some(a => a.lifecycle === 'finalized')`; today's practice sessions + `session_reviews` loaded by `(program_id, kind, local_date)` (the 3.3.2 index); `guardRealm(ctx, rows)` applies `assertOwnRealm` to every loaded session and attempt row → 422 `realm_mismatch` with the 3.2.4 fixed message, never filtering silently; `blocks = deriveBlocks(...)` (4.2.1) with the governing revision's `settings.practiceTargetSeconds`; `revision = governingRevisionFor(revisions, day)` (4.1.1); `nextAction = deriveNextAction(...)` (4.2.2). Respond `{ program, revision, slots (toSlotDto with attempts), day, nextAction }` with `Cache-Control: no-store`. The route is strictly read-only.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/programService.test.ts` (extend): (1) the empty snapshot for no program yields nextAction setup, `day: null` (not 0), `revision: null`, `slots: []`; (2) `slotAttemptStates`: a slot with a finalized row → hasFinalizedAttempt true; only an abandoned row → false with attempts [{ lifecycle 'abandoned', eligible null, excludedByAmendment false }]; no rows → false with attempts []; (3) `guardRealm` throws DomainError `realm_mismatch` on one foreign row among demo rows and passes on an empty list.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/current.test.ts`: (1) no program → 200, program null, revision null, day null, slots [], nextAction setup; (2) draft program → readiness, `day` computed; (3) complete four-slot PUT (4.3.2) on a draft with baselineDate = today in tz → 200, then GET current → status baseline_ready, day 0, nextAction benchmark with slot A's id, slots length 4 each with `attempts: []`, `focus_sessions` count 0 (Save readiness lands on the next benchmark, no timer); (4) plus insertSession finalized benchmark on slot A → nextAction benchmark B and slot A `attempts` = [{ sessionId, lifecycle 'finalized', eligible equal to the stored value, excludedByAmendment false }]; (5) seedActiveProgram day 4 → day 4, nextAction practice block 1; (6) plus a finalized complete practice session today → practice block 2; (7) seedActiveProgram day 14 → nextAction final A with final A's slotId; (8) day 15 with both finals pending → final A; with final A finalized → final B (D23); (9) day 15 with both finals finalized → progress; (10) program status set to 'completed' directly → program null / setup; (11) another principal's open program is invisible → program null; (12) `user_profiles.timezone` changed to 'Pacific/Kiritimati' on a program stored in 'America/Bogota' → `day` and `localDate` unchanged; (13) DST: program tz 'America/New_York', baselineDate 2026-10-28 (DST ends 2026-11-01), setDemoNow 2026-11-02T04:30Z → day 4, localDate '2026-11-01' (23:30 EST; a UTC-4 bug would give Day 5), then setDemoNow 2026-11-02T05:30Z → day 5, localDate '2026-11-02' (no day skipped or duplicated); (14) setDemoOffsetSeconds +4 days on a seeded Day-0 active program → day 4 (server-side clock, D8); (15) `Cache-Control: no-store` present; (16) `revision` is the governing one: with revision 2 effective_day 8 inserted directly, Day 4 returns revision 1 and Day 9 returns revision 2; (17) a practice session inserted today with realm 'pilot' → 422 `realm_mismatch` whose message contains neither 'demo' nor 'pilot'; (18) baseline_ready program on Day 2 with baseline B never attempted → nextAction benchmark B; (19) an abandoned attempt on slot A plus a finalized attempt with a `session_amendments` row (exclude_from_report true) inserted directly → slot A attempts list both, the abandoned one with eligible null and the finalized one with excludedByAmendment true, and nextAction is benchmark B.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/current.test.ts src/services/program/programService.test.ts` → vitest reports 33 named cases passed (14 in programService: 9 + 2 + 3; 19 integration), 0 failed.

### 4.4.1 — POST /programs/{id}/revisions with required reason and effective day

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/revisions.ts` exporting `nextRevisionNumber(existing)`, `normalizeReason(reason)` (trim; empty → `MalformedError` with `fieldErrors.reason: 'is required'`), `effectiveDayAllowed(effectiveDay, currentDay)` (= `effectiveDay >= max(currentDay, 0) && effectiveDay <= 14`, D33) and `composeSettings(bodySettings, program)` = `{ practiceTargetSeconds: bodySettings.practiceTargetSeconds, bandCeilings: BAND_CEILINGS (2.5.1; server-owned, never client-supplied), leisureAllowanceMin: bodySettings.leisureAllowanceMin ?? program.leisure_allowance_min }`, and register `POST /api/v1/programs/:id/revisions` with the 2.7.3 `CreateRevisionBody` (`effectiveDay` 0..14, `settings: { practiceTargetSeconds 300..1500 step 300, leisureAllowanceMin? }` with `additionalProperties: false` so `bandCeilings`, `benchmarkSeconds` or any benchmark duration in the body is 400, `reason` minLength 1) backed by `createRevision(ctx, programId, body)`: one transaction: `loadOwnedProgram(forUpdate)`; terminal status → 409 `program_terminal`; whitespace reason → 400 `fieldErrors.reason`; `effectiveDay` checked against the current day from `currentProgramDay` (2.1.2) with ctx.now and the program timezone, else 422 `effective_day_in_past`; insert `protocol_revisions` with `revision = nextRevisionNumber(...)`, `settings = composeSettings(...)`, the trimmed reason; update `programs.current_revision_id` to the new (newest) revision (D33) and `version += 1`; never touch `focus_sessions` (sessions keep the `revision_id` stamped at start by 5.1 via `governingRevisionFor`). Respond 201 `{ revision, program }` no-store. No update or delete route for revisions exists (404). This route is the accept path for a progression suggestion: the client (8.2.4) posts `reason: PROGRESSION_ACCEPTED_REASON` ('progression accepted', 2.5.2) with the suggested `practiceTargetSeconds` and `effectiveDay` = the current day.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/revisions.test.ts`: (1) nextRevisionNumber([1,2]) = 3, ([]) = 1; (2) normalizeReason('   ') rejected with fieldErrors.reason, (' more time ') accepted and trimmed to 'more time'; (3) effectiveDayAllowed: current day 8 accepts 8 and 14, rejects 7 and 15; current day −1 accepts 0; (4) composeSettings fills bandCeilings from BAND_CEILINGS and leisureAllowanceMin from the program row when omitted, and keeps an explicit body leisureAllowanceMin.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/revisions.test.ts`: (1) seedActiveProgram day 8; insertSession finalized practice for Days 1–7 with `revision_id` = revision 1; POST { effectiveDay 8, settings { practiceTargetSeconds 1200, leisureAllowanceMin 20 }, reason 'more time' } → 201 revision.revision 2, effectiveDay 8, settings.bandCeilings equal to BAND_CEILINGS; SELECT shows all seven sessions still reference revision 1; `programs.current_revision_id` = revision 2 and version incremented; (2) empty reason → 400 fieldErrors.reason and no row; (3) whitespace reason → 400; (4) effectiveDay 5 on Day 8 → 422 `effective_day_in_past`; (5) settings with extra `benchmarkSeconds` → 400 (benchmark duration never follows progression); (6) reason 'progression accepted' with practiceTargetSeconds 1200 → 201 (suggestion accept path); (7) PATCH, PUT and DELETE on `/programs/:id/revisions/:rid` → 404 (immutable); (8) another principal's program → 404; (9) two revisions posted on Day 8 both effective 8 → `governingRevisionFor` (4.1.1) picks revision 3 and `current_revision_id` is revision 3; (10) body containing `realm` → 400; (11) settings containing `bandCeilings` → 400 (server-owned); (12) settings omitting leisureAllowanceMin on a program with leisure_allowance_min 30 → stored settings.leisureAllowanceMin 30.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/revisions.test.ts src/services/program/revisions.test.ts` → vitest reports 16 named cases passed (4 unit + 12 integration), 0 failed.

### 4.4.2 — PATCH /programs/{id} with version, draft-only dates and explicit status

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/patch.ts` exporting `canTransition(from, to)` over the D33 table — `completed` from baseline_ready | active; `archived` from draft | baseline_ready | active; nothing else (baseline_ready → active happens automatically in 5.8 when the second baseline attempt is finalized and is never a PATCH transition) — `dateEditAllowed(status)` (draft only) and `recomputeAssignedDates(slots, baselineDate)` using `assignedDateFor` (4.3.1); register `PATCH /api/v1/programs/:id` with the 2.7.3 `PatchProgramBody` `{ expectedVersion, status?: 'completed' | 'archived', baselineDate?, leisureAllowanceMinutes? }` (the contract's status literal is narrowed to these two values, so 'active', 'draft' and 'baseline_ready' are 400; `timezone` is not in the contract — program timezone is immutable after creation) backed by `patchProgram(ctx, programId, body)`: one transaction: `loadOwnedProgram(forUpdate)`; `expectedVersion` mismatch → 409 `stale_version` with `details: { current: toProgramDto(program) }` (D18); terminal status → 409 `program_terminal`; `baselineDate` accepted only while status = 'draft' else 422 `date_locked`; when baselineDate changes, rewrite every slot's `assigned_local_date` in the same transaction; `leisureAllowanceMinutes` updates `programs.leisure_allowance_min` only, on any non-terminal program, creating no revision (D33); `status` not allowed by `canTransition` → 409 `invalid_status_transition`; `version += 1`; respond 200 `{ program }` no-store.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/patch.test.ts`: (1) canTransition: baseline_ready→completed, active→completed, draft→archived, baseline_ready→archived, active→archived true; draft→completed, completed→archived, archived→completed, active→active, baseline_ready→active false; (2) dateEditAllowed true for draft, false for baseline_ready and active; (3) recomputeAssignedDates maps baseline slots to the new Day 0, midpoint to Day 7 and finals to the new Day 14.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/patch.test.ts`: (1) insertProgram draft + insertSlotSet → PATCH baselineDate +3 days → 200, every slot's `assigned_local_date` shifted by 3 local days; (2) baseline_ready program PATCH baselineDate → 422 `date_locked`, dates unchanged; (3) wrong expectedVersion → 409 `stale_version` with `details.current.version` equal to the stored version; (4) leisureAllowanceMinutes 30 on an active program → 200, `leisure_allowance_min` 30, `protocol_revisions` count unchanged; (5) status 'archived' on an active program → 200, then POST /programs with a new key → 201 (explicit archive frees the one-program rule); (6) status 'completed' from active → 200; from baseline_ready → 200; (7) PATCH on a completed program → 409 `program_terminal`; (8) status 'draft', 'baseline_ready' or 'active' in body → 400 (contract); (9) body with `timezone` → 400; (10) another principal's program → 404; (11) draft → 'completed' → 409 `invalid_status_transition`; (12) status 'archived' on a draft → 200 status 'archived'.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/patch.test.ts src/services/program/patch.test.ts` → vitest reports 15 named cases passed (3 unit + 12 integration), 0 failed.

### 4.5.1 — Progression suggestion adapter over stored sessions (pure)

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Create `apps/api/src/services/program/suggestion.ts` exporting `toPracticeBlockRecords(rows: SuggestionRow[], realm: Realm): PracticeBlockRecord[]` and `deriveSuggestion({ day, currentTargetSeconds, rows, realm })`. `SuggestionRow = { id, realm, kind, lifecycle, localDate, startedAt, targetSeconds, completeInterval, outputQuality, episodeCount: ReportedCount }` (focus_sessions joined to session_reviews for Days 1..day−1). The adapter (a) asserts every row's `realm` equals the request realm and throws `RealmMixingError` (types.ts; 3.2.4 maps it to 422 `realm_mismatch`) otherwise — never filters silently; (b) keeps only `kind = 'practice'` rows with lifecycle ≠ 'abandoned' and, per local date, the first two by `startedAt` (the two blocks; a third session of a day is never a block, mirroring 4.2.1); (c) maps each to the 2.5.1 `PracticeBlockRecord` `{ sessionId, realm, kind, localDate, targetSeconds, completeInterval, outputQuality, episodeCount (null stays null; no `?? 0`), finalized: lifecycle === 'finalized' }`; (d) `deriveSuggestion` returns `null` for day > 14 (no band beyond Day 14) and otherwise calls `suggestProgression({ day, currentTargetSeconds, blocks })` from `packages/shared/src/domain/progression.ts` (2.5.2) — two consecutive qualifying local days, both blocks finalized at the current target with output 'yes' and `episodeCount` a number ≤ 1 (null never qualifies), Partly breaks qualification, missed days hold, ceiling by band (Days 1–3: 600 s, 4–7: 900 s, 8–10: 1200 s, 11–14: 1500 s) — returning `{ suggestedTargetSeconds, qualifiedOn: [LocalDate, LocalDate] } | null`. No hold state is stored: the suggestion is recomputed on every request until accepted (a revision, 4.4.1) or the pair no longer qualifies. Benchmark rows and the benchmark duration never enter.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/suggestion.test.ts`: (1) Days 4–5 both qualifying at 900 s, request day 6 → null (ceiling 900); (2) same rows, day 8 → suggestedTargetSeconds 1200, qualifiedOn [Day 4 date, Day 5 date]; (3) Days 6–7 missing between → still 1200 on day 8 (missed days hold); (4) Day 5 has one block 'partly' → null; (5) episodeCount null on one block → null (unknown ≠ zero); (6) episodeCount 0 and 1 qualify, 2 does not; (7) block finalized with targetSeconds 600 when the current target is 900 → not at target → null; (8) completeInterval null → not completed → null; (9) benchmark rows ignored even with output yes; (10) abandoned rows ignored; (11) a row with realm 'pilot' among demo rows → throws RealmMixingError; (12) after acceptance (current target 1200 on day 8, prior days at 900) → null; (13) Days 1–2 qualifying at 300 s on day 3 → 600 (ceiling 600 permits); (14) Days 1–2 qualifying at 600 on day 3 → null (ceiling 600 reached); (15) day 15 → null regardless of rows; (16) a third practice session on a qualifying day is not passed to suggestProgression (records per date ≤ 2).
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 4.5.3
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- src/services/program/suggestion.test.ts` → vitest reports 16 named cases passed, 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.5, 2.5.1, 2.5.2, 2.7.1
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Two qualifying days · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Partly output breaks the streak of qualifying days · practice-sessions: Practice requires a short intended output / Hold the target · practice-sessions: Practice metrics stay separate from benchmarks · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result

### 4.5.3 — GET /programs/{id}/today route with blocks, check-in status and suggestion

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Register `GET /api/v1/programs/:id/today` backed by `getToday(ctx, programId)` in `programService.ts`: `loadOwnedProgram` (404; foreign realm 422); `loadProgramSnapshot` (4.2.3) for localDate, day, governing revision, slots with attempts and today's blocks (4.2.1) — day derived with ctx.now (demo clock honored, D8) in the program timezone; `checkin`: load `daily_checkins WHERE program_id AND local_date` (checked with `assertOwnRealm`) plus its `feed_usage` rows mapped to `FeedRowInput`, then `{ status, missing } = checkinStatus(row ? { sleepMinutes: row.sleep_minutes } : null, feedRows)` from `packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts` (2.6.2; the single completeness implementation, also used by 6.1; status ∈ complete | incomplete | not_reported) and `values = { sleepMinutes: row's sleep_minutes or null, phoneFeedMinutes: feedAggregates(feedRows).feedByDevice.phone, desktopFeedMinutes: feedAggregates(feedRows).feedByDevice.desktop }` (2.6.1; a device with no feed-scope row → null, an explicit zero row → 0, app_total rows never summed) per D22; `suggestion`: load practice sessions + `session_reviews` for Days 1..day−1 by `(program_id, kind, local_date)`, pass through `deriveSuggestion` (4.5.1) with `currentTargetSeconds` = the governing revision's `settings.practiceTargetSeconds` (RealmMixingError → 422 via 3.2.4); `nextAction` from the snapshot (4.2.2). Export `toTodayResponse(...)` building `{ day, localDate, blocks, checkin: { status, missing, values }, suggestion?, nextAction }` per the 2.7.3 `TodayResponse` amended by D22 — the `suggestion` key is omitted when null; `Cache-Control: no-store`. The response carries no streak, missed-day or punitive derived fields; stress, mindfulness and note are not returned here (they belong to the days route, 6.1).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/src/services/program/today.test.ts`: (1) `toTodayResponse` omits the `suggestion` key when the suggestion is null and includes `{ suggestedTargetSeconds, qualifiedOn }` when defined; (2) output keys are exactly {day, localDate, blocks, checkin, nextAction} (+ suggestion) and checkin keys exactly {status, missing, values} — no streak or missed-day fields; (3) `checkinValuesFrom(row, feedRows)`: no row → all three null; sleep_minutes 0 → 0; an explicit-zero phone feed row → phoneFeedMinutes 0; desktop with no feed-scope row → null; a phone app_total-only row → phoneFeedMinutes null.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/programs/today.test.ts`: (1) seedActiveProgram day 4 with one finalized complete practice session today → blocks [completed, not_started], nextAction practice block 2, blocks[].targetSeconds 900 from revision 1; (2) day 6 with no Day 5 sessions → day 6, both blocks not_started, nextAction practice 1, response keys exactly {day, localDate, blocks, checkin, nextAction}; (3) today's check-in inserted with sleep only (insertCheckin) → checkin.status 'incomplete', missing ['feed'], values { sleepMinutes: 420, phoneFeedMinutes: null, desktopFeedMinutes: null }; (4) no check-in → 'not_reported', missing ['sleep','feed'], values all null; (5) sleep + explicit-zero phone feed row → 'complete', missing [], phoneFeedMinutes 0, desktopFeedMinutes null; (6) Days 4–5 qualifying inserted, request on Day 6 → no `suggestion` key; (7) same rows on Day 8 → suggestion { suggestedTargetSeconds 1200, qualifiedOn [Day 4 date, Day 5 date] }; (8) one Partly block on Day 5 → no suggestion; (9) `episode_count` NULL on one block → no suggestion (unknown ≠ zero); (10) two consecutive GETs on Day 8 return the identical suggestion (no server-side hold state); (11) accept via POST revisions (4.4.1) reason 'progression accepted' 1200 → next GET has no suggestion and blocks[].targetSeconds 1200; (12) a benchmark session inserted today → not a block; (13) a practice row with realm 'pilot' → 422 `realm_mismatch`, not silently filtered; (14) setDemoOffsetSeconds +4 days on a Day-0 seeded program → day 4; (15) Day 15 with finals pending → nextAction final A (D23); (16) Day 14 → nextAction final A; (17) Day 14 with a finalized complete practice session today → blocks [completed, not_started], nextAction still final A, both final slots still without a finalized attempt (practice never fills a benchmark slot); (18) another principal's program → 404; (19) `Cache-Control: no-store` present; (20) phone app_total 60 alongside phone feed 25 → phoneFeedMinutes 25 (app totals never summed).
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/programs/today.test.ts src/services/program/today.test.ts` → vitest reports 23 named cases passed (3 unit + 20 integration), 0 failed.
- **After:** 4.1.1, 4.1.2, 4.2.1, 4.2.2, 4.2.3, 4.4.1, 4.5.1, 2.6.1, 2.6.2, 2.7.3, 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.3.3

## 5. Sessions API

### 5.1.3 — Build sessions-only test seed helpers, reusing 3.2.1's app builder and 4.1.1's program helpers

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/test/helpers/sessions.ts holds ONLY the session-level seed helpers (D16 — no unit re-creates a lower layer): seedSession(db, overrides) inserting a focus_sessions row with defaults matching the 3.3 schema { id randomUUID(), user_id 'local-demo', program_id, revision_id, slot_id null, realm 'demo', kind 'practice', lifecycle 'running', target_seconds 600, started_at new Date(), ended_at null, paused_seconds 0, current_pause_started_at null, local_date today in the program tz, intended_output 'seeded output', time_source 'measured', timer_quality 'ok', clock_gap_seconds null, complete_interval null, eligible null, exclusion_reasons [], replacement_reason null, version 1 }, plus a session_reviews row via seedReview unless the caller passes review: false; seedReview(db, sessionId, overrides) inserting/upserting session_reviews with every measurement column NULL, recall_flags [], observed_conditions { deviceFormat: null, language: null, materialLevel: null, accommodations: [] }, finalized_at null, version 1; seedEvents(db, sessionId, rows) inserting session_events rows with defaults { client_event_id randomUUID(), occurred_at = received_at = now, voided_at null, details {} }; seedAmendment(db, sessionId, { reason, excludeFromReport }) inserting session_amendments with created_at = now; seedAgentPlan(db, sessionId, overrides) inserting agent_plans with defaults { workstream null, waiting_task null, resume_note null, review_checkpoint 'end_of_block', review_at null, version 1 } (new helper so group 5b's agent-plan unit does not recreate it); withIdempotencyKey() -> { 'idempotency-key': randomUUID() }. Program, slot, profile and app-level fixtures are never redefined here — every 5a/5b integration test composes these session helpers with 3.2.1's buildTestApp()/truncateAll() and 4.1.1's insertProgram/insertSlotSet/seedActiveProgram/setDemoOffsetSeconds/setDemoNow/insertOtherPrincipalProgram/ensurePrincipalProfile from apps/api/test/helpers/programs.ts.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: test infrastructure; the harness smoke test below (against the real test database) is its verification
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/helpers/harness.test.ts (session-helpers slice; buildTestApp/truncateAll/insertProgram/insertSlotSet are already smoke-tested by 3.2.1 and 4.1.1): 'seedSession defaults: realm demo, user_id local-demo, lifecycle running, version 1, paused_seconds 0, eligible NULL, exclusion_reasons empty array'; 'seedSession without review:false also creates a session_reviews row with episode_count SQL NULL (not 0) and observed_conditions all-null fields with accommodations []'; 'seedReview overrides merge onto the all-null defaults'; 'seedEvents inserts rows with voided_at NULL and details preserved (alsoOffTask true survives the round trip)'; 'seedAmendment inserts a row with exclude_from_report true and created_at set'; 'seedAgentPlan inserts a row with review_checkpoint end_of_block and version 1'; 'withIdempotencyKey returns a fresh UUID on each call'; 'seeding a second running session for the same principal throws on the 3.3 partial unique index (the harness never bypasses constraints)'; 'composing insertProgram (4.1.1, status baseline_ready) + insertSlotSet + seedSession with slotId set produces a benchmark session joined to its slot'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/helpers/harness.test.ts  -> 9 named cases passed, 0 failed; npm run typecheck exits 0.
