### 5.2.1 — Implement pure session timing and tally derivation

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/sessionTiming.ts exporting deriveTiming({ startedAt, endedAt, targetSeconds, pausedSeconds, currentPauseStartedAt }, now) -> { elapsedSeconds, remainingSeconds, deadlineReached, isPaused } per D5: effective end = endedAt ?? now; an open pause counts as paused from currentPauseStartedAt up to the effective end so remaining is frozen while paused; elapsedSeconds = floor((end - startedAt) / 1000) - pausedSeconds - openPauseSeconds, never below 0; remainingSeconds = max(0, targetSeconds - elapsedSeconds); deadlineReached = elapsedSeconds >= targetSeconds; isPaused = currentPauseStartedAt !== null. The module returns no lifecycle and mutates nothing — this is what makes GET routes (5.2.2, 5.2.3) strictly read-only under D24 (the server only confirms a reached deadline on the next end transition, 5.4.2). apps/api/src/services/sessionTallies.ts exporting deriveTallies(events) -> { offTask, external, agentChecks } per D11 — the exact D20 tallies shape: rows with voided_at are skipped; off_task -> offTask + 1; external -> external + 1; agent_check -> agentChecks + 1 and, only when details.alsoOffTask === true, offTask + 1; pause, resume, clock_gap and visibility contribute to no tally (D15/D39). The returned object has exactly those three keys; no total and no cross-tally sum is exported anywhere. Both files are pure TypeScript with no DB import so 5.1.1's serializer, 5.2.2, 5.3.1 and 5.4.x share them.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/sessionTiming.test.ts: '6:00 into a 15-minute block -> elapsed 360, remaining 540, deadlineReached false'; 'past the target -> remaining 0, deadlineReached true, no lifecycle key in the result'; 'a closed 240 s pause is excluded: 900 s of wall time gives elapsed 660'; 'an open pause freezes elapsed: identical results at now and now + 120 s, isPaused true'; 'endedAt set -> elapsed is measured to endedAt not now (early finish at 480 s)'; 'benchmark 1200 s at wall 1200 s with no pause -> deadlineReached true'. apps/api/test/unit/sessionTallies.test.ts: 'off_task + agent_check(alsoOffTask true) + agent_check(alsoOffTask false) + external -> { offTask 2, external 1, agentChecks 2 } and Object.keys length is 3'; 'voided off_task is excluded'; 'five visibility events -> all zero'; 'clock_gap, pause and resume -> all zero'; 'one off_task -> offTask 1 (one departure = one episode; server infers nothing)'; 'agent_check with alsoOffTask true adds one to each tally, never two to offTask'; 'empty events -> zeros (an event count, distinct from the review's ReportedCount which stays null — asserted in 5.2.2)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure functions, covered by API integration in 5.1.1, 5.2.2 and 5.4.2
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/unit/sessionTiming.test.ts test/unit/sessionTallies.test.ts  -> 13 named cases passed, 0 failed.
- **After:** 1.2, 1.5
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Practice timer and elapsed time · practice-sessions: Pause and resume are explicit / Paused seconds excluded · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Agent check that was also off-task · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / One departure, five apps · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Hidden page creates nothing · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session

### 5.1.1 — Implement POST /sessions practice start with idempotency and active-session conflict

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** New files: apps/api/src/routes/sessions/index.ts (registers the /api/v1/sessions group), apps/api/src/routes/sessions/start.ts, apps/api/src/services/session.ts exporting startSession(ctx, body) where ctx = { principalId, realm, now, demoClockOffsetSeconds, timeSource } comes from the identity plugin (3.2.3), and apps/api/src/services/sessionSerializer.ts exporting serializeSession(session, review, events, plan, amendments, now) — the single D20 response shape reused by every sessions route (5.1.1, 5.1.2, 5.2.2, 5.2.3, and group 5b's transitions/clock-gap/recall/finalize): stored focus_sessions columns in camelCase, serverNow = now.toISOString(), timing = deriveTiming(session, now) (5.2.1), tallies = deriveTallies(events) (5.2.1), eventCount = events.length (every stored session_events row, voided included — distinct from tallies which exclude voided rows), events[] (voided rows included with voidedAt), review (every ReportedCount column serialized as JSON null, never 0), agentPlan (null when absent), amendments[] (empty array when none). Check order, fixed for all of 5a: contract 400 (2.7, includes realm-in-body and >200-char intendedOutput) -> Idempotency-Key handling (3.4: header absent 400; same key + same request hash -> replay, respond 200; same key + different hash -> 409 idempotency_mismatch; replay re-serializes the session named by mutation_receipts.result_ref so timing is fresh, never a stored body) -> ownership 404 -> active-session 409 -> domain 422; a first (non-replay) write responds 201, a replay responds 200 (D21). Practice path (kind 'practice'): load programs WHERE id = programId AND user_id = principalId (404 otherwise, nothing written); compute localDate and program day with packages/shared domain/calendar.ts (2.1) from programs.baseline_date, programs.timezone and ctx.now; governing revision = protocol_revisions row with the greatest effective_day <= day (ties: highest revision) — matches 4.1.1's governingRevisionFor; intendedOutput trimmed: absent, empty or whitespace-only -> 422 fieldErrors.intendedOutput (the 200-char maximum is the 2.7 contract's -> 400); slotId on a practice body -> 422 benchmark_only_field (practice never fills a slot; the DB CHECK (kind='benchmark') = (slot_id IS NOT NULL) from 3.3 is the backstop); targetSeconds omitted -> settings.practiceTargetSeconds of the governing revision; supplied -> any value 300..1500 step 300 is accepted by the 2.7 contract (400 otherwise) and stored exactly as given — holding the target is a client choice (D30). Active-session check: any focus_sessions row for the principal with lifecycle IN (running, paused, awaiting_review) -> 409 active_session_exists with details.activeSessionId (D18); a unique violation on the 3.3 partial unique index during insert maps to the same 409 (race backstop). In one transaction insert focus_sessions { user_id = principalId, program_id, revision_id, slot_id null, realm = ctx.realm (never from the body), kind, lifecycle 'running', target_seconds, started_at = ctx.now, ended_at null, paused_seconds 0, current_pause_started_at null, local_date, intended_output, time_source = ctx.timeSource (3.2.3 already derives 'measured' | 'demo_clock' from the demo offset per D8 — no separate lookup needed), timer_quality 'ok', clock_gap_seconds null, complete_interval null, eligible null, exclusion_reasons [], replacement_reason null, version 1 } and a session_reviews row { session_id, every count/score/recall column NULL, recall_flags [], observed_conditions = body.conditions ?? { deviceFormat: null, language: null, materialLevel: null, accommodations: [] }, finalized_at null, version 1 } — the review row is created at session start, not deferred to recall/finalize (D31); group 5b's recall and finalize routes UPDATE this row, never INSERT. Respond 201 with serializeSession(row, review, [], null, [], ctx.now). Cache-Control: no-store comes from the 3.2.2 plugin. Tests compose 5.1.3's session helpers (seedSession, withIdempotencyKey, etc.) with 4.1.1's insertProgram/insertSlotSet and 3.2.1's buildTestApp/truncateAll.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/session.start.test.ts: 'governing revision picks the greatest effective_day <= day'; 'ties resolve to the highest revision number'; 'day before any later revision falls back to revision 1'; 'ctx.timeSource measured is stored verbatim on the session (offset 0)'; 'ctx.timeSource demo_clock is stored verbatim (nonzero or negative offset)'; 'practice conditions default to all-null fields with empty accommodations'; 'normalizeIntendedOutput: whitespace-only is treated as absent, interior whitespace is preserved'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/start.practice.test.ts (Fastify inject, attention_lab_test): 'creates a running practice session with target from the governing revision, lifecycle running, version 1, paused_seconds 0, started_at = ctx.now'; '201 body is the D20 shape: serverNow present, timing { elapsedSeconds 0, remainingSeconds = target, deadlineReached false, isPaused false }, tallies { offTask 0, external 0, agentChecks 0 }, eventCount 0, events [], review.episodeCount null, agentPlan null, amendments []'; 'explicit targetSeconds equal to the current target is stored (hold the target)'; 'explicit targetSeconds 900 on a 600 target is stored as given'; 'targetSeconds 700 (not a multiple of 300) -> 400, no row'; 'targetSeconds 1800 (above the 1500 max) -> 400, no row'; 'empty or whitespace-only intendedOutput -> 422 naming intendedOutput, no focus_sessions row'; '201-character intendedOutput -> 400 from the contract, no row'; 'slotId on a practice body -> 422 benchmark_only_field, no row (practice can never become a baseline)'; 'body with realm pilot -> 400 and no row (client cannot choose the realm)'; 'missing Idempotency-Key -> 400'; 'replay: same key + same body -> 200, same session id, exactly one row, fresh timing'; 'same key + different intendedOutput -> 409 idempotency_mismatch, still one row'; 'second start while a session is running -> 409 active_session_exists with details.activeSessionId'; 'start while a seeded session is awaiting_review -> 409 with that id'; 'start while a seeded session is paused -> 409'; 'start after a seeded abandoned session -> 201'; 'start after a seeded finalized session -> 201'; 'stored realm is demo and user_id is the principal'; 'demo offset 900 -> time_source demo_clock; offset 0 -> measured'; 'local_date uses the program timezone: Pacific/Auckland program at 2026-09-06T11:30Z stores 2026-09-06, at 2026-09-06T12:30Z stores 2026-09-07'; 'program owned by another principal -> 404, no row'; 'nonexistent programId -> 404 with the identical envelope shape'; 'session_reviews row exists with episode_count SQL NULL (JSON null, never 0) and all-null observed_conditions with accommodations []'; 'revision with effective_day 8 is not used for a Day 7 start and is used for a Day 8 start'; 'practice started on Day 14 has kind practice and slot_id null'; 'two concurrent starts with different keys (Promise.all of two injects) -> exactly one 201 and one 409 active_session_exists, one row (unique-index backstop)'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/unit/session.start.test.ts test/sessions/start.practice.test.ts  -> vitest reports all 34 named cases passed (7 unit + 27 integration), 0 failed; npm run typecheck exits 0.
- **After:** 1.2, 1.4, 1.5, 2.1, 2.7, 3.2, 3.2.1, 3.3, 3.4, 4.1.1, 5.1.3, 5.2.1
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Practice requires a short intended output / Start without output · practice-sessions: Practice requires a short intended output / Hold the target · practice-sessions: One unfinished session per user / Start while awaiting review · practice-sessions: Practice metrics stay separate from benchmarks / Practice on Day 14 · benchmark-assessment: Benchmark ready state / Practice can never become a baseline · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Records created in demo mode · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Client cannot choose the realm · identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode / Skip to Day 14 in demo mode · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner / Cross-program references are rejected

### 5.1.2 — Implement POST /sessions benchmark start with slot, date and replacement rules

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add apps/api/src/services/slotAttempts.ts exporting the pure checkSlotStart({ todayLocalDate, slotAssignedLocalDate, attempts: [{ eligible: boolean | null, lifecycle, excludedByAmendment: boolean }], replacementReason }) -> { ok: true, isReplacement } | { ok: false, reason: 'before_slot_date' | 'slot_full' | 'replacement_reason_required' | 'eligible_attempt_not_retaken' } — these four reason strings ARE the D19 422 wire codes, so the route wraps a failure directly as new DomainError(reason, message) with no separate translation table — and the pure defaultConditionsFromSlot(slot) -> ObservedConditions. Extend startSession (5.1.1) with the benchmark branch, keeping 5.1.1's check order (contract 400 -> idempotency -> ownership 404 -> active-session 409 -> domain 422): slotId missing on a benchmark body -> 422 benchmark_only_field; intendedOutput on a benchmark body -> 422 practice_only_field (mirror of slotId-on-practice); load benchmark_slots joined to programs WHERE benchmark_slots.id = slotId AND benchmark_slots.program_id = body.programId AND programs.user_id = principalId — any miss -> 404, nothing written; inside the transaction SELECT ... FOR UPDATE on the slot row; collect focus_sessions WHERE slot_id = slot.id across all lifecycles (abandoned included) with each row's eligible and whether a session_amendments row with exclude_from_report = true exists; apply checkSlotStart with todayLocalDate from calendar.ts: today before assigned_local_date -> 422 before_slot_date; two attempts -> 422 slot_full; second attempt without replacementReason -> 422 replacement_reason_required; second attempt when the first has eligible = true and no excluding amendment -> 422 eligible_attempt_not_retaken; a first attempt needs no reason even when late (D23: a benchmark may start on or after its assigned date, never before; a late first start needs no reason and is recorded with the actual local_date, and eligibility labels it timing_deviation, 5.8); a replacementReason supplied on a first attempt is accepted (not an error) but is not stored — only the second, replacement attempt has a reason to record (decision, not covered by D16-D40; see notes). target_seconds = 1200 always; a body targetSeconds other than 1200 -> 422 benchmark_only_field (benchmark duration never follows progression, whatever the governing revision's practiceTargetSeconds says). The session_reviews row's observed_conditions = body.conditions ?? defaultConditionsFromSlot(slot) = { deviceFormat: slot.device_format, language: slot.language, materialLevel: slot.material_level, accommodations: [] } (D31). Store replacement_reason on the second attempt only. Set benchmark_slots.frozen_at = ctx.now when null — 5.1.2 is the sole writer of this column, firing at the first attempt; 4.3.2's isFrozen check also inspects live focus_sessions rows independently, so the two units agree without reading each other's writes in the same transaction. Realm, time_source (ctx.timeSource, D8), local_date, revision_id, the active-session 409, idempotent replay (200) / first-write (201, D21) and the D20 serialized body behave exactly as in 5.1.1.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/slotAttempts.test.ts: 'no attempts on the assigned date -> ok, not a replacement'; 'today before the assigned date -> before_slot_date'; 'today after the assigned date with no attempts -> ok (late start recorded, not refused, D23)'; 'replacementReason on a first attempt -> ok, isReplacement false'; 'one ineligible attempt + reason -> ok, isReplacement'; 'one ineligible attempt without reason -> replacement_reason_required'; 'one eligible attempt + reason -> eligible_attempt_not_retaken'; 'one eligible attempt with an excluding amendment + reason -> ok'; 'one abandoned attempt (eligible null) + reason -> ok'; 'two attempts -> slot_full even with a reason'; 'defaultConditionsFromSlot copies device_format, language, material_level and sets accommodations []; a body conditions object replaces the default as a whole'; 'checkSlotStart's four failure reasons equal the D19 wire codes verbatim (before_slot_date, slot_full, replacement_reason_required, eligible_attempt_not_retaken)'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/start.benchmark.test.ts: 'slot A on its assigned date -> 201, kind benchmark, target_seconds 1200, slot_id set, benchmark_slots.frozen_at now set'; 'observed_conditions defaulted from the slot with accommodations []'; 'body conditions with accommodations [increased_font_size] stored on the review'; 'slot assigned tomorrow -> 422 before_slot_date, no row, frozen_at still null'; 'baseline B started on Day 1 with no attempts -> 201 without a reason, local_date = the Day 1 date, eligible null (D23: late start recorded, timing_deviation is 5.8's job)'; 'final A started the day after Day 14 -> 201 with local_date = the Day 15 date; seeded earlier sessions and reviews unchanged'; 'slotId missing on a benchmark body -> 422 benchmark_only_field'; 'intendedOutput on a benchmark body -> 422 practice_only_field'; 'targetSeconds 1500 -> 422 benchmark_only_field; omitted -> 1200; a governing revision with practiceTargetSeconds 900 still yields 1200'; 'second attempt without replacementReason -> 422 replacement_reason_required'; 'second attempt after a seeded finalized attempt with eligible false and exclusion_reasons [materially_disrupted] + reason -> 201, both rows present, replacement_reason stored on the second only'; 'second attempt after a seeded abandoned first attempt + reason -> 201'; 'second attempt when the first is eligible true -> 422 eligible_attempt_not_retaken, still one row'; 'second attempt when the first is eligible true but has an excluding amendment -> 201'; 'third attempt -> 422 slot_full, count stays 2'; 'slot in another principal's program -> 404, no row'; 'slotId from a different program of the same principal -> 404'; 'a seeded practice session on the slot date does not count as a slot attempt'; 'benchmark start while a practice session is running -> 409 active_session_exists before any slot rule'; 'idempotent replay -> 200, returns the same benchmark session id and adds no second attempt'; 'replacementReason supplied on a first attempt -> 201, replacement_reason column stays null (accepted but not stored)'.

### 5.2.2 — Implement GET /sessions/{id} with derived timing, tallies and ownership 404

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/sessions/read.ts plus readSession(ctx, id) in apps/api/src/services/session.ts. Query focus_sessions WHERE id = :id AND user_id = principalId; nonexistent and not-owned both return the identical 404 envelope (existence is not disclosed). Load session_events ordered by elapsed_ms, received_at (voided rows included, each with voidedAt), the session_reviews row, agent_plans (null when absent), and session_amendments ordered by created_at (empty array when none). Respond 200 with serializeSession(row, review, events, plan, amendments, ctx.now) from 5.1.1 — the D20 shape: stored columns camelCase, serverNow (ISO), timing { elapsedSeconds, remainingSeconds, deadlineReached, isPaused } from deriveTiming, tallies { offTask, external, agentChecks } from deriveTallies, eventCount (all stored rows, voided included), events[], review (ReportedCount columns as JSON null, never 0), agentPlan, amendments[]. GET is strictly read-only (D24): it never changes lifecycle even when deadlineReached is true and performs no writes to any table — the server only confirms a reached deadline on the next end transition (5.4.2). Cache-Control: no-store comes from the 3.2.2 plugin. This unit also owns the serializer's unit tests.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/serializeSession.test.ts: 'ReportedCount null survives serialization as null (episodeCount, externalCount, unplannedAgentChecks, mindWanderingCount, recallScore)'; 'voided events keep voidedAt'; 'timing and tallies are present for every lifecycle value'; 'serverNow equals now.toISOString()'; 'eventCount counts every stored row including voided ones and differs from tallies, which exclude voided rows'; 'amendments defaults to [] and passes seeded rows through unchanged'; 'no key of the serialized object equals offTask + agentChecks or offTask + external, and no key is named like focusedSeconds or score (no invented aggregate; elapsed is never presented as focused time)'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/read.test.ts: 'owner GET -> 200 with serverNow, timing, tallies, eventCount 0, events, review, agentPlan null, amendments []'; 'session seeded with user_id someone-else -> 404 with no session fields in the body'; 'unknown id -> 404 with the same envelope shape'; 'practice 900 s with demo offset +360 s -> remainingSeconds 540, elapsedSeconds 360 (refresh during practice)'; 'demo offset +1000 s -> remainingSeconds 0, deadlineReached true, lifecycle still running, review.finalizedAt null, eligible null (timer expiry never proves completion, D24)'; 'benchmark hidden 15 minutes: offset +900 s on a 1200 s benchmark with no events -> remainingSeconds 300 and every tally 0'; 'seeded off_task, agent_check{alsoOffTask:true}, agent_check{alsoOffTask:false}, external -> tallies { offTask 2, external 1, agentChecks 2 }, eventCount 4, and the body has no total key'; 'voided event is listed with voidedAt and excluded from tallies but included in eventCount'; 'review.episodeCount is JSON null for a never-finalized session'; 'seeded paused session -> isPaused true and remainingSeconds identical after advancing the offset 120 s'; 'seeded amendment on a finalized session -> amendments[] contains it with reason and excludeFromReport, and the session's stored eligible/exclusionReasons are unchanged (D32: amendments never rewrite stored fields)'; 'response carries Cache-Control: no-store'; 'seeded finalized session is readable with its review values intact'; 'a GET against a session past its deadline makes zero writes: SELECT version before and after is unchanged'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/unit/serializeSession.test.ts test/sessions/read.test.ts  -> 21 named cases passed (7 unit + 14 integration), 0 failed; the tally-rule case's response JSON contains "offTask":2,"external":1,"agentChecks":2,"eventCount":4.
- **After:** 5.1.1, 5.1.3, 5.2.1, 2.7, 3.2, 4.1.1

- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/sessions/active.ts: select the single focus_sessions row WHERE user_id = principalId AND lifecycle IN ('running', 'paused', 'awaiting_review') (at most one by the 3.3 partial unique index); none -> 204 with an empty body; found -> 200 with serializeSession loaded exactly as 5.2.2's readSession (same events/review/agentPlan/amendments loading), so the D20 shape — including serverNow, eventCount and amendments[] — is identical to GET /sessions/{id} and the client's reload path renders it the same way. Register the route before /:id so the literal 'active' is never parsed as an id. Strictly read-only (D24); no-store header from 3.2.2.

### 5.3.1 — Implement POST /sessions/{id}/events batch with dedupe, offset check and late-event warning

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/sessions/events.ts plus appendEvents(ctx, sessionId, events) in apps/api/src/services/session.ts and pure helpers in apps/api/src/services/sessionEvents.ts (partitionBatch, findImpossibleOffsets, withReconciliationWarning, EVENT_OFFSET_TOLERANCE_MS = 5000 — 5 seconds; D16–D40 fixes no exact value, this unit decides it and records it in notes/LIMITATIONS.md). Contract (2.7): events is an array of 1..100 { clientEventId uuid, type off_task | external | agent_check | clock_gap | visibility, elapsedMs integer >= 0, occurredAt date-time, details? object }; pause and resume are not in the client enum (400 from the contract) — those rows are written only by the transitions route (5.4.2); agent_check requires details.alsoOffTask boolean (domain 422 fieldErrors naming the clientEventId); clock_gap requires details.gapSeconds (number) and accepts an optional details.resolution: 'continued' | 'uncertain' (D26) — an event stored without resolution is the unresolved/open state (the field is simply absent, never null), and a later batch may re-post the same-shaped event with resolution now present (a fresh clientEventId; events are append-only, D9). Session must be owned (404). Compute deriveTiming(session, ctx.now).elapsedSeconds (measured to ended_at for ended sessions); any event with elapsedMs > elapsedSeconds * 1000 + EVENT_OFFSET_TOLERANCE_MS rejects the whole batch with 422 impossible_offset whose fieldErrors names every offending clientEventId, and nothing is stored. Insert with ON CONFLICT (session_id, client_event_id) DO NOTHING; ids already stored or repeated within the batch go to duplicates[], inserted rows (serialized) to accepted[]; received_at = ctx.now (D8: one clock for every service, including this audit column); voided_at null; session_events has no realm column in the 3.3 schema, so realm scoping relies entirely on the parent focus_sessions row rather than a stamped column (decision, recorded in notes). If the session lifecycle is finalized or abandoned, still store each row with details merged with { reconciliation_warning: true } and never touch session_reviews (counts, finalized_at and version unchanged); running, paused and awaiting_review sessions store rows without the warning (awaiting_review is where finalize's lastBatch lands, D21). The only insert sites into sessionEvents under apps/api/src/routes and apps/api/src/services are services/session.ts (appendEvents here, transitionSession in 5.4.2) — a repository guard test enforces this allowlist (apps/api/src/db/seed is excluded; group 5b's clock-gap/finalize units must extend the allowlist if they write a row). Nothing on the server reacts to visibility (D15/D39).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/sessionEvents.test.ts: partitionBatch — 'a clientEventId repeated within the batch puts the second copy in duplicates'; 'an id already stored goes to duplicates'; 'all-new ids go to accepted'. findImpossibleOffsets — 'elapsedMs equal to elapsed*1000 + 5000 is allowed'; 'one millisecond more is flagged with its clientEventId as impossible_offset'; 'two offenders are both listed'. withReconciliationWarning — 'adds reconciliation_warning true and keeps details.alsoOffTask'. clock_gap shape — 'an event stored without details.resolution round-trips with no resolution key present'; 'an event with resolution "continued" round-trips unchanged'. Repository guard — 'every file under apps/api/src/routes and apps/api/src/services that inserts into sessionEvents is services/session.ts (a hidden tab can never create an episode server-side)'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/events.test.ts: 'one off_task at elapsedMs 462000 -> accepted; GET shows the event with elapsedMs 462000 and tallies.offTask 1 (record after returning)'; 'the same batch sent twice -> second response accepted [] and duplicates listing every id; SELECT count(*) unchanged between calls (batch retried)'; 'a batch with an internal duplicate id -> one row, one duplicate'; 'an offset beyond elapsed + 5000 ms -> 422 impossible_offset naming the clientEventId and zero rows stored, including the valid events of that batch'; '101 events -> 400; 100 events -> 200'; 'agent_check without details.alsoOffTask -> 422'; 'type pause in a client batch -> 400 from the contract'; 'one off_task after five app visits -> exactly one row and tallies.offTask 1 (one departure, five apps)'; 'a visibility event -> accepted and stored, tallies all still zero (hidden page creates nothing)'; 'a clock_gap event with details {gapSeconds: 90} and no resolution -> accepted, GET shows the event with no resolution key (unresolved, D26)'; 'a clock_gap event with details {gapSeconds: 90, resolution: "continued"} -> accepted and stored with resolution present'; 'clock_gap without details.gapSeconds -> 422'; 'another principal's session -> 404, nothing stored'; 'seeded finalized session (review episode_count 3, finalized_at set, version 1): a late off_task -> accepted with details.reconciliation_warning true; review.episodeCount still 3, review.version still 1, finalizedAt unchanged (late event)'; 'events on an awaiting_review session -> accepted without reconciliation_warning (last batch before finalize)'; 'events on a seeded abandoned session -> stored with reconciliation_warning true'; 'stored rows join to a focus_sessions row with realm demo (no session_events.realm column exists)'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/unit/sessionEvents.test.ts test/sessions/events.test.ts  -> 27 named cases passed (10 unit + 17 integration), 0 failed; the retried-batch case asserts SELECT count(*) FROM session_events WHERE session_id = <id> is unchanged between the two calls.
- **After:** 5.1.1, 5.1.3, 5.2.1, 5.2.2, 2.7, 3.3, 4.1.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Batch retried · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Impossible offset · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Late event · benchmark-assessment: Interruption recording during the benchmark / Record after returning · benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / One departure, five apps · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Hidden page creates nothing · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Records created in demo mode

### 5.3.2 — Implement POST /sessions/{id}/events/{clientEventId}/void as an undo marker

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/sessions/void.ts plus voidEvent(ctx, sessionId, clientEventId) in services/session.ts and the pure canVoid(lifecycle) / isVoidableType(type) in services/sessionEvents.ts: owned session (404); event row by (session_id, client_event_id) (404); lifecycle finalized -> 409 already_finalized with voided_at untouched (D9: finalized attempts are immutable; abandoned sessions remain voidable, so this code names the specific finalized-immutability rule rather than a general 'not active' state); server-written pause and resume rows -> 422 invalid_transition (voiding a transition-record would falsify pausedSeconds while marking the row undone; D19 names no dedicated code for this, so this unit reuses invalid_transition — recorded as a decision in notes); already voided -> 200 returning the row unchanged (idempotent); otherwise set voided_at = ctx.now and return the serialized event. Rows are never deleted (D9). Undo surfaces only through 5.2.1's voided-row exclusion in the tallies; no count column is edited; eventCount in the D20 response is unaffected by voiding, since it counts every stored row regardless of voidedAt.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/sessionEvents.test.ts (append): canVoid — 'finalized -> refused (already_finalized)'; 'running, paused, awaiting_review and abandoned -> allowed'. isVoidableType — 'off_task, external, agent_check, clock_gap, visibility -> true; pause and resume -> false'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/void.test.ts: 'record off_task twice, void the second -> GET tallies.offTask 1 and eventCount 2, both rows still present, one with voidedAt (undo accidental duplicate)'; 'void on a seeded finalized session -> 409 already_finalized and voided_at still null'; 'void an unknown clientEventId -> 404'; 'void on another principal's session -> 404'; 'void the same event twice -> second call 200 with the same voidedAt'; 'void an agent_check with alsoOffTask true -> offTask and agentChecks each drop by exactly one'; 'void on an awaiting_review session -> 200 (unfinalized events stay undoable)'; 'void a seeded pause row -> 422 invalid_transition, voided_at null'; 'void on a seeded abandoned session -> 200 (abandoned attempts stay editable at the event level, only finalized ones freeze)'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/void.test.ts test/unit/sessionEvents.test.ts  -> the 9 void integration cases and the 4 appended unit cases pass alongside 5.3.1's 10 unit cases, 0 failed; the duplicate case ends with SELECT count(*) FROM session_events WHERE session_id = <id> = 2 and count(*) WHERE voided_at IS NULL = 1.
- **After:** 5.3.1, 5.2.2
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Interruption recording during the benchmark / Undo accidental duplicate · benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes

### 5.4.1 — Implement pure session transition table and paused-seconds accounting

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/sessionTransitions.ts (pure, no DB import) exporting applyTransition(session: { kind, lifecycle, startedAt, targetSeconds, pausedSeconds, currentPauseStartedAt, endedAt, completeInterval }, type: 'pause' | 'resume' | 'end' | 'abandon', now) -> { ok: true, patch: { lifecycle, pausedSeconds, currentPauseStartedAt, endedAt, completeInterval }, eventRow?: { type: 'pause' | 'resume', elapsedMs } } | { ok: false, reason: 'invalid_for_kind' | 'invalid_from_state' }, implementing design.md's session lifecycle: running --pause--> paused (practice only; benchmark -> invalid_for_kind, likewise resume on a benchmark); paused --resume--> running with pausedSeconds += now - currentPauseStartedAt and the pause cleared; running | paused --end--> awaiting_review (an open pause is closed and added first; endedAt = now; completeInterval = deriveTiming(...).elapsedSeconds >= targetSeconds, i.e. unpaused elapsed, same rule for practice and benchmark; this is also how 'target reached' lands — the client sends end at the deadline and the server confirms, D5/D24); running | paused | awaiting_review --abandon--> abandoned (endedAt = now when null; open pause closed; completeInterval untouched; eligible untouched; paused -> abandoned is drawn as allowed here even though design.md's ASCII diagram only shows Running|AwaitingReview --abandon-> abandoned, because D29 states plainly that abandon is always available); every other pair -> invalid_from_state (anything from finalized or abandoned, resume while running, end from awaiting_review). Reason handling (D29): a reason string, when present, is written only into the eventRow for a pause transition (details.reason, e.g. 'planned_break'); resume, end and abandon produce no reason in this pure function's output at all — the route (5.4.2) is what receives and discards a body.reason on end/abandon. The function never yields lifecycle finalized and never touches review columns. eventRow.elapsedMs = unpaused elapsed at now so stored pause/resume rows line up with client events.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/sessionTransitions.test.ts, table-driven over 4 types x 5 lifecycles x 2 kinds (40 rows asserting ok/reason) plus named cases: 'practice running pause -> paused with a pause eventRow'; 'benchmark running pause -> invalid_for_kind'; 'benchmark paused resume -> invalid_for_kind'; 'resume after 240 s -> pausedSeconds 240, pause cleared, resume eventRow'; 'end during a pause closes the open pause then ends'; 'practice end at 480 s of 900 -> completeInterval false, endedAt now (early finish)'; 'benchmark end at 840 s -> completeInterval false (stop early)'; 'end at wall 1140 s with 240 s paused on a 900 s target -> completeInterval true (paused seconds excluded)'; 'benchmark end at wall 1200 s -> completeInterval true and lifecycle awaiting_review, never finalized (interval reached)'; 'abandon from running, paused and awaiting_review -> abandoned with completeInterval unchanged, the open pause closed and no eligibility set (D29: abandon is always available, including from paused)'; 'every transition from finalized -> invalid_from_state'; 'every transition from abandoned -> invalid_from_state'; 'end from awaiting_review -> invalid_from_state'; 'resume while running -> invalid_from_state'; 'pause eventRow.elapsedMs equals unpaused elapsed at now'; 'a reason passed alongside end or abandon never reaches the returned patch or an eventRow (reason is pause-only, D29)'.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.4.2
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/unit/sessionTransitions.test.ts  -> the 40-row matrix plus 16 named cases pass, 0 failed.
- **After:** 5.2.1, 1.5
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Pause and resume are explicit / Paused seconds excluded · practice-sessions: Pause and resume are explicit / Planned break · practice-sessions: Practice timer and elapsed time / Target reached · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Interval reached · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available

### 5.4.2 — Implement POST /sessions/{id}/transitions with expected version and pause events

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/sessions/transitions.ts plus transitionSession(ctx, id, body) in services/session.ts. Body { expectedVersion, type: pause | resume | end | abandon, reason? } (2.7). Check order: owned session (404) -> in one transaction SELECT ... FOR UPDATE -> if row.lifecycle is 'finalized' or 'abandoned', 409 session_not_active immediately, before calling applyTransition (both are terminal; the session simply cannot transition again) -> row.version !== expectedVersion -> 409 stale_version carrying details.current = the serialized session (D18: the current state is returned so a client can reconcile without a second GET) -> applyTransition (5.4.1): any invalid_for_kind or invalid_from_state failure -> 422 invalid_transition (both reasons collapse to this one D19 code once the terminal-state case above has already been carved out) -> UPDATE focus_sessions SET <patch>, version = version + 1 WHERE id = :id AND version = :expectedVersion (zero rows updated is the same 409 stale_version, the race backstop) -> on pause and resume insert one session_events row { client_event_id: randomUUID(), type 'pause' | 'resume', elapsed_ms from eventRow, occurred_at = received_at = ctx.now, details: { reason: body.reason ?? null } } so paused intervals are stored (a planned break is a pause with reason 'planned_break', D29) -> respond 200 with serializeSession (5.1.1, D20 shape including serverNow, eventCount, amendments[]). reason on end/abandon is accepted by the 2.7 contract but is discarded — it never reaches a stored column or an event row (D29: reason is pause-only). Only this route (and group 5b's clock-gap save_incomplete and finalize) changes lifecycle; GET stays read-only (D24). This is the only insert into sessionEvents besides appendEvents (5.3.1 guard).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/sessionTransitions.test.ts (append): buildPauseEventRow — 'produces a uuid client_event_id, occurred_at = received_at = now and details.reason from the body or null'.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/sessions/transitions.test.ts: 'benchmark pause -> 422 invalid_transition, lifecycle running, version unchanged, no pause event'; 'benchmark resume -> 422 invalid_transition'; 'practice pause with reason planned_break -> 200 paused, version 2, one pause event with details.reason planned_break; GET timing.isPaused true and remainingSeconds identical after +120 s offset (planned break)'; '240 paused seconds: start a 900 s practice, pause at +300 s, resume at +540 s -> pausedSeconds 240; GET at +1140 s wall -> elapsedSeconds 900, deadlineReached true; end -> awaiting_review, completeInterval true, endedAt = start + 1140 s (paused seconds excluded)'; 'end at +480 s of 900 -> awaiting_review, completeInterval false, timing.elapsedSeconds 480 (early finish)'; 'benchmark end at +840 s -> awaiting_review, completeInterval false, exactly one focus_sessions row for the slot and GET /sessions/active returns it as awaiting_review (stop early never starts a new attempt)'; 'benchmark end at +1200 s -> completeInterval true, review.finalizedAt null, eligible null (interval reached, nothing finalized)'; 'practice end at +900 s of 900 -> awaiting_review, completeInterval true, review.finalizedAt null (target reached, no completion recorded)'; 'end during a pause -> awaiting_review with the open pause counted in pausedSeconds'; 'two end requests with the same expectedVersion -> first 200 with version 2, second 409 stale_version carrying details.current with lifecycle awaiting_review and version 2; endedAt unchanged and version still 2 (two tabs end a session)'; 'stale expectedVersion on pause -> 409 stale_version, lifecycle unchanged'; 'abandon from running -> abandoned, endedAt set, eligible null, review.finalizedAt null, still readable by GET /sessions/{id}; a following POST /sessions -> 201'; 'abandon from paused -> abandoned with the open pause closed into pausedSeconds (D29: paused -> abandoned allowed)'; 'abandon from awaiting_review -> abandoned'; 'transition on a seeded finalized session -> 409 session_not_active'; 'transition on a seeded abandoned session -> 409 session_not_active'; 'transition on another principal's session -> 404'; 'resume while running -> 422 invalid_transition (not a terminal-state 409, since the session is still active)'; 'pause and resume rows appear in events[] but never in tallies, and both add to eventCount'; 'reason sent on an end body is accepted by the contract and discarded: no session_events row is written for the end and no column stores it'; 'version increments by exactly one per successful transition (pause 2, resume 3, end 4)'.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/transitions.test.ts test/unit/sessionTransitions.test.ts  -> the 21 integration cases and the appended unit case pass alongside 5.4.1's matrix, 0 failed; the 240-second case's final response JSON contains "pausedSeconds":240 and "completeInterval":true with "lifecycle":"awaiting_review".
- **After:** 5.1.1, 5.1.3, 5.4.1, 5.2.2, 5.2.3, 2.7

### 5.5.1 — Clock-gap route: continued, uncertain and save_incomplete resolutions

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap` to `apps/api/src/routes/sessions.ts` and `resolveClockGap(ctx, sessionId, body)` in `apps/api/src/services/session.ts`, contract from 2.7 (`{ gapSeconds: integer >= 0, resolution: 'continued'|'uncertain'|'save_incomplete' }` → session in the 5.2 shape, `additionalProperties: false`). One transaction with `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on `focus_sessions`. Ownership `focus_sessions.user_id = ctx.principalId` else 404 `not_found`. Lifecycle must be `running|paused|awaiting_review`; `finalized|abandoned` → 409 `session_not_active` (D19). Common to all three resolutions: `clock_gap_seconds` becomes `gapSeconds` when the column is NULL (no gap ever reported) and `clock_gap_seconds + gapSeconds` otherwise — an explicit NULL branch, never `?? 0` / `COALESCE(...,0)` (D26, keeps the 2.9 guard clean); `version + 1`. `continued`: `timer_quality` unchanged (stays `ok`; an earlier `uncertain` is never reset back). `uncertain`: `timer_quality = 'uncertain'`. `save_incomplete` (D25): `timer_quality = 'uncertain'` and end the session through the same code path as 5.4's `end` transition (extract 5.4's end logic into an internal `endSession(tx, session, now)` helper in `session.ts` if 5.4 did not already): fold an open pause into `paused_seconds`, clear `current_pause_started_at`, `ended_at = ctx.now`, `lifecycle = 'awaiting_review'`, `complete_interval = false` always — even when elapsed >= target_seconds, because timer expiry never proves completion; save_incomplete never finalizes by itself (finalize remains a separate, later call); if the session is already `awaiting_review`, only the flags change and `ended_at` is preserved. `continued` and `uncertain` never change lifecycle: a running session whose target passed during the gap stays `running` until the client posts `end` (D5). Nothing here writes `session_reviews`, `finalized_at`, `eligible` or `exclusion_reasons` (finalize is the only writer of derived fields, D4). No `session_events` row is inserted here: the client posts its own `clock_gap` event through 5.3 (D26). Applies to both kinds; a practice session only carries the flag. Create or extend `apps/api/test/helpers/sessions.ts` (`seedProgramWithSlots`, `startSession`, `seedStartedAt`, `endSession`) for this and later units.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/clock-gap.unit.test.ts` (fake `ctx.now`, tx double): `continued keeps timer_quality ok and records gap`; `uncertain sets timer_quality uncertain`; `continued after an earlier uncertain does not reset to ok`; `second gap accumulates clock_gap_seconds`; `NULL clock_gap_seconds becomes gapSeconds without coalescing`; `save_incomplete on running sets uncertain, ends now, complete_interval false`; `save_incomplete during pause folds the open pause into paused_seconds`; `save_incomplete on awaiting_review keeps ended_at and sets the flags`; `elapsed beyond target still yields complete_interval false`; `negative gapSeconds rejected by contract`; `unknown resolution rejected by contract`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/clock-gap.test.ts` (inject, real `attention_lab_test`): `benchmark running + continued 300 → 200, timer_quality ok, clock_gap_seconds 300, version+1, lifecycle running, eligible null`; `benchmark running + uncertain → timer_quality uncertain, lifecycle running, finalized_at null`; `practice paused + uncertain → timer_quality uncertain only: lifecycle paused, eligible null, exclusion_reasons empty, session_events count unchanged`; `benchmark running whose target passed during the gap (started_at seeded 25 min before ctx.now) + continued 600 → still running, no session_reviews.finalized_at; the following end transition (5.4) yields awaiting_review with complete_interval true and lifecycle still not finalized`; `benchmark running at 14 min → save_incomplete → 200 awaiting_review, ended_at = ctx.now, complete_interval false, timer_quality uncertain, clock_gap_seconds recorded, no session_reviews.finalized_at`; `practice paused for 240 s → save_incomplete → awaiting_review, paused_seconds 240, current_pause_started_at null`; `benchmark running with started_at seeded 25 min ago → save_incomplete → complete_interval false`; `benchmark already awaiting_review → save_incomplete → ended_at unchanged, timer_quality uncertain`; `GET /sessions/{id} after save_incomplete shows lifecycle awaiting_review and timerQuality uncertain`; `finalized session → 409 session_not_active`; `abandoned session → 409 session_not_active`; `another principal's session → 404`; `extra body field → 400`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/clock-gap.test.ts test/services/clock-gap.unit.test.ts` → 13 integration cases and 11 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 2.7, 3.2, 3.3, 5.1, 5.2, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Laptop slept during a benchmark · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Deadline passed during sleep · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available / Save incomplete after uncertainty · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Interval reached · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early

### 5.6.1 — Agent-plan PUT: practice only, upsert with optimistic version

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `PUT /sessions/{id}/agent-plan` to `apps/api/src/routes/sessions.ts` and `putAgentPlan(ctx, sessionId, body)` in `apps/api/src/services/session.ts`, contract from 2.7 (`{ expectedVersion, workstream?, waitingTask?, reviewCheckpoint?, resumeNote? }`, `additionalProperties: false` so credential/output/log fields are 400 `malformed_request`). Ownership 404 `not_found`. `focus_sessions.kind = 'benchmark'` → 422 `practice_only` (D19). Lifecycle not in `running|paused|awaiting_review` → 409 `session_not_active`. Upsert on `agent_plans.session_id` (PK) in one transaction: no row → `expectedVersion` must be 0 (else 409 `stale_version`), insert with `version = 1` and `review_checkpoint` defaulting to `'end_of_block'` when omitted; row exists → `UPDATE agent_plans SET <provided fields>, version = version + 1 WHERE session_id = ? AND version = expectedVersion`; zero rows affected → 409 `stale_version` whose body carries `details: { current: <the stored plan> }` per D18 — the same envelope shape 5.4 uses for a stale session transition. Unspecified fields keep their stored values. `review_at` is untouched (no contract field). The `focus_sessions` row is never modified (its `version`, lifecycle and events are unchanged: the session continues uninterrupted). Response `{ sessionId, workstream, waitingTask, reviewCheckpoint, resumeNote, version }`. `GET /sessions/{id}` (5.2) already embeds the plan.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/agent-plan.unit.test.ts`: `insert path requires expectedVersion 0`; `default review_checkpoint end_of_block`; `partial update keeps unspecified fields`; `benchmark kind rejected`; `stale version returns details.current with the stored plan`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/agent-plan.test.ts`: `first PUT with expectedVersion 0 → 200, version 1, reviewCheckpoint 'end_of_block'`; `second PUT expectedVersion 1 changing waitingTask → version 2, workstream preserved`; `two tabs both send expectedVersion 1: first 200, second 409 stale_version whose details.current equals the first write; DB holds the first`; `expectedVersion 0 when a row already exists → 409 stale_version`; `benchmark session → 422 practice_only`; `finalized practice → 409 session_not_active`; `body with credentials/agentOutput/log fields → 400`; `other principal → 404`; `focus_sessions.version, lifecycle and session_events count unchanged after plan writes`; `GET /sessions/{id} returns the stored plan`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/agent-plan.test.ts test/services/agent-plan.unit.test.ts` → 10 integration cases and 5 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 2.7, 3.2, 3.3, 5.1, 5.2, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Optional agent waiting plan / Save a plan mid-session · practice-sessions: Optional agent waiting plan / Stale plan write · session-recovery: Stale writes conflict instead of overwriting

### 5.7.2 — Recall lock route: idempotent, benchmark-only, delay and overrun flags

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `POST /sessions/{id}/recall` (Idempotency-Key header required, UUID, else 400 `malformed_request`) to `apps/api/src/routes/sessions.ts` and `lockRecall(ctx, sessionId, body, idempotencyKey)` in `apps/api/src/services/review.ts`, contract from 2.7 (`{ points: string[5], startedAt: ISO datetime, durationSeconds: integer >= 0 }` → review). Per D27, `startedAt` is sent in server-aligned time (the client's `serverNowMs`-derived clock, 7.2.1, which already carries the demo offset). The shared `recallDelaySeconds(endedAt, startedAt)` (2.3.2) throws when `startedAt < ended_at`; the route catches that and returns 422 `recall_before_interval_end`. The route additionally rejects `startedAt > ctx.now` with the same code before calling it. Checks in order: ownership 404 `not_found`; `kind !== 'benchmark'` → 422 `benchmark_only`; lifecycle `running|paused` → 409 `interval_not_ended`; `finalized|abandoned` → 409 `session_not_active`; then the `startedAt` range check above. Idempotency via the 3.4 helper (`operation = 'recall'`, `result_ref = session_id`): same key + same hash → replay the stored review; same key + different hash → 409 `idempotency_mismatch`. State-based lock independent of the key: if `session_reviews.recall_locked_at` is already set, compare stored `recall_points` with body points — equal → 200 with the stored review (timestamps and flags untouched), different → 409 `recall_locked`. Otherwise, in one transaction update the `session_reviews` row created at start (5.1, D31) setting `recall_points` (5 strings, blanks preserved as ''), `recall_started_at = startedAt`, `recall_locked_at = ctx.now`, `recall_delay_seconds = recallDelaySeconds(ended_at, startedAt)` (2.3.2, whole seconds, floor), `recall_duration_seconds = durationSeconds`, `recall_flags = deriveRecallFlags({ delaySeconds, durationSeconds })` (2.3.2 — imports `RECALL_DELAY_FLAG_SECONDS`/`RECALL_OVERRUN_FLAG_SECONDS`/`recallDelaySeconds`/`deriveRecallFlags` from `packages/shared/src/domain/recall.ts`, D16), bump `session_reviews.version`, write the receipt. Early-stopped benchmarks (`complete_interval = false`) accept recall too — recall on an incomplete benchmark is allowed (D25) and remains optional at finalize (5.8.3). Point text is never logged (3.6 harness). The finalize contract has no points field, so no later request can change the texts (asserted in 5.8.3). Extend `apps/api/test/helpers/sessions.ts` with `lockRecall`.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/recall.unit.test.ts` (fake `ctx.now`): `delay computed via the shared recallDelaySeconds in whole seconds`; `same points after lock → replay without touching recall_locked_at`; `different points after lock → RecallLockedError`; `flags come from the shared deriveRecallFlags (2.3.2)`; `practice kind → BenchmarkOnlyError`; `startedAt before ended_at → recall_before_interval_end (delegates to recallDelaySeconds' throw)`; `startedAt after ctx.now → recall_before_interval_end`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/recall.test.ts`: `benchmark ended 40 s ago → 200, recall_delay_seconds 40, recall_flags [], recall_locked_at = ctx.now`; `ended 1500 s ago → 200 with recall_flags ['recall_delayed'] (deviation, not rejection)`; `durationSeconds 230 → ['recall_overrun']`; `same key + same body → 200 identical review, one session_reviews row, recall_locked_at unchanged`; `new key + same points → 200 unchanged`; `new key + different point 3 → 409 recall_locked and stored points unchanged`; `same key + different body → 409 idempotency_mismatch`; `practice awaiting_review → 422 benchmark_only`; `benchmark still running → 409 interval_not_ended`; `finalized → 409 session_not_active`; `missing Idempotency-Key → 400`; `points array of length 4 → 400`; `two blank points → 200 with '' preserved`; `early-stopped benchmark (complete_interval false) → 200 (recall on an incomplete benchmark is allowed, D25)`; `startedAt before ended_at → 422 recall_before_interval_end`; `startedAt after ctx.now → 422 recall_before_interval_end`; `GET /sessions/{id} shows recallPoints, recallFlags, recallLockedAt, recallDelaySeconds`; `other principal → 404`; `log capture (3.6 harness): point texts absent from Pino output for the 409 recall_locked response`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/recall.test.ts test/services/recall.unit.test.ts` → 19 integration cases and 7 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 2.3.2, 2.7, 3.2, 3.3, 3.4, 3.6.1, 5.1, 5.2, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall started promptly · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall started after a long break · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall text is immutable after save · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early

### 5.8.1 — Finalize mechanics: idempotency key, expected event count, last batch, atomic commit

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `POST /sessions/{id}/finalize` (Idempotency-Key required, UUID, else 400 `malformed_request`) to `apps/api/src/routes/sessions.ts` and `finalizeSession(ctx, sessionId, body, idempotencyKey)` in `apps/api/src/services/review.ts`, contract from 2.7 (`{ expectedEventCount, lastBatch?, review: {...} }` → `{ session, review, eligible, exclusionReasons[] }`, `additionalProperties: false` so `eligible`/`exclusionReasons`/`points`/`realm` in the body are 400). Checks before any write: ownership 404 `not_found`; lifecycle must be `awaiting_review` — `running|paused` → 409 `session_not_ended` (an expired timer alone never finalizes; the client posts `end` via 5.4), `abandoned` → 409 `session_not_active` (mirrors 5.5.1/5.6.1's convention: a closed-off lifecycle is `session_not_active`, an interval that hasn't ended yet is `session_not_ended`). Already finalized, keyed by the 3.4 receipt (`operation = 'finalize'`, `result_ref = session_id`): same key + same hash → replay, returning the current stored rows as `{ session, review, eligible, exclusionReasons }` (the review is immutable after finalize so this equals the recorded result); same key + different hash → 409 `idempotency_mismatch`; any other key on an already-finalized session → 409 `already_finalized`. Per D21, a receipt is written ONLY on a successful (2xx) commit: every rejection in this unit — the lifecycle 409s above, kind-specific 400/422 from 5.8.2/5.8.3, and the `event_count_mismatch` 409 below — writes no receipt, so the identical Idempotency-Key is accepted again on retry once the underlying problem (e.g. a missing event) is fixed. Kind-specific validation (5.8.2/5.8.3) runs before the transaction so a 400/422 never applies `lastBatch`. Transaction: `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on `focus_sessions`; apply `lastBatch` (≤ 100, through the 5.3 insert with `(session_id, client_event_id)` dedupe and impossible-offset 422); `stored = COUNT(*) FROM session_events WHERE session_id = ?` — per D21 this counts every stored row regardless of type or void state (voided rows included, D9 makes void a marker not a delete, so the client's count of posted events is the stored count; `clock_gap` and `visibility` rows included); `stored !== expectedEventCount` → rollback (the `lastBatch` rows are not retained; they remain in the client outbox for the retry) and 409 `event_count_mismatch` with `details: { expected, stored }` (D18), `retryable: true`; else upsert `session_reviews` from `body.review` column-for-column (absent → NULL, never 0; `observed_conditions` keeps the value already on the row from start, D31, unless the kind-specific branch replaces it), set `session_reviews.finalized_at = ctx.now`, `session_reviews.version + 1`, `focus_sessions.lifecycle = 'finalized'`, `focus_sessions.version + 1`, write the receipt, commit. The error path logs only code/route/request id — never the body (3.6 asserts this). Response carries `Cache-Control: no-store` via 3.2. This unit's tests use practice sessions with `review: { outputQuality: 'yes' }` and assert nothing about `eligible`.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/finalize.unit.test.ts`: `request hash covers expectedEventCount, lastBatch and review`; `mismatch error carries details.expected/details.stored and triggers no receipt write (D21)`; `lastBatch duplicates are counted once`; `non-awaiting_review lifecycle rejected`; `lifecycle gate runs before any lastBatch insert`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/finalize-mechanics.test.ts`: `practice awaiting_review with 4 stored events, expectedEventCount 5 → 409 event_count_mismatch, details {expected:5, stored:4}, no session_reviews.finalized_at, lifecycle still awaiting_review, no mutation_receipts row for this key (D21: rejection writes no receipt)`; `retry the identical key after posting the missing event → 200, one review row, lifecycle finalized, finalized_at set, version+1`; `lastBatch carrying the missing event with expectedEventCount 5 → 200 in one call`; `lastBatch re-sending an already-stored client_event_id → deduped, count unchanged`; `voided event still counts toward stored: 5 rows with 1 voided → expectedEventCount 5 → 200, expectedEventCount 4 → 409`; `replay same key + same body → 200 deep-equal result, one review row, session version unchanged`; `same key + different body → 409 idempotency_mismatch`; `new key on a finalized session → 409 already_finalized`; `running practice → 409 session_not_ended`; `paused practice → 409 session_not_ended`; `benchmark running with started_at seeded 25 min before ctx.now (target passed) → 409 session_not_ended and nothing finalized`; `abandoned → 409 session_not_active`; `missing Idempotency-Key → 400`; `expectedEventCount omitted → 400`; `body containing eligible or exclusionReasons → 400`; `other principal → 404`; `late event after finalize is accepted with reconciliation_warning (5.3) and the session_reviews row is byte-identical before and after`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/finalize-mechanics.test.ts test/services/finalize.unit.test.ts` → 17 integration cases and 5 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 2.7, 3.2, 3.3, 3.4, 3.6.1, 5.1, 5.2, 5.3, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Finalize retried · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Late event · benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Interval reached · practice-sessions: Practice timer and elapsed time / Target reached

### 5.8.2 — Practice finalize semantics: required output quality, nullable counts, no sums

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** In `finalizeSession` (`apps/api/src/services/review.ts`) add the `kind = 'practice'` branch, validated before the transaction: `review.outputQuality` (`yes|partly|no`) required → else 400 `fieldErrors.outputQuality = 'required'`; `outputNote` optional. Counts `episodeCount`, `externalCount`, `unplannedAgentChecks`, `mindWanderingCount` are `ReportedCount`: absent → NULL column, explicit 0 → 0; `countMethod` (`event|retrospective`) required whenever `episodeCount` is present (else 400 `fieldErrors.countMethod`), stored NULL when no count. Off-task and agent-check counts are stored in their own columns and nothing is ever added across them (D11). Benchmark-only fields on a practice body (`recallScores`, `materiallyDisrupted`, `disruptionNote`, `conditions`, `firstSwitchEstimateSeconds`) → 422 `benchmark_only_field` (D19). Practice leaves `focus_sessions.eligible` NULL and `exclusion_reasons` empty, `first_switch_*` NULL, recall columns NULL; `observed_conditions` was already fixed to the empty `ObservedConditions` at session start (D31, 5.1.1) and is never touched here since `conditions` is rejected on a practice body; response `eligible: null`, `exclusionReasons: []`. The 2.7 finalize response schema types `eligible` as `boolean | null` (`Type.Union([Type.Boolean(), Type.Null()])`). `complete_interval`, `ended_at`, `paused_seconds` come from the 5.4 end transition and are not modified. No benchmark slot is ever touched by a practice finalize, and finalizing frees the one-active-session partial unique index so a new start succeeds. `reviewNote` (D31) is not accepted on a practice finalize in this group (it is wired only into 5.8.3's benchmark branch); a practice `session_reviews.review_note` stays NULL.
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/finalize-practice.unit.test.ts`: `absent counts map to null`; `explicit 0 maps to 0`; `outputQuality missing → field error naming outputQuality`; `recallScores on practice → benchmark_only_field`; `countMethod required with episodeCount`; `practice never yields an eligibility value`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/finalize-practice.test.ts`: `Partly: {outputQuality:'partly', episodeCount:1, countMethod:'event'} → 200, output_quality 'partly', lifecycle finalized, eligible null`; `early finish: practice with started_at seeded 480 s before the 5.4 end → finalize → complete_interval false, (ended_at − started_at − paused_seconds) = 480 s, and a new POST /sessions practice start afterwards succeeds (no active_session_exists)`; `blank counts: review without any count → episode_count, external_count, unplanned_agent_checks, mind_wandering_count all NULL in DB and null in the response, count_method NULL`; `explicit 0 for all four → 0 stored and 0 in the response`; `agent_check{alsoOffTask:true} event + body episodeCount 1, unplannedAgentChecks 1, countMethod event → stored 1 and 1, and GET /sessions/{id} tallies expose offTask 1 and agentChecks 1 with no field equal to 2 (D11)`; `episodeCount 4 with countMethod retrospective → stored with method retrospective`; `episodeCount present without countMethod → 400 fieldErrors.countMethod`; `recallScores on practice → 422 benchmark_only_field`; `materiallyDisrupted on practice → 422 benchmark_only_field`; `outputQuality omitted → 400 fieldErrors.outputQuality`; `first_switch_kind and recall_* NULL after practice finalize, observed_conditions still the empty value set at start`; `slot_id stays NULL and benchmark_slots unchanged`; `practice finalized on Day 14 (baseline_date seeded 14 days before ctx.now) → eligible null, final A/B slots unchanged and still without attempts`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/finalize-practice.test.ts test/services/finalize-practice.unit.test.ts` → 13 integration cases and 6 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 5.8.1, 2.7, 5.1, 5.2, 5.3, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Session review saves honest outcomes / Partial output · practice-sessions: Session review saves honest outcomes / Early finish · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Agent check that was also off-task · practice-sessions: Practice metrics stay separate from benchmarks / Practice on Day 14 · practice-sessions: One unfinished session per user · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown

### 5.8.3 — Benchmark finalize gate: recall lock, attestation, recall scoring, conditions

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** In `finalizeSession` add the `kind = 'benchmark'` branch, validated before the transaction (so a rejection applies no `lastBatch`), reading the session's `complete_interval` and the `session_reviews` row first: (a) recall gate, D25 — when `complete_interval === true` and `session_reviews.recall_locked_at` is NULL → 422 `recall_not_locked` naming the recall step, whether or not `recallScores` is present in the body; when `complete_interval === false` and `recall_locked_at` is NULL, finalize proceeds without recall — the attempt is accepted as incomplete and eligibility (5.8.4) records `recall_missing` and `scoring_incomplete`; independent of `complete_interval`, `recallScores` present while `recall_locked_at` is NULL → 422 `recall_not_locked` (self-scoring always requires a lock, D10). (b) `review.materiallyDisrupted` must be a boolean → else 400 `fieldErrors.materiallyDisrupted = 'required'`; `disruptionNote` optional. (c) `recallScores` optional `(0|1|null)[5]`, scored only when `recall_locked_at` is set: pass the stored `recall_points` and the body's `recallScores` through the shared `deriveRecallScore(points, scores)` (`packages/shared/src/domain/recall.ts`, 2.3.2, imported per D16) — `ok: true` → store its `recallScores`/`recallScore`; `ok: false` (a non-blank point left unscored) → store both `recall_scores` and `recall_score` as NULL (scoring stays incomplete, never a partial array, never 0); `recallScores` absent while locked → store both NULL. (d) `conditions` optional `ObservedConditions` (all four keys) → replaces `observed_conditions`; absent → the value defaulted from the slot at start (D7.5/D31) is kept. (e) `outputQuality`/`outputNote` on a benchmark → 422 `practice_only_field` (D19). (f) counts follow the 5.8.2 rules (`episodeCount` S, `externalCount` E, `mindWanderingCount` M, `unplannedAgentChecks` accepted and stored; blank → NULL; `countMethod` required with `episodeCount`); `firstSwitchEstimateSeconds` (integer >= 0) accepted and held for 5.8.4. (g) `reviewNote` (D31, the optional free-text note distinct from `outputNote`) — extend `FinalizeBody.review` (2.7.4) with an optional `reviewNote: string`, stored to `session_reviews.review_note text NULL` (3.3 adds the column per D31); absent → NULL. The recall texts cannot be altered: the contract has no points field. Inside the same transaction as 5.8.1's commit, after `focus_sessions.lifecycle = 'finalized'` is written for a baseline-phase attempt (`benchmark_slots.phase = 'baseline'` via `slot_id`), auto-activate (D33): `UPDATE programs SET status = 'active', version = version + 1 WHERE id = program_id AND status = 'baseline_ready' AND (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT bs.label) FROM focus_sessions fs JOIN benchmark_slots bs ON fs.slot_id = bs.id WHERE bs.program_id = program_id AND bs.phase = 'baseline' AND fs.lifecycle = 'finalized') = 2` — a no-op once status has already left `baseline_ready` or fewer than two labels are finalized, so it is safe on every benchmark finalize and on idempotent replays. Eligibility and first-switch derivation are 5.8.4 and land in the same transaction; this unit's tests assert nothing about `eligible`, `exclusion_reasons` or `first_switch_*`, and a benchmark finalize is not considered complete until 5.8.4 lands. Extend `apps/api/test/helpers/sessions.ts` with `finalizableBenchmark({ completeInterval })` (program + slots + start + end + optional recall lock) and `finalizableIncompleteBenchmark()` (stop-early, no recall).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/finalize-benchmark.unit.test.ts`: `complete_interval true, no recall lock → RecallNotLockedError even with recallScores`; `complete_interval false, no recall lock, recallScores omitted → proceeds (no error)`; `complete_interval false, no recall lock, recallScores present → RecallNotLockedError`; `materiallyDisrupted undefined → field error`; `recallScores mapped through the shared deriveRecallScore, ok:true stores its recallScores/recallScore`; `deriveRecallScore ok:false → recall_score and recall_scores both null`; `recallScores absent while locked → recall_score null`; `conditions replace the defaulted value`; `outputQuality on benchmark rejected`; `reviewNote absent → null, present → stored verbatim`; `auto-activation SQL only targets baseline-phase slots and is a no-op for a final-phase finalize`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/finalize-benchmark.test.ts`: `complete benchmark awaiting_review without recall → finalize with recallScores → 422 recall_not_locked; without recallScores → same 422; review row unchanged and session_events count unchanged (no lastBatch applied)`; `stopped-early benchmark (complete_interval false) finalized without any recall → 200, recall_locked_at null, recall_score null, recall_scores null, lifecycle finalized (D25)`; `stopped-early benchmark finalized with recallScores in the body but no recall lock → 422 recall_not_locked`; `stopped-early benchmark that DID lock recall before stopping, then finalized with recallScores → 200, scored normally`; `after 5.7.2 lock on a complete benchmark, body without materiallyDisrupted → 400 fieldErrors.materiallyDisrupted, not finalized`; `points [a,b,c,'',''] locked + recallScores [1,1,1,1,1] → recall_score 3, recall_scores [1,1,1,0,0]`; `recallScores omitted while locked → recall_score NULL and recall_scores NULL`; `recallScores [1,1,null,0,0] with point 3 non-blank → recall_score NULL, recall_scores NULL`; `materiallyDisrupted true + disruptionNote → both stored`; `externalCount 2 + materiallyDisrupted false → stored, materially_disrupted false`; `paper tally: no events, episodeCount 4, countMethod retrospective → episode_count 4, count_method retrospective`; `unplannedAgentChecks 1 on a benchmark → stored 1, off-task columns untouched`; `conditions with accommodations ['increased_font_size'] on a final slot (baseline_date seeded 14 days before ctx.now) → observed_conditions stores it; omitted → slot-defaulted conditions remain`; `reviewNote 'felt distracted by noise' → session_reviews.review_note stores it verbatim; omitted → NULL`; `outputQuality on benchmark → 422 practice_only_field`; `body with points → 400 and stored recall_points unchanged`; `replay same key → 200 same review, recall fields unchanged`; `unknown key on finalized → 409 already_finalized`; `finalizing baseline B when baseline A is already finalized → programs.status becomes active and programs.version increments (D33)`; `finalizing baseline A first (B not yet attempted) → programs.status stays baseline_ready`; `finalizing a final-phase benchmark never touches programs.status`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/finalize-benchmark.test.ts test/services/finalize-benchmark.unit.test.ts` → 21 integration cases and 11 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 5.8.1, 2.3.2, 5.7.2, 4.3, 5.3

## 3. API foundation

### 3.6.2 — Add finalize log-privacy test proving note text never reaches Pino

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/test/privacy/finalizeLogs.test.ts driving the real routes: POST /programs (4.1.2) and PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots (4.3.2); POST /demo/clock (3.5.2) to reach the baseline slot date; start a practice session via POST /sessions (5.1.1) with intendedOutput 'PRIVATE-OUTPUT-1b2c' and end it with `transitions { type: 'end' }` (5.4.2, D24) so finalize is reachable; start a benchmark (5.1.2), end it, save recall via POST /sessions/{id}/recall (5.7.2) with points containing 'PRIVATE-RECALL-5a9c'. Finalize paths are 5.8.1 (mechanics), 5.8.2 (practice) and 5.8.3 (benchmark gate). No production code is expected to change; if a marker leaks, the leaking logger/serializer/error message is fixed in the owning unit and the test re-run.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: this unit is an end-to-end privacy assertion over routes implemented in 4.x/5.x; the unit-level coverage is 3.2.4 and 3.6.1
- **Integration tests:** finalizeLogs.test.ts: (a) practice finalize with `expectedEventCount` mismatched and review { outputNote: 'PRIVATE-NOTE-7c1d' } → 409 event_count_mismatch whose details carry only expected and stored; (b) practice finalize with review { episodeCount: 'three', outputNote: 'PRIVATE-NOTE-7c1d' } → 400 with fieldErrors['review.episodeCount']; (c) benchmark finalize before the recall lock with disruptionNote 'PRIVATE-DISRUPTION-2e4b' → 422 recall_not_locked (D25); (d) benchmark finalize after the lock without materiallyDisrupted → 400 with fieldErrors.materiallyDisrupted (5.8.3); (e) a successful practice finalize with outputNote 'PRIVATE-NOTE-7c1d' → 200 and its completion log entry contains no marker. For every case: the response body contains none of the four markers; error entries carry reqId equal to x-request-id and the error code; logs.text() over the whole flow contains none of PRIVATE-OUTPUT-1b2c, PRIVATE-RECALL-5a9c, PRIVATE-NOTE-7c1d, PRIVATE-DISRUPTION-2e4b.
