## 5. Sessions API

### 5.8.4 — Benchmark eligibility and first-switch derived by shared domain at finalize

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Complete the benchmark branch of `finalizeSession` inside the same transaction as 5.8.1: before deriving eligibility, check for an unresolved clock-gap (D26) — `SELECT 1 FROM session_events WHERE session_id = ? AND type = 'clock_gap' AND voided_at IS NULL AND details->>'resolution' IS NULL LIMIT 1`; when found, `UPDATE focus_sessions SET timer_quality = 'uncertain' WHERE id = ? AND timer_quality <> 'uncertain'` before evaluating eligibility, so the persisted column and the exclusion reason agree (unresolved uncertainty persists as a flag even if the client never called `/clock-gap`, D26). Build the first-switch input from non-voided `session_events` of type `off_task` plus `agent_check` with `details.alsoOffTask = true` (D11), ordered by `elapsed_ms` — `visibility` (D15), `external`, `pause`, `resume`, `clock_gap` and voided rows never enter it — and call the shared `deriveFirstSwitch({ events, episodeCount, countMethod, estimateSeconds: firstSwitchEstimateSeconds })` (2.2.2) to get `{ firstSwitch, method } | null`; persist `first_switch_kind`/`first_switch_seconds`/`first_switch_method` via `toStoredFirstSwitch` (2.2.2) — NULL/NULL/NULL when `episodeCount` is null. Then call the shared `evaluateEligibility` (2.3.1) with `{ completeInterval: focus_sessions.complete_interval, recallLockedAt: session_reviews.recall_locked_at, recallScores: session_reviews.recall_scores, episodeCount: session_reviews.episode_count, materiallyDisrupted: session_reviews.materially_disrupted, timerQuality: focus_sessions.timer_quality (post clock-gap force above), sessionLocalDate: focus_sessions.local_date, slotAssignedLocalDate: benchmark_slots.assigned_local_date (joined via slot_id), excludedByAmendment: false (amendments are only accepted after finalize, 5.9.1; the report overlay is 5.9.3), realm: focus_sessions.realm, timeSource: focus_sessions.time_source }` and write `focus_sessions.eligible`/`exclusion_reasons` (text[], in 2.3.1's declaration order) from its `{ eligible, exclusionReasons }`; the response is read back from the stored row. Under D25, an incomplete benchmark finalized without a recall lock naturally yields `recall_missing` (from `recallLockedAt` null) and `scoring_incomplete` (from `recallScores` null) alongside `interval_incomplete` — no special-casing needed, 2.3.1 already emits both. `simulated_time` can only appear for realm `pilot`. Nothing from the client is trusted for eligibility or realm (the contract has no such fields); a `null` count is passed as unreported, never 0. Benchmark starts after (not before) the assigned date are accepted by 5.1, so `timing_deviation` is reachable without a replacement (D23).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/finalize-eligibility.unit.test.ts`: `null episodeCount is passed as unreported, not 0`; `voided off_task events are excluded from first-switch input`; `visibility events are excluded from first-switch input`; `agent_check alsoOffTask counts as off-task for first switch`; `agent_check without alsoOffTask does not`; `slot date compared to focus_sessions.local_date`; `demo realm never produces simulated_time`; `excludedByAmendment is false at finalize`; `an unresolved clock_gap event forces timer_quality to uncertain before evaluateEligibility is called`; `a clock_gap event with details.resolution set does not force timer_quality`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/finalize-eligibility.test.ts` (each case prepares a benchmark through the 5.8.3 helper): `all met (complete_interval true, recall locked, recallScores given, episodeCount 0 countMethod event, materiallyDisrupted false, timer ok, local_date = assigned) → eligible true, exclusionReasons []`; `blank S → eligible false, ['count_unknown'], episode_count NULL, DB and response agree`; `explicit 0 → eligible true, first_switch_kind none_capped, first_switch_seconds NULL`; `retrospective S=3 without estimate → first_switch_kind unknown, first_switch_seconds NULL, eligible true`; `retrospective S=3 with firstSwitchEstimateSeconds 300 → known 300, method estimate`; `off_task event at elapsed_ms 370000 → known 370, method event`; `voided off_task at 100000 + live off_task at 370000 → known 370; visibility events only with S 0 → none_capped`; `agent_check alsoOffTask at 200000 before off_task at 370000 → known 200, method event (D11)`; `stopped early at 14 min + blank S → ['interval_incomplete','count_unknown']`; `materiallyDisrupted true → ['materially_disrupted']`; `externalCount 2 + materiallyDisrupted false → no materially_disrupted reason`; `recall locked but recallScores omitted → ['scoring_incomplete']`; `stopped-early benchmark finalized with no recall at all (D25) → ['interval_incomplete','recall_missing','scoring_incomplete']`; `clock-gap resolved uncertain via 5.5.1 → ['timer_uncertain']`; `save_incomplete (5.5.1) → ['interval_incomplete','timer_uncertain']`; `an unresolved clock_gap event (posted via 5.3, no details.resolution) present at finalize → timer_quality forced uncertain, exclusionReasons includes timer_uncertain, GET /sessions/{id} shows timer_quality uncertain (D26)`; `a clock_gap event with details.resolution set does not force timer_quality and contributes no reason by itself`; `baseline B started with the demo clock on Day 1 → ['timing_deviation'] and GET /sessions/{id} still returns it`; `final A finalized on Day 15 → ['timing_deviation'] and both baseline session_reviews rows are row-for-row identical before and after`; `demo realm with demo clock offset (time_source demo_clock) → no simulated_time`; `practice finalize (5.8.2) still leaves eligible NULL`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/finalize-eligibility.test.ts test/services/finalize-eligibility.unit.test.ts` → 21 integration cases and 10 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 5.8.3, 2.2.2, 2.3.1, 5.5.1, 3.5, 4.3, 5.1, 5.3
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / All conditions met · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / Multiple failing conditions · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / Attempt outside its date · benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / Retrospective count without time · benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / No switches · benchmark-assessment: First-switch time has three distinct states / Event-timed first switch · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Blank count · benchmark-assessment: Counts are confirmed at review and blank means unknown / Explicit zero · benchmark-assessment: Disruption is a required self-attestation / External interruptions without disruption · benchmark-assessment: Final benchmarks on Day 14 / Final completed on Day 15 · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Laptop slept during a benchmark · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available / Save incomplete after uncertainty

### 5.9.1 — Amendments route: append-only reason with exclude flag

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** Add `POST /sessions/{id}/amendments` to `apps/api/src/routes/sessions.ts` and `addAmendment(ctx, sessionId, body)` in `apps/api/src/services/review.ts`, contract from 2.7 (`{ reason: string minLength 1, excludeFromReport: boolean }` → `{ id, sessionId, reason, excludeFromReport, createdAt }`, 201). Ownership 404 `not_found`. Per D32, amendments are accepted on a finalized session of EITHER kind (practice or benchmark) — lifecycle must be `finalized` else 409 `not_finalized`; kind is never checked. Insert one `session_amendments` row with `user_id = ctx.principalId`, `reason`, `exclude_from_report`, `created_at = ctx.now`. No update or delete route exists; the insert never modifies `session_reviews`, `focus_sessions.eligible`, `exclusion_reasons`, recall or count columns (eligibility is written only by finalize; report-side honoring is 5.9.3). `amendments[]` is already part of the canonical session response shape (D20); populate it in `serializeSession` (5.1.1) by loading `session_amendments` in `created_at` order, so `GET /sessions/{id}` and every other session-shaped response list both attempts. Reason text is never logged (3.6 harness).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/amendments.unit.test.ts`: `empty reason rejected`; `excludeFromReport must be boolean`; `unfinalized session rejected`; `addAmendment issues a single INSERT and no UPDATE` (tx double records statements).
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/amendments.test.ts`: `finalized benchmark → POST {reason:'wrong material', excludeFromReport:true} → 201 with id and createdAt`; `second POST {reason:'context', excludeFromReport:false} → 201, two rows in creation order, the first unchanged`; `after both amendments the session_reviews row and focus_sessions.eligible/exclusion_reasons are byte-identical to before`; `PUT/PATCH/DELETE /sessions/{id}/amendments/{amendmentId} → 404 not_found (no route)`; `empty reason → 400`; `awaiting_review session → 409 not_finalized`; `other principal → 404`; `GET /sessions/{id} lists both amendments in order`; `log capture (3.6 harness): reason text absent from Pino output`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/amendments.test.ts test/services/amendments.unit.test.ts` → 9 integration cases and 4 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 2.7, 3.2, 3.3, 3.6.1, 5.1, 5.2, 5.8.1
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only / Exclude a finalized attempt

### 5.9.2 — Shared amendment-exclusion overlay on stored eligibility

- **Layer:** shared
- **Implementation:** Add to `packages/shared/src/domain/eligibility.ts` (exported via `packages/shared/src/index.ts`) the pure `applyAmendmentExclusion(stored: { eligible: boolean | null; exclusionReasons: readonly ExclusionReason[] }, amendments: readonly { excludeFromReport: boolean }[])` → same shape: any excluding amendment yields `eligible: false` and appends `'excluded_by_amendment'` exactly once after the stored reasons (originals kept, order preserved); otherwise the input is returned unchanged; `eligible: null` (practice) stays `null` even when excluded. No other stored value is touched. This is the single function 5.9.3 uses in the report mapper and in the 5.1 replacement rule.
- **Unit tests:** `packages/shared/src/domain/eligibility.test.ts` (append): `no amendments → unchanged`; `explaining amendment only → unchanged`; `excluding amendment → eligible false and excluded_by_amendment appended once`; `already ineligible with count_unknown + excluded → ['count_unknown','excluded_by_amendment']`; `two excluding amendments → reason appears once`; `eligible null (practice) stays null`.
- **Integration tests:** n/a: pure function, covered by API integration in 5.9.3
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/shared -- src/domain/eligibility.test.ts` → the six overlay cases pass alongside 2.3.1's cases and `npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/shared` is clean.

## 6. Days, report, export and research API

### 6.1.1 — Implement GET days route and the check-in view builder

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/checkinView.ts `buildCheckinView(row: DailyCheckinRow | null, feedRows: FeedUsageRow[], localDate)` — the pure builder that 6.1.2's PUT response and 6.2.4's days[] reuse. It returns the D22 / 2.7.5 `DayResponse` shape `{ checkin: { localDate, sleepMinutes, stress, mindfulnessMinutes, note }, feed: FeedRow[], status: { status, missing[] }, aggregates, version }` and computes nothing itself: `status` = `checkinStatus(row ? { sleepMinutes: row.sleep_minutes } : null, feedRowsAsInputs)` from packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts (2.6.2; D16: the only completeness implementation — 4.5.2's local module is not used here); `aggregates` = `feedAggregates(feedRowsAsInputs)` (2.6.1) = `{ unitLabel: 'device-minutes', feedDeviceMinutes, feedByDevice, partial, shortVideoDeviceMinutes, appTotals }` where a `platform = 'all'` scope-`feed` row is the device's headline total (D36: a device carries either its `all` row or platform rows, never both, so a device's `feedByDevice` entry is that device's feed-scope sum either way), `feedByDevice[device]` is null when the device has no feed-scope row (absence = unknown), `partial` is true when phone or desktop lacks a feed-scope row, `appTotals` lists scope-`app_total` rows separately and never sums them, `shortVideoDeviceMinutes` is a subset never added as a category. No row → `{ checkin: { localDate, sleepMinutes: null, stress: null, mindfulnessMinutes: null, note: null }, feed: [], status: { status: 'not_reported', missing: ['sleep','feed'] }, aggregates: feedAggregates([]), version: 0 }` (D22). Stored nulls pass through as JSON null; each `feed[]` row keeps `device, platform, minutes, shortVideoMinutes, measurementScope, source, plannedWindow`. The response carries no `realm` field; when `row.realm !== ctx.realm` the route throws via `assertOwnRealm` (3.2.3) → 422 `realm_mismatch`. apps/api/src/routes/days.ts `GET /api/v1/programs/:id/days/:date`: `:date` validated by the 2.7.5 params schema (YYYY-MM-DD, otherwise 400 `malformed_request`); program via `loadOwnedProgram(db, ctx, id)` (4.1.1: id AND `user_id = ctx.principalId` AND `realm = ctx.realm`, otherwise 404 `not_found` with message 'Not found', never 403); `programDayForLocalDate(baseline_date, date)` (2.1.1) outside 0..14 → 404 `not_found` (the day is not a resource of this program); load `daily_checkins` on `UNIQUE(program_id, local_date)` plus its `feed_usage` rows; return `buildCheckinView(...)`; `Cache-Control: no-store` via the 3.2.2 plugin. Response schema is 2.7.5 `DayResponse`. No `?? 0`, `|| 0` or `COALESCE(...,0)` anywhere (2.9.1 guard).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/checkinView.test.ts: `no row → checkin all-null with localDate set, feed [], status not_reported with missing ['sleep','feed'], aggregates.feedDeviceMinutes null, version 0`; `desktop 'all' 30 and no phone row → feedByDevice.phone null, partial true, feedDeviceMinutes 30`; `phone 'all' 0 + desktop 'all' 20 → feedByDevice.phone 0 (not null), partial false, feedDeviceMinutes 20`; `phone instagram app_total 60 + phone instagram feed 25 → feedDeviceMinutes 25 and appTotals [{device:'phone', platform:'instagram', minutes:60}]`; `phone 20 + desktop 20 → feedDeviceMinutes 40 under unitLabel 'device-minutes' and no elapsed-minutes field exists`; `short-video 10 + 5 across two feed rows → shortVideoDeviceMinutes 15 and feedDeviceMinutes unchanged`; `sleep only → status incomplete missing ['feed']; sleep null with one feed row → incomplete missing ['sleep'] and sleepMinutes stays null`; `every feed row keeps device, platform, minutes, shortVideoMinutes, measurementScope, source, plannedWindow with nulls preserved`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/days.get.test.ts (Fastify inject against attention_lab_test; program via `insertProgram` from 4.1.1; `daily_checkins` + `feed_usage` rows seeded directly through Drizzle using the 3.3.3 schema, no PUT needed): `date inside the window with no row → 200 with the D22 empty shell (checkin all-null, feed [], status not_reported, missing ['sleep','feed'], version 0)`; `desktop 'all' 30 only → feedByDevice.phone null and partial true`; `explicit zero phone 'all' row → feedByDevice.phone 0 and feedDeviceMinutes includes it`; `app_total 60 alongside feed 25 → feedDeviceMinutes 25 and appTotals carries the 60`; `two devices 20+20 → feedDeviceMinutes 40`; `sleep only → status incomplete, missing ['feed']`; `row with source device_report → feed[0].source === 'device_report'`; `:date '2026-13-01' → 400 malformed_request`; `date two days before baseline → 404 not_found`; `Cache-Control: no-store present`; `programs row for user_id 'other-principal' → 404 with message 'Not found' and no program detail`; `daily_checkins row with realm 'pilot' under the demo program → 422 realm_mismatch with the fixed message and no realm value in the body`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- days.get checkinView → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 20 passed, 0 failed (8 unit + 12 integration named cases)
- **After:** 1.4, 1.5, 2.1.1, 2.6.1, 2.6.2, 2.7.5, 3.2.2, 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.3.3, 4.1.1
- **Spec refs:** daily-checkin: Blank is unknown; zero is explicit / No phone entry · daily-checkin: Blank is unknown; zero is explicit / Explicit zero · daily-checkin: Aggregates respect scope and subset rules / App total alongside feed estimate · daily-checkin: Aggregates respect scope and subset rules / Two devices at once · daily-checkin: Incomplete saves and completeness status / Sleep only · daily-checkin: Feed rows carry device, platform, scope and source / Device report transcription · daily-checkin: Overruns and missed days never block / Skipped day · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

### 6.1.2 — Implement PUT days atomic write path with version conflict

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/routes/days.ts: register `PUT /api/v1/programs/:id/days/:date` with the 2.7.5 TypeBox contract (`PutDayBody { expectedVersion, sleepMinutes?, stress?, mindfulnessMinutes?, note?, feed: FeedRow[] }`, `additionalProperties: false`, so a client-supplied `realm` field is 400 `malformed_request` with `fieldErrors.realm`; `:date` YYYY-MM-DD else 400). apps/api/src/services/checkin.ts `saveCheckin(ctx, programId, localDate, body)` runs in ONE transaction, in this order: (1) `loadOwnedProgram(tx, ctx, programId, { forUpdate: true })` (4.1.1) → 404 `not_found` otherwise; status `completed|archived` → 409 `program_terminal` (check-ins are editable until the program is completed); `programDayForLocalDate` (2.1.1) outside 0..14 → 404 `not_found`; (2) validate rows with `validateFeedRows` from packages/shared/src/domain/feed.ts (2.6.1) — this unit adds the D36 rule to that function additively as error code `platform_conflict` (a device with a `platform = 'all'` scope-`feed` row plus any other scope-`feed` row for the same device) — and map codes to D19: `short_video_exceeds_minutes` → 422 `feed_subset_violation` with `fieldErrors['feed[i].shortVideoMinutes'] = 'must not exceed minutes'`, `duplicate_row` → 422 `duplicate_feed_row` with `fieldErrors['feed[i]'] = 'duplicate device, platform and scope'`, `platform_conflict` → 422 `feed_platform_conflict` with `fieldErrors['feed[i].platform'] = 'device has both an all row and platform rows'`; the response code is the first error's code and `fieldErrors` lists every offending row; the 3.3.3 CHECK and `UNIQUE(checkin_id, device, platform, measurement_scope)` are the backstop; the 422 body never echoes `note` text; (3) load the `daily_checkins` row on `UNIQUE(program_id, local_date)` `FOR UPDATE`; stored version is the row's `version` or 0 when no row (D22: the first PUT sends `expectedVersion: 0`); mismatch → 409 `stale_version` with `details: { current: buildCheckinView(currentRow, currentFeedRows, localDate) }` (D18) and nothing written; otherwise upsert stamping `realm = ctx.realm` (D34), writing absent optional fields as NULL (never 0), `version = stored + 1` (1 on create); (4) delete every `feed_usage` row of the check-in and insert the submitted set (atomic replace) — an explicit `minutes: 0` row is inserted like any other; `feed: []` with no sleep is a valid save (savable at any level of completeness); over-allowance minutes (55 against `leisure_allowance_min` 20) save with no warning or blocking field; a save for Day 10 with no Day 9 row succeeds; (5) return `buildCheckinView(...)` from 6.1.1 (status, missing[], aggregates, rows, new version) with `Cache-Control: no-store` via 3.2.2. No `?? 0` / `COALESCE(...,0)` (2.9.1 guard). Test helper `putDay(app, programId, date, body)` in apps/api/test/helpers/days.ts defaults `expectedVersion` to the current GET's version.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/checkin.write.test.ts (pure mappers/validators exported from services/checkin.ts and the extended feed.ts): `absent sleepMinutes/stress/mindfulnessMinutes/note map to null, never 0`; `explicit minutes 0 feed row is kept as 0`; `shortVideoMinutes 45 over minutes 30 → code feed_subset_violation with fieldErrors['feed[0].shortVideoMinutes']`; `two rows sharing device+platform+scope → duplicate_feed_row`; `phone 'all' feed row plus phone instagram feed row → feed_platform_conflict; phone 'all' plus desktop instagram → valid (the conflict is per device); phone 'all' feed plus phone instagram app_total → valid (scope app_total is not a feed row)`; `validation error object contains no note text`; `version check: no row and expectedVersion 0 passes, no row and expectedVersion 1 → stale_version with details.current.version 0; stored version 2 and expectedVersion 1 → stale_version carrying the stored view`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/days.put.test.ts (Fastify inject against attention_lab_test; program via `insertProgram` from 4.1.1): `create: expectedVersion 0 + sleep + one phone 'all' row → 200, status complete, version 1, stress/mindfulness/note NULL in daily_checkins`; `sleep only → 200, status incomplete, missing ['feed']`; `all blank: feed [] and no sleep → 200, status incomplete, missing ['sleep','feed'], one daily_checkins row`; `edit twice: PUT sleep (v0) then PUT sleep+feed (v1) on the same date → exactly one daily_checkins row holding both, version 2`; `stale save: two PUTs with the same expectedVersion → second is 409 stale_version whose details.current equals the first save's view, the row is unchanged and a GET returns the first save's values`; `first PUT with expectedVersion 1 and no row → 409 stale_version with details.current.version 0 and no row written`; `short-video 45 over 30 with a note → 422 feed_subset_violation naming feed[0].shortVideoMinutes, zero feed_usage rows written, body does not contain the note text`; `phone 'all' + phone instagram feed rows → 422 feed_platform_conflict, nothing written`; `duplicate device/platform/scope → 422 duplicate_feed_row, nothing written`; `explicit zero: phone 'all' minutes 0 → feed_usage row with minutes 0 (not NULL, not dropped)`; `source device_report persists on the row`; `55 feed minutes against leisure_allowance_min 20 → 200 with no warning/blocking field`; `atomic replace: PUT 3 rows then PUT 1 row → exactly 1 feed_usage row`; `GET after a second PUT returns the incremented version and the replaced rows`; `Day 10 saved with no Day 9 row → 200`; `date outside Days 0–14 → 404 not_found and no row`; `:date '2026-9-1' → 400 malformed_request and no row`; `realm in body → 400 with fieldErrors.realm and no row; stored daily_checkins.realm is 'demo'`; `program owned by 'other-principal' → 404 with no program detail`; `program status set to 'completed' → 409 program_terminal and no row`; `Cache-Control: no-store present`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- days.put checkin.write → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 28 passed, 0 failed (7 unit + 21 integration named cases)

### 6.2.1 — Implement report core: attempts, samples, realm guard, comparison

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/report/attempts.ts `loadAttemptSummaries(db, ctx, programId)`: program via `loadOwnedProgram` (4.1.1; 404 otherwise); `benchmark_slots` LEFT JOIN `focus_sessions` (`kind='benchmark'`, `slot_id`) LEFT JOIN `session_reviews` (always present, D31) LEFT JOIN `session_amendments`. Rows are loaded by ownership and NOT filtered by realm; `assertSameRealm` (types.ts) runs over `{ realm }` projections of the program row plus every attempt; `RealmMixingError` → 422 `realm_mismatch` through the 3.2.4 mapper with the fixed message 'Simulated and real results are never combined.' and no partial body. Map each row to the widened `AttemptSummary` (2.7.1: `countMethod: CountMethod | null`, `firstSwitch: FirstSwitch | null`) plus `{ lifecycle, replacementReason, excludedByAmendment }` — these three fields are added additively to `AttemptSchema` in packages/shared/src/contracts/report.ts (2.7.5) by this unit. `episodeCount = episode_count` (NULL stays null), `recallScore`, `externalCount`, `unplannedAgentChecks`, `mindWanderingCount`, `countMethod`, `firstSwitch` via `fromStoredFirstSwitch` (2.2.2; `unknown` is never turned into `none_capped`; NULL kind → null), `conditions = observed_conditions`, `timeSource`, `timerQuality`, `localDate`, `revisionId`, `recallFlags`, `materiallyDisrupted`; `eligible`/`exclusionReasons` = `applyAmendmentExclusion({ eligible, exclusionReasons }, amendments)` (5.9.2, D32) so an excluding amendment yields `eligible=false` with `excluded_by_amendment` appended while original values stay and no column is rewritten; sessions never finalized (`eligible IS NULL`: running, paused, awaiting_review, abandoned) are listed with `eligible: false`, `exclusionReasons: []` and their `lifecycle` (timer expiry never proves completion; no reason is invented). `samples = sampleCounts(selectSlotCandidates(attempts))` (2.4.1) → `{ baselineEligible, finalEligible }`; `midpoint` attempts are listed but never counted. `revisions[]` from `protocol_revisions` as `{ id, revision, effectiveDay, settings, reason, createdAt }`. apps/api/src/services/report/index.ts `buildReport(ctx, programId)`: today's program day via `currentProgramDay` (2.1.2) from `ctx.now`, `programs.timezone` and `baseline_date`; `comparison = computeComparison(attempts)` (2.4.1; null unless every baseline and final slot has an eligible attempt; `firstSwitchMeanSeconds` only when all four are `known`; field `percentageReduction`); `resultState = resolveResultState({ baselineEligible, finalEligible, finalAttemptsExist, day14Finished: day > 14, comparison })` (2.4.2) where `finalAttemptsExist` is true only when a final-phase attempt with lifecycle `finalized` exists — the API never computes S0/S14 or a percentage itself (D4). apps/api/src/routes/report.ts `GET /api/v1/programs/:id/report` → `{ realm, samples, attempts, comparison?, resultState, warnings: [], practice: [], days: [], revisions }` per 2.7.5 (`warnings/practice/days` filled by 6.2.3/6.2.4); `Cache-Control: no-store`. The report carries the demo label as `realm` only. No COALESCE / `?? 0` (2.9.1 guard).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/report.attempts.test.ts: `episode_count NULL → episodeCount null (never 0)`; `first_switch_kind none_capped / known(370 s) / unknown map to the three FirstSwitch variants, unknown is not none_capped, NULL kind → null`; `exclude_from_report amendment → eligible false with excluded_by_amendment appended, excludedByAmendment true and counts unchanged`; `unfinalized session (eligible NULL, lifecycle awaiting_review) → eligible false, exclusionReasons [], lifecycle 'awaiting_review', countMethod null`; `awaiting_review benchmark whose target elapsed → eligible false and not counted in samples (timer expiry is not completion)`; `samples: baseline A eligible + B ineligible → baselineEligible 1`; `phase midpoint attempt is listed in attempts and leaves samples unchanged`; `finalAttemptsExist is true for a finalized final attempt and false when the only final attempt is abandoned`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/report.core.test.ts (scenarios via `POST /api/v1/demo/scenarios/{name}/load` from 3.5.4, no body; extra rows via Drizzle): `one eligible baseline: samples.baselineEligible === 1 and B is listed with its exclusionReasons`; `replaced slot lists both attempts with replacementReason and only the eligible one is the comparison candidate`; `amendment-excluded baseline B keeps original S/recall, shows excluded_by_amendment and excludedByAmendment true`; `capped T: two attempts none_capped and two known → T listed per attempt with distinct kinds and comparison.firstSwitchMeanSeconds is null`; `all four T known → firstSwitchMeanSeconds is a number`; `comparable-change: comparison present (2+2) with percentageReduction 40 and body realm 'demo'`; `missing-final: comparison absent and resultState insufficient_samples`; `timing-deviation (D35): baseline A listed with lifecycle 'running', eligible false, exclusionReasons [], samples.baselineEligible 0, resultState baseline_pending`; `two protocol_revisions rows → revisions[] lists both with effectiveDay and reason`; `mixed realm: a realm='pilot' focus_sessions + session_reviews row inserted under the demo program → 422 realm_mismatch, message 'Simulated and real results are never combined.', body has no attempts, samples or realm values`; `another principal's program → 404`; `Cache-Control: no-store`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- report.core report.attempts → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 20 passed, 0 failed (8 unit + 12 integration named cases)
- **After:** 1.4, 1.5, 2.1.2, 2.2.2, 2.4.1, 2.4.2, 2.7.1, 2.7.5, 2.8.1, 2.8.2, 3.2.3, 3.2.4, 3.3.2, 3.5.4, 4.1.1, 5.9.2

### 6.2.2 — Add report acceptance table for PRD §6 scenarios and extra states

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/test/report.scenarios.test.ts plus apps/api/test/helpers/reportVariants.ts. Table-driven over `DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES` (new-user, working-day, comparable-change, mixed-result, missing-final, zero-baseline, recovery, timing-deviation): `POST /api/v1/demo/scenarios/{name}/load` (3.5.4; no body, D22; loading resets the demo clock and anchors `baseline_date = today − viewDay`, D35) then `GET /api/v1/programs/{programId}/report`, asserting `resultState` equals `DEMO_SCENARIOS[name].expected.resultState` (2.8.x; every state has a fixture, D7.4). For `new-user` the loader returns `{ programId: null }` (D22), so the test creates an empty program via `POST /api/v1/programs` (4.1.2) and reads its report (`baseline_pending`). The helper derives four extra row sets from the loaded comparable-change scenario by updating `session_reviews.episode_count` / `recall_score` through Drizzle: more switches (baseline 3,3 → final 5,5), unchanged (3,3 → 3,3), low baseline (2,2 → 1,1), zero-beats-direction (0,0 → 1,1). The report returns the state identifier only; copy is asserted against `RESULT_STATE_COPY[resultState].headline` and `CAUSE_NOTE` exported from packages/shared/src/domain/comparison.ts (2.4.2). The suite also enforces no invented score: the serialized report has no key matching /score|confidence|significance|pValue/i other than `recallScore`, and no text matching /attention \+/i; and every report body carries `realm: 'demo'`.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: test-only unit; the table-driven acceptance suite is the deliverable (D14)
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/report.scenarios.test.ts: `new user → baseline_pending, samples { baselineEligible 0, finalEligible 0 }, attempts [], comparison absent`; `working day (Day 4, D35) → final_pending with two eligible baselines and comparison absent`; `comparable change → improvement_maintained_recall; s0 5, s14 3, absoluteChange 2, percentageReduction 40, recall means 4 → 4; headline matches /fewer switches/ and /reported/ and CAUSE_NOTE is present`; `mixed result → fewer_switches_lower_recall; recall 4 → 2; headline 'Switches decreased, but recall was lower. These results are mixed.'`; `missing final → insufficient_samples, no percentage, eligible attempts listed`; `zero baseline → zero_baseline; percentageReduction null; absolute counts present; JSON contains no 'Infinity', 'NaN' or '100'`; `recovery → final_pending, two eligible baselines, attempts contain no practice session`; `timing deviation → baseline_pending; attempts lists baseline A with lifecycle 'running', eligible false, exclusionReasons []; zero finalized attempts; samples.baselineEligible 0`; `more switches (3 → 5) → more_switches with headline 'More switches were reported in the final sessions.'`; `unchanged (3 → 3) → unchanged with headline 'The reported switch count did not change.'`; `low baseline (2 → 1) → absoluteChange 1 and comparison.lowBaseline true`; `zero beats direction (0 → 1) → zero_baseline, not more_switches`; `no invented score keys or 'attention +' text in any scenario`; `realm is 'demo' in every scenario report and resultState equals the fixture's expected.resultState wherever it is non-null`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- report.scenarios → Test Files 1 passed; Tests 14 passed, 0 failed
- **After:** 6.2.1, 2.4.2, 2.8.1, 2.8.2, 3.5.4, 4.1.2

### 6.2.3 — Add comparability warnings to the report

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/report/warnings.ts `reportWarnings(attempts: readonly AttemptSummary[])`: `selectSlotCandidates(attempts)` (2.4.1) picks the single eligible attempt per slot (amendment-excluded attempts are already `eligible: false` after the 6.2.1 overlay, so they are never compared); `comparabilityWarnings(candidates)` (2.4.3) compares baseline:A with final:A and baseline:B with final:B only when both candidates exist, field by field over `conditions` (`deviceFormat`, `language`, `materialLevel` by string inequality with null vs value reported as 'not recorded at baseline' / 'not recorded at final'; `accommodations` as an order-insensitive set difference); a label with no eligible attempt on either side emits no warning (no fallback attempt is invented). The wrapper maps each warning to the 2.7.5 contract shape `{ label, field, baseline, final, message }`. Warnings never read or write `eligible`/`exclusionReasons` and never suppress `comparison`. Wire into `buildReport` (6.2.1) as `warnings[]`. This unit owns no comparison logic of its own (D16: one owner, 2.4.3).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/report.warnings.test.ts: `language en vs es on label B → exactly one warning { label: 'B', field: 'language', baseline: 'en', final: 'es' } with a message naming both`; `final A adds increased_font_size → warning field accommodations naming increased_font_size`; `an amendment-excluded final B (eligible false) is not compared and the eligible replacement is`; `baseline materialLevel null vs final 'B2' → warning with baseline null and a 'not recorded' message, not suppressed`; `input attempt objects are deep-equal before and after the call (eligible/exclusionReasons untouched) and a label with no eligible attempt on one side yields no warning`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/report.warnings.test.ts: `comparable-change scenario with final B observed_conditions.language changed via Drizzle → warnings names language for B and comparison is still present (2+2)`; `accommodation increased_font_size added on final A → warning for A and eligible unchanged for every attempt`; `scenario without condition drift → warnings []`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- report.warnings → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 8 passed, 0 failed (5 unit + 3 integration named cases)
- **After:** 6.2.1, 2.4.1, 2.4.3
- **Spec refs:** progress-report: Comparability warnings / Language differs · benchmark-assessment: Observed conditions live on the attempt / Accommodation added at final

### 6.2.4 — Add practice and daily sections with gaps to the report

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/report/practice.ts: `focus_sessions` where `program_id` and `kind='practice'` (benchmarks never included) LEFT JOIN `session_reviews` (present from start, D31), ordered by `local_date, started_at`; each row → the 2.7.5 practice entry `{ localDate, targetSeconds: target_seconds, completeInterval, outputQuality, episodeCount, externalCount, unplannedAgentChecks, revisionId }` plus `{ sessionId, programDay (programDayForLocalDate, 2.1.1), lifecycle, completedSeconds: ended_at ? ended_at − started_at − paused_seconds : null, timerQuality, countMethod, mindWanderingCount }` — these extra fields are added additively to the practice entry schema in packages/shared/src/contracts/report.ts by this unit; every count is a `ReportedCount` (null preserved); the stored off-task tally already includes agent checks marked also-off-task and nothing is summed across tallies (D11); a running or abandoned practice session is listed with its lifecycle and `completedSeconds` null (recovery scenario, D35). apps/api/src/services/report/days.ts: for programDay 0..min(today's program day via `currentProgramDay` (2.1.2), 14) compute `localDate` via `localDateForProgramDay` (2.1.1), LEFT JOIN `daily_checkins` + `feed_usage`; entry `{ programDay, localDate, checkin: null | buildCheckinView(row, feedRows, localDate) }` (6.1.1 builder; status from shared `checkinStatus`, D16) — a missing day is `checkin: null`, never a zero record; days after today are not listed. Practice rows and check-ins are added to the `assertSameRealm` projection of 6.2.1 (silent filtering forbidden; `RealmMixingError` → 422 `realm_mismatch`). Wire both into `buildReport` as `practice[]` and `days[]`; the 2.9.1 grep guard must still pass over the new files.
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/report.sections.test.ts: `completedSeconds = ended − started − paused (240 s paused excluded); null when ended_at is null`; `blank practice counts stay null`; `kind='benchmark' rows are filtered out of practice`; `practice row with episode_count 3 and unplanned_agent_checks 2 → both reported as-is and no field equals 5 (nothing summed across tallies)`; `days builder emits checkin null for a day with no row and stops at min(today's program day, 14)`; `daily entry for a desktop-only check-in has aggregates.feedByDevice.phone null`; `running practice session (ended_at null) → listed with lifecycle 'running' and completedSeconds null`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/report.sections.test.ts: `practice growth: sessions with target 600, 900, 1500 s across days → practice[].targetSeconds ascending and comparison/resultState identical to the report taken before the practice rows were inserted`; `Day 9 with no check-in → days[9].checkin === null while days[10].checkin is present`; `benchmark attempts never appear in practice[] and practice sessions never appear in attempts[]`; `practice review with blank S → episodeCount null in practice[]`; `finalized practice session on Day 14 of the comparable-change scenario → appears in practice[] with programDay 14 and comparison/resultState unchanged`; `pilot-realm daily_checkins row under the demo program → 422 realm_mismatch`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- report.sections → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 13 passed, 0 failed (7 unit + 6 integration named cases)

## 5. Sessions API

### 5.9.3 — Honor amendment exclusions in the report and the replacement rule

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** In `apps/api/src/services/report.ts`, at the step 6.2 uses to map `focus_sessions` + `session_reviews` rows into `AttemptSummary` (types.ts), load `session_amendments` per session and apply `applyAmendmentExclusion` (5.9.2) to `eligible`/`exclusionReasons` only — `episodeCount`, `recallScore`, `firstSwitch`, `externalCount`, `unplannedAgentChecks`, `countMethod`, `conditions`, `localDate` pass through untouched and no `focus_sessions` or `session_reviews` column is rewritten. The `samples` counts and the 2+2 gate consume the overlaid `eligible`. In `apps/api/src/services/session.ts`, the 5.1 replacement check ('permitted only when the first is ineligible or excluded by amendment') calls the same function over the first attempt's stored eligibility plus its `session_amendments` rows, so an excluded eligible attempt is replaceable and an eligible unamended one still returns 422 `eligible_attempt_not_retaken`. Null counts pass through as null (2.9 guard).
- **Unit tests:** `apps/api/test/services/report-amendments.unit.test.ts` (row fixtures, no DB): `mapper overlays eligible and exclusionReasons only and leaves the other AttemptSummary fields identical`; `replacement check treats an excluded eligible attempt as replaceable`; `replacement check still refuses an eligible unamended attempt`.
- **Integration tests:** `apps/api/test/sessions/amendment-exclusion.test.ts`: `program with eligible baseline A and B (S 4 and 6, recall 4); amend B excludeFromReport true → GET /programs/{id}/report attempts[] lists B with eligible false, exclusionReasons ['excluded_by_amendment'], episodeCount 6, recallScore 4 and the finalized firstSwitch; samples show 1 of 2 baseline eligible; DB focus_sessions.eligible for B still true and exclusion_reasons still []`; `explaining amendment (excludeFromReport false) → B still eligible in the report`; `excluding an already-ineligible attempt with ['count_unknown'] → report shows ['count_unknown','excluded_by_amendment'], DB exclusion_reasons unchanged`; `after excluding eligible baseline A, POST /sessions replacement for slot A with replacementReason → 201 and both attempts appear in the report; without the amendment the same start → 422 eligible_attempt_not_retaken`; `B finalized with a NULL externalCount and excluded → report externalCount null, never 0`.
- **Verify:** `npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- test/sessions/amendment-exclusion.test.ts test/services/report-amendments.unit.test.ts` → 5 integration cases and 3 unit cases pass.
- **After:** 5.9.1, 5.9.2, 5.8.4, 5.1, 6.2
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only / Exclude a finalized attempt · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons · benchmark-assessment: One replacement per slot with a reason / Replacement after disruption · benchmark-assessment: One replacement per slot with a reason / Replacement of an eligible attempt

## 6. Days, report, export and research API

### 6.3.1 — Implement GET export format=csv

- **Layer:** backend
- **Implementation:** apps/api/src/services/export/model.ts `buildExportModel(ctx, programId)`: reuse `loadAttemptSummaries` (6.2.1), the days loader (6.2.4) and `protocol_revisions`; `assertSameRealm` at entry → 422 `realm_mismatch` on mixing (module table: report/export). Sections, in fixed order: `program` (`program_id, realm, identity_mode, baseline_date, timezone, status, leisure_allowance_min, feed_estimate_min, exported_at` = `ctx.now` ISO); `revisions` (`revision, effective_day, practice_target_seconds, band_ceilings, leisure_allowance_min, reason, created_at`); `attempts` with exactly the progress-spec columns `attempt_id, phase, label, local_date, realm, time_source, s, s_method, t_state ('20+, capped' | 'known' | 'Unknown' | '' when firstSwitch is null), t_seconds, t_method, recall_score, e, m, disruption (yes|no|''), device_format, language, material_level, accommodations (';'-joined), eligible, exclusion_reasons (';'-joined reason ids), protocol_revision, recall_flags (';'-joined; recall_delayed/recall_overrun visible), replacement_reason, lifecycle`; `days` (`program_day, local_date, sleep_minutes, stress, mindfulness_minutes, status`); `feed_rows` (`local_date, device, platform, minutes, short_video_minutes, measurement_scope, source, planned_window`). Free text — check-in `note`, `output_note`, `disruption_note`, `recall_points`, `review_note`, `intended_output` — and practice sessions are not exported. Blank rule: `ReportedCount null` → empty cell, `0` → '0', never 0 for null; `t_state` from `formatFirstSwitch` (2.2.2). apps/api/src/services/export/csv.ts: RFC 4180 quoting; when `ctx.realm === 'demo'` the FIRST line is the demo label `# Demonstration data — synthetic local-demo records, not a real measurement`; each section is introduced by a single line `# <section-name>` followed by its header row and rows, sections separated by one blank line. apps/api/src/routes/export.ts `GET /api/v1/programs/:id/export?format=csv` → `text/csv; charset=utf-8`, `Cache-Control: no-store`, no `Content-Disposition` (download naming is 8.8's), 404 `not_found` when not owned, 400 `malformed_request` on an invalid `format` (2.7.5 `ExportQuery`).
- **Unit tests:** apps/api/test/unit/export.csv.test.ts: `null ReportedCount → empty cell and 0 → '0'`; `FirstSwitch none_capped → t_state '20+, capped'; known 370 → t_state 'known' with t_seconds 370; unknown → 'Unknown' (never '20+'); null → ''`; `fields containing commas, quotes or newlines are RFC 4180 quoted`; `demo realm prepends the label line; pilot realm does not`; `attempt with episodeCount null and exclusionReasons ['count_unknown','recall_missing'] → s cell empty and exclusion_reasons cell 'count_unknown;recall_missing'`; `a model carrying note, output_note, disruption_note, recall points and review_note markers produces a CSV containing none of them`.
- **Integration tests:** apps/api/test/export.csv.test.ts (Date pinned with vi.setSystemTime so exported_at is stable; before snapshot comparison every UUID in the body is replaced with `<uuid-N>` in first-seen order so the snapshot does not depend on the loader's id choice): `comparable-change scenario CSV matches the committed snapshot`; `blank-count fixture (finalized benchmark + session_reviews with episode_count NULL and exclusion_reasons ['count_unknown'] inserted via Drizzle) → s cell empty and exclusion_reasons contains count_unknown`; `first line is the demo label`; `Cache-Control: no-store and Content-Type text/csv`; `format=xml → 400 malformed_request`; `another principal's program → 404`; `pilot row injected under the demo program → 422 realm_mismatch`.
- **Verify:** npm run db:up && npm run test -w @attention-lab/api -- export.csv → Test Files 2 passed; Tests 13 passed, 0 failed (6 unit + 7 integration named cases); apps/api/test/__snapshots__/export.csv.test.ts.snap is committed and its first line is the demo label
- **After:** 6.2.1, 6.2.4, 2.2.2, 2.7.5, 2.8.1, 3.2.2, 3.5.4
- **Spec refs:** progress-report: Export preview and download / Export with a blank count · progress-report: Export preview and download / Demo export · identity-realm: Demo mode is permanently and unmistakably labeled / Export carries the demo label · identity-realm: Realms are never mixed in a result · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely · identity-realm: Every resource is scoped to its owner

### 6.3.2 — Implement GET export format=markdown

- **Layer:** backend

any D3 step-back recorded in LIMITATIONS.md). vite.config.ts: @vitejs/plugin-react + @tailwindcss/vite; server { host: '127.0.0.1', port: 5173, proxy: { '/api': 'http://127.0.0.1:8787' } }. vitest.config.ts: `environmentMatchGlobs: [['src/**/*.test.tsx', 'jsdom'], ['src/**/*.test.ts', 'node']]` so component tests (this harness) run in jsdom while 7.2/7.3's lib tests stay node; `setupFiles: ['src/test/setup.ts']`; `include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}']`. tsconfig.json extends ../../tsconfig.base.json with lib ['ES2023','DOM','DOM.Iterable'], jsx 'react-jsx', types ['vite/client'], noEmit true, include ['src','vite.config.ts'] (so *.test-d.ts files under src are typechecked). package.json scripts: dev 'vite', build 'tsc --noEmit && vite build', typecheck 'tsc --noEmit', test 'vitest run'. src/index.css: `@import "tailwindcss";`. src/main.tsx renders <Providers/>. src/app/providers.tsx: QueryClientProvider with a placeholder `new QueryClient()` (7.4.2 replaces it) + RouterProvider. src/app/Root.tsx: the root route element, rendering <Outlet/> only; later units wrap its children (7.4.2 SessionModeProvider, 7.1.2 AppBootstrap, 7.4.3 SessionLeaveGuard, 7.1.6 LiveRegion). src/app/router.tsx: createBrowserRouter([{ path: '/', element: <Root/>, errorElement: <RouteError/>, children: [ index -> <Navigate to="/today" replace/>, { id: 'rail', element: <Outlet/> (7.1.3 replaces), children: /setup, /setup/readiness, /today, /checkin/:date, /progress, /research, /settings, '*' -> NotFound }, { id: 'session', element: <Outlet/> (7.1.4 replaces), children: /benchmark/:slotId, /benchmark/:sessionId/recall, /benchmark/:sessionId/scoring, /focus/:sessionId, /review/:sessionId } ] }]) — the scoring path is nested under the session id (design.md writes the step only as a relative '-> /scoring' arrow; read literally here, recorded as a decision in notes). Each leaf renders src/app/ScreenPlaceholder.tsx ({ name: string }) that group 8 replaces. src/app/NotFound.tsx: 'This page is not available' + Link to /today. src/app/RouteError.tsx: 'Something went wrong' + Link to /today; never renders error.message, a stack, or any id. Component-test harness (D16, created once here — absorbs 8.1.1 and 8.6.1, which no longer create their own): src/test/setup.ts imports '@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest', stubs window.matchMedia exporting `setPrefersReducedMotion(bool)`, guarantees crypto.randomUUID, stubs URL.createObjectURL/revokeObjectURL, resets mocks after each test. src/test/renderWithProviders.tsx: `renderWithProviders(ui, { route = '/', routes? })` wraps a QueryClientProvider built with the 7.4.2 session-aware policy (retry disabled for tests) and a MemoryRouter at `route`; returns Testing Library utilities plus `{ user: userEvent.setup(), queryClient, router }`. src/test/mockClient.ts: a `vi.mock('@/lib/api/client')` factory exposing one `vi.fn()` per 7.4.1 client method plus `respond(method, value)` and `reject(method, { status, code, fieldErrors?, details? })` helpers that build the D18 error envelope `{ code, message, fieldErrors?, details?, retryable, requestId }`. src/test/fakeOutbox.ts: an in-memory stand-in for the 7.3 outbox interface (`enqueue`, `listUnsent`, `ack`, `purgeSession`) with an `emit(state)` helper. src/test/expectNoIdentifiers.ts: asserts a container's rendered text contains no UUID pattern and neither of the raw realm words 'demo'/'pilot' as standalone tokens. src/test/harness.test.tsx: smoke test for the harness itself.

### 7.1.6 — Accessibility baseline primitives, reduced motion, live-region policy, copy guard

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/index.css (@layer base): a global `:focus-visible` outline (2 px solid, 2 px offset, a token pair documented at >= 3:1 against page and control backgrounds) for every interactive element; theme color tokens with documented 4.5:1 text/background pairs; `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` forcing animation-duration and transition-duration to 0.01ms, animation-iteration-count 1, scroll-behavior auto. src/ui/Button.tsx: variant 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'quiet' exposed as data-variant (one primary per screen is enforced by the screens; the primitive only exposes the variant), type="button" by default, min-h-11 min-w-11, focus-visible ring. src/ui/VisuallyHidden.tsx. src/ui/LiveRegion.tsx: one aria-live="polite" region mounted once in Root.tsx (7.1.1) plus useAnnouncer().announce(text) via context; it is the only aria-live element the primitives create. src/lib/a11y/usePrefersReducedMotion.ts (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')). src/lib/a11y/useMilestoneAnnouncements.ts({ remainingSeconds, enabled = false, milestonesSeconds }) calls announce only when enabled and remainingSeconds crosses downward through a listed milestone; never on ordinary ticks. This primitive stays generic (any milestonesSeconds list); its `enabled` prop is sourced by callers from `me.preferences.milestoneAnnouncements` (7.1.2 MeContext; the preference itself defaults to false per D22), and production callers (8.4/8.5) pass `milestonesSeconds: [300, 0]` — 5:00 and 0:00 only, per D39. src/test/copyGuard.test.ts: reads apps/web/src/**/*.{ts,tsx} excluding *.test.*, *.test-d.* and src/test/**, strips // and /* */ comments, and fails on any case-insensitive match of 'streak', 'confetti', 'attention score', 'attention +', 'damaged attention', 'restart the program', 'start over'.
- **Unit tests:** Button variant/type/name; reduced-motion hook; CSS contains focus-visible + reduced-motion rules; zero live updates when disabled; exactly one milestone update when enabled; copy guard passes on tree and fails on fixture
- **Component:** Button({ variant, ...buttonProps }), VisuallyHidden, LiveRegion + useAnnouncer(), hooks usePrefersReducedMotion(), useMilestoneAnnouncements({ remainingSeconds, enabled, milestonesSeconds })
- **State:** Live-region text (single polite region); reduced-motion match state
- **API integration:** None
- **Validation:** No user input. Tests: src/ui/Button.test.tsx, src/lib/a11y/reducedMotion.test.ts (mocked matchMedia + fs read of index.css), src/lib/a11y/milestones.test.tsx (fake timers, rerender per second, count live-region mutations), src/test/copyGuard.test.ts (fs scan plus an inline fixture run through the exported scanner function).
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/ui src/lib/a11y src/test/copyGuard.test.ts` — 7 passed: 'Button primary renders type=button with data-variant=primary and its accessible name', 'usePrefersReducedMotion returns true when matchMedia reduce matches', 'index.css contains a :focus-visible rule and a prefers-reduced-motion: reduce block', 'with announcements disabled (the default, matching preferences.milestoneAnnouncements=false per D22), remaining 120 -> 0 over fake-timer seconds leaves the live region text unchanged (zero updates)', 'with enabled and milestones [60], exactly one live-region update occurs, at 60 s', 'copy guard passes on the current tree', 'copy guard scanner flags an inline fixture containing "streak" and ignores the same word inside a comment'.
- **After:** 7.1.1
