### 7.2.1 — lib/clock: remaining-time derivation from server fields and monotonic delta

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/clock/remaining.ts, pure and stateless (D5). Types: ServerTimerFields { startedAt: string; targetSeconds: number; pausedSeconds: number; currentPauseStartedAt: string | null; lifecycle: 'running'|'paused'|'awaiting_review'|'finalized'|'abandoned' } (mirrors focus_sessions.started_at, target_seconds, paused_seconds, current_pause_started_at, lifecycle); ClockAnchor { serverNowAtLoadMs: number; monotonicAtLoadMs: number } captured once per server response, with serverNowAtLoadMs = Date.parse(response.serverNow) — D20's serverNow ISO field carried on every session response. serverNowMs(anchor, monotonicNowMs) = serverNowAtLoadMs + (monotonicNowMs − monotonicAtLoadMs). deriveRemaining(fields, anchor, monotonicNowMs) -> { elapsedSeconds, remainingSeconds (clamped >= 0), deadlineReached, display: 'running'|'paused'|'deadline_reached'|'awaiting_review'|'ended' }: elapsed = serverNow − startedAt − pausedSeconds·1000 − (serverNow − currentPauseStartedAt when paused), floored to whole seconds; remaining = targetSeconds − elapsed; display = 'ended' for finalized/abandoned, 'awaiting_review' for awaiting_review, 'paused' for paused, 'deadline_reached' when running and remaining is 0, else 'running'. The display union deliberately has no 'completed' member (deadline reached means awaiting review pending the server's confirmation). elapsedMsForEvent(fields, anchor, monotonicNowMs): the same arithmetic in ms, unclamped, for event stamping. formatRemaining(seconds) -> 'mm:ss', flooring at '00:00'. No Date.now(), no setInterval, no accumulation anywhere in the module.
- **Unit tests:** remaining.test.ts cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** None (pure module src/lib/clock/remaining.ts)
- **State:** None; callers hold the anchor
- **API integration:** Consumes the timer fields and the server time from GET /sessions/active and GET /sessions/{id} responses (5.2); server time is the response's serverNow field (ISO string, D20) — ClockAnchor.serverNowAtLoadMs = Date.parse(response.serverNow)
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/lib/clock/remaining.test.ts with fixed ISO inputs, vi.setSystemTime for the Date.now-independence case, expectTypeOf for the display union.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/clock/remaining.test.ts` — 9 passed: 'refresh at 06:00 of a 15-minute block -> remainingSeconds 540', '240 paused seconds are excluded from elapsed', 'currently paused: remaining unchanged while monotonic advances 30 s', 'target passed -> remaining 0, deadlineReached true, display deadline_reached', 'display union contains no completed value (expectTypeOf)', 'Date.now mocked 1 h off has no effect on the result', 'elapsedMsForEvent 100 ms apart differs by exactly 100 ms', 'serverNowMs advances exactly with the monotonic delta', 'formatRemaining(-5) is 00:00 and formatRemaining(540) is 09:00'.
- **After:** 7.1.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session / Refresh during practice · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session / Refresh after target passed · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Deadline passed during sleep

### 7.2.2 — lib/clock: 5-second heartbeat gap detector with 60-second threshold

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/clock/gapDetector.ts: createGapDetector({ intervalMs = 5000, thresholdSeconds = 60, now = () => Date.now(), mono = () => performance.now(), onGap }) -> { start(), stop(), rebase() }. On each tick drift = (now() − wallAtBase) − (mono() − monoAtBase); when |drift| > thresholdSeconds·1000 call onGap({ gapSeconds: Math.round(drift / 1000), detectedAtMs: now() }) exactly once and rebase so the same gap is not re-reported; negative drift (clock set back) is reported too. The detector posts nothing and records nothing; 8.10 shows the prompt, posts POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap with the user's resolution and the clock_gap event is recorded there (D5). It ignores document visibility entirely and keeps running while hidden. Drift is the only signal this detector implements (D5): a frozen tab, or a suspended tab on a platform where performance.now() keeps advancing, produces no drift and therefore raises no prompt through this module. D16–D40 does not add a separate tick-gap (frozen-tab) signal, so this unit keeps its original drift-only design; recorded as a decision in notes.
- **Unit tests:** gapDetector.test.ts cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** None (pure module src/lib/clock/gapDetector.ts)
- **State:** Internal base timestamps only
- **API integration:** None (the consumer posts POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap in 8.10)
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/lib/clock/gapDetector.test.ts with fake timers and controllable wall/monotonic sources.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/clock/gapDetector.test.ts` — 8 passed with vi.useFakeTimers and injected now/mono: 'both clocks advance 10 min together over 120 ticks -> onGap never called', 'wall jumps +300 s at one tick -> onGap once with gapSeconds 300', 'wall jumps +59 s -> no gap (threshold boundary)', 'wall jumps +61 s -> gap', 'after a gap the next drift-free tick reports nothing (rebase)', 'clock set back 120 s -> gapSeconds -120', 'stop() -> no further ticks', 'visibilitychange with document.hidden true during the run -> onGap not called and no other side effect'.
- **After:** 7.1.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Laptop slept during a benchmark

### 7.2.3 — useSessionClock hook: ticking re-derivation plus gap detection

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/clock/useSessionClock.ts: useSessionClock(input: { fields: ServerTimerFields; serverNowMs: number } | null) — serverNowMs is `Date.parse(response.serverNow)` from the same session response the fields came from (D20's `serverNow` ISO field on GET /sessions/active and GET /sessions/{id}). A new ClockAnchor { serverNowAtLoadMs: input.serverNowMs, monotonicAtLoadMs: performance.now() } is captured whenever input.serverNowMs changes (load, pause/resume, transition responses). A 1000 ms setInterval only bumps a tick counter so deriveRemaining runs again; missed or throttled ticks lose nothing. Returns { remainingSeconds, display, deadlineReached, stampEvent(): { elapsedMs, occurredAt } (elapsedMsForEvent + new Date(serverNowMs(anchor, performance.now())).toISOString(), for the POST /sessions/{id}/events item shape), gap: { gapSeconds, detectedAtMs } | null, acknowledgeGap() }. Mounts createGapDetector (7.2.2) while lifecycle is running or paused; stops it on awaiting_review, finalized, abandoned or null input. With null input the hook returns null, registers no interval and no detector (no timer for a session the server has not acknowledged). No visibilitychange listener; emits no events; deadlineReached is never turned into a completion.
- **Unit tests:** useSessionClock.test.tsx cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** Hook useSessionClock(input) in src/lib/clock/useSessionClock.ts
- **State:** Anchor ref, 1 s tick counter, pending gap state
- **API integration:** Reads session fields and serverNow (D20) from useActiveSession/useSession results; makes no requests
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/lib/clock/useSessionClock.test.tsx using renderHook, vi.useFakeTimers with a controllable performance.now stub, and a fixture input.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/clock/useSessionClock.test.tsx` — 7 passed (renderHook + fake timers + performance.now stub): 'advancing 90 s reduces remainingSeconds by 90', 'performance.now jumps +90 s while only three 1 s ticks fire -> still −90 s (no accumulation)', 'new input with lifecycle paused freezes remaining', 'past the deadline display is deadline_reached and no completion value is ever returned', 'wall jump +300 s -> gap.gapSeconds 300; acknowledgeGap() clears it', 'document.hidden true plus visibilitychange for 3 min -> gap null, no callback invoked, remaining keeps counting (hidden tab creates nothing)', 'null input returns null and no setInterval or detector tick occurs over 60 s of fake time'.
- **After:** 7.2.1, 7.2.2
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Deadline passed during sleep

### 7.3.1 — lib/outbox: IndexedDB store with expiry, local-write failure path, and purge-by-exclusion

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Add dev dep fake-indexeddb. src/lib/outbox/store.ts: database 'attention-lab-outbox' version 1 with a single object store 'events' (keyPath clientEventId; indexes bySession on sessionId, byCreatedAt on createdAt) holding records of exactly { clientEventId, sessionId, type, elapsedMs, occurredAt, details?, createdAt } — the POST /sessions/{id}/events item shape from 2.7 plus sessionId and createdAt (D6: the buffer holds only { sessionId, events[] }). API: openOutbox({ now = Date.now } = {}) opens the DB and runs expireOlderThan(now() − 7·86_400_000) before resolving; enqueue(sessionId, draft) assigns clientEventId = crypto.randomUUID() BEFORE the put and resolves { clientEventId }; any IndexedDB failure (indexedDB undefined, open blocked/error, put error, QuotaExceededError) rejects with OutboxWriteError { draft, clientEventId } so the caller can send it directly; listUnsent(sessionId) resolves rows ordered by elapsedMs ascending; ack(clientEventIds[]) deletes those rows; purgeSession(sessionId) deletes all rows for the session; purgeOtherSessions(keepSessionId: string | null) deletes every row whose sessionId !== keepSessionId (or every row when keepSessionId is null) — added to support D17's boot-time cleanup in 7.4.2, the module's only caller for it; expireOlderThan(ms) deletes rows with createdAt < ms. No tokens, preferences, note text or request ids are ever written; `details` carries only the contract's structured fields (e.g. alsoOffTask).
- **Unit tests:** store.test.ts cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** None (module src/lib/outbox/store.ts exporting openOutbox, enqueue, listUnsent, ack, purgeSession, purgeOtherSessions, expireOlderThan, OutboxWriteError)
- **State:** IndexedDB store 'events' only
- **API integration:** None; the record shape matches the events contract so 7.3.2 can post rows unchanged
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/lib/outbox/store.test.ts with fake-indexeddb, a failing put stub for the write-failure case and `delete globalThis.indexedDB` for the unavailable case.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/outbox/store.test.ts` — 9 passed with fake-indexeddb/auto: 'enqueue assigns a UUID clientEventId before the put resolves and persists it', 'listUnsent returns only that session's rows in elapsedMs order', 'ack removes exactly the given ids', 'purgeSession leaves zero rows for the session and leaves another session's rows untouched', 'purgeOtherSessions(keepId) removes rows for every other session while keeping keepId's rows, and purgeOtherSessions(null) removes every row', 'rows with createdAt older than 7 days are removed on open and 6-day-old rows are kept', 'a failing put rejects with OutboxWriteError carrying the draft and clientEventId and nothing is stored', 'indexedDB undefined rejects with OutboxWriteError', 'stored record keys are a subset of {clientEventId, sessionId, type, elapsedMs, occurredAt, details, createdAt} and never include note, token, requestId or preference keys'.
- **After:** 7.1.1, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged / Buffer after sync · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Local storage unavailable · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

### 7.4.1 — Typed API client from shared TypeBox contracts with error mapping

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/api/routes.ts + src/lib/api/client.ts: request/response types are `Static<>` of the contract schemas in packages/shared/src/contracts (2.7); base '/api/v1'; fetch with credentials 'same-origin', Accept application/json (Content-Type application/json when a body is sent), signal AbortSignal.timeout(30_000). Methods mirror design.md's API table one-to-one: me.get, me.patchPreferences(body), demo.clock(body), demo.loadScenario(name, body), demo.reset, programs.create(body, { idempotencyKey }), programs.current, programs.patch(id, body), programs.putSlots(id, body) [PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots], programs.createRevision(id, body), programs.today(id), sessions.create(body, { idempotencyKey }), sessions.active (204 -> null), sessions.get(id), sessions.postEvents(id, body), sessions.void(id, clientEventId) [POST /sessions/{id}/events/{clientEventId}/void], sessions.transition(id, body), sessions.clockGap(id, body), sessions.putAgentPlan(id, body), sessions.recall(id, body, { idempotencyKey }), sessions.finalize(id, body, { idempotencyKey }), sessions.amend(id, body) [POST /sessions/{id}/amendments], days.get(programId, date), days.put(programId, date, body), report.get(programId), export.get(programId, 'csv' | 'markdown') -> string, research.cards. The `Idempotency-Key` header is sent only when the option is given; src/lib/api/newIdempotencyKey.ts wraps crypto.randomUUID() so callers create one key per logical mutation and reuse it on every retry. src/lib/api/errors.ts: ApiError base { status, code, message, retryable, requestId, fieldErrors?, details? } from the D18 envelope { code, message, fieldErrors?, details?, retryable, requestId }: 400/422 -> ValidationError (keeps fieldErrors), 404 -> NotFoundError, 409 -> ConflictError (keeps the full parsed body including details, e.g. { activeSessionId } for active_session_exists or { expected, stored } for event_count_mismatch per D18), 429 -> RateLimitError, >= 500 or unparseable body -> ServerError (retryable from the body, default true), fetch rejection or timeout -> NetworkError (retryable true). requestId is a property for diagnostics and is never interpolated into `message`. No localStorage, sessionStorage or document.cookie access anywhere under src/lib/api.
- **Unit tests:** client.test.ts: typed GET, 204 -> null, stable Idempotency-Key across retries, NetworkError on rejection, ConflictError body/details, ValidationError fieldErrors, NotFoundError, no browser-storage access; client.test-d.ts: realm in a create body is a type error
- **Component:** No React component; modules src/lib/api/{client,routes,errors,newIdempotencyKey}.ts exporting `api`, `newIdempotencyKey` and the ApiError hierarchy
- **State:** Stateless; idempotency keys are owned by callers (hooks/screens) and passed in
- **API integration:** Every route in design.md's API table with its method and body; errors map 400/422 -> ValidationError, 404 -> NotFoundError, 409 -> ConflictError (full D18 body retained, including details), 429 -> RateLimitError, >= 500 -> ServerError, network/timeout -> NetworkError (retryable)
- **Validation:** No user input; bodies are typed from the shared contracts so a `realm` field or any unknown property is a compile error (and the API rejects unknown properties at runtime). Tests: src/lib/api/client.test.ts with vi.stubGlobal('fetch') for the 8 runtime cases, and src/lib/api/client.test-d.ts for the compile-time realm rejection.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/api && npm run typecheck -w @attention-lab/web` — vitest reports 8 passed in src/lib/api/client.test.ts: 'GET /api/v1/me resolves a typed MeResponse', 'sessions.active maps 204 to null', 'Idempotency-Key header equals the option and is byte-identical across two calls with the same key', 'fetch rejection yields NetworkError with retryable=true and no value', '409 yields ConflictError exposing body.details (e.g. expected/stored or activeSessionId)', '422 yields ValidationError.fieldErrors', '404 yields NotFoundError', 'no localStorage/sessionStorage/document.cookie access during any request (spies never called)'; typecheck exits 0 with src/lib/api/client.test-d.ts holding an `@ts-expect-error` on a programs.create body literal that includes `realm` (an unused directive fails the typecheck).
- **After:** 7.1.1, 2.7, 3.2
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start while offline · session-recovery: Stale writes conflict instead of overwriting · identity-realm: Every user-owned record carries a realm / Client cannot choose the realm · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely · app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing

### 7.3.2 — lib/outbox: batched flush, replay on load, direct send

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/outbox/flush.ts: flush(sessionId, api) reads listUnsent, chunks <= 100 rows in elapsedMs order, POSTs /sessions/{id}/events per chunk sequentially with { events: rows minus sessionId/createdAt }, treats accepted[] and duplicates[] both as acknowledged and calls ack for them; returns { outcome: 'synced' | 'network_error' | 'rejected', acceptedCount, duplicateCount, offendingClientEventId?, fieldErrors? }. On NetworkError: stop, keep the remaining rows, outcome network_error. On ValidationError (422, impossible offset): stop, keep rows, outcome rejected; offendingClientEventId = chunk[i].clientEventId when a fieldErrors key matches /^events\[(\d+)\]/, else undefined — flush.ts's job stops at reporting which event was rejected; whether the consuming screen (8.5) retries, drops, or holds that entry is that screen's decision (D16–D40 does not settle it; recorded in notes). Nothing is acked before the response resolves. One in-flight flush per sessionId (concurrent callers get the same promise). replayOnLoad(activeSessionId, api) = openOutbox() then flush(activeSessionId, api) — the 'replay outbox' step of design.md's Reload flow, and D17's boot-time call site (7.4.2). sendDirect(sessionId, draft, api) assigns clientEventId = crypto.randomUUID() and POSTs a one-event batch without touching IndexedDB (the could-not-save fallback: keep trying the server directly).
- **Unit tests:** flush.test.ts cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** None (module src/lib/outbox/flush.ts exporting flush, replayOnLoad, sendDirect)
- **State:** In-flight promise map per sessionId
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/events with <= 100 events per batch; { accepted[], duplicates[] } -> ack; NetworkError -> keep and report; 422 -> keep, report offending id
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/lib/outbox/flush.test.ts with fake-indexeddb and a mocked api.sessions.postEvents returning accepted/duplicates, rejecting with NetworkError, or rejecting with ValidationError carrying fieldErrors.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/outbox/flush.test.ts` — 7 passed (fake-indexeddb + vi.fn api): '250 queued -> three POSTs of 100/100/50 in elapsedMs order, all acked, buffer empty', 'retry after a network error re-sends identical clientEventIds; the server lists them in duplicates[] -> acked, duplicateCount 100, buffer empty', 'network error on the second batch keeps the second batch and returns network_error with acceptedCount 100', '422 keeps rows and returns rejected with offendingClientEventId resolved from fieldErrors', 'two concurrent flush() calls produce one POST', 'sendDirect posts one event with a pre-assigned clientEventId and writes nothing to IndexedDB', 'rows remain present while the POST promise is pending (no ack before response)'.
- **After:** 7.3.1, 7.4.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Batch retried · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Impossible offset · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Acknowledged after retry · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged / Buffer after sync · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session

### 7.3.3 — Sync-state derivation (Pending/Saved/Could-not-save) and useOutbox hook

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/outbox/syncState.ts: deriveSyncState({ unsentCount, inFlight, localWriteFailures }) -> 'could_not_save' when any unacknowledged local write failure exists; else 'pending' when unsentCount > 0 or inFlight; else 'saved' (dispatching a request never yields saved — only an ack does). SYNC_STATE_COPY = { pending: 'Pending', saved: 'Saved', could_not_save: 'Entries could not be saved on this device' }. src/lib/outbox/useOutbox.ts: useOutbox(sessionId, api) -> { record(draft: { type, elapsedMs, occurredAt, details? }): Promise<{ clientEventId }>, retry(), sendDirectNow(), markVoided(clientEventId), state, stateLabel, unsentCount, recordedCount, tallies: { offTask, external, agentCheck } }. record(): dispatches the reducer synchronously (tallies update in the same tick, before any await), then enqueue (7.3.1) and a flush (7.3.2); on OutboxWriteError adds the draft to localWriteFailures (state could_not_save) and sendDirectNow() posts those drafts via sendDirect, clearing them on success. On mount with a non-null sessionId the hook calls replayOnLoad once (inFlight true until it settles) — a per-screen defence-in-depth alongside 7.4.2's app-boot replay; both are idempotent since flush.ts dedupes concurrent calls per session. Tally rule per D11: off_task -> offTask +1; agent_check -> agentCheck +1 and, when details.alsoOffTask is true, offTask +1 as the same single event; external -> external +1; pause, resume, clock_gap and visibility change no tally; voided ids are excluded; there is no combined total. Tallies are counts of events the user recorded and are never a ReportedCount; per D31 a review screen (8.4/8.6) prefills a count field with the tally and countMethod 'event' when recordedCount >= 1 for that session, otherwise leaves it blank — this hook exposes tallies and recordedCount so those screens can apply that rule without recomputing from raw events.
- **Unit tests:** syncState.test.ts and useOutbox.test.tsx cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** Hook useOutbox(sessionId, api) in src/lib/outbox/useOutbox.ts; pure deriveSyncState and SYNC_STATE_COPY in syncState.ts
- **State:** useReducer: recorded events list, voided set, unsentCount, inFlight flag, localWriteFailures list; derived state, stateLabel, recordedCount and tallies
- **API integration:** flush (POST /sessions/{id}/events) after each record, on mount (replayOnLoad) and via retry(); sendDirect via sendDirectNow(); no other routes
- **Validation:** No free-text input; drafts are typed event shapes from the contract. Tests: src/lib/outbox/syncState.test.ts (pure derivation, copy regex) and src/lib/outbox/useOutbox.test.tsx (renderHook, fake-indexeddb with a deferred put, mocked api).
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/outbox/syncState.test.ts src/lib/outbox/useOutbox.test.tsx` — 10 passed: 'record() increments offTask synchronously before a deferred IDB put resolves (asserted within 100 ms)', 'two events then a network error -> state pending, unsentCount 2, and retry() re-attempts the flush', 'state stays pending while a flush is in flight even though the request was dispatched', 'saved only after acknowledgement with unsentCount 0', 'OutboxWriteError -> could_not_save with stateLabel "Entries could not be saved on this device"; sendDirectNow() posts the draft with its pre-assigned clientEventId and clears the failure', 'agent_check with alsoOffTask true -> offTask +1 and agentCheck +1; alsoOffTask false -> agentCheck +1 only; the tallies object has no combined key', 'pause, resume, clock_gap and visibility events change no tally', 'markVoided removes the event from tallies and recordedCount', 'mounting with two persisted unsent rows flushes them once (replayOnLoad) and ends saved', 'SYNC_STATE_COPY values match no UUID fragment /[0-9a-f]{8}-/i'.
- **After:** 7.3.2
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Connection lost mid-session · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Local storage unavailable · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Acknowledged after retry · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session · app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing / Sync state display

### 7.3.4 — finalizeWithSync and abandonWithPurge helpers

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/outbox/finalize.ts: finalizeWithSync({ sessionId, idempotencyKey, knownServerEventCount, review, api }): (1) unsent = listUnsent(sessionId); if unsent.length > 100 run flush(sessionId, api) first and re-list; lastBatch = remaining unsent (<= 100 rows in contract item shape); expectedEventCount = knownServerEventCount + lastBatch.length (D21: knownServerEventCount is the session's eventCount field); (2) POST /sessions/{id}/finalize { expectedEventCount, lastBatch, review } with the caller-owned Idempotency-Key; (3) on ConflictError with code event_count_mismatch and body { expected, stored }: run flush(sessionId, api) (the server dedupes by client_event_id), then retry ONCE with the SAME key, expectedEventCount = stored + flush.acceptedCount and lastBatch = [] (duplicates were already inside stored); (4) on success purgeSession(sessionId) and return the server's { session, review, eligible, exclusionReasons } unchanged — no local finalized state is ever synthesized; (5) NetworkError from either call is rethrown with rows untouched so the caller retries later with the same key; any other error (including a second consecutive mismatch on the retry) rejects, purges nothing. abandonWithPurge({ sessionId, expectedVersion, api }): POST /sessions/{id}/transitions { expectedVersion, type: 'abandon' } then purgeSession (abandon discards unsent local entries; the server keeps minimal attempt status).
- **Unit tests:** finalize.test.ts cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** None (module src/lib/outbox/finalize.ts exporting finalizeWithSync, abandonWithPurge)
- **State:** None beyond the outbox rows; the idempotency key is created once by the calling hook (7.3.5) via newIdempotencyKey()
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/finalize (IK) with expectedEventCount and lastBatch; ConflictError event_count_mismatch {expected, stored} -> flush then retry same key with stored + acceptedCount; POST /sessions/{id}/transitions type abandon
- **Validation:** No user input (the review body is built by 8.4/8.6). Test: src/lib/outbox/finalize.test.ts with a mocked api.sessions.finalize sequence [409 mismatch, 200], a mocked postEvents, and Idempotency-Key capture.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/outbox/finalize.test.ts` — 6 passed (fake-indexeddb + mocked api with header capture): '409 event_count_mismatch {expected 5, stored 4} with one unsent row -> flush posts that row (accepted 1) -> retry carries the identical Idempotency-Key with expectedEventCount 5 and lastBatch [] -> success -> buffer purged', 'NetworkError on the first call rethrows and leaves rows; a second call with the same key sends the same header', 'success purges the buffer and resolves exactly the server payload (deep-equal, no added fields)', 'a non-mismatch 409 or ServerError leaves rows in place, purges nothing and rejects without any finalized result', 'with 130 unsent rows the pre-flush posts 100 and lastBatch has 30', 'abandonWithPurge posts { type: "abandon", expectedVersion } and leaves zero rows for the session'.
- **After:** 7.3.2, 7.4.1
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Finalize retried · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged

### 7.4.2 — Query keys, session-mode provider, session-aware refetch policy, boot-time outbox replay and purge

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/query/keys.ts: queryKeys.me, .programs.current, .programs.today(programId), .sessions.active, .sessions.byId(id), .days(programId, date), .report(programId), .research.cards. src/lib/query/sessionMode.tsx: createSessionModeRef() -> { isActive(): boolean; set(session | null) } (created in providers.tsx before the QueryClient so the policy functions can read it synchronously); SessionModeProvider({ modeRef, children }) is mounted in Root.tsx (inside the router) and runs useQuery({ queryKey: queryKeys.sessions.active, queryFn: api.sessions.active }) once for the whole app; an effect calls modeRef.set(data) so isActive() = data !== null && data.lifecycle in {running, paused, awaiting_review}. On the FIRST successful resolution of that query (a ref guards this to run exactly once per app boot, D17): when data is a session, call replayOnLoad(data.id, api) (7.3.2) then purgeOtherSessions(data.id) (7.3.1); when data is null, call purgeOtherSessions(null) (purges every outbox row, since D6's buffer holds only an active session's events and none is active). No navigation or redirect happens as part of this — screens alone decide what to render (Today shows ActiveSessionCard; a session route re-derives remaining time from server fields). A later mutation that writes queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessions.active, session) or invalidates that key flips isActive() through the same subscription but does NOT re-run the boot replay/purge. Route location is NOT part of the flag (a /benchmark/:slotId ready screen with no session is not session mode). useSessionMode() exposes { isActive, activeSession }. src/lib/query/client.ts: createQueryClient(modeRef) with defaultOptions.queries { refetchOnWindowFocus: () => !modeRef.isActive(), refetchOnReconnect: () => !modeRef.isActive(), refetchInterval: false, staleTime: 30_000, retry: (n, err) => err instanceof NetworkError && n < 2 } and defaultOptions.mutations { retry: false }. src/lib/query/hooks.ts: useMe() (queryKeys.me -> api.me.get), useActiveSession() (reads the provider's query), useSession(id) (queryKeys.sessions.byId(id) -> api.sessions.get). No visibilitychange listener exists under src/lib/query and visibility never triggers a mutation. Replace the placeholder QueryClient in providers.tsx (7.1.1) with createQueryClient(modeRef) and wrap Root's children with <SessionModeProvider modeRef={modeRef}>.
- **Unit tests:** policy.test.tsx: no refetch on focus in session, refetch outside session, retry policy; hooks.test.tsx: 204 -> null, awaiting_review active / finalized inactive, setQueryData flips the flag, no event POST on visibility; sessionMode.test.tsx: boot replay+purge cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** SessionModeProvider({ modeRef, children }), hooks useSessionMode/useMe/useActiveSession/useSession; providers.tsx and Root.tsx updated
- **State:** TanStack Query cache keyed by queryKeys; session-mode ref derived from the queryKeys.sessions.active cache entry (lifecycle only); a one-shot boot-replay guard ref (D17)
- **API integration:** GET /me, GET /sessions/active (204 -> null), GET /sessions/{id} through the typed client; on the first active-session resolution, replayOnLoad (7.3.2, POST /sessions/{id}/events) and purgeOtherSessions (7.3.1, IndexedDB only) run once; no refetch on focus/reconnect while a session is active per D15
- **Validation:** No user input. Tests render QueryClientProvider (createQueryClient) + createMemoryRouter whose root element mounts SessionModeProvider, with vi.mock of src/lib/api/client (sessions.active returning a fixture or null), fake-indexeddb seeded with rows for the active session and a stale session, and a vi.fn() query function; focus/visibility are simulated by defining document.visibilityState and dispatching the events; call counts, modeRef.isActive() and remaining outbox rows are asserted.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/query/policy.test.tsx src/lib/query/hooks.test.tsx src/lib/query/sessionMode.test.tsx` — 11 passed: policy.test.tsx (3, unchanged): 'with a running session in the cache, dispatching window focus and document visibilitychange leaves a stale query fn called exactly once (no refetch)', 'with no active session and staleTime 0, visibilitychange visible triggers exactly one refetch', 'NetworkError is retried at most twice and ConflictError is never retried'; hooks.test.tsx (4, unchanged): 'useActiveSession maps 204 to null and isActive() is false', 'lifecycle awaiting_review keeps isActive() true and finalized makes it false', 'setQueryData(queryKeys.sessions.active, runningSession) flips isActive() to true before the next focus event', 'visibility changes during an active session call api.sessions.postEvents and api.sessions.transition zero times'; sessionMode.test.tsx (4, new, D17): 'on boot with an active running session, replayOnLoad is called exactly once with that session id and purgeOtherSessions is called with that id', 'outbox rows belonging to a stale session are removed on boot while the active session's rows survive', 'on boot with no active session, purgeOtherSessions(null) removes every outbox row', 'a later mutation that flips isActive() via setQueryData does not trigger a second replay or purge'.
- **After:** 7.4.1, 7.3.1, 7.3.2
- **Spec refs:** app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged / Buffer after sync

### 7.1.5 — Playwright shell smoke test and reusable support helpers (demo, clock, copy, a11y)

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** e2e/smoke/shell.spec.ts plus a `webServer` array in e2e/playwright.config.ts (from 1.5.3): API via `npm run dev:api` with the identity-mode and host variables named in .env.example (1.3) set to local-demo/127.0.0.1, readiness url http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/v1/me (200 in local-demo); web via `npm run dev:web`, url http://127.0.0.1:5173 (the Vite proxy from 7.1.1 carries /api) — this dev-server pair is separate from 1.5.3's build-and-serve default project, a decision recorded in notes since design.md's single-origin static-serving path has no owning task. Tests: (1) desktop 1280x800: goto /today; the aside named 'Demonstration data notice' is visible and toBeInViewport() with window.scrollY === 0; the links Today/Progress/Research/Settings are visible inside nav[data-placement=rail]. (2) mobile 390x844: banner in viewport without scrolling; nav[data-placement=bottom] visible; document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= clientWidth (no horizontal scroll). (3) goto /focus/00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000: banner visible and no navigation landmark. Support helpers (D16, absorbing 9.1.1's demo fixture and 9.1.2's clock/copy/a11y helpers so every later e2e unit imports rather than re-creates them): e2e/support/demo.ts exports `test` (extended @playwright/test) with a `demo` fixture over an APIRequestContext on http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/v1, typed against the 2.7 Static<> contracts: reset() (POST /demo/reset -> 204), load(name) (POST /demo/scenarios/{name}/load -> {programId}), setClock(offsetSeconds) (POST /demo/clock; sets the ABSOLUTE offset, distinct from clock.ts's relative advance), offset() (demoClockOffsetSeconds from GET /me), current() (GET /programs/current), today(programId) (GET /programs/{id}/today), active() (GET /sessions/active; 204 -> null), session(id) (GET /sessions/{id}), report(programId) (GET /programs/{id}/report), readyProgram() (POST /programs with a UUID Idempotency-Key, baselineDate = today in the GET /me timezone, practiceTargetSeconds 900; PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots with expectedVersion, four refs, baseline A 09:00 / B 14:00, finals defaulted; pins the demo clock to 09:00 local on Day 0; returns {programId, slots, revision}). e2e/support/clock.ts: installClock(page) = page.clock.install() before the first goto; advance(page, demo, seconds) = page.clock.runFor(seconds*1000) then demo.setClock(await demo.offset() + seconds) (relative, moves browser and server clock together); sleep(page, demo, seconds) = page.clock.setSystemTime(Date.now()+seconds*1000) WITHOUT running timers, then demo.setClock(offset()+seconds), then page.clock.runFor(5000) so the 5 s heartbeat (7.2.2) observes the drift; hideTab(page)/showTab(page) via addInitScript redefining document.visibilityState/hidden as overridable getters and dispatching visibilitychange. e2e/support/copy.ts: expectNoPunitiveCopy(page) (body text matches no /streak|confetti|restart the program|damaged|attention \+|attention score|confidence interval|significan/i), expectDemoBanner(page) (element containing 'demonstration data' and 'not a real measurement', fully inside the viewport at scrollY 0), expectNoTechnicalIds(locator) (text matches no UUID regex). e2e/support/a11y.ts: expectNoSeriousViolations(page, label) via @axe-core/playwright AxeBuilder.withTags(['wcag2a','wcag2aa','wcag21aa','wcag22aa']), failing on any violation with impact serious or critical and attaching the JSON; expectFocusVisible(page) (activeElement is not body, matches :focus-visible, and its outline-width or box-shadow differs from the same element blurred). These four support files import only Playwright/axe types and the 2.7 shared contracts, so they typecheck with no server running (npm run typecheck:e2e, established by 1.5.3); the shell smoke spec is the only test here that needs the dev servers.
- **Verify:** `npm run typecheck:e2e` — exit 0, typechecking e2e/support/{demo,clock,copy,a11y}.ts alongside the smoke spec with no server running. Then from the repo root with .env present and `npm run db:up && npm run db:push` done: `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/smoke/shell.spec.ts` — output '3 passed'.
- **After:** 7.1.3, 7.1.4, 1.3, 1.4, 1.5, 2.7, 3.5
- **Spec refs:** identity-realm: Demo mode is permanently and unmistakably labeled / Banner on every screen · app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only / Navigation on Today · app-shell: Responsive layout

### 7.3.5 — useFinalizeSession hook: wraps finalizeWithSync/abandonWithPurge with a three-mismatch cap

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/outbox/useFinalizeSession.ts: useFinalizeSession(sessionId) -> { finalize(review): Promise<void>, abandon(expectedVersion, reason?): Promise<void>, status: 'idle'|'flushing'|'submitting'|'mismatch'|'unsaved_entries'|'error'|'success', mismatch: { expected, stored } | null, mismatchAttempts: number, retry(), result: { session, review, eligible, exclusionReasons } | null } (D16, absorbing 8.10.5; consumed by 8.4.5's Scoring screen and 8.6.2's PracticeReview). On the first call to finalize() for this sessionId the hook creates one Idempotency-Key (crypto.randomUUID) and persists it in sessionStorage under `finalize:{sessionId}` — the only browser-storage write besides IndexedDB (D16) — so a reload before success reuses it; the key is read back from sessionStorage on mount if present. finalize(review) sets status 'flushing', reads `session.eventCount` from the cached GET /sessions/{id} result (D20/D21: expectedEventCount derives from session.eventCount plus the rows finalizeWithSync itself flushes and includes as lastBatch), sets status 'submitting' and calls finalizeWithSync({ sessionId, idempotencyKey, knownServerEventCount: session.eventCount, review, api }) (7.3.4 already performs its own flush plus one same-key retry on event_count_mismatch). If that call resolves, status becomes 'success', the sessionStorage key is removed (finalizeWithSync already purged the outbox), and result holds the server payload unchanged. If it rejects with ConflictError event_count_mismatch, mismatchAttempts increments and, while mismatchAttempts < 3, the hook calls finalizeWithSync again with the SAME key (status passes through 'mismatch' then back to 'submitting'); on the 3rd consecutive mismatch it stops calling the server, sets status 'unsaved_entries' ('Some entries are still unsaved. Retry.') and waits for the caller's retry() (which re-enters the same loop without resetting the counter). Any other rejection (NetworkError, ServerError, a non-mismatch 409) sets status 'error' and keeps the key and mismatchAttempts for retry(). abandon(expectedVersion, reason?) calls abandonWithPurge({ sessionId, expectedVersion, api }) (7.3.4); on success it also removes the sessionStorage finalize key and sets status 'success' with result null (there is no review payload for an abandon); on failure it sets status 'error' without purging. Nothing but the finalize:{sessionId} string key is ever written to sessionStorage or localStorage.
- **Unit tests:** useFinalizeSession.test.tsx cases as listed in verify
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend hook wrapping 7.3.4's already-integration-tested finalizeWithSync/abandonWithPurge; server round-trip covered by 9.1's acceptance journeys
- **Component:** Hook useFinalizeSession(sessionId) in src/lib/outbox/useFinalizeSession.ts
- **State:** sessionStorage key finalize:{sessionId} holding the idempotency key; in-hook mismatchAttempts counter (max 3); status/result/mismatch state; reads the cached GET /sessions/{id} query for eventCount
- **API integration:** Calls only finalizeWithSync and abandonWithPurge (7.3.4) — never POST /sessions/{id}/finalize or the transitions route directly; event_count_mismatch retried up to 3 times with the same key; any other error surfaces as status 'error' with the key retained for retry()
- **Validation:** No user input (the review body is built by 8.4/8.6). Rules: the same Idempotency-Key is used across every retry regardless of mismatch count; no 'success' status is ever reported without a 200 from finalizeWithSync; sessionStorage holds only the one finalize key. Test src/lib/outbox/useFinalizeSession.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, mocked finalizeWithSync/abandonWithPurge): cases listed in verify.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/outbox/useFinalizeSession.test.tsx` — 7 passed: '409 mismatch then success: finalizeWithSync is called twice with the identical Idempotency-Key and status ends success', 'three consecutive mismatches yield status unsaved_entries and a 4th finalizeWithSync call is never made until retry()', 'retry() after unsaved_entries calls finalizeWithSync again with the same key', 'a reload before success reads the key back from sessionStorage instead of minting a new one', 'success removes the sessionStorage key and sessionStorage holds no other key', 'abandon() calls abandonWithPurge with the given expectedVersion and reason, and on success sets status success with result null', 'a non-mismatch rejection sets status error and retry() reuses the same key'.
- **After:** 7.3.4, 7.4.1, 7.4.2
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Finalize retried · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged / Buffer after sync · identity-realm: Private data is never written to logs or stored insecurely

### 7.4.3 — Route-leave guard warning when a session is active

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/app/SessionLeaveGuard.tsx mounted in Root.tsx (inside SessionModeProvider, above both layouts): useBlocker(({ currentLocation, nextLocation }) => modeRef.isActive() && isSessionPath(currentLocation.pathname) && !isSessionPath(nextLocation.pathname)), where isSessionPath = pathname starts with /benchmark/, /focus/ or /review/ — so focus -> review and recall -> scoring proceed, and a user who already chose Leave anyway is not re-prompted on every later navigation. When blocked, renders a Radix AlertDialog titled 'A session is in progress' (final copy per D38, no longer a placeholder), primary ui/Button 'Return to session' (blocker.reset(); focus returns to the trigger) and secondary 'Leave anyway' (blocker.proceed()); Escape behaves as Return. The guard makes no API call — no transition, no abandon, no event — so the server session is unaffected by navigation. No beforeunload handler (leaving via the browser is permitted; reload recovery is 7.3.2/7.4.2).
- **Unit tests:** SessionLeaveGuard.test.tsx cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** SessionLeaveGuard (no props) using useBlocker, Radix AlertDialog, ui/Button
- **State:** Blocker state from React Router; session-mode ref from 7.4.2
- **API integration:** None; asserted by spies that no session route is called
- **Validation:** No user input. Test: src/app/SessionLeaveGuard.test.tsx with createMemoryRouter (data router) at /focus/s1, the active session seeded with queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessions.active, runningSession), vi.mock of src/lib/api/client with spies, user-event for buttons and keyboard.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/app/SessionLeaveGuard.test.tsx` — 6 passed: 'at /focus/s1 with a running session in the cache, navigate(/progress) shows the dialog, location stays /focus/s1, and api.sessions.transition and api.sessions.postEvents were never called', 'Return to session closes the dialog and stays', 'Leave anyway navigates to /progress and isActive() remains true', '/focus/s1 -> /review/s1 is not blocked', 'no active session -> no dialog on navigation', 'Tab reaches both dialog buttons and Escape returns to the session'.
- **After:** 7.4.2, 7.1.6
- **Spec refs:** app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only / Leaving a session by URL · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available

### 7.4.4 — useStartSession: THE start hook, one idempotency key per logical start

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** src/lib/query/useStartSession.ts: useStartSession() -> { start(body): Promise<Session>, status: 'idle' | 'pending' | 'error' | 'success', error: ApiError | null, activeSessionId: string | null } — the single start hook for both Today's practice form (8.2) and Benchmark ready's Start (8.3); D16 names it THE start hook, absorbing 8.10.6 so neither screen builds its own. A logical start owns one key: keyRef = newIdempotencyKey() is created on the first start(body) call and reused for every retry of an UNCHANGED body (the hook's own automatic retry — at most 2 further attempts on NetworkError, 1 s apart — or the user pressing Retry, which calls start() again with the same body); when the caller passes a body that differs from the one the current key was minted for (deep-equal check), the hook mints a fresh key instead of replaying stale content under the old one — an edited draft after a failed start is a new logical start. Every attempt sends the identical Idempotency-Key for identical content via api.sessions.create(body, { idempotencyKey }). On success: queryClient.setQueryData(queryKeys.sessions.active, session) (this alone flips session mode in 7.4.2), invalidate queryKeys.programs.current and queryKeys.programs.today(body.programId), clear keyRef, resolve the session. On ConflictError with code active_session_exists: read activeSessionId from the error's details.activeSessionId (D18's error envelope — never a bespoke top-level field) and set it on the hook's state, and invalidate queryKeys.sessions.active so this tab renders the existing session (design.md's 'Second tab' flow; 8.2.5's ActiveSessionCard reads this state). On NetworkError after the automatic retries: status 'error', error retained, keyRef retained, active-session cache untouched — the screen (8.2/8.3) shows that the session could not be started and offers Retry. On ValidationError (422 slot rules): status 'error' with error.fieldErrors exposed for the screen to render beside the named field; keyRef retained since the content has not changed yet. Nothing here starts a clock or navigates; a timer exists only once useSessionClock (7.2.3) receives a server session, and navigation to the session route is the caller's job once start() resolves.
- **Unit tests:** useStartSession.test.tsx cases as listed in verify
- **Component:** Hook useStartSession() in src/lib/query/useStartSession.ts — the sole start hook for Today (8.2) and Benchmark ready (8.3)
- **State:** Key ref per logical start (minted per distinct body, deep-equal checked); mutation status/error; activeSessionId from a 409's details; writes queryKeys.sessions.active on success
- **API integration:** POST /sessions (IK) via api.sessions.create; 409 active_session_exists -> details.activeSessionId (D18) + invalidate sessions.active; 422 -> fieldErrors surfaced; NetworkError -> retry same key twice then surface error
- **Validation:** No user input (the intended-output requirement is validated by the screens in 8.2). Rules: identical body -> identical key across retries; changed body -> new key. Test: src/lib/query/useStartSession.test.tsx with renderHook inside QueryClientProvider + SessionModeProvider, vi.mock of src/lib/api/client capturing the Idempotency-Key option per call, fake timers for the retry delay.
- **Verify:** `npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/lib/query/useStartSession.test.tsx` — 6 passed: 'NetworkError then 200: both requests carry the identical Idempotency-Key and the active-session cache holds the returned session', 'while the request is pending the active-session cache is unchanged (no timer state before acknowledgement)', 'exhausted NetworkError leaves the cache unchanged with status error, and a later start() with the same body reuses the same key', 'ConflictError active_session_exists reads details.activeSessionId (D18) and invalidates queryKeys.sessions.active', 'after a success the next logical start uses a different key', 'calling start() again with an edited body before the first attempt's key is cleared mints a new Idempotency-Key instead of replaying the old content'.
- **After:** 7.4.1, 7.4.2
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start while offline

## 8. Web screens

### 8.1.2 — Build Setup PlanForm at /setup with timezone confirm and skippable optionals

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/setup/PlanForm.tsx plus the `/setup` route in app/router.tsx under RailLayout. Fields: baseline local date (Day 0), timezone select prefilled from Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone with an explicit 'Confirm timezone' checkbox, duration radio 5/10/15 min (practiceTargetSeconds 300/600/900), leisure allowance number defaulting to 20, feed estimate number labeled 'estimate' with helper 'leave blank if you do not know'. Submit -> POST /programs {baselineDate, timezone, practiceTargetSeconds, leisureAllowanceMinutes, feedEstimateMinutes?} with an Idempotency-Key UUID generated once per form instance and reused on retry; feedEstimateMinutes is present only when the field is non-blank (a blank is omitted, never 0); no bandCeilings are sent (the server's revision 1 owns them). 201 -> navigate('/setup/readiness'). 409 (open program exists) -> GET /programs/current and route to /setup/readiness when status is draft, else /today, with notice 'You already have a program'. Network/timeout -> inline 'Could not save the plan' + Retry, form values kept. Never calls POST /sessions.
- **Component:** PlanForm (no props; uses useNavigate) with TimezoneConfirm subcomponent (props: value, confirmed, onChange, onConfirm).
- **State:** Controlled local form state; `confirmed` boolean; TanStack useMutation(createProgram) with a stable idempotencyKey ref; invalidates ['programs','current'] on success.
- **API integration:** POST /programs (IK) — 400 fieldErrors map to per-field messages; 409 -> existing program route via GET /programs/current; network error -> inline retryable message with the same key.
- **Validation:** Rules: date required ISO local date; timezone must be a valid IANA name AND the confirm checkbox checked; duration in {5,10,15}; leisure integer >= 0; feed estimate blank or integer >= 0 and blank never coerced to 0. Test apps/web/src/features/setup/PlanForm.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, api mocked): 'omits feedEstimateMinutes when blank', 'never sends feedEstimateMinutes 0 for a blank field', 'blocks save until timezone is confirmed and sends the confirmed value', 'accepts 5-minute duration as practiceTargetSeconds 300 and sends no bandCeilings', '409 routes to the existing program (draft -> readiness, otherwise today)', 'network failure shows could-not-save and does not navigate', 'retry reuses the same Idempotency-Key', 'never calls POST /sessions', '400 fieldErrors render beside the named field'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/setup/PlanForm.test.tsx -> 9 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 7.1, 7.4, 2.7

### 8.1.3 — Build ReadinessForm with missing-slot list, one-hour rule and frozen slots

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/setup/ReadinessForm.tsx at `/setup/readiness` under RailLayout. Loads GET /programs/current (program.id, program.version, program.status, slots[]). Four rows (baseline A, baseline B, final A, final B): materialRef text, optional language/deviceFormat/materialLevel, plannedLocalTime for baseline A/B; final A/B times prefilled from the baseline values and kept in sync until the user edits them. Instruction copy: 'Reading elsewhere is allowed; tallying on paper is fine.' Save -> PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots {expectedVersion, slots:[filled rows]} (partial saves are sent; the server keeps draft while incomplete). Response program.status !== 'draft' (baseline_ready or active) -> navigate('/today'); draft -> stay and render a MissingSlots list naming exactly the slots lacking a material reference or (baseline) time. Slots with frozenAt render read-only with 'This slot is frozen because it already has an attempt'; server 409 frozen renders the same explanation and keeps the form; 422 too-close renders 'Baseline A and B must be at least one hour apart' (also checked client-side before sending); 409 stale version -> refetch and notice 'The program changed in another tab'. Never calls POST /sessions. Reachable while the program is open via Settings' 'Edit benchmark materials' link (D38, built in 8.9.5's group), not only from the setup flow; this form itself supports the active-program edit whether reached from readiness or from Settings.
- **Component:** ReadinessForm (no props) with SlotRow (props: slot, frozen, value, onChange) and MissingSlots (props: missing: SlotKey[]).
- **State:** Controlled rows keyed by phase+label; derived `missing` list; `finalsTouched` flags for the default-from-baseline behavior; useMutation(putBenchmarkSlots); invalidates ['programs','current'].
- **API integration:** GET /programs/current; PUT /programs/{id}/benchmark-slots — 409 frozen -> explanation; 409 stale -> refetch + notice; 422 one-hour -> message; 400 fieldErrors -> per-row messages.
- **Validation:** Rules: materialRef trimmed non-empty for completion; times HH:MM; |baselineA - baselineB| >= 60 min client pre-check; frozen rows not editable. Test apps/web/src/features/setup/ReadinessForm.test.tsx: 'two blank references -> stays on page and lists exactly baseline B and final B as missing', 'baseline times 30 min apart -> one-hour message and no request', 'final times prefilled from baseline and follow baseline edits until touched', 'response status baseline_ready -> navigates to /today and POST /sessions never called', 'frozen slot renders read-only with the frozen explanation', 'server 409 frozen renders the same explanation and keeps the form', 'active program: unfrozen final B time change is in the PUT body and status active returns to /today', '409 stale version refetches and shows the notice', 'instruction copy mentions reading elsewhere and paper tally'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/setup/ReadinessForm.test.tsx -> 9 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 7.1, 7.4, 2.7

### 8.2.1 — Build Today frame, NextAction, empty and missed-day states

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/today/Today.tsx and NextAction.tsx at `/today` under RailLayout. Data: GET /programs/current (nextAction 'setup' with no program, or 404 -> redirect /setup) then GET /programs/{id}/today. Header 'Day N of 14' from the server `day` (never computed client-side). NextAction renders exactly one primary control per nextAction: setup -> /setup; readiness -> 'Finish readiness' /setup/readiness; benchmark(slot) -> 'Start with your baseline' /benchmark/:slotId; practice(block) -> focuses the next BlockCard's start form (slot filled by 8.2.2); final(slot) -> 'Final benchmark A/B' /benchmark/:slotId (per D23, nextAction stays final(slot) beyond Day 14 while any final slot lacks a finalized attempt, so this control remains reachable on Day 15+); progress -> /progress. Empty state (baseline_ready, no sessions) shows 'Start with your baseline' and no improvement, percentage or score cards. Missed-day copy is neutral ('Day 6 — Block 1 is next'); the words streak, lost, broken, restart, behind never appear. Two BlockCard slots and a CheckinCard slot are rendered from `blocks[2]` and `checkin` (components from 8.2.2/8.2.3); the SuggestionBanner slot is filled by 8.2.4. Fetch errors render a Retry control.
- **Component:** Today (no props); NextAction (props: nextAction, programId, slots, onFocusBlock).
- **State:** TanStack queries ['programs','current'] and ['today', programId]; no local state.
- **API integration:** GET /programs/current (nextAction setup / 404 -> /setup); GET /programs/{id}/today; fetch errors render a Retry control that refetches both queries.
- **Validation:** No inputs. ### 8.2.2 — Build BlockCard and practice start via useStartSession (7.4.4)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/today/BlockCard.tsx rendered twice from Today: status badge (not started / in progress / completed / partial) from today.blocks[i].status and target from revision.settings.practiceTargetSeconds rendered as '15 min'. The next block's card hosts StartPracticeForm: textarea 'What will you produce?' with a counter, read-only duration = current target, Start button. Start calls useStartSession().start({programId, kind:'practice', intendedOutput, targetSeconds: revision.settings.practiceTargetSeconds}) (7.4.4 — no local Idempotency-Key handling, no local mutation); slotId is never present. Only after the hook resolves does the app navigate to /focus/:sessionId; no client timer runs before that. On active_session_exists, 7.4.4 sets its own activeSessionId and invalidates ['sessions','active'], and Today's own ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) then renders in place of every BlockCard — BlockCard performs no 409 routing of its own. Network failure -> the hook's 'error' status renders 'The session could not be started' + Retry (calls start() again, reusing the hook's own key), typed output preserved. Returning user path is two actions: type, Start.
- **Component:** BlockCard (props: block, target, isNext, programId); StartPracticeForm (props: programId, targetSeconds, onStarted) using useStartSession (7.4.4).
- **State:** Controlled intendedOutput only; useStartSession() (7.4.4) owns the mutation, key and status.
- **API integration:** useStartSession().start -> POST /sessions (IK) — 400 fieldErrors.intendedOutput -> field message; active_session_exists -> handled entirely by 7.4.4 plus Today's ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5); network -> hook's error status renders inline could-not-start with Retry reusing the same key.

### 8.2.3 — Build CheckinCard reading values from today.checkin.values (D22)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/today/CheckinCard.tsx rendered from Today, entirely from the parent's ['today', programId] query with no query of its own. Status, missing[] and values (sleepMinutes, phoneFeedMinutes, desktopFeedMinutes — nulls preserved) all come from today.checkin per D22, so no request to GET /programs/{id}/days/{date} is made from this card. phoneFeedMinutes/desktopFeedMinutes are the server's scope-feed aggregate (packages/shared domain/feed.ts on the API side; app_total never added). Any value that is null or listed in missing[] renders 'not yet reported' and never '0'; an explicit 0 value renders '0 min'. Link 'Open check-in' to /checkin/:localDate (screen is 8b, 8.7). No form inputs on the card.
- **Component:** CheckinCard (props: programId, localDate, checkin: {status, missing[], values}).
- **State:** No query of its own; reads the parent Today ['today', programId] cache entry.
- **API integration:** None directly — reads today.checkin.values from the parent's GET /programs/{id}/today (8.2.1); the link to /checkin/:localDate is the only navigation.
- **Validation:** No inputs. Test apps/web/src/features/today/CheckinCard.test.tsx: 'missing sleep renders not yet reported and no 0', 'complete check-in shows sleep, phone and desktop values from checkin.values and no missing list', 'phone missing but desktop reported renders the desktop value and phone as not yet reported', 'explicit zero feed value renders 0 min while a null value renders not yet reported', 'card issues no request to GET /programs/{id}/days/{date}', 'link targets /checkin/:localDate'.

### 8.2.4 — Build progression SuggestionBanner with Accept revision and Hold

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/today/SuggestionBanner.tsx rendered by Today only when today.suggestion is present: 'Ready for +5 minutes? (to N min)'. Accept -> POST /programs/{id}/revisions {effectiveDay: day, settings: {...revision.settings, practiceTargetSeconds: revision.settings.practiceTargetSeconds + 300}, reason: 'progression accepted'} -> invalidate ['programs','current'] and ['today'] so BlockCards show the new target. Hold -> hides the banner for this visit only (sessionStorage flag keyed by programId+day, every access wrapped in try/catch) with no server write; BlockCard keeps the current target and Start still posts the current revision's targetSeconds; the suggestion reappears on the next visit because the server recomputes it on every GET /programs/{id}/today (Hold is a client-only dismissal, not a server acknowledgement). Ceiling and Partly rules are the server's (2.5/4.5): no suggestion in the payload -> no banner. Copy contains no streak, unlock or level wording.
- **Component:** SuggestionBanner (props: suggestion, day, programId, revision).
- **State:** useMutation(createRevision); local `held` boolean seeded from sessionStorage; queries invalidated on accept.
- **API integration:** POST /programs/{id}/revisions — 400 (reason missing) surfaces a message; 409 stale -> refetch ['programs','current']. Suggestion presence read from GET /programs/{id}/today.
- **Validation:** No free-text inputs. Test apps/web/src/features/today/SuggestionBanner.test.tsx (renders Today with mocked api): 'no suggestion -> no banner', 'Accept posts a revision with reason progression accepted and practiceTargetSeconds +300 and invalidates today', 'Hold posts nothing and BlockCard target is unchanged', 'after Hold, Start posts targetSeconds equal to the current revision target (900, not 1200)', 'banner reappears on a fresh mount when the server still sends the suggestion', 'sessionStorage throwing does not crash and Hold still hides the banner', 'banner copy has no /streak|unlock|level/i text'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/today/SuggestionBanner.test.tsx -> 7 tests pass
- **After:** 8.2.1, 8.2.2, 7.1.1, 7.4, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Suggestion accepted · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Two qualifying days · practice-sessions: Progression suggestion follows the protocol rule / Partly output breaks the streak of qualifying days · practice-sessions: Practice requires a short intended output / Hold the target · program-setup: Protocol settings are immutable revisions · app-shell: Copy never punishes or gamifies

### 8.2.5 — Build ActiveSessionCard: Today, Benchmark Ready and stale-conflict notice (absorbs 8.10.4)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/session/ActiveSessionCard.tsx (re-exported from features/today for Today's existing import) mounted by three call sites: Today (8.2.1) and Benchmark Ready (8.3.1) render it in place of every Start control whenever the ['sessions','active'] query holds a session (200) — practice running|paused -> /focus/:id; benchmark running -> /benchmark/:slotId; practice awaiting_review -> 'Finish your pending review' /review/:id; benchmark awaiting_review -> /benchmark/:id/recall when review.recallLockedAt is null else /benchmark/:id/scoring; 204 -> the caller renders its normal Start controls. Absorbs 8.10.4 (ActiveSessionNotice, D16): when POST /sessions returns 409 active_session_exists, useStartSession (7.4.4) invalidates ['sessions','active'] and the same card renders for the returned id. Session-mode screens (Running 8.3.3, TransitionControls 8.5.4, AgentPanel 8.5.5) pass a staleNotice prop when their own transition mutation receives 409 stale_version: the error's details.current (D18) seeds ['sessions', id] before a confirming refetch, and ActiveSessionCard renders 'This session was updated in another tab' above the same kind/lifecycle/Return line, computed from the now-current lifecycle (so a session another tab ended shows the awaiting_review or finalized state, never the stale one); the caller sends no second transition.
- **Component:** ActiveSessionCard (props: session, staleNotice?: boolean).
- **State:** Query ['sessions','active'] (mounted by Today/Ready) or the caller's own ['sessions', id] cache entry (session screens); no local state.
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/active (200 session | 204); consumes 409 active_session_exists (via 7.4.4) and 409 stale_version { details: { current } } (via 8.3.3/8.5.4/8.5.5) without an extra round trip beyond the caller's own confirming refetch.
- **Validation:** No inputs. Test apps/web/src/features/today/TodayActiveSession.test.tsx and apps/web/src/features/session/ActiveSessionCard.test.tsx: 'running practice hides Start and links Return to /focus/:id', 'paused practice links /focus/:id', 'awaiting-review practice links /review/:id', 'awaiting-review benchmark links recall when not locked', 'awaiting-review benchmark links scoring when locked', 'running benchmark links /benchmark/:slotId', '204 renders the start controls', 'Ready mounts the same card and hides its own Start control when a session exists', 'staleNotice renders This session was updated in another tab above the current lifecycle line', 'a stale awaiting_review current renders the Finish-review link, not the stale running one'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/today/TodayActiveSession.test.tsx src/features/session/ActiveSessionCard.test.tsx -> 10 tests pass
- **After:** 8.2.1, 8.2.2, 7.1.1, 7.4, 7.4.4

### 8.3.1 — Build Benchmark Ready screen with date gate, slot.attempts and replacement reason

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Ready.tsx rendered by features/benchmark/BenchmarkRoute.tsx at `/benchmark/:slotId` under SessionLayout (8.3.3 later adds the Running branch). Loads GET /programs/current (slots[] with attempts per D22: {sessionId, lifecycle, eligible, excludedByAmendment}) and GET /programs/{id}/today (localDate); finds the slot and its attempts[]. Header 'Fixed 20-minute assessment — {phase} {label}' with a distinct benchmark visual token (data-mode="benchmark", different accent from practice). Shows materialRef, plannedLocalTime, the protocol checklist and the note 'Leaving this page to read does not count as distraction.' Date gate: today < slot.assignedLocalDate -> no Start and 'Scheduled for {date} at {time}'; today >= assignedLocalDate -> Start is offered, with the timing-deviation notice 'This slot was assigned to {date}. An attempt today will be labeled a timing deviation.' shown whenever today > assignedLocalDate (D23). Prior attempts come from slot.attempts[] (D22, no separate report call): zero -> Start with no reason field; one ineligible/excludedByAmendment -> required 'Reason for replacement' textarea sent as replacementReason; one eligible -> no Start and 'Eligible attempts are not retaken'; two -> no Start and 'This slot already has two attempts' (whether an abandoned or unfinalized attempt counts toward that limit is the server's call — the client renders whatever slot.attempts and the resulting 422 say, asserting no count of its own). Mounts ['sessions','active'] and renders ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) instead of every Start control whenever it holds a session. Start calls useStartSession().start({programId, kind:'benchmark', slotId, replacementReason?}) (7.4.4 — no local Idempotency-Key handling); no intendedOutput, no targetSeconds is sent (D30: server stores 1200 for benchmarks). 422 slot rule -> the server message. Final phase uses the same screen labeled 'Final A/B'.
- **Component:** Ready (route param slotId); Checklist (props: items); ReplacementReasonField (props: value, onChange, required); ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5, also mounted here).
- **State:** Queries ['programs','current'], ['today', programId], ['sessions','active']; controlled replacementReason; useStartSession (7.4.4) owns the start mutation and key.
- **API integration:** GET /programs/current (slots[].attempts per D22), GET /programs/{id}/today, GET /sessions/active; useStartSession().start -> POST /sessions (IK) — 422 slot rules (before date, third attempt, eligible replacement) render the server message; active_session_exists -> ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) via the hook's own cache update; network -> hook's error status renders could-not-start with Retry.
- **Validation:** Rules: replacementReason required (1–500 chars) whenever a prior non-eligible/excluded attempt exists; Start offered on or after the assigned date, never before. Test apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Ready.test.tsx: 'slot on its date offers Start with no timing-deviation notice', 'slot before its date hides Start and shows scheduled date and time', 'slot after its date offers Start with the timing-deviation notice', 'body carries kind benchmark and slotId and no intendedOutput or targetSeconds', 'prior ineligible attempt from slot.attempts requires a reason before Start enables and sends replacementReason', 'prior eligible attempt shows not-retaken and no Start', 'two prior attempts -> no Start', 'server 422 message is rendered', 'network failure -> could not be started and no countdown rendered', 'an existing active session renders ActiveSessionCard instead of every Start control', 'labels fixed 20-minute, carries the leaving note and data-mode benchmark', 'final phase renders Final A', 'no navigation landmarks are rendered'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/benchmark/Ready.test.tsx -> 13 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1, 7.1.1, 7.4, 7.4.4, 4.2, 8.2.5, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Benchmark ready state / Slot on its assigned date · benchmark-assessment: Benchmark ready state / Slot before its date · benchmark-assessment: One replacement per slot with a reason / Replacement after disruption · benchmark-assessment: One replacement per slot with a reason / Third attempt · benchmark-assessment: One replacement per slot with a reason / Replacement of an eligible attempt · benchmark-assessment: Benchmarks are visually distinct from practice / Benchmark ready screen · benchmark-assessment: Final benchmarks on Day 14 · benchmark-assessment: Eligibility is derived server-side with explicit reasons / Attempt outside its date · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start while offline · app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only

### 8.3.2 — Build shared TimerDisplay with hide, chime and milestone announcements (D39)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/focus/TimerDisplay.tsx shared by Benchmark Running, Focus and Recall. Props {remainingSeconds, hidden, onToggleHidden}. Renders mm:ss with no aria-live on the digits; Hide/Show toggle whose initial state comes from preferences.hideTimerDefault (GET /me); hidden -> 'Timer hidden' text and no digits. useEndChime(remainingSeconds, preferences.endChime) plays one short WebAudio tone when remaining reaches 0, exactly once, also while hidden. useMilestoneAnnouncements (7.1.6) posts a polite live-region message at 5:00 and 0:00 only, driven directly by preferences.milestoneAnnouncements (GET /me, D22/D39; default false) rather than a caller-passed flag — never per second. prefers-reduced-motion removes transition classes. remainingSeconds is always supplied by lib/clock (7.2, D5) from server fields via useRemaining(session); the component never accumulates ticks.
- **Component:** TimerDisplay; hooks useEndChime, useRemaining(session) wrapper over lib/clock, useMilestoneAnnouncements (7.1.6) reading preferences.milestoneAnnouncements.
- **State:** hidden local state seeded from preference; chimeFired ref; milestone last-announced ref (owned by 7.1.6's hook).
- **API integration:** GET /me (preferences.hideTimerDefault, preferences.endChime, preferences.milestoneAnnouncements) read-only.
- **Validation:** No inputs beyond the toggle. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/TimerDisplay.test.tsx with fake timers: 'hidden renders no digits', 'chime fires exactly once at zero while hidden', 'no element with aria-live contains the countdown digits while running', 'milestone announcement emitted only when preferences.milestoneAnnouncements is true', 'milestone announcement suppressed when the preference is false or absent', 'prefers-reduced-motion removes transition classes', 'remaining derives from server fields: 60 s pause then 90 s advance -> remaining reflects paused seconds', 'hideTimerDefault preference seeds hidden state'.
