### 8.4.1 — Build Recall screen with skip-recall for incomplete attempts and server-aligned startedAt

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Recall.tsx at `/benchmark/:sessionId/recall` under SessionLayout (no navigation). Loads GET /sessions/{id} (kind benchmark). lifecycle running -> 'The interval has not been confirmed yet' with Retry (refetch) and no Start recall; review.recallLockedAt set -> redirect to /benchmark/:sessionId/scoring. When session.completeInterval === false (incomplete attempt, D25) the screen shows the 'Incomplete attempt' label plus a 'Skip recall' action alongside 'Start recall': Skip recall navigates directly to /benchmark/:sessionId/scoring with no recall write at all (eligibility later carries recall_missing per D25); Start recall remains available for a user who wants to attempt it anyway. For a complete interval only 'Start recall' is offered. Step 1 (when not skipped): 'Is your reading material closed?' -> 'Start recall' captures startedAt = serverNowMs(anchor, performance.now()) (7.2.1, D27 — never client Date.now(), which would let a skewed client clock distort recall_delay_seconds) and starts a 3:00 countdown (TimerDisplay). Five textareas 'Point 1'..'Point 5', blank allowed. At 0 show 'Time is up — save when you are ready'; editing stays enabled (overrun is a server flag at 210 s, never a client block). 'Save recall' -> POST /sessions/{id}/recall (IK) {points: five strings with '' for blank, startedAt, durationSeconds: round((serverNowMs(anchor, performance.now()) - startedAt)/1000)}; 200 -> navigate to scoring; 409 (already locked with different content) -> notice 'Recall was already saved' and navigate to scoring showing the stored points. No delay/overrun computation on the client.
- **Component:** Recall (route param sessionId); RecallPoints (props: values, onChange, disabled).
- **State:** phase 'confirm' | 'writing'; startedAtMs from serverNowMs (7.2.1); controlled five strings; stable idempotencyKey; useMutation(saveRecall).
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/{id}; POST /sessions/{id}/recall (IK) — 200 same-content replay, 409 different content -> notice + scoring, 422 not benchmark -> error, 422 recall_before_interval_end (D27) -> retry with a fresh serverNowMs, network -> retry with the same key.
- **Validation:** Rules: each point <= 2000 chars with counter; blank allowed; Save enabled only after 'Start recall'; Skip recall available only when completeInterval is false. Test apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Recall.test.tsx (fake timers): 'countdown does not run before material-closed confirm', 'startedAt equals serverNowMs at confirm and durationSeconds equals elapsed at save (190 s)', 'blank points sent as empty strings and the array has length 5', 'after 3:00 the form stays editable, Time is up is shown and Save still posts', 'locked review redirects to scoring', '409 shows notice and navigates to scoring', 'retry reuses the same Idempotency-Key', 'incomplete attempt renders Skip recall alongside Start recall and Skip navigates to scoring with no POST to /recall', 'complete attempt renders only Start recall, no Skip recall control', 'lifecycle running renders interval-not-confirmed with Retry and no Start recall', 'no navigation landmarks are rendered'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/benchmark/Recall.test.tsx -> 11 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 8.3.2, 7.1, 7.2.1, 7.4, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall started promptly · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall started after a long break · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall text is immutable after save · benchmark-assessment: Fixed interval with no valid pause / Stop early · benchmark-assessment: Self-scoring is unavailable until recall is locked / Incomplete attempt finalized without recall · app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only

### 8.4.2 — Build Scoring section: locked points, Accurate/Not accurate, blanks scored 0, recall missing

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Scoring.tsx, first section of `/benchmark/:sessionId/scoring` (page shell BenchmarkReviewPage.tsx under SessionLayout, created here; later sections are 8.4.3/8.4.4/8.4.5). Reads session and review from GET /sessions/{id}. When review.recallLockedAt is null and session.completeInterval is true, render 'Recall must be saved first' with a link to recall and NO scoring controls in the DOM. When review.recallLockedAt is null and session.completeInterval is false (incomplete attempt, recall skipped per D25), render 'Recall not saved — this attempt will be recorded with recall missing' with NO scoring controls and no link back to recall — Finalize (8.4.5) is reachable and allowed without a lock on an incomplete attempt. Otherwise list the five locked points as read-only text (no textarea or input for the text); each non-blank point gets a required radio group Accurate / Not accurate; blank points show 'Scored 0 because blank' with no radios. Preview line 'Recall score (self-reported, preview): n/5' = count of Accurate. review.recallFlags render as visible deviations: recall_delayed -> 'Recall started more than 10 minutes after the interval', recall_overrun -> 'Recall ran more than 30 s over 3:00', each labeled 'noted, not excluding'. Emits recallScores (5 x 0|1, blanks 0) and a complete flag to the parent for finalize; when recall is missing entirely, complete is emitted true with recallScores omitted (8.4.5 sends no recallScores in that case).
- **Component:** Scoring (props: session, review, onChange(recallScores, complete)); PointRow (props: index, text, value, onChange); BenchmarkReviewPage (route param sessionId).
- **State:** Local answers array of 'accurate' | 'not_accurate' | null per point; derived complete flag; derived preview score; derived recallMissing = !review.recallLockedAt && !session.completeInterval.
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/{id} only; no writes.
- **Validation:** Rules: every non-blank point must be answered before the parent enables Finalize; blank points not operable; recall-missing and recall-not-locked states each render zero radios. Test apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Scoring.test.tsx: 'unlocked review on a complete interval renders zero radio inputs and the recall-first link', 'unlocked review on an incomplete interval renders Recall not saved — this attempt will be recorded with recall missing, zero radios and no link back to recall', 'locked points render as read-only text with no textarea or text input', 'points 1–3 Accurate with 4–5 blank yields preview 3, two Scored 0 because blank and no radios for the blanks', 'score label contains self-reported', 'recall_delayed flag renders visible deviation copy labeled not excluding', 'recall_overrun flag renders visible deviation copy', 'complete flag false until every non-blank point is answered', 'recall-missing state reports complete true with no recallScores', 'Tab order reaches every radio in point order'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/benchmark/Scoring.test.tsx -> 10 tests pass
- **After:** 8.4.1, 7.1.1, 7.4, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** benchmark-assessment: Self-scoring is unavailable until recall is locked / Attempt to score before recall · benchmark-assessment: Self-scoring is unavailable until recall is locked / Score with two blank points · benchmark-assessment: Self-scoring is unavailable until recall is locked / Incomplete attempt finalized without recall · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall text is immutable after save · benchmark-assessment: Recall is a separate, timed, then locked step / Recall started after a long break · app-shell: Accessibility baseline / Keyboard-only session review

### 8.4.3 — Build review count fields, first-switch preview and toFinalizeReview body builder

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/CountFields.tsx and features/benchmark/toFinalizeReview.ts, second section of BenchmarkReviewPage. S (off-task episodes) and E (external interruptions) numeric inputs prefilled from the session's non-voided event tallies with badge 'from recorded events (method: event)'; with no events they start blank with placeholder 'leave blank if unknown'. 'Replace with a paper tally' toggle makes S editable with countMethod 'retrospective'. M (noticed mind-wandering) optional, labeled 'descriptive only'. toFinalizeReview(state) omits episodeCount/externalCount/mindWanderingCount when blank (never 0) and sends 0 only for an explicit '0'; countMethod accompanies episodeCount. First-switch preview via packages/shared domain/firstSwitch.ts: S=0 -> '20+, capped'; event-derived -> first non-voided off_task elapsed as m:ss '(event)'; retrospective S>0 -> 'Unknown' plus an optional 'Estimated minute of first switch' input (0–20) -> firstSwitchEstimateSeconds (minutes × 60) previewed as '≈ m min (estimate)'; 'Unknown' is never rendered as 20+. No total is computed across S and E.
- **Component:** CountFields (props: session, value, onChange); CountField (props: label, value, method, onChange, prefilled); FirstSwitchPreview (props: firstSwitch, estimateMinutes).
- **State:** Slice of the review reducer {episodeCount: string, countMethod, externalCount: string, mindWanderingCount: string, estimateMinutes: string}; derived firstSwitch via domain/firstSwitch.ts.
- **API integration:** Reads GET /sessions/{id} (events, tallies); no writes here (finalize is 8.4.5).
- **Validation:** Rules: counts integer >= 0 or blank; estimate integer 0–20 or blank; blank never coerced to 0. ### 8.4.4 — Build disruption attestation, conditions confirm, incomplete banner and eligibility preview

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/DisruptionField.tsx, ConditionsFields.tsx and EligibilityPreview.tsx, third section of BenchmarkReviewPage. Disruption: radio 'Was this session materially disrupted?' Yes/No (required, no default) with optional note (<= 500, counter). Conditions: deviceFormat, language, materialLevel inputs and accommodations checkboxes from ACCOMMODATIONS, defaulted from review.observedConditions (which the server defaulted from the slot at start, D7.5), with a 'These conditions are correct' confirm checkbox. - **API integration:** Reads GET /sessions/{id} (review.observedConditions, completeInterval, timerQuality, localDate) and GET /programs/current (slot assignedLocalDate); no writes here.

### 8.4.5 — Build Finalize via useFinalizeSession (7.3.5) and eligibility summary

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** FinalizeBar and EligibilitySummary in apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Finalize.tsx and EligibilitySummary.tsx, footer of BenchmarkReviewPage. Finalize is disabled with the named message 'Answer whether the session was materially disrupted' until the disruption radio is answered, and also until Scoring reports complete and conditions are confirmed (each missing item listed); on an incomplete attempt with recall missing (D25) Scoring's complete flag is already true with no recallScores required, so Finalize does not additionally wait on recall. A small optional 'Anything else to note?' textarea (reviewNote, D31, <=500 chars) sits above the Finalize button. On click: useFinalizeSession(sessionId).finalize({...toFinalizeReview(state), materiallyDisrupted, disruptionNote?, recallScores? (omitted when recall is missing), conditions, reviewNote?}) (7.3.5 — owns the outbox flush, expectedEventCount, the Idempotency-Key in sessionStorage, the 409 event_count_mismatch flush-and-retry loop and the three-mismatch 'unsaved_entries' ceiling; no local mismatch or retry logic here). status 'unsaved_entries' -> 'Some entries are still unsaved. Retry.' with a manual Retry calling retry(); 400/422 fieldErrors.materiallyDisrupted -> shown next to the radio; 'success' -> EligibilitySummary: 'Eligible' or 'Not eligible' with each reason rendered via EXCLUSION_REASON_COPY (including recall_missing and scoring_incomplete together for an incomplete attempt); S shown as 'not reported' when null (never 0); recall score 'n/5 (self-reported)' or 'not reported' when recall is missing; first-switch state text; no percentage or score framing anywhere. Primary 'Continue' refetches GET /programs/current and routes to /benchmark/:slotId when nextAction is benchmark/final, else /today.
- **Component:** FinalizeBar (props: canFinalize, missing: string[], onFinalize, status); EligibilitySummary (props: session, review, eligible, exclusionReasons, nextAction).
- **State:** useFinalizeSession(sessionId) (7.3.5) owns the mutation, key, flush/retry/mismatch state; local reviewNote string; result state for the summary.
- **API integration:** useFinalizeSession -> POST /sessions/{id}/finalize (IK) — 409 event_count_mismatch handled entirely inside 7.3.5 (flush + retry same key, up to three attempts); 400/422 fieldErrors -> per-field; replay with same key -> same 200; GET /programs/current for Continue routing.
- **Validation:** Rules: disruption answered, scoring complete and conditions confirmed before enabling; recall lock not required when the attempt is incomplete (D25). ### 8.5.1 — Build useSessionEvents hook, EventButtons with agent-check subtype, opt-in visibility events

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/focus/useSessionEvents.ts, EventButtons.tsx, Tallies.tsx and tallies.ts (standalone; consumed by Focus 8.5.3 and Benchmark Running 8.3.3). useSessionEvents(sessionId, session) keeps {events[], voided set} seeded from GET /sessions/{id}, subscribes to the outbox (7.3) for sent/unsent status, and exposes record(type, details?) and undo(). Events {clientEventId: crypto.randomUUID(), type, elapsedMs from lib/clock (paused time excluded), occurredAt, details?} are written to the outbox first, the tally re-renders synchronously, then batches go to POST /sessions/{id}/events. undo() targets the most recent non-voided event: unsent -> dropped from the outbox; sent -> POST /sessions/{id}/events/{clientEventId}/void (D9). EventButtons variant 'practice': 'Record off-task episode' (the single primary), 'External interruption' (secondary), 'Agent check' (secondary) opening an inline confirm with checkbox 'This was also an off-task episode' -> {type:'agent_check', details:{alsoOffTask}}, 'Undo'; variant 'benchmark': same without Agent check. tallies(events) per D11: offTask = non-voided off_task + non-voided agent_check with alsoOffTask; agentChecks = every non-voided agent_check; external = non-voided external; no total is computed or rendered; 'visibility' events are never counted in any tally. When preferences.visibilityContext (GET /me) is true, the hook attaches a document visibilitychange listener for the life of the session that records {type:'visibility', details:{hidden: document.hidden}} through the same outbox path (D39); when the preference is false or absent, no visibilitychange listener is attached at all (D15's default).
- **Component:** EventButtons (props: sessionId, variant: 'practice'|'benchmark', onRecord, onUndo, canUndo); Tallies (props: offTask, external, agentChecks?); AgentCheckConfirm (props: onConfirm(alsoOffTask), onCancel).
- **State:** Reducer in useSessionEvents {events[], voided set}; undo target derived from the last non-voided event; outbox subscription for sent/unsent status; conditional visibilitychange listener gated by preferences.visibilityContext.
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/{id} (events seed); GET /me (preferences.visibilityContext); POST /sessions/{id}/events via outbox batches (duplicates ignored by server); POST /sessions/{id}/events/{clientEventId}/void (409 after finalize -> notice).
- **Validation:** Rules: one click = one event; undo only the latest non-voided event. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/EventButtons.test.tsx and tallies.test.ts (fake outbox, fake timers): 'agent check marked also off-task -> offTask 1, agentChecks 1, and no element with text 2', 'agent check without the flag -> offTask 0, agentChecks 1', 'one Record after five simulated hidden/shown toggles -> exactly one event', 'preferences.visibilityContext false: visibilitychange dispatch creates no event and no listener is attached', 'preferences.visibilityContext true: visibilitychange dispatch records one visibility event excluded from every tally', 'Undo removes an unsent event from the outbox', 'Undo of a sent event calls void with its clientEventId', 'benchmark variant renders no Agent check control', 'tally updates before the network promise resolves (<100 ms)', 'recorded elapsedMs excludes paused time (60 s pause then record at 5 min wall -> 240000)', 'tallies() excludes voided and visibility events and exposes no total'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/focus/EventButtons.test.tsx src/features/focus/tallies.test.ts -> 11 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 7.2, 7.3, 7.4, 2.7
- **Spec refs:** practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Agent check that was also off-task · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / One departure, five apps · practice-sessions: Interruption events with undo and subtypes / Hidden page creates nothing · benchmark-assessment: Interruption recording during the benchmark / Record after returning · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged

### 8.5.2 — Build SyncStatus: Pending/Saved/could-not-save bound to the outbox (absorbs 8.10.1)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/focus/SyncStatus.tsx (renamed from SyncStateWord, absorbing 8.10.1 per D16) and useOutboxStatus(sessionId) in apps/web/src/lib/outbox, used by Focus (8.5.3) and Benchmark Running (8.3.3). Renders plain words only, driven by the outbox (7.3) state for the session: local write succeeded and unacknowledged -> 'Pending' with a 'Retry' button (calls outbox.flush(sessionId)); acknowledged (every buffered clientEventId in accepted[] or duplicates[]) -> 'Saved', shown only after the ack, never on dispatch (D6); local write failed -> 'Entries could not be saved on this device' with a 'Keep trying the server directly' button (sendDirect(): the unsaved events are held in an in-memory queue and posted straight to POST /sessions/{id}/events; new events recorded while local writes keep failing are also sent directly; 'Saved' appears only once the server acknowledges them). A 422 impossible_offset response marks that one entry rejected and shows 'One entry was rejected and will not be retried' without looping it back into the retry queue. The status region uses aria-live="polite" and updates only on state change, never per tick. No identifier, key, realm, revision or UUID-shaped text is ever rendered.
- **Component:** SyncStatus (props: sessionId); hook useOutboxStatus(sessionId).
- **State:** Outbox subscription (useSyncExternalStore) -> 'saved' | 'pending' | 'local_write_failed'; ack tracking by clientEventId; in-memory fallback queue for direct mode.
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/events { events: [...] } (<=100) -> { accepted[], duplicates[] } observed via the outbox; 422 impossible_offset -> one entry marked rejected; network failure -> stays Pending.
- **Validation:** No inputs beyond Retry / Keep trying. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/SyncStatus.test.tsx (fake outbox from 7.1.1's harness): 'unacknowledged local write renders Pending with Retry', 'dispatched request without ack still renders Pending', 'ack via accepted or duplicates renders Saved and the buffer is empty', 'local write failure renders Entries could not be saved on this device with the keep-trying offer', 'keep-trying posts events directly and renders Saved after ack', '422 impossible_offset marks one entry rejected without a retry loop', 'no UUID-like text and no word idempotency/realm/revision in the DOM', 'status region is aria-live polite and a render-count spy shows no re-render per timer tick', 'Saved is not shown after dispatch before ack'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/focus/SyncStatus.test.tsx -> 9 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 7.3, 5.3
- **Spec refs:** app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing / Sync state display · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Impossible offset

### 8.3.3 — Build Benchmark Running view: events, undo, stop early, explicit end transition

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Running.tsx; BenchmarkRoute.tsx (from 8.3.1) gains the branch: GET /sessions/active is a running benchmark whose slotId matches the route -> Running (also on reload). Uses TimerDisplay (8.3.2) with target 1200 s, useSessionEvents + EventButtons variant 'benchmark' (8.5.1: Record off-task episode primary, External interruption secondary, Undo; no Agent check) and SyncStatus (8.5.2). 'Stop early' (secondary, confirm 'This attempt will be recorded as incomplete') -> POST /sessions/{id}/transitions {expectedVersion, type:'end'} -> navigate to /benchmark/:sessionId/recall with an 'Incomplete attempt' label; recall is skippable there for an incomplete attempt (D25, 8.4.1). No pause control and no Abandon control exist in the DOM — Abandon is the shared AbandonSession (8.10.7) mounted once by SessionLayout for every session route, so Running renders no Abandon of its own. No visibilitychange or focus listeners (D15). At remaining 0 the screen shows 'Close your reading material' and 'I'm ready for recall'; clicking it posts POST /sessions/{id}/transitions {expectedVersion, type:'end'} (D24 — the server sets ended_at and complete_interval = elapsed >= target) and, on success, navigates to /benchmark/:sessionId/recall; 409 stale -> ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) renders the notice from the refreshed session and no second transition is sent. Copy states the fixed 20-minute interval and the leaving note; SessionLayout (no navigation).
- **Component:** Running (props: session); reuses EventButtons (variant benchmark), Tallies, TimerDisplay, SyncStatus.
- **State:** useSessionEvents(sessionId) reducer {events[], voided set, version}; lifecycle from the session query; remaining from lib/clock; useMutation(transition) for both Stop early and the deadline end.
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/active, GET /sessions/{id}; POST /sessions/{id}/events via the outbox (batches <= 100; duplicates ignored); POST /sessions/{id}/events/{clientEventId}/void (409 after finalize -> notice); POST /sessions/{id}/transitions type 'end' both at the deadline and on Stop early (409 stale -> ActiveSessionCard notice per 8.2.5/D18; type pause never sent).
- **Validation:** Rules: no pause control, no Abandon control; events only from explicit clicks; elapsedMs only from lib/clock; every 'end' transition carries expectedVersion. Test apps/web/src/features/benchmark/Running.test.tsx (fake timers, mocked api and fake outbox): 'no control named pause exists', 'no control named abandon exists', 'Record twice then Undo leaves one non-voided event and tally 1', 'dispatching visibilitychange with hidden=true creates no event and remaining is still derived from server fields', 'recorded event carries elapsedMs from the clock (07:42 -> 462000)', 'deadline shows Close your reading material and I am ready for recall with no finalize call and no navigation before the click', 'I am ready for recall posts transitions end with expectedVersion then navigates to /benchmark/:sessionId/recall only after it resolves', 'Stop early confirms, sends transition end and routes to recall with the incomplete label', '409 stale on the end transition renders ActiveSessionCard's notice and sends no second transition', 'tally updates before the network promise resolves (<100 ms)', 'no navigation landmarks are rendered and SyncStatus is present'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/benchmark/Running.test.tsx -> 11 tests pass

Assert no button named /pause/i and no button named /abandon/i exists on Running itself (Abandon lives in SessionLayout, 8.10.7). ### 8.5.3 — Build Focus frame: header, timer from server fields, explicit end transition, no navigation

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/focus/Focus.tsx at `/focus/:sessionId` under SessionLayout (no navigation; the 7.4 route-leave guard applies). Loads GET /sessions/{id} (kind practice; 404 -> /today with notice) and derives remaining via lib/clock from startedAt, targetSeconds, pausedSeconds and currentPauseStartedAt (D5), never from tick accumulation. SessionHeader shows the intended output and the target; elapsed time is labeled 'elapsed', never 'focused' or 'attention'. Renders TimerDisplay (8.3.2), EventButtons variant 'practice' + Tallies via useSessionEvents (8.5.1), SyncStatus (8.5.2), and a TransitionControls slot (8.5.4). At remaining 0 (or when mounted after the target passed) the screen shows 'Block time reached — save your review to record it' with primary 'Review': clicking it posts POST /sessions/{id}/transitions {expectedVersion, type:'end'} (D24 — the server sets ended_at and complete_interval) and, only once that resolves, navigates to /review/:sessionId; 409 stale -> ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) renders the notice from the refreshed session and no second transition is sent. Navigating away while running or awaiting review renders the 7.4 leave-guard prompt offering to return; the session is untouched.
- **Component:** Focus (route param sessionId); SessionHeader (props: intendedOutput, targetSeconds).
- **State:** Query ['sessions', id] with the session-aware policy; remaining from useRemaining(session); useMutation(transition) for the deadline Review action; no other local lifecycle state (pause/resume/finish-early are 8.5.4).
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/{id} (404 -> /today with notice; other errors -> Retry); events via 8.5.1; POST /sessions/{id}/transitions type 'end' only at the deadline Review action (409 stale -> ActiveSessionCard notice).
- **Validation:** No inputs. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/Focus.test.tsx (fake timers, mocked api, fake outbox): 'no navigation landmarks are rendered', 'header shows the intended output and no text matches /focused time|attention score/i', 'mounting with a session started 6 minutes ago shows about 09:00 remaining', 'mounting after the target passed shows Block time reached with the Review CTA and no completed wording', 'Review posts transitions end with expectedVersion and navigates to /review/:sessionId only after it resolves', 'reaching zero via fake timers calls no finalize and does not navigate on its own', '404 routes to /today with a notice', 'navigating to /progress while running renders the leave-guard prompt and sends no transition', '409 stale on the end transition renders ActiveSessionCard's notice and sends no second transition', 'sync status is rendered from the outbox state'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/focus/Focus.test.tsx -> 10 tests pass
- **After:** 7.1.1, 8.2.5, 8.3.2, 8.5.1, 8.5.2, 7.1, 7.2, 7.4, 2.7

### 8.5.4 — Build Focus TransitionControls: pause/resume, finish early, stale conflict (no Abandon)

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** apps/web/src/features/focus/TransitionControls.tsx rendered by Focus (8.5.3) and a shared useTransition(sessionId) mutation hook (also used by AgentPanel 8.5.5). 'Pause' (secondary) -> POST /sessions/{id}/transitions {expectedVersion, type:'pause'} -> remaining frozen, 'Paused' label, 'Resume' -> type 'resume'. 'Finish early' (secondary, confirm) -> type 'end' -> navigate /review/:sessionId; available while running or paused. No Abandon control exists here — Abandon is the shared AbandonSession (8.10.7) mounted once by SessionLayout for every session route, so TransitionControls renders only Pause/Resume and Finish early. Every transition carries the session's current version; 409 stale -> refetch GET /sessions/{id} and render ActiveSessionCard's (8.2.5) 'This session was updated in another tab' notice in place of the controls, sending no second transition; 422 -> message. Optimistic timer freeze on pause is rolled back on error. 'Record off-task episode' (8.5.1) remains the single primary control; Pause, External interruption and Finish early are secondary.
- **Component:** TransitionControls (props: session, onTransition); hook useTransition(sessionId).
- **State:** useMutation(transition) keyed by expectedVersion; local lifecycle mirror for the optimistic freeze, rolled back on error; invalidates ['sessions', id] and ['sessions','active'].
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/transitions (409 stale -> refetch and render ActiveSessionCard's notice per 8.2.5/D18; 422 -> message; network -> message, no lifecycle change).
- **Validation:** Rules: transitions always carry the current version; confirm dialogs on finish early. - **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- src/features/focus/TransitionControls.test.tsx -> 9 tests pass

Click Agent check, tick 'also an off-task episode', confirm; assert on-screen tallies Off-task 1 and Agent checks 1, no element whose text is '2', GET /api/v1/sessions/{id} tallies offTask 1 and agentChecks 1 with exactly one stored event of type agent_check and details.alsoOffTask true. Assert the SyncStatus (8.5.2) region reads 'Saved' after acknowledgement and never 'Saved' while the batch request is held (route.fulfill delayed).

### 8.6.2 — Build PracticeReview screen consuming useFinalizeSession

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/review/PracticeReview.tsx at route /review/:sessionId under SessionLayout (7.1.4, no nav), with OutputQualityField (Radix RadioGroup Yes/Partly/No), CountField (numeric input that may stay blank; shows 'prefilled from recorded events' when prefilled), OutputNoteField (one-line optional text, 'what did you finish' — session_reviews.output_note, D31) and ReviewNoteField (optional longer free-text note — session_reviews.review_note, D31) in the same folder. On mount the screen awaits outbox.flush(sessionId) (7.3) and then queries GET /sessions/{id}; when the flush lands events the query is invalidated so prefills refresh unless the user has already edited a field. The screen shows: the session's intended_output as the read-only planned-output line; a recorded-time line 'X min recorded of Y min target (pauses excluded)' where X = floor(response.timing.elapsedSeconds / 60), timing.elapsedSeconds taken verbatim from the server (D20) — the word 'focused' never appears; three separate tallies taken verbatim from the response's tallies { offTask, external, agentChecks } (D20; off-task already includes agent_check events with details.alsoOffTask; agent checks = all agent_check events; external = external events; voided rows excluded; nothing is summed across tallies, D11; the client never recomputes tallies from events, D4); a 'Timing uncertain' chip when timer_quality === 'uncertain'. Counts episodeCount / externalCount / unplannedAgentChecks are prefilled from the tallies with countMethod 'event' when the corresponding tally is ≥ 1, otherwise blank (D31 prefill rule). Editing a prefilled S sets countMethod 'retrospective'; a user-typed S with no events is 'retrospective'; a blank count is omitted from the body (null, never 0; no countMethod). outputNote and reviewNote are each omitted from the body when empty, sent verbatim when non-empty (the finalize contract's review object carries an optional reviewNote string alongside outputNote). One primary action 'Save review' calls useFinalizeSession(sessionId).finalize(review) (the hook from 7.3.5); status 'success' → invalidate programs/current, programs/{id}/today and sessions/active (7.4) then navigate('/today', { state: { notice: 'Review saved.' } }); status 'mismatch' → 'Some entries have not been saved yet' with Retry (same key, via retry()); 'unsaved_entries' → the hook's copy; 'error' → 'The review could not be saved. Retry.' keeping form state. The screen never says saved on dispatch.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: frontend unit; component tests listed under validation
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.6.3 Playwright against the real API
- **Component:** PracticeReview (props: none; reads sessionId from route), OutputQualityField, CountField, OutputNoteField, ReviewNoteField; rendered inside SessionLayout (7.1.4).
- **State:** TanStack Query GET /sessions/{id} (key from lib/query); useReducer form state { outputQuality, outputNote, reviewNote, episodeCount, countMethod, externalCount, unplannedAgentChecks, touched }; useFinalizeSession (7.3.5) status drives the Save button (disabled while flushing/submitting) and the message region.
- **API integration:** GET /sessions/{id} (session with events, timing, tallies, review, plan; 404 → 'Session not found' with a link to Today; lifecycle 'finalized' → 'This review was already saved' with a link to Today). POST /sessions/{id}/finalize only through useFinalizeSession (7.3.5). 400 fieldErrors → inline messages by field name (no note text echoed).
- **Validation:** Rules: outputQuality required (submit shows 'Choose Yes, Partly or No' and sends nothing); counts are integers ≥ 0 or blank; blank is never converted to 0 in state or body; explicit 0 is sent as 0; outputNote and reviewNote are each optional and sent only when non-empty; Save is the only primary control. Test apps/web/src/features/review/PracticeReview.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, mocked client, fake outbox, useFinalizeSession real): 'flushes the outbox on mount then prefills S/E/agent counts from the server tallies with countMethod event', 'renders blank counts when tallies are 0 and sends no episodeCount, externalCount, unplannedAgentChecks and no countMethod', 'editing a prefilled S switches countMethod to retrospective', 'explicit 0 is sent as 0, never omitted', 'tallies off-task 1 and agent checks 1 (alsoOffTask) render both and no summed figure 2 appears', 'outputNote is optional and omitted from the body when blank; a typed one-liner is sent verbatim', 'reviewNote is optional and independent of outputNote; both can be set at once', 'Partly saves and navigates to /today with the notice Review saved.', 'recorded-time line reads X min recorded of Y min target from response.timing.elapsedSeconds and the word focused never appears', 'missing outputQuality blocks submit with Choose Yes, Partly or No and no request is sent', 'a 409 event_count_mismatch renders Some entries have not been saved yet with Retry and keeps the typed values', 'GET 404 renders Session not found with a link to Today', 'expectNoIdentifiers passes on the rendered screen'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- features/review/PracticeReview → exit 0 with all 13 named cases passing

### 8.7.1 — Build CheckinForm core: sleep, phone, desktop, save, status, stale conflict

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/checkin/CheckinForm.tsx at route /checkin/:date under RailLayout (7.1.3), plus CheckinStatus.tsx and DeviceMinutesField.tsx. Program id comes from GET /programs/current (404 → 'Set up your program first' with a link to /setup and no form). Default render shows exactly: sleep minutes, phone feed minutes, desktop feed minutes, a 'More detail' expand control (content built in 8.7.2) and the single primary 'Save' button. All numeric inputs start blank. Each headline device input maps to one feed row { device: 'phone'|'desktop', platform: 'all', minutes, measurementScope: 'feed', source: 'estimate' } (D36); a blank device input produces NO row (unknown); an explicit 0 produces a row with minutes: 0. When one or more detail rows (8.7.2) exist for a device (platform other than 'all'), that device's headline input is disabled and displays the read-only sum of that device's feed-scope minutes (D36) instead of an editable field. A server 422 feed_platform_conflict (an 'all' row and platform rows coexisting for the same device) maps to that device's headline field with 'Clear this total or remove the detail rows below' and blocks Save. Sleep blank → sleepMinutes omitted. After load/save, CheckinStatus shows 'Complete' or 'Incomplete — missing: …' from the response's status and missing fields, never inferred from record existence (D12/D36: complete = sleepMinutes reported AND at least one scope-feed row). A device with no row of any kind is labelled 'Not reported' in the summary; an explicit 0 shows '0 min'. Over-allowance minutes save with the same neutral confirmation ('Check-in saved.') — no warning tone and no comparison against leisure_allowance_min in copy. Success → navigate('/today', { state: { notice: 'Check-in saved.' } }) after invalidating programs/{id}/today.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: frontend unit; component tests listed under validation
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.7.3 Playwright against the real API
- **Component:** CheckinForm (props: none; reads date from route), CheckinStatus (props: { status, missing[] }), DeviceMinutesField (props: { device, value, onChange, disabled }).
- **State:** Query GET /programs/{id}/days/{date} — a date with no check-in returns the D22 all-null shape { checkin: all-null, feed: [], status: { status: 'not_reported', missing: ['sleep','feed'] }, aggregates, version: 0 } and the first PUT sends expectedVersion: 0; useReducer form state { sleepMinutes, deviceRows, detailRows, stress, mindfulnessMinutes, note, version }; mutation PUT with expectedVersion; on 409 stale → replace form values with the returned current record, show 'This check-in was updated elsewhere; showing the current values' and keep the user's typed values behind a secondary 'Re-apply my values' action.
- **API integration:** GET /programs/current → program.id. GET /programs/{id}/days/{date} → checkin with status (D22 all-null shape when none exists). PUT /programs/{id}/days/{date} { expectedVersion, sleepMinutes?, stress?, mindfulnessMinutes?, note?, feed: [...] } → checkin with status; 400 fieldErrors → inline; 409 stale → current values shown; 422 feed_platform_conflict → the conflicting device's headline field; other 422 → row-level messages (mapped in 8.7.2); network error → 'Could not save. Retry.' with form state kept.
- **Validation:** Rules: sleepMinutes integer 0–1440 or blank; feed minutes integer ≥ 0 or blank; blank is never coalesced to 0; explicit 0 allowed; a device's headline input and its detail rows are mutually exclusive (D36). Test apps/web/src/features/checkin/CheckinForm.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, mocked client): 'default render shows only sleep, phone, desktop, More detail and Save', 'blank sleep is omitted from the PUT body', 'blank phone sends no phone row and the summary shows phone Not reported', 'explicit phone 0 sends a row with minutes 0 and the summary shows 0 min, not Not reported', 'phone headline row is sent with platform all', 'sleep 420 and phone 30 saves with status Complete and the body carries no stress, mindfulnessMinutes or note', 'sleep-only save shows Incomplete — missing: feed taken from the response, not inferred from the record', 'adding a desktop detail row disables the desktop headline input and shows the summed total', '422 feed_platform_conflict renders on the phone headline field and blocks Save', 'stale 409 replaces the form with the returned current values and offers Re-apply my values', '55 minutes against a 20-minute allowance saves with Check-in saved. and no text matching /exceeded|over your allowance|too much|warning/i', 'after a save returning version 2, remounting and saving again sends expectedVersion 2 to the same date', 'a date with no check-in renders the D22 all-null shape and the first PUT sends expectedVersion 0', 'GET /programs/current 404 renders the Setup link and no form'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- features/checkin/CheckinForm → exit 0 with all 14 named cases passing

### 8.7.2 — Build FeedRows detail, optional fields and feed totals preview

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/checkin/FeedRows.tsx inside a Radix Collapsible opened by 'More detail': a list of feed rows with device select (phone, desktop, tablet, unspecified), platform text (required, and rejected inline as 'all' is reserved for the headline total, D36), minutes, short-video minutes, measurement-scope segmented control ('Feed only' = feed / 'Whole app' = app_total), source toggle labelled 'From device report' (device_report vs estimate), planned-window checkbox, add/remove row; plus OptionalFields with stress (0–10), mindfulness minutes and note. Adding the first detail row for a device clears and disables that device's 8.7.1 headline input (mutually exclusive per D36); removing the last detail row for a device re-enables its headline input. Create FeedTotals.tsx using packages/shared domain/feed.ts (2.6) — no client-side arithmetic of its own: 'N device-minutes (feed)' summing only scope feed rows (headline 'all' rows and detail rows alike); app_total rows listed separately as 'Broad app total: 60 min — phone Instagram'; short-video shown as 'of which short video: X min' and never as a separate category; the total is labelled 'partial' when a visible device (phone or desktop) has no row of any kind, per feed.ts. A server 422 feed_platform_conflict is shown on the device's row group rather than a specific field.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: frontend unit; component tests listed under validation
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.7.3 Playwright against the real API

- **State:** Rows live in the 8.7.1 reducer; uniqueness of (device, platform, measurementScope) enforced client-side with a row error; adding/removing the first/last detail row for a device toggles that device's 8.7.1 headline enabled/disabled state through the shared reducer; short-video error stored per row; collapsible open state local.
- **API integration:** Rows serialize into the PUT feed array from 8.7.1 as { device, platform, minutes, shortVideoMinutes?, measurementScope, source, plannedWindow? }; platform 'all' is never sent from a detail row; server 422 naming the subset rule → mapped to that row's short-video field; 422 duplicate row → row error; 422 feed_platform_conflict → the device's row group.
- **Validation:** Rules: shortVideoMinutes ≤ minutes (inline error 'Short video is a subset of feed minutes and cannot exceed them'; blocks Save); stress integer 0–10 or blank; mindfulnessMinutes integer ≥ 0 or blank; platform required on a detail row and 'all' is rejected inline; all numeric inputs start blank; optional blanks omitted from the body. ### 8.8.1 — Build Progress shell, samples line and AttemptTable

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/progress/Progress.tsx at /progress under RailLayout (7.1.3), SamplesLine.tsx, AttemptTable.tsx and ProgressEmptyState.tsx. Progress resolves GET /programs/current → GET /programs/{id}/report; with no program (404) it renders a neutral empty state linking to /setup with no zero-valued cards. SamplesLine: '{n} of 2 baseline samples eligible · {m} of 2 final samples eligible' plus 'All counts are self-reported'. AttemptTable columns: phase, label, local date, time source ('Measured' / 'Demo clock' / 'Attested'), S with method ('6 (events)' / '4 (retrospective)' / 'Not reported' — never 0 for null), T rendered from the FirstSwitch union ('20+, capped' / 'm:ss' / 'Unknown' — unknown is never rendered as 20+), recall score ('Not reported' when null; labelled self-reported), E, M, disruption answer, conditions, eligibility, exclusion reasons via EXCLUSION_REASON_COPY, protocol revision number. A row whose lifecycle is not 'finalized' (running, paused, awaiting_review, abandoned) renders that lifecycle word in place of the finalized-only columns (S, T, recall score, E, M, disruption, eligibility all show 'Not finalized' rather than a blank-as-null value) so an in-progress or abandoned attempt is never mistaken for a finalized attempt with unreported counts. Each finalized row mounts the 'Explain or exclude' AmendmentDialog (8.8.6). Realm 'demo' is shown as 'Demonstration data', never the raw value; no ids in the DOM. Sections for result state (8.8.2), trends (8.8.3) and export (8.8.4) mount as children.

- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.8.5 Playwright against the real API

- **State:** Queries programs/current and programs/{id}/report with the 7.4 refetch policy; report data passed down as props; no derived scores computed client-side (D4).
- **API integration:** GET /programs/current (404 → empty state). GET /programs/{id}/report → { realm, samples, attempts[], comparison?, resultState, warnings[], practice[], days[], revisions[] }; 422 realm mixing → error panel with the server message and no partial table; other errors → 'Report unavailable. Retry.'

### 8.8.3 — Build practice and daily trend sections with Recharts and exact values

- **Layer:** frontend

The dialog has a required reason text field (submit blocked and 'A reason is required' shown when empty) and an 'Exclude from the comparison' checkbox mapping to excludeFromReport. Submit → POST /sessions/{id}/amendments { reason, excludeFromReport } (5.9.1) → 201 → invalidate GET /programs/{id}/report (8.8.1's query) and close the dialog; the row expands to list the attempt's existing amendments (reason, exclude flag, createdAt) in creation order, taken verbatim from the session/report response's amendments[] — nothing here recomputes eligible or exclusionReasons client-side (D4); an excluding amendment is reflected only after the report refetch shows excluded_by_amendment in the row's exclusion reasons (the shared overlay is server-side, 5.9.2/5.9.3). 400 fieldErrors → inline on the reason field; 409 not_finalized (a second tab changed the row's status) → 'This attempt is no longer finalized' and the dialog closes without posting; network error → 'Could not save. Retry.' keeping the typed reason and checkbox state.

### 8.10.2 — Build ClockGapPrompt with continued, uncertain and save-incomplete resolutions

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/focus/ClockGapPrompt.tsx, mounted from SessionLayout (7.1.4) whenever the sessions/active query reports running or paused. It subscribes to the 7.2 gap detector (Date.now drift vs performance.now > 60 s on the 5 s heartbeat, D5). On a gap it enqueues a clock_gap event { clientEventId, type: 'clock_gap', elapsedMs, occurredAt, details: { gapSeconds } } through the outbox (no resolution yet) and opens a Radix AlertDialog that cannot be dismissed by outside click or Escape: title 'Did the interval continue uninterrupted?' (D26) with 'Yes, it continued' → the outbox event's details are updated to { gapSeconds, resolution: 'continued' } and POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap { gapSeconds, resolution: 'continued' } is sent; 'No' and 'Not sure' → resolution 'uncertain', same update-then-post pattern; 'Save as incomplete' → resolution 'save_incomplete', after which the session is awaiting review and the app navigates to the incomplete review — /review/:id for practice or the benchmark review route (8.4) for benchmarks, where recall is offered but may be skipped (D25). The resolution lives only in the clock_gap event's details.resolution (D26); clock_gap_seconds on the session is the server's accumulated total, not a resolution flag. After any resolution the session query is refetched and lib/clock (7.2) is re-seeded from the response's server-derived remaining, so post-gap remaining is server truth, not client arithmetic; a 'Timing uncertain' chip is shown while timer_quality === 'uncertain'. If the re-seeded remaining is ≤ 0, the screen shows awaiting review beside the prompt and never the word 'completed'. On reload, a session whose most recent unvoided clock_gap event has no details.resolution re-opens the prompt (D26); a resolved clock_gap event never re-opens it.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: frontend unit; component tests listed under validation
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.10.8 Playwright with page.clock against the real API
- **Component:** ClockGapPrompt (props: { session }), useClockGap(sessionId) hook wrapping the 7.2 detector.
- **State:** Detector subscription; dialog open flag; mutation POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap; the clock_gap event's details.resolution field, read from the session's events[] to decide whether reload re-opens the prompt; session query invalidation and clock re-seed on success; navigation after save_incomplete.
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/clock-gap { gapSeconds, resolution: continued|uncertain|save_incomplete } → session; clock_gap event (details.gapSeconds, then details.resolution) via POST /sessions/{id}/events through the outbox; 409/404 → 'This session has changed. Reloading.' then refetch.
- **Validation:** Rules: gapSeconds integer > 60; exactly one resolution per prompt; no dismissal without a choice. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/ClockGapPrompt.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, fake detector, mocked client): 'drift of 61 s opens the prompt titled Did the interval continue uninterrupted? and enqueues a clock_gap event with no resolution; 59 s does nothing', 'Yes posts resolution continued, updates the event details.resolution to continued and no uncertain chip appears', 'No and Not sure post uncertain and show Timing uncertain', 'Save as incomplete posts save_incomplete and navigates to the incomplete review where recall is skippable', 'after a resolution the clock store is re-seeded from the refetched server remaining', 'remaining ≤ 0 after the gap renders awaiting review and never completed', 'Escape and outside click do not close the dialog', 'reload with a clock_gap event whose details carry no resolution re-opens the prompt; a resolved event does not'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- features/focus/ClockGapPrompt → exit 0 with all 8 named cases passing
- **After:** 7.1.1, 8.6.2, 7.1.4, 7.2, 7.3, 7.4, 5.5, 5.3, 8.4
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Laptop slept during a benchmark · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Deadline passed during sleep · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available / Save incomplete after uncertainty

### 8.10.7 — Build AbandonSession control: confirm, abandon transition, buffer and draft purge

- **Layer:** frontend
- **Implementation:** Create apps/web/src/features/focus/AbandonSession.tsx, mounted from SessionLayout (7.1.4) as a visually subordinate 'Abandon session' action whenever the sessions/active query reports running, paused or awaiting_review (so it is available on Focus, benchmark running, recall, scoring and review screens without editing those screens). Activating it opens a Radix AlertDialog 'Abandon this session? Unsent entries on this device will be discarded; the attempt stays in your record as abandoned.' (D38) with an optional reason field. Confirm → useAbandonSession(sessionId) calls abandonWithPurge({ sessionId, expectedVersion, api }) from 7.3.4, which posts /sessions/{id}/transitions { expectedVersion, type: 'abandon', reason? } and purges the session's outbox rows itself; on success this component also removes the sessionStorage finalize:{sessionId} key (owned by 7.3.5), resets in-memory form drafts by unmounting the session route, invalidates sessions/active and programs/{id}/today, and navigates('/today', { state: { notice: 'Session abandoned.' } }). The word 'completed' never appears in this flow. 409 stale (already ended elsewhere) → refetch, show 'This session was updated in another tab', render the current state, send no second transition.
- **Unit tests:** n/a: frontend unit; component tests listed under validation
- **Integration tests:** n/a: frontend unit; covered by 8.10.8 Playwright abandon test against the real API
- **Component:** AbandonSession (props: { session }), useAbandonSession(sessionId) → { abandon(reason?), status } wrapping abandonWithPurge (7.3.4).
- **State:** Dialog open flag; abandonWithPurge (7.3.4) owns the transition call and the outbox purge; this component additionally clears the sessionStorage finalize key (7.3.5) in onSuccess.
- **API integration:** POST /sessions/{id}/transitions { expectedVersion, type: 'abandon', reason? } via abandonWithPurge (7.3.4) → session (lifecycle abandoned); 409 stale → current state returned; network error → 'Could not abandon. Retry.' with the session untouched.
- **Validation:** Rules: confirmation required; reason optional, sent only when non-empty. Test apps/web/src/features/focus/AbandonSession.test.tsx (7.1.1 harness, fake outbox, mocked client): 'renders for running, paused and awaiting_review sessions and not for finalized', 'nothing is posted without confirmation; confirm calls abandonWithPurge with type abandon and expectedVersion', 'success purges the outbox via abandonWithPurge and this component clears the finalize key, then navigates to /today with Session abandoned.', 'no text matching /\bcompleted\b/i is rendered at any step', '409 stale refetches, shows the updated-in-another-tab message and sends no second transition', 'after abandon, sessionStorage and the outbox hold nothing for the session id'.
- **Verify:** npm run test -w @attention-lab/web -- features/focus/AbandonSession → exit 0 with all 6 named cases passing
- **After:** 7.1.1, 7.3.4, 7.1.4, 7.3, 7.4, 5.4
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged · session-recovery: Stale writes conflict instead of overwriting / Two tabs end a session · app-shell: Navigation exists outside session mode only / Navigation hidden during practice · app-shell: One dominant action per screen

### 8.10.8 — Playwright: offline pending/retry, simulated sleep, reload, two contexts, abandon

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** Write e2e/recovery.spec.ts against the built app in local-demo mode; load the recovery scenario via request.post('/api/v1/demo/scenarios/{name}/load') (no body, D22) where needed; page.clock.install() in beforeEach; every time advance uses advance(page, request, seconds) from e2e/support/clock.ts (7.1.5) so the server's demo clock moves with the browser clock; afterEach reset(request) from the same module. Test 1 'connection lost': start a practice block; context.setOffline(true); click Record off-task twice → the tally reads 2 within 100 ms (expect with timeout 100), status 'Pending' with Retry (SyncStatus, 8.5.2), the countdown still decreasing; setOffline(false); click Retry → 'Saved'; request.get('/api/v1/sessions/{id}') shows exactly 2 non-voided off_task events. Test 2 'start offline': setOffline(true) on Today, type an output, press Start → 'The session could not be started' (useStartSession, 7.4.4) and the URL stays /today; setOffline(false), Retry → /focus/:id and GET /api/v1/sessions/active returns one session. Test 3 'laptop slept during a benchmark': start baseline A; advance(3 min); page.clock.setSystemTime(Date.now() + 5 min) together with request.post('/api/v1/demo/clock') adding 5 min (wall clock jumps, performance.now does not); page.clock.runFor(5 s) → the ClockGapPrompt (8.10.2) 'Did the interval continue uninterrupted?' appears; choose 'Not sure' → 'Timing uncertain' chip; Stop early, complete recall and finalize with disruption No and S 0 → the eligibility summary lists the timer_uncertain reason copy. Test 4 'Yes variant': same setup, choose 'Yes, it continued' → no 'Timing uncertain' chip and, after Stop early and finalize, the summary does not list the timer_uncertain copy. Test 5 'deadline passed during sleep': start baseline A; setSystemTime(+21 min) with the matching demo-clock offset; runFor(5 s) → prompt; choose 'Yes, it continued' → the screen shows awaiting review and no text matches /\bcompleted\b/i. Test 6 'refresh during practice': start a 15-minute block; advance(6 min); setOffline(true); Record once; setOffline(false); page.reload() → the SessionModeProvider (7.4.2) restores the session, timer reads 09:00 ± 2 s and a POST /api/v1/sessions/{id}/events request with the buffered client event id is observed; GET /api/v1/sessions/{id} shows that event once. Test 7 'refresh after target': advance(16 min); reload → awaiting review, no completed text. Test 8 'second tab': context A starts a practice block; context B opens /today → ActiveSessionCard (8.2.5) shows 'Return to session' and no Start; B's request.post('/api/v1/sessions') → 409 active_session_exists. Test 9 'two tabs end': A and B both on /focus/:id; A clicks Finish early; B clicks Finish early → B's ActiveSessionCard shows 'This session was updated in another tab' and lands on the review; GET /api/v1/sessions/{id} lifecycle is awaiting_review with a single end transition recorded. Test 10 'abandon purges the buffer': start a block; setOffline(true); Record once; Abandon session (8.10.7) → confirm; setOffline(false) → Today shows 'Session abandoned.'; page.evaluate reads the outbox store and finds no entries for the session id; GET /api/v1/sessions/{id} lifecycle abandoned; no text matches /\bcompleted\b/i.
- **Verify:** npx playwright test e2e/recovery.spec.ts → 10 passed
- **After:** 8.5.2, 8.10.2, 7.4.2, 8.2.5, 7.3.5, 7.4.4, 8.10.7, 8.6.3, 7.1.5, 8.3, 8.4, 8.5, 3.5, 2.8, 1.5
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Connection lost mid-session · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Acknowledged after retry · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Batch retried · session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start while offline · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Laptop slept during a benchmark · session-recovery: Clock gaps are detected and resolved by the user / Deadline passed during sleep · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session / Refresh during practice · session-recovery: Reload recovers the active session / Refresh after target passed · session-recovery: Stale writes conflict instead of overwriting / Two tabs end a session · session-recovery: Abandon and save-incomplete are always available · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged · practice-sessions: One unfinished session per user / Second tab · app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing / Sync state display

## 9. End-to-end acceptance and accessibility

### 9.1.1 — Acceptance harness: Playwright project config and harness spec

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** Extend `e2e/playwright.config.ts` (1.5) with project `acceptance`: `testMatch` covering `e2e/acceptance/**`, `e2e/invariants/**`, `e2e/a11y/**`; `workers: 1`, `fullyParallel: false` (one fixed principal `local-demo`, D2); `retries: 0` (a retry would mask an idempotency defect); `trace: 'retain-on-failure'`; `use.baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:8787'` (single origin, D37 — the API serves the built web app, no separate preview server). `webServer`: run `npm run build -w @attention-lab/web` then start the API's `tsx` entry (3.2.5, D37 — no compiled API artifact) with env `IDENTITY_MODE=local-demo`, `API_HOST=127.0.0.1`, `API_PORT=8787`, `WEB_DIST_DIR=apps/web/dist` (SPA fallback for non-`/api` paths), `DATABASE_URL` set to the `db:up` Postgres (D37 — not `attention_lab_test`; tests isolate with `POST /demo/reset`), readiness `url` `http://127.0.0.1:8787/api/v1/me`. Playwright support helpers (`e2e/support/{demo,clock,copy,a11y}.ts`) are owned by 7.1.5 (D16) and imported here, never redefined: `e2e/acceptance/harness.spec.ts` imports the `demo`/`test` fixture from `e2e/support/demo.ts`, `DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES` (2.8) and the `Static<>` contract types (2.7) from `@attention-lab/shared`. Named tests: (1) 'reset returns 204 and Today shows the new-user empty state: /today routes to /setup and GET /programs/current has nextAction setup'; (2) 'every slug in `DEMO_SCENARIO_NAMES` (new-user, working-day, comparable-change, mixed-result, missing-final, zero-baseline, recovery, timing-deviation) loads with a programId and GET /programs/current plus /report match the expectations the fixture module exports (working-day: day 4; comparable-change, mixed-result, missing-final, zero-baseline: resultState; recovery: an active running practice session; timing-deviation: a running baseline A on Day 0 with an unresolved clock_gap event, D35)'; (3) 'readyProgram() yields status baseline_ready, four slots, nextAction benchmark for baseline A and no active session; completePracticeBlock() returns a finalized practice session with countMethod retrospective'; (4) 'skip to Day 14: on Day 0 the final A ready screen offers no operable Start and shows its scheduled local date; after setDay(14) Today next action is final A and a benchmark started from it has timeSource demo_clock'.
- **Verify:** `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts --list` shows the `acceptance` project; `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/acceptance/harness.spec.ts` -> 4 passed against the built web app served single-origin by the API on 127.0.0.1:8787.
- **After:** 1.2, 1.3, 1.4, 1.5, 2.7, 2.8, 3.2.5, 3.5, 4.1, 4.2, 4.3, 5.1, 5.2, 5.4, 5.8, 6.2, 7.1.5, 8.2, 8.3

- **Layer:** e2e

Add `e2e/support/benchmark.ts` exporting `runBenchmark(page, demo, {slotId, events: [{atSeconds, type: 'off_task'|'external'}], undoAt?, recallDelaySeconds, recallPoints (5 strings, blanks allowed), recallDurationSeconds, accurate: boolean[], episodeCountOverride?, disruption: 'no'|'yes'})` that drives ready -> Start -> events via the UI at the given elapsed seconds (using `advance`) -> deadline -> 'Close your reading material' -> the ready-for-recall control after the delay -> five inputs -> Save recall -> Accurate/Not accurate -> S/E confirm -> disruption -> Finalize, returning the session id (reused by 9.1.9 and 9.2.3). Named tests: (1) 'Day 0: Today next action is baseline A; the ready screen is labeled a fixed 20-minute assessment, shows the material reference and planned time, carries the leaving this page to read does not count note, and GET /sessions/active is 204 until Start'; (2) 'baseline A events: Record at 07:42 stores one off_task with elapsedMs 462000 +/-1000 and tally 1; External at 12:00 stores one external; Record twice at 15:00 then Undo leaves two non-voided off_task events and one with voided_at set (D9); tally reads 2'; (3) 'baseline A review: ready for recall 40 s after the deadline -> review.recallDelaySeconds in 38..42 and recallFlags []; Save recall locks the five points and the scoring screen shows them read-only with no editable input; Accurate x3 with points 4-5 blank -> recallScore 3 and the blanks read scored 0 because blank; S prefilled 2 method event, E prefilled 1; disruption No -> Finalize returns eligible true, exclusionReasons [], firstSwitch {kind: known, seconds: 462}; the summary labels counts self-reported'; (4) 'baseline B after advance(3600): no events; S entered 4 -> countMethod retrospective and T renders Unknown, never 20+; Save recall after 240 s -> recallFlags [recall_overrun] visible in review; eligible true'; (5) 'Progress after both baselines: 2 of 2 baseline samples eligible, final-pending copy Your final comparison is available after the Day 14 assessments., no percent sign, attempts table lists A with T of 462 s rendered as minutes and seconds (method event) and B with T Unknown, every count labeled self-reported; CSV rows carry S 2 and 4, methods event and retrospective, T states known and unknown'; (6) 'a practice block completed on Day 0 via completePracticeBlock stays practice: samples remain 0 of 2, the attempts table stays empty and Today still offers baseline A'.

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** `e2e/acceptance/recovery.spec.ts`; `demo.load('recovery')` (existing running practice session); `installClock`. Named tests: (1) 'Focus resumes the scenario session instead of offering a new start' - `/today` leads to `/focus/:id` where id equals `demo.active().id`; (2) 'offline: two records show Pending with Retry, the timer keeps counting, each tally update renders within 100 ms' - `context.setOffline(true)`; around each Record click a `page.evaluate` MutationObserver stamps `performance.now()` of the tally change; delta < 100; sync word Pending; Retry visible; `advance(10)` reduces the countdown by 10 +/-1 s; `demo.session(id).events` (server side) still holds neither; (3) 'Retry after reconnect -> Saved; the server holds exactly two events; the outbox is empty' - `setOffline(false)`, Retry -> Saved; two non-voided off_task events with distinct clientEventId; IndexedDB outbox (database name per 7.3) has 0 rows; (4) 're-sending the same batch stores nothing twice' - `POST /sessions/{id}/events` with the same two clientEventIds -> `accepted: []`, `duplicates` lists both; still 2 events; (5) 'sync state is plain words and no technical identifier' - the sync-state locator text is one of Pending / Saved / could not be saved; `expectNoTechnicalIds`; (6) 'IndexedDB refused -> could not be saved on this device with a keep-trying-the-server control; the event still reaches the server' - new page with `addInitScript` making `indexedDB.open` throw; (7) 'Finish early with time remaining: the review prefills S 2 method event; Save -> finalized with completeInterval false and elapsed (endedAt - startedAt - pausedSeconds) below targetSeconds; Today shows the block as finished early, never completed, and the next block still available with neutral copy' plus `expectNoPunitiveCopy`.
- **Verify:** `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/acceptance/recovery.spec.ts` -> 7 passed.
- **After:** 9.1.1, 9.1.2, 8.2, 8.5, 8.6, 8.10, 7.3, 5.3, 5.4, 5.8
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Events are buffered, deduplicated and acknowledged / Batch retried · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Connection lost mid-session · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Local storage unavailable · session-recovery: Pending, saved and could-not-save are distinct / Acknowledged after retry · session-recovery: Recovery buffer is bounded and purged / Buffer after sync · practice-sessions: Session review saves honest outcomes / Early finish · app-shell: Implementation details are not user-facing / Sync state display · identity-realm: Demo-only controls exist only in demo mode / Load a demonstration scenario

### 9.2.4 — Invariant: idempotent start and finalize under lost responses and mismatches

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** `e2e/invariants/idempotency.spec.ts`; `demo.load('working-day')`, start block 2 via the UI unless stated. Named tests: (1) 'server truth: 4 stored, finalize claims 5 -> 409 `event_count_mismatch` (D19) whose `details` (D18) is `{expected: 5, stored: 4}`, plus `retryable: true` and `requestId`; the missing batch then a retry with the same Idempotency-Key -> 200 and one review' - record 4 events; `POST /sessions/{id}/transitions {type:'end', expectedVersion}`; `POST /sessions/{id}/finalize` with `Idempotency-Key: K`, `expectedEventCount: 5`, `review: {outputQuality:'yes'}` -> the 409 above; `POST /sessions/{id}/events` with a fifth clientEventId; retry with K -> 200; `demo.session(id)` finalized with exactly one review; the captured body is the reference the mock in test 2 imitates; (2) 'browser: a 409 on the first finalize makes the client flush its outbox and retry with the same Idempotency-Key; one finalized session with five events' - `page.route` on `**/finalize`: first call `route.fulfill({status: 409, json: <the shape captured in test 1>})`, later calls `route.continue()`; record 5 events with the fifth Pending while the events route is aborted; unroute events; Finish early; Save review; the `Idempotency-Key` headers of both finalize requests are equal and the retry carries the pending event in `lastBatch` or a preceding events POST; server: 5 events, one review, lifecycle finalized; no error remains on screen; (3) 'replaying finalize with the same key returns the recorded result' - same K -> 200, `finalizedAt` unchanged; (4) 'a late event after finalize is stored with a reconciliation warning and the review is unchanged' - `POST events` -> response carries the reconciliation warning field of the 5.3 contract; `review.episodeCount` unchanged; Progress practice count unchanged; (5) 'start while offline: no timer starts, the screen says the session could not be started, and GET /sessions/active is 204 after reconnecting' - `context.setOffline(true)` before Start, no `/focus` route; (6) 'start whose response was lost: `page.route` fetches POST /sessions then aborts it; the screen says the session could not be started and no timer runs; pressing Start again renders the existing session (same-key replay -> 200, or a fresh key -> 409 `active_session_exists` with `details.activeSessionId`, D18/D19) and exactly one session exists'.
- **Verify:** `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/invariants/idempotency.spec.ts` -> 6 passed.
- **After:** 9.1.1, 9.1.2, 8.2, 8.5, 8.6, 8.10, 3.4, 5.1, 5.3, 5.4, 5.8
- **Spec refs:** session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Event count mismatch · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Finalize retried · session-recovery: Finalization is atomic and idempotent / Late event · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start retried after timeout · session-recovery: Starting a session requires the server / Start while offline · benchmark-assessment: Finalized attempts are immutable; amendments are append-only

### 9.4.1 — Live-region assertion: countdown emits no per-second announcements

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** `e2e/a11y/timer-live-region.spec.ts`. An `addInitScript` installs a `MutationObserver` on `document` (childList, characterData, subtree) that appends to `window.__liveLog` every mutation whose target lies inside `[aria-live]:not([aria-live="off"]), [role=status], [role=alert], [role=log], [role=timer][aria-live=polite], [role=timer][aria-live=assertive]`. Milestone announcements follow `preferences.milestoneAnnouncements` (D39; default false, 5:00 and 0:00 only). Named tests: (1) 'Focus countdown with the default preference: 120 simulated seconds produce zero live-region mutations, and the countdown element carries no aria-live or aria-live=off' - `demo.load('working-day')`, start, `advance(120)`, `__liveLog.length === 0`; (2) 'Benchmark countdown: zero live-region mutations over 120 s' - `demo.readyProgram()`; (3) 'Recall countdown: zero live-region mutations over 60 s'; (4) 'timer hidden: zero live-region mutations over 120 s, the remaining time is not in the DOM until revealed, and at the target the session still reaches awaiting review'; (5) 'milestone announcements enabled (D39): `PATCH /me/preferences {milestoneAnnouncements: true}`, then advancing a Focus countdown across 5:00 and across 0:00 each produce exactly one polite live-region mutation naming that milestone and no others'. Each test also proves the observer is live by asserting the countdown text changed across `advance` (or, when hidden, that the revealed value differs).
- **Verify:** `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/a11y/timer-live-region.spec.ts` -> 5 passed.
- **After:** 9.1.1, 9.1.2, 8.3, 8.4, 8.5

### 9.4.2 — Reduced motion disables transitions and animations

- **Layer:** e2e
- **Implementation:** `e2e/a11y/reduced-motion.spec.ts` with `page.emulateMedia({ reducedMotion: 'reduce' })` before navigation. A helper `expectNoMotion(page)` evaluates over every element: each `transitionDuration` entry parses to 0 and (`animationName === 'none'` or `animationDuration` parses to 0); `document.getAnimations().length === 0`; no SVG `<animate>` / `<animateTransform>` elements. Named tests: (1) 'Today -> Progress route change under reduce runs no animations' - `demo.load('comparable-change')`, navigate, `expectNoMotion` on both screens; (2) 'Focus: opening/closing the agent panel and the clock-gap prompt runs no animations and the progress indicator updates without transition' - `demo.load('working-day')`, start, open/close panel, `sleep(300)` prompt, `expectNoMotion` after each; (3) 'Progress charts and the result-state card render with no animations' - Recharts must render with animation disabled under reduce (verified by `getAnimations()` and absence of `<animate>`); (4) 'Check-in expand control swaps content instantly' - click expand, `expectNoMotion` immediately after.
- **Verify:** `npx playwright test -c e2e/playwright.config.ts e2e/a11y/reduced-motion.spec.ts` -> 4 passed.
- **After:** 9.1.1, 9.1.2, 7.1, 8.2, 8.5, 8.7, 8.8, 8.10
- **Spec refs:** app-shell: Accessibility baseline / Reduced motion

the API runs from TypeScript source under tsx and serves the Vite build on one origin via WEB_DIST_DIR (3.2.5, D37) — no compiled API artifact. ## What was tested — unit (every packages/shared domain function; PRD §6 / PoC §12 table-driven); API (Fastify inject against attention_lab_test: idempotency replay/409, one active session, one open program, slot freeze, recall lock, finalize count mismatch, blank count stored null, realm stamped demo regardless of body, ownership 404, mode/host/real refusals, no note text in logs, no-store header, no COALESCE(...,0) grep, AJV configured coerceTypes: false, removeAdditional: false, useDefaults: false so a JSON null is never coerced to 0 and unknown request keys are rejected as 400 — D22); e2e (eight journeys, hidden-tab-no-episode, refresh mid-benchmark, sleep past deadline stays awaiting review, two-tab conflict, keyboard-only review, axe on six screens, no per-second live-region announcements, touch targets sized to the WCAG 2.2 AA 24×24 px minimum verified manually against the core P0 screens — D40) with the file or command as evidence. ## Recovery: simulated vs field-tested — route.abort, context.setOffline and page.clock simulations only, never field-tested on real network loss or device sleep; recovery and hidden-tab behavior specifically verified under paired simulated clocks (page.clock + POST /demo/clock), not field-tested (D40); graceful shutdown (SIGTERM/SIGINT) verified on POSIX only, not on Windows (D40). ## Identity gate — D2 + D7.6: real refuses listing AUTH_SECRET, GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID, GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET, AUTH_INVITE_ALLOWLIST; no pilot row can exist; demo bound to loopback; Douglas cannot start a real Day 0 on this build. ## Thresholds chosen — recall delay 600 s and duration 210 s (flags, never exclusions), clock-gap 60 s, heartbeat 5 s, batch <= 100 events, mutation_receipts expire after 7 days measured on the demo clock (D40, not wall-clock time), baseline A/B >= 1 h, band ceilings, each with the shared/domain file that holds the constant; 429 write_limit is a reserved error code with no rate limiter implemented in this change — loopback only (D19). ## Deferred — proposal.md's list plus attested time-source entry UI, Day 7 midpoint UI, full offline, and the three research cards' provenance, recorded as abstract-reviewed pending Douglas's confirmation (D40). ## Reversible decision: local-pilot — what one decision would change (identity plugin only; realm and user_id columns exist); preference defaults (hideTimerDefault, endChime, milestoneAnnouncements, visibilityContext) are constants in code, not env settings (D22). ## Task-group coverage — a table with rows | 1 | … | 10 | naming each group's evidence. Add scripts/check-limitations.mjs asserting the ten headings, the ten group rows, and the literals 600, 210, 60 s, local-pilot, attention_lab_test, AUTH_INVITE_ALLOWLIST, coerceTypes: false, write_limit, demo clock, POSIX, 24×24, abstract-reviewed.
